(5) pacumai
putumaiyāṉa muṉ paruvattil, atāvatu iḷamparuvattil, payir paccaikaḷ pacumaiyāyiruppatāl, putumaik karuttil pacumaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. pacumai ceḻippaik kuṟikkumātalāl, putumaiccol uṭaṟ ceḻippaiyum uṇarttum.
uṭal ceḻimpāyiruttalaip putupputu veṉṟiruttal eṉpatu marapu. putu eṉṉum col ipporuḷil perumpālum pucu eṉṟu tiriyum. pucuppucu veṉṟiruttal = makkaḷ uṭal ceḻimpāyiruttal. putu - pucu - pucuppu = uṭaṟceḻippu.
pucu - pacu - pacumai = putumai, iḷamai, meṉmai, paccai niṟam, ceḻimpu.
putumeḻukkaip pacumeḻukku eṉṟār kaṭiyalūr
uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār  (paṭṭiṉappālai, 166.)
pacu - pacca - paccai = pacumai, paciya poruḷ. pacumai kaṇṇukkiṉiya niṟamātalāl, kaṇṇukkiṉitāyiruttalaip pacceṉṟiruttal eṉpar.
paccuṭampu = iḷanta uṭampu.

paccu - paccaṭi = vēvikkāmaṟ paccaiyākac ceyyum kūṭṭu. camaikkāta kāykaṟikaḷ perumpālum paccai niṟamāyirup patāl, pacumaiccol camaiyāmaip poruḷait tantatu. paccaiveṭṭu paccūṉ paccarici paccai veṇṇey paccait taṇṇīr mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷ ikkaruttuppaṟṟiyaṉa. paccai eṉṉuñcol, uṇaviṉ tiruntā nilaiyaik kuṟittalpōl, moḻiyiṉ tiruntā nilaiyaiyum kuṟikkum. iṭakkarppēccu paccaippēccu eṉappaṭum.
pacu - paca. pacattal = paccaiyātal, paca - pacaḷ - pacaṇṭai = pacamai.
paca - pacal - pacalai = paciya poṉṉiṟat tēmal, pacalai - payalai = tēmal.
pacu - pācu - pācam (moss). pācu - pāci = pācam, paccaippayaṟu.
pācu - pācavaṉ = paccūṉ viṟpavaṉ.
pacu - (paci) - (payi) - pai. paimai = pacumai, iḷamai. meṉmai.
akara ikaram aikāramākum (tol. 54.)
pai - paittu = pacumai. pai - paiyal = iḷaiñaṉ, ciṟuvaṉ. paiyal - paiyaṉ.
paiyal - payal - payaṉ - pacaṉ.
pai - paitu - paital = paiyaṉ, kuḻavi.
pai - payir = paccaiyāṉa pulceṭi koṭi. payirpaccai paiṅkūḻ eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
pai - payaṟu = paccaippayaṟu, piṟapayaṟu, payaṟu; payaṟupaccai eṉpatu vaḻakku. payaṟu - payaṟi = payaṟṟammai.

(6) meṉmai
iḷamaiyil evvuyiriyum meṉmaiyāyiruppatāl. iḷamaik karuttil meṉmaik karuttum tōṉṟum. kutalai = meṉmai, meṉmoḻi.
maḻa - maḻamaḻappu = meṉmai. maḻalai = meṉmai, meṉmoḻi, maḻaṟutal = meṉmaiyātal.
maḻa - mata - maṭa - maṭam = meṉmai.
paiya = pacumai, iḷamai, meṉmai.
paimai = pacumai. iḷamai, meṉmai.
paiya = mella. paiyap paiya - payappaya - paippaya.
pai - paiyā. paiyāttal = ciṟumaippaṭutal, tuṉpattait tāṅkamuṭiyāvāṟu meṉmaiyātal, tuṉpuṟutal. paiyuḷ = tuṉpam.

(7) muṉ taḷḷi varutal
tōṉṟiya poruḷ muṉṉāka nīṇṭu varutal muṉ taḷḷi varutalākum.
tuntu - tunti = muṉtaḷḷiya vayiṟu. tunti - tonti.
turuttutal = muṉ taḷḷutal. vayiṟu turuttik koṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
(tuḷ) - taḷ.
puṭaittal - muṉ taḷḷutal, vīṅkutal.
mulai = muṉ taḷḷiya mārpu. (moñcu) - moñci = mulai.

(8) tarutal
vāḻai kulaiyīṉutalaik kulaitaḷḷutal eṉṟum, teṉṉai paṉai pāḷai viṭutalaip pāḷai taḷḷutal eṉṟum, nel pul katir īṉutalaik katir taḷḷutal eṉṟum, kūṟuvar. marañceṭi koṭikaḷ kāy kaṉiyōṭirukkum pōtu makkaḷ avaṟṟaip paṟittal avai koṭukka avar koḷvatu pōliruttalāl; avai kāyttuk kaṉitalaip palaṉ tarutal allatu palaṉ koṭuttal eṉpar. itaṉāl, taḷḷutaṟ karuttil tarutaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
taḷ - taru - tār - tā - ta. tār - tar.
taḷ eṉṉum viṉai tarutaṟ poruḷil taru eṉat tirintu, ataṉ mēlum, etirmaṟai viṉaivil tār allatu tā eṉṟum (tārāṉ, tarāṉ), ēvalviṉaiyil tā eṉṟum, iṟanta kāla viṉaiyil ta eṉṟum (tantāṉ), tirintuḷḷatu.
yakara meyyīṟākat tiriyāta ḷakara meyyīṟṟu iyaṟ coṟka ḷellām, potuvāka rukaravīṟṟavākat tirikiṉṟaṉa.
v.L.: kaḷ - karu - kār (karumai).
iḷamaiyai uṇarttum kuru nuru muru mutaliya aṭikaḷellām, kul nul pul mul mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa kuḷ nuḷ puḷ muḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyē.
kaḷ - karu - kār eṉṉum tiripu pōṉṟatē taḷ - taru - tār eṉpatum, muḻuttiripu nilaiyilum, tarutal viṉai varutal viṉaiyai ottuḷḷatu.
vaḷ - varu - vār - vā - va - vār - var.
arumpaṇṭattaik kuṟikkum tāram eṉṉum col. tār eṉṉum tiripaṭiyākap piṟantatu. kaṭal tarum pala poruḷ kaṭaṟpaḵṟāram (puṟam. 30).
uyartiṇaiyilum iyaṅkutiṇaiyāṉa aḵṟiṇaiyilum, piḷḷaiyai allatu kuṭṭiyai īṉum tāy kulai taḷḷum allatu kāyīṉum marattaip pōliruppatāl, tāykkut taḷḷai eṉṟu peyar. oru peṇṭu piḷḷai peṟṟāl, avaḷ pukuntakattiṟku allatu kaṇavaṉ kuṭikku oru piḷḷai tanta tākak karutappaṭuvāḷ. makap peṟutal tarutal eṉappaṭum.
puyaṅkamelāñ cutaiyeṉṉumātu tantāḷ  (kampa. caṭāyukāṇ. 28).
piḷḷai peṟuvatil tāy tantaiyar iruvar viṉaiyuṅ kalan tiruttalāl, avviruvarukkum tā eṉpatu potup peyarām. āyiṉum, īṉṟāḷukku āy, tāy (tam + āy) mutaliya taṉip peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutalālum, piḷḷaiyait tōṟṟu vippavaṉ tantaiyēyātalāṉum, tā eṉpatu perumpālum tantaiyaiyē kuṟikkum.
tātā - tāttā = tantaiyiṉ tantaiyākiya pāṭṭaṉ. tātā - tātai.
pukunta kuṭikkup piḷḷaiyait tarupavaḷ tāyeṉṟum, maṉaivikkup piḷḷaiyait tarupavaṉ tantai yeṉṟum, vēṟu pāṭaṟiyappaṭum. tantai taruvatu vintu nilai; tāy taruvatu piḷḷai nilai.
(9) muṟcelavu
nutalutal = muṟkūṟutal, kūṟit toṭaṅkutal.
nutalippukutal = iṉṉatu kūṟuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟappōkum poruḷaic collit toṭaṅkum nūlutti.
tūtu = muṉviṭukkum ceyti. acceyti colpavar, tūtu - tūtaṉ tūtuvaṉ. aracarum kātalarum oruvaroruvariṭaic celvatai muṉṉaṟivittaṟkāka viṭukkum ceytiyē, mutaṉmutal tūtu eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpu atu ceyti eṉṉum potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭatu. kaṇṇaṉ tūtu, aṅkataṉ tūtu mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka.

	ūkāraccuṭṭu - 3) muṟcelavu
iyakkattil muṉṉuṟalukku aṭuttatu muṟcelavu. muṉṉatu iṭattu nikaḻvum piṉṉatu iṭam peyarvum ākum.
muṟcelavu, tāṉē cellalum taḷḷappaṭṭuc cellalum eṉa iruvakaittu.
(1) muṉtaḷḷal
(i) kāṟṟai mūkkālum vāyālum taḷḷal
uy - uyir. uyirttal = mūccuviṭutal. mūccē uyirnāṭiyum uyirnilaikku aṭaiyāḷamumātalāl, āvi uyir eṉappaṭṭatu. uḷḷē pōṉāla mūccu, veḷiyē pōṉāl pōccu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyai nōkkuka. uyiraiyum uyirppaiyum vaṭanūlār pirāṇaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutalaiyuṅ kāṇka. uyttal = muṉ taḷḷal, celuttutal.
kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuppatum veḷiviṭuvatum ākiya iraṇṭum cērntatē mūccāyiṉum mika veḷippaṭaiyāṉatum oli viḷaippatum veḷiyiṭuvatē yātaliṉ, mūccu uyirppeṉap paṭṭatu. mūccu viṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē.
untu - unti = pēcumpōtu vāyvaḻik kāṟṟait taḷḷuvatākak karutappaṭum koppūḻiṉ uṭpakkam. koppūḻ.
unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (piṟappiyal 1).
untiyiliruntu eḻuppappaṭum kāṟṟaik kuṟikkum utāṉaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacol, utu eṉṉum aṭiyaik koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
untiyil tōṉṟum utāṉa vaḷippiṟantu
eṉpatu nēminātam (6).
tummutal = mūkku vaḻiyāyk kāṟṟait taḷḷutal.
turattutal = vāyvaḻit tīya kāṟṟai vaṉmaiyāyc celuttutal (Cough).
mucumucuttal = kuṟaṭṭai viṭutal.
muka - mūcu. mūcutal = mūccai viṭutal, mōppam piṭittal.
mūcu - mūccu. mūcu - mūñcu - mūñci = mūkku, mūkkuḷḷa mukappakuti (muzzle). 
mūñcutal = mūccu viṭutal, mōppam piṭittal.
mūñcu - mūñcuṟu - mūñcūṟu = mūñci nīṇṭa allatu etaiyum mōppam piṭikkiṉṟa eli; teṉṉāṭṭār itaṉai mūñceli eṉpar.
mūñcu - mūñcai = nīṇṭa mūkku allatu mukam.
oruvaṉ iḷaittuk kaḷaitta nilaiyil viraintu valitāy mūccu viṭiṉ, mūcu mūceṉṟu iḷaikkiṟāṉ eṉpar. ivvolik kuṟippu oppoliyum cuṭṭoliyum kalantatākum.
muka - muci, mucittal = mūcciḻaittal, kaḷaittal, iḷaittal, melital.
mucippāṟutal = iḷaippāṟutal.
(ii) kāṟṟaik kaiyāl taḷḷal
ūtutal = vāyāl ūtutalpōl kollaṉ turuttiyāṟ kāṟṟait taḷḷutal.

utaittal = muṟceluttutal, kālāl muṉ taḷḷutal.

untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
untu - uñcu - uñcal = untappaṭum tāppicai.
uñcal - ūñcal - ūcal.
uṉṉutal = untutal.
(untu - unti = taḷḷit taḷḷip paṟakkum taṭṭāṉ).

koḻittal = muṉ taḷḷutal. puṭaippavaḷ kūlattōṭu kalantuḷḷa maṇṇaiyum, nīralai aṭimaṇṇaiyum, muṉ taḷḷutal koḻittal eṉappaṭum. 
cuṇṭutal = viralāl muṉ taḷḷutal allatu teṟittal.
(tuḷ) - taḷ. taḷḷutal eṉṉum viṉai mutalāvatu muṉ taḷḷutalaiyē kuṟittatu.
tūṇṭutal = viḷakkut tiriyai muṉ taḷḷutal, ūrti vilaṅkai muṟceluttutal, ēvalaṉai muṉ taḷḷuvatu pōl oru viṉaimēl ēvutal.
tūṇṭu - tīṇṭu. tūṇṭāviḷakku tīṇṭāviḷakku eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
nuntutal = tiriyait tūṇṭutal. nuntu - nontu. nuntāviḷakku nontāviḷakku eṉavum paṭum.
nūṅku - nūkku. nūkkutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
nūvutal = tiriyait tūṇṭutal, nīrai muṉ iṟaittup pāyccutal.
nūvu - nīvu; nīvāta tīpam (matāṉ. 140)
(nū) - nī. nīttal = taḷḷutal, celuttutal,

(2) nīkkal
vēṇṭāveṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭa poruḷ nīkkap paṭṭatātaliṉ, taḷḷaṟkaruttu nīkkaṟkaruttait taḻuviyatākum.
uttutal = kaḻittal, nīkkutal.
uttu - ottu. ottutal = taḷḷutal. otti vaittal= taḷḷi vaittal.
otuṅkutal = tāṉē taḷḷatal (ta.vi.) otukkutal = oru poruḷait taḷḷutal, oru kuṟippiṭṭa payaṉukkāka oṉṟait taḷḷi vaittal. (pi.vi.)
otuṅkavaittal = vaḻiyilum aṟai naṭuvilum uḷḷavaṟṟai oru puṟamākat taḷḷi vaittal.
otuṅku - otukku - otukkam.
koḻi - kaḻi. kaḻital = nīṅkutal. kaḻittal = nīkkutal. oru tokai yiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru tokaiyai nīkkutal pōṟ kuṟaital.
(tuḷ) - taḷ. taḷḷutal = nīkkutal.
tura - tuṟa. tuṟattal = nīkkutal. tuṟa - tuṟavu - tuṟavi = paṟṟu nīkkiyavaṉ.
tuṟa - tuṟakkam = tuṟavāṟpeṟum vīṭu.
o. nō. viṭu - vīṭu = paṟṟa viṭukaiyāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam.
tuṟakkam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu vīṭṭaiyē kuṟittatu; piṉ, ataippōṉṟē viṇṇiluḷḷatum iṉpantuykkap peṟuvatumāṉa (allatu aṅṅaṉam uḷḷatum peṟuvatumākak karutappaṭu vatāṉa) tēvarulakattaik kuṟittatu. varkkam eṉṉum vaṭacol cuvar (uyarattiluḷḷatu) eṉṉum pakutiyaṭi yākap piṟantiruppatāl, atai itaṉoṭu mayakkuvatu cariyaṉṟu. mēlēḻulakaṅkaḷuḷ cuvarlōkam eṉpatoṉṟākak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. cuvar eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērp poruḷai mēṟ celaviyalil uyartaṟ pakutiyiṟ kāṇka.
tūrttal = kuppai kūḷattaik kūṭṭit taḷḷutal.
nututtal = nīkkutal, aḻittal, avittal.
iṉṉal nutukkum taṇkavitai vaḷḷal  (vināyaka pu. 46, 4).
neyyāl erinutuppēm eṉṟaṟṟāṟ keḷavaiyāl 
kāmam nutuppēm eṉal   (kuṟaḷ, 1158).
(nū) - nī, nīttal = nīkkutal. nīttār = tuṟantār.
(nūṅku) - nīṅku. nūkku - nīkku.
(3) tuḷḷutal
tuḷḷutalāvatu kutittal, atu muṉṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum mēṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum eṉa iruvakaittu. mēṉōkkum pōtu mēṟṟicaiyum muṉṟicaiyāki viṭuvatāl atuvum muṉṟicaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
ukaḷutal = tāvutal. ukaḷittal = kutittal.
kutittal = tāvutal, tāṇṭutal, kaṭattal kūttāṭutal.
kuti - kutirai = tāvic cellum vilaṅku
kūṟṟaṅ kutittaluṅ kaikūṭum  (kuṟaḷ 269)
kutittal kaṭattal (vellutal)
kuti - kūttu - kūttaṉ.
tuḷḷutal = kutittal. tuḷḷal = kūttaṉ.
tuḷ - tuḷḷi - tuḷi = tuḷḷiviḻum nīrttivalai, ciṟṟaḷavu. tuḷ - tuḷḷam = tuḷi. tuḷi - tumi.
(tūṇṭu) - tāṇṭu. tāṇṭutal = tāvutal, kutittal, kaṭattal.
tāy eṭṭaṭi tāṇṭiṉāl kuṭṭi patiṉāṟaṭi tāṇṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.

tumukku (te.) = tāṇṭu.
(tūvu) - tāvu. tāvul = tāṇṭutal.
(4) teḷittal
teḷittalāvatu oṉṟai aḷḷi muṉṉāka eṟital allatu iṭutal. nīrait teḷikkumpōtu atu tuḷit tuḷiyākat tuḷḷi viḻutalāl, tuḷḷutaṟ karuttil teḷittaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
ukutal = muṉtuḷḷi viḻutal, cintutal. ukuttal (pi.vi.) = cintutal, tūvutal.
tuḷittal = tuḷi viḻutal (ta.vi.); tuḷikaḷaic cintutal, teḷittal, teṟittal, iṟaittal, cintutal (pi.vi.).tuḷi - teḷi. teḷittal = tuḷi tuḷiyāyc cintutal; malar, arici mutaliyavaṟṟaic ciṟṟaḷavāyt tūvutal.
teḷi - teṟi. teṟittal = tuḷi tuḷḷi viḻutal, tuḷḷutal, viraliṉāl oṉṟai muṉṉōkkic cuṇṭutal allatu tuḷḷac ceytal.
tuḷḷu naṭaiyait teṟinaṭai eṉpar.
teṟinaṭai maraikkaṇa miriya  (akam. 224)
tāvupu teṟikku māṉ  (puṟam. 259)
tūvutal = teḷittal, iṟaittal, citaṟutal.
tūvāṉam = iṟaikkum maḻai, teṟikkum maḻaittuḷi.

tuvaṟu - tūṟu. tuvaṟṟu - tūṟṟu.

tūṟṟutal = pataraiyum maṇiyaiyum pirittaṟkuk kūlattai muṉṉāka vāriyiṟaittal, maṇṇaivāri yiṟaittal, paḻic coṟkaḷaip parapputal. tūṟal = maḻait tuḷi, paḻiccol.
tūṟu = paḻiccol. avatūṟu = paḻippu.
tuṉṉal = ciṟu tivalai.
nīrttivalaiyaik kuṟittaṟkut tuṉṉal eṉṟoru colluṇ maiyālum, teṟikkappaṭum oru vakaik kāy tel eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppa tālum, tuḷ tuṉ eṉṉum īraṭikaṭkum potuvākat tul eṉṟoru vēruṇmai ūkikkappaṭum.
paṟavaikaḷ puṇarcci vēḷaiyiṟ ciṟappākavum, piṟa vēḷaikaḷiṟ potuvākavum, tam uṭampiṉiṉṟu melliṟakukaḷait tūvuvatiṉāl, avaṟṟiṟkut tūvu eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
tūvu - tūvi.
tūvu - tūval = iṟaku, iṟakiṉālāṉa eḻutukōl. Pen eṉṉum  āṅkilac collum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē. L. penna, feather.
uku tuḷi tūvu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, mutalāvatu muṉ cintutal eṉṟē poruḷpaṭṭu, piṉpu cintutal eṉṉum potup poruḷai uṇartti varukiṉṟaṉa. nīṇṭa kālakkaṭappiṉāl avaṟṟiṉ muṉmaik karuttu iṉṟu maṟaintu viṭṭatu. iṉṟum teḷittal viṉai muṉṉaṉṟi vēṟu puṟattil nikaḻāmai kāṇka. 
(5) muṟcellutal
iyaṟkaiyāṉa celavellām muṟcelavē.

tuḷḷutal eṉpatu oru muṟai muṉṉōkkit tāvutal; muṟ celavu eṉpatu neṭukac cellutal. itu cellutal celuttu tal ākiya iraṇṭaiyun taḻuvum.

iṉi, eynaṉ - eyiṉaṉ - eyiṉ eṉṟamām.

o. nō. pura - puram - purantā - purantaraṉ = aracaṉ. (kāvalaṉ), viṇṇaracaṉ.
turam = poṟuppu, talaimai.
tura - turai = talaivaṉ, ciṟṟaracaṉ.
muṉ. muṉṉutal = muṟpaṭutal, cellutal.
muḷ - (muṭu) - viṭu. viṭuttal = muṟ celuttutal, aṉupputal, viṭu - viṭai - viṭaiyil - viṭaiyilāṉ.
viṭu - viṭuppu. viṭai = celavu, cella uttaravu.
muḷ - (muy) - (muyam) - viyam = muṟceluttutal, celuttutal, ēvutal tūṇṭutal.
viyaṅkoḷ - viyaṅkōḷ = ēval, matippēval.
viyaṅkoḷḷutal = māṭu kutirai mutaliya vilaṅkait tūṇṭic celuttutal, tēroṭṭutal, tūṇṭutal, ēvutal.
tērviyaṅkoṇṭa pattu eṉṉum aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟup pirivup peyarai nōkkuka.

(6) viraivu (tuṭukku)
viraivu allatu vēkam eṉpatu, oppu nōkkic collum uṟavup paṇpākum. oṉṟaṉ celavait taṉippaṭa vēkam eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. māṭṭu vaṇṭiyaiviṭak kutirai vaṇṭiyum, kutirai vaṇṭiyai viṭa mitivaṇṭiyum, mitivaṇṭiyaiviṭap pukaivaṇṭiyum, pukaivaṇṭiyaiviṭa iyaṅki (Motor) vaṇṭiyum, vēkamākac cellum, orē vaṇṭiyai eṭuttuk koḷḷiṉum, ataṉ meṉ celavoṭu oppu nōkkiyē ataṉ kaṭuñcelavu vēkam eṉappaṭum.
ākavē, iyaṅkum poruḷ oṉṟāyiṉum palavāyiṉum, oṉṟaiviṭa iṉṉoṉṟum orucelavaiviṭa iṉṉoru celavum muṉ celvatē vēkam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, muṟcelavu eṉpatu iyalpāṉa celaveṉṟum, oru celaviṉum iṉṉoru celavu muṟpaṭuvatē vēkam eṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
viraivu eṉpatu oru viṉaiyai muntit toṭaṅki munti muṭippataiyum, vēkam eṉpatu oruviṉai ceytaṟkaṇ oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuriya viṉai vītattaiyum, kuṟikkum.
(i) lakara ṉakara vetukai
ol - olleṉa = viraivāka.
oy - oyyeṉa = viraivāka.
tuḷḷutal = viraital. tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇṭai = tuṭukkāṉavaṉ.
tuṇṇeṉal = viraital.
tuṉaital = viraital.
tuval = viraivu, tuvalutal = viraital.
poḷ - poḷḷeṉa = viraivāka.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = viraintu cellutal.
kaṭaṟpaṭai kuḷippa maṇṭi  (puṟam 6)
(muḷ) = (muy) - muyal = vēkamāka ōṭum ciṟu vilaṅku vakai.
(muḷ) - (maḷ) - maḻa - maḻamaḻa (viraivuk kuṟippu)
(mul) - (mal) - val = viraivu. vallē = viraivāka.

(puru) - para - parapara - paraparappu.
(puru) - paru - pari = vēkam. vēkamāṉa celavu, vēkamākac celluṅ kutirai. parital = ōṭutal.
(puru) - poru - porukku (vi.ku.) porukkeṉṟeḻuttāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. porukku - pokku (koccai vaḻakku). porukka = viraivāka.
(muru) - viru - viruviru - viruviruppu = viraivu. viruviruveṉṟu pō eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
viru - viruṭṭu (vi.ku.).
viru - vira - virai - viraivu. virai - viraici.
(iii) ṟakara vetukai
kuṟu - kuṟukuṟu. kuṟu kuṟuttal = vēṇṭātataic ceyya viraital. kuṟu - kuṟumpu = cēṭṭai.
cuṟu - cuṟucuṟu - cuṟucuṟuppu. cuṟu - cuṟuti = vēkam.
tuṟu - tuṟutuṟu. tuṟutuṟuttavaṉ - vēṇṭāviṉai allatu kuṟumpu ceytu koṇṭēyiruppavaṉ.
(nūṟu) - noṟu - noṟil = viraivu.
(muṟu) - muṟukku = vēkam, tuṭukku.
(iv) ṭakara vetukai
(uṭu) - (oṭu) - ōṭu. ōṭutal = viraintu cellutal.

kaṭukutal = viraital.

emmampu kaṭi viṭutum  (puṟam. 9.)  (mu.tā.)

tuṭu - tuṭukku = vēkam, tuṇivu, kuṟumpu. tuṭu - tuṭum (vi.ku.). tuṭu - tiṭu - tiṭum (vi; ku.). tiṭu - tiṭīr (vi.ku.) tuṭu - tuṭi - tuṭippu + oṉṟaic ceyya viraital. tuṭittal = viraintu aṭittuk koḷḷutal.
(nuṭu) - noṭu - noṭunoṭu. noṭunoṭuttal = tuṭukkā yiruttal. noṭu - noṭukku (vi.ku.).
(puṭu) - poṭu - poṭukku (vi.ku.). poṭukkeṉṟu pōy viṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka. poṭu - poṭupoṭu (vi.ku.).
(puṭu) - paṭu) - paṭa - paṭapaṭa - paṭapaṭappu.
paṭa-paṭakku. paṭakkuppaṭakkeṉal = tuṭittal, accattāl neñcam tuṭittal.

(v) takara vetukai
kutu - kutukutu - kutukutuppu = viraipu.
kutu - katu - katum (vi.ku.).
katu - kataḻ - kataḻvu = viraivu.

(putu) - (patu) = pataṟu - pataṟṟam = pataṭṭam.

(7) (naṭukkam) accam
uṭalum neñcāṅkulaiyum viraintu acaivatu accattaik kuṟikkumātalāl, viraivuk karuttil accak karuttup piṟantatu.
tuḷ - tuṇ. tuṇṇeṉal = tiṭukkiṭutal, añcutal. 
tuṇ - tuṇukku. tuṇukkiṭutal = tiṭukkiṭutal. tuṇukku - tuṇukkam = naṭukkam, accam.
tuṇ - tiṇ - tiṭu - tiṭukku.
nuṭu - naṭu - naṭuṅku - naṭukku - naṭukkam.
vitu - vitir - vitirppu = naṭukkam. vitirvitirppu = naṭunaṭukkam. vitu - vitukku. vitukku vitukkeṉal = accattāl neñcam paṭakkup paṭakkeṉṟu aṭittuk koḷḷutal, iṅṅaṉam aṭittuk koḷḷutalai verukku verukkeṉṟirukkiṉṟatu eṉpar. vitukku - virukku, - verukku - veruvu - veru = accam. veruvutal = añcutal. veru - veruḷ - veruḷi = accuṟuttum
pulluru. veruḷ - viraḷ - miraḷ. viraḷ - viraṭṭu. viraṭṭutal = accuṟuttutal, accuṟutti vēkamāka ōṭṭutal.
(8) vīcutal
oruvaṉ vēkamāy naṭakkumpōtu, avaṉ kai vēkamāy vīcuvatālum, aṅṅaṉam vīcumpōtu atu nīḷuvatālum, vēkamāy naṭappataik kuṟikkum vicu eṉṉum col, vēkamāy oṉṟai vīcuvataiyum nīṭciyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. coṟkaḷaip piṟappittuḷḷatu.
vicu - viciṟu - viciṟi - civiṟi. viciṟutal = vēkamāy vīcutal, vīci yeṟital.
vicu - vicukku. vicukkutal = viraintu kaivīcutal. viciṟutal. vicukku vicukkeṉṟu naṭakkiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
vicu - vīcu. vīcutal = vīciyeṟital, vārikkoṭuttal, nīḷutal, mikutal, nīḷavācaṉai varutal.
vīcu - vīccu = vēkam, vīcutal. ūñcalāṭṭu, nīḷam, mikuti.
vīccāy naṭakkiṟāṉ, kaivīccup peritāyirukkiṟatu, ūñcalai oru vīccu āṭṭiṉāṉ, vīṭu vīccāyirukkiṟatu, vīccāykkoṭu eṉpaṉa, muṟaiyē mēṟkuṟitta poruḷkaṭkeṭuttukkāṭṭām. 
vīccu - vīccam = nāṟṟam.
kāṟṟāṉatu nīṇṭu celvatāl, ataṉoṭu kalanta vācaṉaiyum nīṇṭu celkiṉṟateṉka. vīcutal eṉṉum col vaḻakkil tīya nāṟṟattaiyē kuṟikkum.
vīcu - vīcai = nīṇṭuvaḷarum mēlutaṭṭu mayir. talaimayirum tāṭiyum nīṇṭu vaḷarvaṉavēyāyiṉum, kuṟukkāka nīṇṭu vaḷarvatu mīcaiyoṉṟē yātaliṉ, atu vīcaiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
vīcai - mīcai.
oru cila coṟkaṭkuk kaṟṟōr vaḻaṅkum vaṭiviṉum kallār vaḻaṅkum vaṭivē muntiṉatāyuḷḷatu.
(9) ūkkutal
ūkkutal ūkkamuṇṭākkutal. ūkkamāvatu muyaṟcikkētu vāṉa muṉaippu. oruvaṉ taṉ maṉattālēṉum piṟaṉālēṉum oruviṉai ceyyat tūṇṭappaṭṭa viṭattē, avaṉiṭattu ataṟkuriya muyaṟci piṟakkum. tūṇṭutal eṉpatu muṟceluttutal. oru kutirai muṟcellumāṟu ataṉai ūrpavaṉ atait tūṇṭiviṭuvatu pōṉṟatē. oruvaṉ oru viṉai ceyyumāṟu avaṉatu uḷḷam avaṉait tūṇṭiviṭuvatum,
uḷ - uḷḷam = ūkkam. uḷḷutal = ūkkutal.
uḷḷa muṭaimai yuṭaimai poruḷuṭaimai
nillātu nīṅki viṭum
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉār ūkkamuṭaimai yatikārattil (592) kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. (uḷḷam eṉpataip parimēlaḻakar āku peyarākak koṇṭatu poruntātu. maṉattaik kuṟikkum uḷḷam eṉṉum collum, ūkkattaik kuṟikkum uḷḷam eṉṉum collum, vevvēṟām. maṉattaik kuṟippatu uḷḷiruppatu eṉṟum, ūkkattaik kuṟippatu muṟceluttuvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum).  (mu.tā.)
uḷ - (uy) - (uyal) - uñal - uñaṟṟu.
uñaṟṟutal = uḷḷan tūṇṭutal, muyaṟci ceytal, varunti yuḻaittal.
muṉ - muṉai, muṉaital = muṟpaṭutal, muyaṟciceytal, ūkkaṅ koḷḷutal. muṉai - muṉaippu.
muḷ - (muy) - muyal - muyaṟci. muyal - muyaṟṟu. muyalutal = muṉaintuḻaittal.
muka - mucumucu - mucumucuppu = ūkkam.
	ūkārac cuṭṭu - 4) mēṟcelavu
tiṟanta veḷiyil nilamaṭṭattiliruntu uyirikaḷ muṟcellak kūṭiya ticaikaḷ, pakkatticai eṭṭum mēṟṟicai oṉṟumāka oṉpatām. avaṟṟuḷ, makkaḷum maṟṟap paṟavāvuyirikaḷum muṟcellakkūṭiya ticaikaḷ pakkatticaiyākiya eṭṭē, paṟavai oṉṟē oṉpāṉ ticaiyum muṟcellak kūṭiyatākum.
nilattilum nīrilumiruntu mēleḻum poruḷkaṭkellām muṟcela veṉpatu mēṟcelavē. mēṟcelavu muṟcelavil oruvakaiyēyātalāl, muṟcelavaik kuṟikkum ūkāramē mēṟcelavaiyum uṇarttum eṉka. mēṟcellutal allatu mēṉmēṟcellutal eṉpatu, muṟcelavaiyum micaic celavaiyum potuppaṭak kuṟittal kāṇka. ēṇ cēṇ mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyarattaiyum tolaivaiyum potuppaṭak kuṟittalaiyum nōkkuka.
(1) eḻutal
eḻutalāvatu kiḷartalum tuḷḷutalum. tuḷḷutal eṉpatu muṉṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum mēṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum eṉa iruvakaittu.
uṉṉutal = uyaraveḻutal.

ucumputal = uṟaṅkiṉavaṉ mella uṭampacaitteḻutal. ucumpu - ucuppu (piṟaviṉai).
ukalutal = alaiyeḻutal.
ukaḷutal = kutittal, ukaittal = eḻutal, eḻupputal, uyarak kutittal.
utittal = uyaraveḻumputal;
kutittal = uyaraveḻumputal. kuti - koti. kontaḷittal = kaṭal kiḷarnteḻutal.
tuḷḷutal = kutittal.
tuḷ - tuḷumpu; tuḷumputal = mīṉ nīrmaṭṭattiṟku mēl mellat tuḷḷutal.
tuḷumpu - taḷumpu. taḷumputal = kalam acaivatāl nīr ciṟitu tuḷḷutal. taḷumpu - tatumpu. taḷumpu - taḷampu.
tuḷumpu - tuṭumpu. tuṭumputal = tatumputal;
tuḷ - taḷ - taḷa - taḷataḷa - taḷataḷi - tattaḷi.  
taḷa - taḷai. taḷaittal = kotittal.
poṅkutal = eḻutal, kotittal, mikutal.
muḷ - muḷi; muḷital - poṅkutal.
muḷ - muḷu. muḷuttal = ulaiyarici mellat tuḷḷutal.
muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭukal = tuḷḷutal, vitai muḷai nilat tuḷḷiruntu muṭṭiyeḻutal.
mucumucuttal = nīr kotittal.
(2) puḷittup poṅkutal
cūṭṭiṉāṟ poṅkuvatu pōṉṟu puḷippiṉālum cila poruḷkaḷ poṅkiyeḻum.
ukiṉ - ekiṉ = puḷi. ukiṉ - ukiṉam - ekiṉam = puḷi.
nura - nurai = puḷippiṉāl eḻum ciṟu kumiḻi, ataṉ niṟamāṉa veṇmai. nurai veṇmaiyāyiruntal kāṇka. nurai - narai = veṇmai. naraittal = veḷuttal.
naraiyāṉ puṟattiṭṭa cūṭu  (paḻamoḻi 48)
nutampu = kaḷ. notittal = puḷittup poṅkutal.
(puḷ) puḷi = puḷittup poṅkuvatu, puḷippuccuvai, puḷippup paḻam. puḷittal = puḷittup poṅkutal. puḷikka vaitta mā eḻumpiyiruttalaik koṇṭē atu puḷittu viṭṭateṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷutal kāṇka.
puḷi - puḷicci = puḷiccai.
(puḷ) - (pura) - purai - pirai = puḷitta mōr, pālaip puḷikka vaikkum mōr.
poṅku - poṅkal = kaḷ. poruṇi = kaḷ. peruku = tayir.
(muḷ) - muḷi. muḷital = poṅkutal. muḷitayir = naṉṟāyt tōynta tayir.
muṇṭakam = kaḷ. (muḷ) - mura - murappu. murattal = puḷittal. mura - mōr.
muramureṉavē puḷitta mōrum eṉṟu auvaiyār kūṟutal kāṇka.
mura - murai = nurai.
mukiṉi = puḷi.
(3) uvarttup poṅkutal
uvar nilamum poṅkiyeḻum.
(uḷam) - uḻam. uḻamaṇ = uvarmaṇ. (uḷam) - aḷam = uppu.
umaṇ = uppu. umaṇ - umaṇaṉ.
upputal = poṅkiyeḻutal. uppu = uvarttup poṅkiyeḻum cattu, uvarppuk kal.
uvar - uvari = uvar nīrkkaṭal.
(cuvar) - cavar. cavarttal = uvarttal.
poṅkutal - nilam uvartteḻutal.
porumutal - uḷam uvartteḻutal.
(4) uyarcci
uṭampāl uyartalum nilaimaiyāl uyartalum iṭattāl uyartalum eṉa uyartal mūvakai.
ū - u - mēl, uyarnta.
e.ṭu. uttuṅkaḷ.
uttari, uttariyam, uttūḷaṉam mutaliya pala vaṭacoṟkaḷil muṉṉoṭṭāyiruntu, uyarcci allatu mēluṟavuk karuttai uṇarttuvatu ukaramē.
ulli - matil.
uḷ = mēṉmai.
ukattal = uyartal.
ukappē uyarvu  (tol. uriyiyal 8).
ukaittal = uyaraveḻumputal.  (mu.tā.)
uccam - uyarcci, ucci = uccamāṉa iṭam. uttaram = uyarvu, uyaravirukkum viṭṭam, uyarnta vaṭaticai.
kumarimalai mūḻkip paṉimalai eḻuntapiṉ, vaṭaticai uyarntatu; teṉṟicai tāḻntatu. itaṉāl, avai muṟaiyē uttaram takkaṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. uttaram (u+taram) = uyarvu. takkaṇam (takku + aṇam) = tāḻvu.
upputal = eḻutal, paruttal, vīṅkutal. uppucam = vayiṟṟup porumal.
umpar = mēl, mēliṭam, ākāyam, tēvarulakam, tēvar.
umparāṉ = uyarnilaiyiliruppavaṉ.
umparam = mēliṭam, ākāyam, tēvarulakam.
umpal = uyaramāṉa vilaṅkākiya yāṉai.
uyar - uyaram, uyarvu, uyarcci, uyartti.
uvaṇam = uyarap paṟakkum paruntu, uvaṇam - uvaṇaṉ.
uyara uyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
uvaṇai = tēvarulakam.
uvaccaṉ - ōccaṉ - ōcaṉ = teyvattai ēttupavaṉ.

(10) taṉmaippeyar
māntaṉukku iyalpāka nāṉ eṉṉum akaṅkāra miruttalālum, avaṉ taṉṉalattaiyē muṉṉalamākap pēṇutalālum, uyarcci kuṟikkum ēkārattiṉaṭiyākat taṉmaip peyarai amaittuk koṇṭāṉ muṉṉait tamiḻaṉ eṉka.
orumai 	paṉmai
ēṉ	ēm (mutal nilai)
yāṉ	yām (2 ām nilai)
nāṉ	nām (3 ām nilai)
vantēṉ vantēm eṉṉum taṉmai viṉaikaḷil. ēṉ ēm eṉṉum taṉmaip peyarkaḷ iṉṟum vikutiyāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. ivai piṟkālattil piṉvarumāṟu tirintaṉa.
ēṉ - eṉ - aṉ - al.
ēm - em.
ēm - ām - ōm
em - am - ōm
yām nām eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vēraḷavil oṉṟē yāyiṉum, tiripu vēṟupāṭu kāraṇamākavum vacati nōkkiyum. muṟaiyē taṉittaṉmaip paṉmaiyākavum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmaiyākavum āḷappaṭṭaṉa.
muṉṉilaiyil nī nīm (nīr) nīṅkaḷ eṉavum, paṭarkkaiyil avaṉ avar avarkaḷ eṉavum, iḻiloppuyarvu ākiya munnilai paṟṟiya peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭamaiyālum; taṉmaiyilum. aracarum iṟaivaṉum tammai yām eṉṟē cuṭṭuvatākak kūṟuvatu marapātalālum, yām nām eṉṉum oṟṟaip paṉmaip peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟu yāṅkaḷ nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum iraṭṭaip paṉmaip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa (4 ām nilai).
mutalāvatu, yāṅkaḷ eṉpatu taṉittaṉmaip paṉmaiyākavum, nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmaiyākavum, vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṉpu, yāṅkaḷ eṉṉuñ col vaḻakkaṟṟup pōy viṭṭamaiyāl, ataṟkup patilāka nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu tavaṟāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu (5 ām nilai).
vēṟṟumait tiripu
eḻuvāy 	vēṟṟumaiyaṭi
ēṉ, yāṉ	eṉ
ēm, yām	em
nāṉ	naṉ
nām	nam
yāṅkaḷ	eṅkaḷ
nāṅkaḷ	naṅkaḷ
tamiḻil, naṉ eṉpatu iruvakai vaḻakkilum, naṅkaḷ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkilum, aṟṟaṉa. naṅkaḷ kōṉ vacutēvaṉ eṉṟu tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār kūṟutal kāṇka.
teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum naṉ eṉṉum aṭi iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.

(11) viṉāccol
oruvaṉai evaṉ eṉṟu viṉāvuvatu, ōr iṭattiluḷḷa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟai uyara eṭuttukkāṭṭi ituvā eṉṟu kēṭpatu pōliruttalāl, viṉāc collum eḻucci allatu uyarcci kuṟitta ēkārac collaṭiyākap piṟantuḷḷatu.
ē? = vJ?, evai? evaṉ? evaḷ? evar? (mutal nilai)
ē - yā = evai? (2 ām nilai)
ē - ē (3 ām nilai) ēvūr? (paṇṭait tamiḻ); evvūr? (iṟṟait tamiḻ).. evaṉ = etu? evai? (4 ām nilai). evaṉ - eṉ.
ē - yā - ā. ēvar - yāvar - yār - ār.
ē - ā - ō. vantāṉā? vantāṉō? (5 ām nilai)
peyar : ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar, ētu, ēvai.
evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, entu, etu, eṉṉatu, ev, evai, eṉṉa.
yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yātu, yā, yāvai;
yāvar - yār, yāvatu - yātu.
peyareccam : e, enta, eṉṉa, eṉai.
viṉaiyeccam : eṉṟu 		(kālam)

ēṉ 					(kāraṇam)
ē e yā eṉṉum mūṉṟē viṉāvaṭikaḷāy amaivataiyum, ā ō eṉṉum iraṇṭum īṟākavaṉṟi vēṟāka varāmaiyaiyum, nōkkuka. e ēyiṉ kuṟukkam; yā ataṉ tiripu. ātalāl, mūlaviṉāvaṭi ē eṉṉum oṉṟē.

(13) kuravar peyar
oruvaṉukku uyarntōrāṉa aiṅkuravaruḷḷum iru mutukuravar mikac ciṟantavarātaliṉ, avaraik kuṟittaṟku uyarvu kuṟitta ukarac cuṭṭiṉ tiripāṉa akarattai aṭiyākak koṇṭa pala coṟkaḷ kiḷaittuḷḷaṉa.
tantai	 	tāy
—— 		akkai
accaṉ		acci
attaṉ		atti
appaṉ		ammai (avvai)
—— 		aṉṉi, aṉi (aṇi)
		aṉṉai (aññai), aṉai
āñaṉ		—— 	
———— 		āttai
  —— 		āy
kuṟippu : (1) kōṭiṭṭa iṭattiṟkuriya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa pōlum.
(2) tāyaik kuṟikkum cila coṟkaḷ aṉ īṟu peṟṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
v.L.: 		akkai - akkaṉ
			ammai - ammaṉ
ivaṟṟiluḷḷa aṉ īṟu orukāl maṟaṅkuṟikka vantirukkalām.
(3) tāyaik kuṟikkum pala coṟkaḷ muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum peṟātum tamakkaiyaiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.

				tamakkai

		avvai	tavvai (tamavvai)
tantai peyarum iṅṅaṉam tamaiyaṉaik kuṟikkum.
e.ṭu. aiyaṉ - tamaiyaṉ.
(4) kuravar peyarkaḷ cila vikutimāṟi avarkaḷiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāraik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. attaṉ - attai (tantaiyōṭuṭaṉ piṟantavaḷ)
ammai - ammāṉ (tāyōṭuṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ)
(5) tantai tāy peyar muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum varum.
tam + appaṉ = tamappaṉ - takappaṉ.
(tam + āy = tāy)
(6) kuravar peyarkaḷuṭ cila piṟavaṟṟiṉ tiripākat terikiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. 	attaṉ - accaṉ
		ammai - avvai
		aṉṉai - aññai
(7) āṇpāl kuṟikkum aṉṉīṟum peṇpāl kuṟikkum ikara vaikāra vīṟukaḷum, mutaṟkālattil kuravar peyarkaḷoṭu cērntiruntatākat teriyavillai.
e.ṭu. 	appaṉ - appu
		ammai - ammu
(8) kuravar peyarkaḷ pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyaraik kuṟittaṟkup pāl mayaṅkātu mīmicaiyāka aṭukki varuvatuṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	appaccu (appacci) = pāṭṭaṉ.
		ammācci = pāṭṭi.
tāy peyarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu tāyaiyē kuṟippatu muṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	ammaṇi (ammaṇi), ammaṉai.
tāypeyar pāṭṭiyaik kuṟippatumuṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	avvai = tāy, pāṭṭi.
(9) ammai appaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka meṅkaṇum avvan nāṭṭiṟ kēṟpat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(10) mēṟkāṭṭiya irumutukuravar muṟaip peyarkaḷai, vitappāka ammai appaṉ eṉpavaṟṟai, kuḻavi vaḷarppoli kaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭuttuvar mēlai moḻi nūlvallār. āyiṉ, aṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyar uyarntōṉukkum tamai yaṉukkum potuvāyiruttalālum, aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu periyōṉaik kuṟittup piṉpu tirintum tiriyātum aiṅkura varaiyum potuppaṭac cuṭṭuvatālum, ammai appaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ mūppum arumaiyum paṟṟip piṟarukkum pāl tavaṟātu vaḻaṅkutalālum, uyarcci kuṟikkum ukaravaṭiyiṉ tiripāṉa akara vaṭiyum uyarcci kuṟittalālum, avaṟṟaic cuṭṭolikaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭuttuvatē cālacciṟappuṭaittām.
āriya moḻikaḷellām tiripuṭai moḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟ ṟait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭa mēṉāṭṭār ammai appaṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷai muṟaiyē mā pā eṉṉuṅ kuḻavi yoli kaḷiṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyākak koḷvatu viyappaṉṟu.
(14) piṉmai
kuṟukkāka vaḷarum vilaṅku paṟavaikaṭku uṭampiṉ uccamāṉa iṭam allatu mēṟpuṟam mutukātaliṉ, ukaraccuṭṭiṟkup piṉmaik karuttum ciṟupāṉmai urittām.
mēlum, kālamuṉ iṭamuṉ eṉa muṉmai iru vakaippaṭṭu muṉmaik karuttil piṉmaik karuttum piṉmaik karuttil muṉmaik karuttum mayaṅkutalāl, ataṉālum muṉmaic cuṭṭu piṉmaik karuttait taḻuvuvatām. iṉi, mēl eṉṉuñ col uccam muṉmai piṉmaikaḷai uṇarttuvataiyuṅ kāṇka. 
e.ṭu. mēl = uccattil, mēṉāḷ = muṉṉāḷ, mēl = piṉpu, ataṉ mēl = ataṉ piṉpu.
uppakkam = piṉpakkam.
uttaram = piṉcollum maṟumoḻi.
uttaram - uttaravu = vēṇṭukōḷiṉ piṉtarum āṇai allatu viṭai.
ummai = piṟkālam (etirkālam)

	ūkārac cuṭṭu - 5) neruṅkal
(1) aṇukal
muṉṉōkkic cellum uyirikaḷ tām aṭaiyak karutiya iṭattai mutalāvatu aṇukum. karuttilātu nīṇṭu cellum oru marakkiḷaiyum kūṭa aṇmaiyiluḷḷa oru poruḷai iyaṟkaiyāka aṭukkum.
neruṅkal eṉpatu, toṭṭu neruṅkalum toṭātu neruṅ kalum eṉa irutiṟattatātalāl. ivviyal muṟcelaviyalaiyum toṭutaliyalaiyum arimā nōkkākat taḻuvum.
(uḷ) - aḷ. aḷḷutal = neruṅkutal.
aḷ - aṇ - aṇuku - aṇukkam.
aṇ - aṇṭu - aṇṭai. aṇ - aṇmu.
aṇ - aṭu.
aḷ - aru - aruku.
uṟutal = neruṅkutal.
kuḷ - kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu. keḻumutal = kiṭṭutal.
(kuṭṭu) - kiṭṭu. kiṭṭutal = neruṅkutal.
kiṭṭu - kiṭṭam = aṇmai. kiṭṭa = neruṅka.
tuṉṉutal = neruṅkutal. tuṉṟutal = neruṅkutal.
tuṟutal = neruṅkutal.
tūrtal = neruṅkutal.
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ - naṇuku.
(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku.
muṭṭutal = neruṅkutal. kiṭṭamuṭṭa eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. muṭṭaṭi = aṇmai.
muṇṭutal = neruṅkutal. muṇṭu - maṇṭu. 
maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal.
(2) ceṟital
iruporuḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟaṇukutal neruṅkalum, pala poruḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟaṇukutal ceṟitalum ākum. neruṅ kaliṉum ceṟivu mika aṇukkamākum.
(uḷ) - aḷ = ceṟivu.
uṟu - uṟa - uṟappu = ceṟivu.
(cuṟu) - ceṟu; ceṟuttal = ceṟital.
ceṟutta ceyyuḷ  (pulam. 53)
ceṟu - ceṟi - ceṟivu.
tuṉṉutal = ceṟital.
tutaital = ceṟital. tutai - tatai.
tuvaṉṟutal = ceṟital.
tuṟutal = ceṟital. tuṟu - tuṟavu - tuṟumu - tuṟumpu. tuṟumutal = ceṟital. tuṟumputal = ceṟital.
nuḷ - naḷ - naḷi = ceṟivu. naḷḷutal = ceṟital.
naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivum ākum  (tol. uriyiyal, 25)
potuḷutal = ceṟital. muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭu = neruṅkutal, ceṟital. muṟu - viṟu - veṟu. veṟuttal = ceṟital.
viṟu - viṟa - viṟappu = ceṟivu.
viṟappum uṟappum veṟuppum ceṟivē  (tol. uriyiyal, 49)
muṭai - miṭai. miṭaital = ceṟital.
moyttal = ceṟital.
(3) oṭuṅkal
oṭuṅkalāvatu nerukkamātal. iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu neruṅkumpōtu nerukkam uṇṭākum. ataṉāl oṭukkam ēṟpaṭum. oṭukkam iṭaiveḷiyoṭukkamum,  poruḷoṭukkamum, oḻukkavoṭukkamum, eṉa mūvakai.
ul - ulli = oṭuṅkiyaāḷ. ul-ullāṭi = ulli. ulli - olli. ul - ol - ollaṭṭai = olli.
ul - ulku - (uṟku) - uṟkam - ukkam = oṭuṅkiya iṭai.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = curuṅkutal; olku - oṟkam = oṭukkam. curukkam. taḷarcci.
ul - al - alku - alkul = curuṅkiya iṭai. alkutal = curukkutal. alku - aḵku. aḵkutal = curuṅkutal.
uḷ - oḷ - oru - oruku - orukku. orukkutal = oṭukkutal. orukku - erukku.
uḷ - uṭu - uṭuku - uṭukku = iṭaiyiloṭuṅkiya kōṭaṅki.
uṭukku - uṭukkai.
uṭuku - iṭuku. iṭukutal = neruṅkutal.
uṭukku - iṭukku - iṭukkam = nerukkam.
iṭukku = ciṟu cantu. iṭukku - iṭukkal.
iṭukkutal = nerukkutal, nerukkip piṭittal allatu ēntutal. piḷḷaiyai iṭuppil iṭukkutal eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
iṭukku = iṭukki = nerukkip piṭikkuṅ karuvi.
uṭu - iṭu - iṭai = oṭuṅkiya maruṅkul. iṭu - iṭuppu.
iṭu - iṭukkaṇ = oṭukki allatu nērukki varuttum tuṉpam.
iṭu - iṭar. iṭarppaṭutal = tuṉpattāl allatu vaṟumaiyāl nerukkuṇṇutal.
iṭu - iṭaṅku - iṭaṅkar = ciṟu vaḻi.
uḷ - iḷ - iṇṭu = ciṟiya iṭukku. iṇṭum iṭukkum eṉpatu vaḻakku.
iḷ - iṭṭu - iṭṭitu = oṭuṅkiṉatu. ciṟiyatu. iṭṭiṭai = ciṟiya iṭaiveḷi. iṭṭēṟi = ēṟṟamāṉa ciṟiya vaṇṭippātai. 
iṭṭikai = ciṟu ceṅkal.
uṭu - oṭu - oṭuṅku - oṭukku - oṭukkam.
oṭuṅku - aṭaṅku - aṭakku - aṭakkam.
aṭaṅkutal = oṭuṅkiyamaital. aṭakka voṭukkam eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
uṟa - uṟaṅku - uṟakku - uṟakkam. uṟaṅkutal = oṭuṅkit tūṅkutal. ūraṭaṅkutal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
(kuṭṭu) - kiṭṭu - kiṭṭi = keṇṭaikkālai nerukkuṅ karuvi.
kuḷ - kuṭku - kukku. kukkutal = oṭuṅkutal.
kuḷ - kuṭu - koṭu - koṭuku. koṭukutal = oṭuṅkutal.
koṭu - (kōṭu) - kōṭaṅki - kōṭāṅki = uṭukku.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceṟi. ceṟittal = nerittal, aṭakkutal.
nuḷ - neḷ - neru - neruṅku - nerukku - nerukkam. neru - neri. nerittal = neruṅkutal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭuku - muṭukku = ciṟu cantu.
muṭukku - muṭukkar.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ = nerukkam.
	ūkāraccuṭṭu - 6) toṭutal
neruṅkalukku aṭuttu nikaḻakkūṭiya toṭarpu toṭutal. muṟcellum uyirikaḷ tām aṭaiyak karutiya poruḷkaḷai aṭuttapiṉ avaṟṟait toṭum; taṟceyalāka etirppaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaiyum toṭalām. iṅkut toṭutal eṉpatu kaiyiṉāl toṭuvatai maṭṭumaṉṟu, uṭampālum kuṇattālum toṭuvataiyum kuṟikkum.
(1) toṭutal tuṟai
melitāyt toṭutal
(uḷ) - aḷ - aḷavu - aḷāvu. aḷavutal = toṭutal.
uṟutal = tōlāl toṭutal. uṟu ūṟu = toṭṭaṟivu.
ul-ol-oṟṟu-ottu.
ottutal = meṉporuḷāl toṭutal.
ottu - ottaṭam. ottu - oṟṟu - oṟṟi.
tuvaḷutal = toṭutal. tuvaḷai - ottaṭam.
tuva - tuvai - tuvaital = cāyantoṭutal.
(tūṇṭu) - tīṇṭu - tīṭṭu. tīṇṭutal = toṭutal.
toṭutal = uṟutal.
tōytal = nīraiyum nilattaiyun toṭutal.
kālnilan tōyāk kaṭavuḷai  (nālaṭiyār, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu)
porutal = poruntit toṭutal. poruṅ katavu, porumuka veḻiṉi mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
muttutal = mukattāl toṭutal.
(i) toṭum uṟuppu (puṟaṇi)
uri = tōl, marappaṭṭai. (ul - ur - uṟu. ur - uri).
(tol) - toli - tōl - tōṭu - ōṭu.
(toṉ) - tōḷ = toṭum uṟuppāṉa kai, allatu cuvalait toṭum puyam.
tuva - tuvakku = tōl.
(ii) toṭaṅkal
oru karuviyait toṭutalē ataikkoṇṭu ceyyum viṉaiyait toṭaṅkalākum. oru viṉaiyait toṭaṅkal atait toṭutal pōlām.
uṟutal = toṭaṅkal.
v.L.: uraikkaluṟṟāṉ = collat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
(tuṭu) - tuṭaṅku - tuṭakkam. tuṭu - toṭu.
toṭu - toṭaṅku - toṭakkam.

toṭutal = toṭaṅkutal. aṉṟu toṭṭu = aṉṟu toṭaṅki.
(tuva) - tuvaṅku - tuvakkam.
toṉṟutoṭṭu, aṉṟu toṭuttu, toṭṭakuṟai (toṭaṅki viṭṭa viṉaikkuṟai) mutaliya maraput toṭarkaḷai nōkkuka.
(iii) toṭuppu
toṭuppāvatu iruporuṭkuṇṭāṉa toṭarpu. atu oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭṭapiṉ nikaḻvatu.
(tuṭu) - tuṭai. toṭu - toṭai.
toṭuttal = iṇaittal.
toṭu - toṭukku. toṭukkutal = toṭṭāl viḻum nilaiyāyt toṭuttuvaittal.
toṭu - toṭuppu = iṇaippu, takāppuṇarcciyuṟavu.
toṭukku - tocukku = takāppuṇarcci.
toṭu - toṭucu = vaippu. toṭucu - toṭicu.
toṭu - toṭuvu = vīṭṭait toṭarnta kollai.
toṭu - toṭuvai = toṭuttiruppatu, putiya yāṉaiyaip payiṟṟumpaṭi toṭuttuviṭṭa yāṉai, pāṅkaṉ, vaippu.
toṭu - toṭar - toṭarpu.
(tol) - toṟṟu - tottu.
(iv) tuṭakku
tuṭakkāvatu toṭuppāluṇṭākuṅ kaṭṭu.
(tuṭu) - tuṭakku = kaṭṭu. toṭu - toṭakku = kaṭṭu.
tuva - tuvakku = kaṭṭu. tuya - tuyakku = kaṭṭu.
toṭu - toṭar - toṭarpu = kaṭṭu, kaṭṭappaṭṭa ceyyuḷ.
(v) toṭarcci
pala poruḷkaḷ muṟaiyē oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭukkap paṭuvatāl toṭarcciyuṇṭākum.
toṭu - toṭar = varicai.
aḷ - aṇ - aṇi = varicai.
aṇi = aṇil = mutukil mūvaricai allatu variyuḷḷa aṇil.
aṇ = aṭu = aṭukku = aṭukkam = aṭukkal.
toṭar = toṭari. toṭar = toṭarcci. toṭar = toṭarvu = toṭarpu.
toṭu = toṭakku = toṭakki = toṭari (caṅkili, koṭi).
toṭu = toṭal = toṭalai = mālai.
toṭu = toṭavu = toṭaval = mālai.
toṭu – toṭai – mālai . toṭai-toṭaiyal= mālai.
(2) muṭṭal tuṟai
i. valitāyt toṭutal
valiya poruḷkaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu vēkamākat tākkuvatu valitāyt toṭutal.
utaittal = tākkutal, eṟṟutal.
oṭṭutal = tākkutal.
māntaruṟai nilattō ṭoṭṭalaritu  (kuṟaḷ, 499)
kuṭṭutal = muṭṭiyāl talaiyait tākkutal.
kuṭṭu - koṭṭu. koṭṭutal = muṭṭiyāl oṉṟait tākkutal, kuccāl paṟaiyai aṭittal, cuttiyalāl oṉṟait taṭṭutal, koṭṭuppiṭi, cempukoṭṭi mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
coṭṭutal = talaiyiṟ kuṭṭutal, paṟavai oṉṟaik kottutal.
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu. taṭṭutal = kaiyāluṅ karuviyālum koṭṭutal. taṭṭu - taṭṭāṉ.
noṭṭai (yiṭutal) = cuvai mikutiyāl aṇṇattil nāvaic cērttuk koṭṭutal.
poṭṭu - paṭṭu. paṭṭutal eṉpatu taṭṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṉa paṇṭai viṉaiccol, iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. paṭṭutaṟku aṭaiyāka vaittuk koḷḷum kal paṭṭaṭai eṉṟum, paṭṭum aṟai paṭṭaṟai eṉṟum, paṭṭum cālai paṭṭacālai eṉṟum, vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
muṭṭutal = talaiyālum mukattālum tākkutal. mukam eṉṟatu oru poruḷiṉ muṉai allatu muṟpakuti. muṭṭu = muṭṭiyil vāṅkum kuṇṭaṭi.
muṭṭu - moṭṭu = talaiyil vāṅkum kuṭṭu. moṭṭu moṭṭeṉṟu talaiyiṟ kuṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. koṅku nāṭṭār kuṭṭai moṭṭukkāy eṉpar.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷ vallōcai mikumāṟu ṭakaram iraṭṭiyavai yātaliṉ, avai perumpālum kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷkaḷai vaṉmaiyākat toṭuvataiyē kuṟikkum. takaravoli ṭakaravoliyiṉum melliyatātaliṉ, takaramiraṭṭiya coṟkaḷ caṟṟu meṉmaiyākat tākkuvataik kuṟikkum. takaram taṉittu varuvaṉavum, ṭakaram taṉittum meliyōṭu kūṭiyum varuvaṉavum, meli taṉittum iraṭṭiyum varuvaṉavum, iṅṅaṉamē.
kuttutal = kaiyāl uṭampiṟ kuttutal, ulakkaiyāṟ kūlattait tuvaittal.
kuttu - kuṟṟu.
kummutal = kuttutal. kumaittal = kuttutal.
muṇṭutal = mella muṭṭutal.
mottutal = uṭampiṟ kaiyālum karuviyālum aṭittal.
mōtutal = muṭṭuvatiṉum caṟṟu melitāyt tākkutal.
kaiyālum ulakkaiyālum kuttutal pōl toṭutalaḷavāṉa kuttutalkaḷē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavai.
ii. muṭṭuk koṭuttal
muṭṭuk koṭuttalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟai muṭṭit tāṅkumpaṭi ceytal.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇ - aṇṭu. aṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, poruntutal, muṭṭutal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭai = muṭṭu.
aṇṭai (aṇṭak) koṭuttal = muṭṭuk koṭuttal.
potu = oppu, poruttam. potu - (pōtu) - pōtikai = tūṇmēl vaikkun tāṅku kaṭṭai.
muḷ - muṭṭu.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = tāṅkutal.
maṇṭukāl = muṭṭukkāl.
iii. olittal
uyirikaḷiṉ vāyolikaḷallāta maṟṟa volikaḷellām, poruḷkaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭuvatiṉālēyē allatu tākkuvatiṉālēyē uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu muṉṉait tamiḻar aṟintu. toṭuvataiyum tākkuvataiyum kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷi ṉiṉṟu olipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uyirikaḷiṉ vāyolikaḷum, eḻuttolikaḷāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ uṭampuḷḷirunteḻuppappaṭum kāṟṟāṉatu, miṭaṟum mūkkum pōṉṟa uṟup piṭaṅkaḷaiyum, aṇṇamum pallum pōṉṟa uṟuppukkaḷaiyum, tākkuvatiṉālēyē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉaveṉ ṟaṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu :
unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
talaiyiṉum miṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaiip
pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ
uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
ellā eḻuttuñ colluṅ kālai
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (83) ūkikkappaṭum.
ul - ulampu. ulamputal = alapputal.
ulampu - alampu. alamputal = olittal.

kalleṉ pērūr  (cilam. 12, 12)

cilamputal = olittal.
cil - cilai. cilaittal = olittal.
cil - cilai, cilaittal = olittal.
cul - col = oli, moḻi, collutal = uraittal.
col oli vaṭivāyiruttalāl, oli paṟṟiya pala coṟkaḷ collaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.	aṟaital = olittal, collutal.
		icaittal = olittal, collutal.
		iyampal = olittal, collutal.
		karaital = olittal, collutal.
tuḻaṉi = oli, toṉittal = olittal, collutal, toṉupputal = alapputal.
tuṇatuṇattal, toṉutoṉuttal, toṇa toṇattal eṉpaṉa, alapputalaiyum viṭātu pēcutalaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
nuḷ - naḷ (naḷḷeṉal) = ōr olikkuṟippu.
nuḷ - (noḷ) - noṭi = oli, col.
pul - pulampu. pulamputal = olittal.
oruvaṉ taṉiyāyiruntu taṉṉoṭu tāṉē pēcuvatu olittalaḷavā yiruppatāl, atu pulamputal eṉappaṭum. ataṉāl pulampu eṉṉum col taṉimaiyaik kuṟikkum.
pulampē taṉimai  (tol. uri. 35)
iṉi, tākkum allatu tākkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ tiṇmaikkum olikkun taṉmaikkum ēṟpap palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa ōcaikaḷ piṟattalāl, avaṟṟaik kuṟittaṟku vevvēṟeḻuttolikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum eṉṟaṟika.
e.ṭu.

iv. taṭṭal (taṭai)
oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷai muṭṭiṉavuṭaṉ, muṉṉataṉ muṟcelavu taṭukkappaṭuvatāl, muṭṭaṟ karuttil taṭaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
oṭṭu - aṭṭu - aṭṭi = taṭai.
aṭṭu - aṭṭam = kuṟukku.
kuṟukku niṟpatu etuvum taṭaiyākum. muṭṭappaṭum cuvar mutaliyavum kuṟukku niṉṟutāṉ taṭukkum. ātalāl, kuṟukku niṟṟal eṉiṉum taṭuttal eṉiṉum oṉṟē.
aṭṭaṅkāl = kuṟukkāka maṭakki vaikkuṅ kāl.
aṭṭu - aṭṭaṇam. aṭṭaṇakkāl (aṭṭaṇaṅkāl) = kuṟukkāka maṭakkivaikkuṅ kāl.
aṭṭap pallakku = kuṟukkākak kāvum pallakku.
aṭṭam pārittal = kuṟukkāka (pakkavāṭṭil) vaḷartal.
kuḷ - kuṟu - kuṟuku - kuṟukku = vaḻiyiṉ pakkavāṭṭu, vaḻittaṭai, mutukiṉ kuṟukkup pakuti.
kuḷ - (kuṭṭu) - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭutal = taṭuttal, vaḻiyiṟ kuṟukkākac cellutal.
kāṭai kaṭṭiṉāl pāṭaikaṭṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
tuḷ - taḷ - taṭṭu. taṭṭutal = taṭuttal, nīruḷ mūḻkiṉavaṉukkut tarai taṭṭutalum, muṟcelpavaṉukkuc cuvar taṭṭutalum taṭaiyākum.
taṭṭu - taṭṭi = aṟaiyai allatu vaḻiyait taṭukkum mūṅkiṟpāy allatu teṉṉaṅkiṭuku.

taḷ - taḷai = taṭukkum kaṭṭu allatu vilaṅku, kaṭṭu allatu piṇippu, ceyyuṭcīriṉ ōcaip piṇippu.
taḷaital = taṭuttal, piṇittal,
taḷ - taṭu - taṭai. taṭu - taṭuppu. taṭu - taṭukku = ciṟu taṭṭipōṉṟa pāy.
taṭu - taṭakku - taṭaṅku - taṭaṅkal.
taṭṭut taṭaiyiṉṟi, taṭṭut taṭaṅkaliṉṟi eṉpaṉa maraput toṭarmoḻikaḷ.
taḷ - (taḻu) - (taku) - takai. takaittal = taṭuttal, 
muḷ - muṭṭu = muṭṭuppāṭu, taṭai.
tīyataik kāṇṭalum kēṭṭalum nalviṉaikkut taṭaiyākak karututaliṉ, avaṟṟai muṟaiyē, kaṇṭu muṭṭu kēṭṭumuṭṭu eṉpar camaṇar.
muḷ - (muṭu) - muṭai = nerukkaṭi, muṭai - muṭaiñcal.
muṭṭu - (muṭṭam) - viṭṭam = mukaṭṭiṉ kuṟukkuttaram.
muḷ - (muṟu) - maṟu - maṟukku = kuṟukku. maṟukkāṭṭutal = kuṟukkāka niṉṟu iṭam valam cellātapaṭi taṭuttal. kuṟukkum maṟukkum eṉpatu vaḻakku.
mul - vil - vilaku - vilaṅku = kuṟukku, kuṟukkāka vaḷarvatu, taṭukkuṅ kaṭṭu (taḷai).
vilaṅkūṭaṟuttatu  (cilap. 16: 213).
vil - villaṭai = taṭai. vilaṅku - vilaṅkam - villaṅkam = taṭai.
vilaṅku - vilaṅkal = nāṭṭiṉ kuṟukkē niṉṟu oṉṟaṉ celavait taṭukkum malai.
kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurīip pōla eṉṉum uvamaiyai nōkkuka.
oruvaṉ ōr aṟiyāta nāṭṭil ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu. vaḻiyil malaiyiruppiṉ, taṭaiyuṇṭu cuṟṟic celvatu allatu mīḷvatu iyaṟkai. karikāl vaḷavaṉiṉ vaṭaticaip paṭaiyeṭuppu paṉimalaiyāltaṭaiyuṇṭatu.
v. kaṭai
oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟai muṭṭum muṉai ataṉ kaṭaiyātalāl, muṭṭaṟkaruttil kaṭaimaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
pakkam nōkkiyatum mēṉōkkiyatum eṉak kaṭai iruvakai. pakkam nōkkiyatu muṉai allatu kaṭai; mēṉōkkiyatu ucci. talai eṉpatu ivviraṇṭiṟkum potu. 
uccimaik karuttum, kaṭaimaik karuttum oṉṟē. āyiṉum, oppiṉ muṭittal eṉṉum utti paṟṟi, mēṉōkkiya uyarcci kuṟitta coṟkaḷuṭaṉ mēṉōkkiya ucci kuṟitta coṟkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum, naṭṭukku niṟkum nilaittiṇaip poruḷkaḷiṉ uccip peyarkaḷ uyartaṟ karuttaiyum, paṭukkaiyāyk kiṭakkum nilaittiṇaip poruḷkaḷiṉ kaṭaip peyarkaḷ muṭṭaṟkaruttaiyum, irunilaiyum aṭaiyak kūṭiya iyaṅku tiṇaip poruḷkaḷiṉ uccip peyarkaḷ av iru karuttaiyum, aṭippaṭaiyāyk koṇṭavai yeṉṟaṟika.
paṭukkaiyāyk kiṭakkum oru tūṇiṉ muṉai, atu naṭṭukku niṟkum pōtu ucciyāy māṟutal kāṇka.
kuṭu - kuṭumi = muṭi, muṭipu (tīrmāṉamāṉa koḷkai)
koṇṭa kuṭumittit taṇpaṇai nāṭē  (puṟam. 32.)
kuṭu - (kaṭu) - kaṭai - kaṭaici. kaṭaiyaṉ = nāṟpāl varicaiyil iṟutiyil kūṟuppaṭum uḻavaṉ. kaṭaicci - kaṭaici = uḻatti.
kuṭu - koṭu - kōṭi = kaṭaici.
tul - (tal) talai = kaṭai, ucci, mēluṟuppu.
irutalai maṇiyaṉ, irutalaik koḷḷi, irutalaik kāmam mutaliya toṭarkaḷil, talai eṉpatu muṉaiyai allatu kaṭaiyaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
naṭuvaṇa teyta irutalaiyum eytum
eṉpatilum aḵtē.
muṇṭu = talai. muṭi = talai, ucci, uccik koṇṭai, talaimayir.
muḷ - muḻu - muku - mukuṭam = muṭi. mukuṭam - makuṭam - muṭi, muṭikkalam, pāṭṭiṉ iṟutit toṭar.
mucci = talaiyucci. mōcikai = uccimuṭi; muñcam = uccayaṇi.
vi. ellai
muṭṭuṅkaṭai oru poruḷiṉ muṭiviṭamāy allatu ellai yāyiruppatāl, muṭṭaṟkaruttil ellaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ul - ula. ulappu = muṭivu, aḷavu, ellai.
ul - ol = ellai, muṭivu.
ol - el - ellai. el - elkai.
el - ēl - ēlvai = kālavellai.
ōr - ōram = muṭiyum pakkam, pakkam.
ōrañcollutal = orupāṟ kōṭic collutal.
kuṭu - koṭu - kōṭi = kaṭaici, ellai, kaṭaikōṭi, teruk kōṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷaik kāṇka.
tuku - (tiruku) - tikai = muṭivu. ellai, tikaital = muṭital, tīrtal. tīrmāṉamātal. mātam tikainta cūli, ataṉ vilai iṉṉum tikaiyavillai, eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
tikai - ticai = muṭivu, ellai, pakkam. (tukku) - tikku.
ticaiccol = pala ticaikaḷil vaḻaṅkum koṭun tamiḻc col.
ticaiccol eṉpatu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭātalālum, mayaṅkutalaik kuṟikkum tikai eṉṉum collum ticai eṉṟu tiritalālum, ellaiyaik kuṟikkum ticai eṉṉum col teṉ collēyām.
nī ticaittatuṇṭō  (kampa. kaikēci cūḻ. 18)
ivaṉ cintai tuḻāyt ticaikkiṉṟatē  (tiv. tiruvāy, 4,6,1.)
ticaippuṟutaluñ cīvarkku  (vētā. cūṭā. 110.)
eṉṉun toṭarkaḷil, mayaṅkutalaik kuṟikkum tikai eṉṉuñ col ticai eṉṟu tirintiruttal kāṇka. ticai eṉṉuñ colliṉ mūlavaṭivāṉa tikai eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkāmaiyaiyum nōkkuka.
ticai - (tēcu) - tēcam - tēyam - tēm = ticai tēcam, pakkam, iṭam.
avaṉ maṟai tēe nōkki  (akam. 48)
tevvar tēettu  (puṟam. 6)
ticai tēcam, eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pakkam eṉṉum poruḷil varutalai, antat ticaikkē pōkamāṭṭēṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ vaḻakkālum, ēkatēcam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkālum, aṟiyalām. ēkatēcam = oru pakkam, oru pakuti.
tēcam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu ellaiyaik kuṟittu, piṉpu ōr ellaiyil allatu pakkattil uḷḷa nāṭṭaik kuṟittatu, o. nō. cīmai = ellai, nāṭu.
tikkut tikkeṉṟu aṭittuk koḷḷukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmaiyālum, nāttaṭṭutal tikkutal eṉappaṭutalālum, tikai eṉṉuñ col kakaravoli koṇṭiruttalālum, tikku eṉṉum collum teṉ collē yeṉka.
muḷ - muḷi - viḷi - viḷimpu = ōram. viḷimpu - (viḷimpu) - 
vaṭimpu.

muṭṭu - maṭṭu = aḷavu. maṭṭu - maṭṭam = aḷavu, cariyāṉa aḷavu, tāḻnta aḷavu, tāḻvu.
kaṭalmaṭṭam, maṭṭappalakai, maṭṭakkutirai, maṭṭat tuṇi mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
vii. muṭital
ulattal = muṭital;
uṟutal = toṭutal, muṭṭutal. uṟu - iṟu - īṟu. iṟu - iṟuti. iṟutal = muṭital;
iṟu - iṟa - iṟappu = cāvu.
iṟuttal = kaṭamaiyait tīrttal, vari celuttutal. iṟu - iṟai = kaṭamai (vari).
iṟuttal = vaḻiccelavai viṭṭiruttal, taṅkutal, iṟu - iṟai = iṟaivaṉ = eṅkun taṅkiyiruppavaṉ.
uṟu - aṟu - aṟuti = muṭivu.
aṟu - aṟavu = nīkkam.
tuvalutal = cātal.
tul - (til) - (tir) - tīr. tīrtal = muṭital, nīṅkutal, tīrmāṉam = muṭivu, muṭipu. tīr - tīrvai.
pol - poṉṟu. poṉṟutal = iṟattal, muṭital.
mul - muṟṟu, muṟṟutal = muṭital, iṟattal.
muḷ - muḷi - viḷi. viḷital = muṭital, iṟattal. muḷital = muṟṟutal, keṭutal. viḷi - vī. vītal = muṭital. cātal. muḷ - viḷ - viḻu - viku - vikuti = iṟuti. īṟu.
muḷi - (muḻi) - muṭi - muṭivu. muṭi - maṭi. maṭital = iṟattal.
muṭi - muci. o. nō. oṭi - oci mucital - muṭital. iṟattal.
mūṅkil pōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr eṉṉum tirumaṇa vāḻttu marapuraiyaik kāṇka.
muṭṭutal = muṭital.
muñcutal = muṭital. cātal. mūcutal = muṭital, cātal. muccutal = muṭittal, mūḻttal = mutirtal. mūḻ - mūy. mūyttal = muṭittal.
viii. maḻuṅkal
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, meliyatu orē muṟaiyilum valiyatu pala muṟaip piṉṉum muṉai maḻuṅki moṭṭaiyākiṉṟaṉa. kūrākac cīviṉa eḻutukōl tēyvatu, eḻutappaṭum karuviyil atu palamuṟai muṭṭuvataṉālēyē.
(nuḷ) - (nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuṅku. naḻuṅkutal = maḻuṅkutal. naḻuṅku - naḻukku - naḻukkam = maḻukkam, maḻuṅkal.
poṭṭu - poṭṭai = kaṇṇoḷi maḻukkam.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maḻu - maḻuku - maḻuṅku - maḻukku - maḻukkam. 
maḻukku - maḻukkai, maḻuṅku - maḻuṅkal.
muḷ - muṭṭu - moṭṭai - maṭṭai. maḻu moṭṭai - maḻumaṭṭai.
mūḷ - (moḷ) - moḻukku - moḻukkaṭṭai - mokkaṭṭai = maḻukkamāṉatu.
moḻukku - mokku - mokkai = kūriṉmai.
(moḷ) - moṇ - moṇṇai = maḻukkam, kūriṉmai.
moṇṇai - maṇṇai = maḻukkam.
maṇṇai - maṇai = maḻukkal, maḻuṅkalāytam.
maṇai - maṇaiyaṉ = maḻuṅkalāytam.
ix. matimuṭṭu
matiyāṉatu kūriya kaṇpōṉṟiruttalāl, maṭamai ataṉ maḻukkamākak kūṟappaṭum. kūriya matikkuk kūr eṉṟē peyar.
kūḻai = kuṭṭaiyāṉatu, matikkuṟaivu.
kūḻai māntartañ celkati  (tēvā: 462, 9)
muṭṭu - muṭṭāḷ. muṭṭu - muṭṭaṉ = mūṭaṉ.
muṭṭu - (muṭṭi) - maṭṭi. muṭṭu - moṭṭai = maṭṭai = mūṭaṉ. muṇṭu = maṭamai. muṇṭam = aṟivillātavaṉ.
x. matimaḻukkam
moṇṇai = kūriṉmai. moṇṇaiyaṉ = aṟivu maḻuṅkiyavaṉ.
moṇṇai - maṇṇai = maḻukkam, maṭamai. maṇaiyaṉ = matik kūrmaiyaṟṟavaṉ.
maḻukku - makku = matiyaṟṟavaṉ. maḻukkaṭṭai - vaḻukkaṭṭai = matimaḻukkam, mūṭattaṉam.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu = matiyaṟṟavaṉ.
xi. moṭṭaiyātal
muṉaimaḻuṅkiyatu moṭṭaiyākum.
moṭṭaiyātal, uṇmaiyāy moṭṭaiyātalum aṇi vakaiyil moṭṭaiyātalum eṉa iruvakai.


uṭampiṟkut talaiyiṉmaiyum, talaikkup pākai makuṭa miṉmaiyum, maṇṭaikku mayiriṉmaiyum, uṟuppiṟku aṇi yiṉmaiyum, mēlukku āṭaiyiṉmaiyum, nilattiṟkup payiriṉ maiyum, muṭaṅkalukkuk kaiyeḻuttiṉmaiyum, marattiṟkuk kiḷaiyiṉmaiyum, kompuḷḷa vilaṅkiṟkuk kompiṉmaiyum, vāluḷḷa uyirikku vāliṉmaiyum, vāliṟku ataṉ nuṉiyiṉ maiyum, ivai pōlvaṉa piṟavum, moṭṭaimaiyākak karutappaṭum.
kuṭṭu - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭāntarai = veṟunilam.
kuḷ - (kūḷ) - kūḻai = moṭṭaivāl, vālaṟṟatu. aḷavaṭiyiṉ iṟutic cīril mōṉai mutaliyaṉa villāta toṭai.
coṭṭu - coṭṭai = vaḻukkai, vaḻukkait talai.
poṭṭu - poṭṭal = pāḻnilam, veṟunilam, talai vaḻukkai.
pōṭu = moṭṭai.
mūḷi = uṟuppukkuṟai. aṇiyaṟṟatu. mūḷikkuṭam (vāyaṟunta kuṭam) mūḷikkātu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka. mūḷi - mūḻi = mūḷikkāti.
muṇṭu - moṭṭaiyāṉatu. kiḷai taṟitta kaṭṭai.
muṇṭu - muṇṭam = talaiyillātatu moṭṭaiyāṉatu, kiḷaitaṟitta kaṭṭai, uṟuppukkuṟai, āṭaiyillāvuṭampu.
āṭaiyillāvuṭampai muṇṭakkaṭṭaiyeṉṟum moṭṭaik kaṭṭai yeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
muṭṭu - moṭṭai. moṭṭait talai = pākaiyaṟṟa talai, mayir veṭṭiṉa talai. moṭṭai maṇṭai = maḻitta talai.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ. maṇṇutal = maḻittal. intak katti maṇṇātu, eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
maḷ - maḻi - vaḻi. maḻittal = moṭṭaiyaṭittal (talai vaṟaṇṭutal).
maḻittalum nīṭṭalum vēṇṭā  (kuṟaḷ 280)
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭam = maḻitta talai. muṇṭaṉ = maḻitta talaiyaṉ. camaṇaṉ, maḻikkum muṭiviṉaiñaṉ. muṇṭai = maḻitta talaicci, kaimpeṇ. muṇṭai - muṇṭaicci. muṇṭu - muṇṭi. o. nō. tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi.
muṇṭittal = talaimaḻittal, muṇṭi = maḻitta talaiyaṉ, maḻiviṉaiñaṉ. muṇṭi + aṉam = muṇṭaṉam. o.nō. kaṇṭi + aṉam = kaṇṭaṉam.
muṇṭi + itam = muṇṭitam.
aṉam itam eṉpaṉa toḻiṟ peyar vikutikaḷ.
e.ṭu. 	vañci - vañcaṉam. tappu - tappitam.
moḻukku - moḻukkaṉ = vēlaippāṭillāta aṇi; maḻuṅku - maḻuṅkaṉ = vēlaippāṭillāta aṇi.
(moḻu) - mōḻai = moṭṭai. kompillā māṭu, maraaṭi muṇṭam.
ēḻaiyaik kaṇṭāl mōḻaiyum pāyum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. mōḻaimukam = (moṭṭai mukamuḷḷa) paṉṟi, mōḻal = paṉṟi.
moṇṭu = moṭṭaikkai. kaikkuṟai. moṇṭu - moṇṭi = kai kuṟaintavaṉ. moṇṭimuṭam eṉpatu vaḻakku.
noṇṭutal = kuṟainta kāloṭu naṭattal. oru kālāl naṭattal allatu tāvic cellutal.
noṇṭu - noṇṭi = noṇṭutal, noṇṭupavaṉ. ōraṭi kuṟukiya īraṭiccintu.
muṭam eṉpatu vaḷaivu eṉṟum, moṇṭi eṉpatu kuṟai eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika.
xii. maṅkal
maṅkal eṉpatu uḷi maḻuṅkal;
maḻuṅku - maṅku - maṅkal.
maṅkal niṟam = mañcaḷ niṟam. veḷḷaiyumaṉṟip paccaiyumaṉṟi iṭai nikarttāy maṅkiya niṟamākaviruttaliṉ, mañcaḷ niṟam mutalāvatu maṅkal eṉappaṭṭatu. maṅkal - mañcal - mañcaḷ, o.nō. kaḻaṅku - kaḻañcu,
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañci.
mañcal = mañcaḷ niṟamuḷḷa carakku.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil kaṇavaṉōṭu kūṭi vāḻum peṇṭir aḻakiṟkum uṭal nalattiṟkum maṇattiṟkum mañcaḷ tēyttuk kuḷippatu vaḻakkamātaliṉ, mañcaṭkum mañcaḷ niṟattiṟkum maṅkalat taṉmai ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu. tirumaṇa varicaiyil vaikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ mañcaḷum oṉṟākum.
maṅkal - maṅkalam = mañcaṭpūccāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum makaḷir vāḻkkai nalam, mañcaḷālum mañcaḷ niṟattālum kuṟikkap peṟum naṟceyti, makaḷir vāḻkkai nalattiṉ aṭaiyāḷamāṉa aṇi (tāli).
maṅkalam - maṅkalai = kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻpavaḷ, kaṇavaṉai iḻavātavaḷ.
maṅkalam - maṅkali = maṅkalai.
maṅkai = maṅkalamākiya maṇapparuvattāḷ.
maṇattiṟkuriyavar patiṉāṟāṭṭaip paruvattāṉum paṉṉīrāṭ ṭaip paruvattāḷum eṉṟu akapporuḷilakkaṇamum,
mākavā ṉikarvaṇkai mānāykaṉ kulakkompar
īkaivāṉ koṭiyaṉṉāḷ īrāṟāṇ ṭakavaiyāḷ.  (maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭal, 2-4)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum kūṟutalaiyum, paṉṉīrāṭṭaip paruvattār maṅkaiyeṉap paṭutalaiyum, nōkkuka.
maṅku - makku - makkal = oḷimaḻuṅkal.
xiii. vaḻittal
maḻi - vaḻi. talai vaḻittal mukam vaḻittal eṉṉum vaḻakkuk kaḷaik kāṇka. talaivaḻittalait talai vaṟaṇṭutal eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. vaḻittal eṉpatu muṇṭittal eṉṟu maṭṭum poruḷ paṭum. vaṟaṇṭutal eṉpatu talaimayiraic cuṟaṇṭiyeṭuttal pōl valitāy vaḻittalaik kuṟikkum.
mayir peyarvatu pōṉṟē tōl peyarvatum oruvakai muṇ ṭaṉam allatu moṭṭaiyātalāl, tōlaip peyarppatum vaḻittal eṉappaṭum. maramuñ cuvarum pōṉṟa karaṭumuraṭāṉa poruḷ uṭampōṭu uracit tōlaip peyarttu viṭṭāl, maram vaḻittuviṭṭatu cuvar vaḻittu viṭṭatu eṉpar. katti vaḻittalum karaṭu muraṭāṉa poruḷ vaḻittalum viṉaivaṭivil ottiruttaluṅ kāṇka. vaḻital eṉpatu tōl peyartalākiya taṉ viṉaiyaik kuṟikkum. vaḻi - vaḻal, vaḻalutal = neruppi ṉāl ventu tōlurital. vaḻal - vaḻaṟṟu - vaḻaṭṭu. vaṟaṟṟutal = neruppil vāṭṭit tōlurittal. allatu mayir nīkkutal.
ōlaiyāl nākkiluḷḷa acaṭṭai nīkkutalum, cīppāl talai mayiraic cīvutalum, maṇveṭṭiyāl vāykkāliluḷḷa cēṟṟai iḻutteṟitalum, ciṟaṅkaiyāl oru poruḷai iḻuttut taḷḷutalum, ivai pōlvaṉa piṟavum, viṉaiyilum viḷai vilum katti vaḻittalai ottiruttalāl, avaiyum vaḻittal eṉappaṭum.
vaṭittal = nāvaḻittal, nāvait tiruttutal.
vaṭiyā nāviṉ vallāṅkup pāṭi  (puṟam. 47)
vaḻi - vaṭi - vār. vaṭittal =talaimayirai vārutal. 
vaṭikkoḷ kūḻai  (naṟ. 23.)

vārtal = urital (akam, 69), vaḻittaḷḷutal, kūṭṭutal, talaimayiraic cīvutal. vāri = cīppu, kuppai vāri mutaliya vāruṅ karuvi. cikkuvāri ciṇukkuvāri eṉpaṉa talaimayir vāruṅ karuvikaḷ. vārkōl - viḷakkumāṟu. vāriyaṉ = kaḷattil nellaik kūṭṭik kuvippavaṉ. aṅṅaṉam kuvittalai mēṟ pārkkum aluvalāḷaṉ. paḷḷar ūrāṇmaiyil āṭkaḷaik kūṭṭupavaṉ. vāriyam - vāriyaṉ toḻil, mēṟpārvai, mēṟpārvaikkuḻu.
kumpa aḷanta paṭiyiṉ mēluḷḷa kūmpiya pakutiyaik kaiyāl taṭṭutalum vaḻittalai ottiruttalāl, talai taṭṭiyaḷattalait talaivaḻittal eṉpar. aḷakkappaṭum poruḷ nīrpporu ḷāyiṉ paṭi maṭṭattiṟku mēl kūmpātoḻukumātaliṉ, atu vaḻital eṉṉun taṉ viṉaiyāṟ kūṟappaṭum. koḷkalaṅkaḷilum ēri kuḷam pōṉṟa koḷḷiṭaṅkaḷilum nīr nirampiyōṭutalai vaḻintōṭutal eṉpatu, immuṟai paṟṟiyē.
xiv. vaṭittal
vaḻi - vaṭi. cōṟu ventapiṉ nīrai vaḻiyac ceytal vaṭittal eṉappaṭum. tāṉāy vaḻintōṭāta nīraik kalattaic cāyttu vaḻiyac ceyvatu vaḻittaliṉum vēṟu paṭṭatātaliṉ, vaṭittal eṉappaṭṭatu. poruḷ vēṟu pāṭṭaic col vēṟupāṭu kuṟittatu. ēri kuḷaṅkaḷil nīr nirampit tāṉāy vaḻiyātu maṭai allatu kaliṅku vaḻiyāyp pāyumāyiṉ, atuvum vaṭital eṉap paṭum. aṅṅaṉam pāyccutal vaṭittal eṉappaṭum. kūḻuṅ kañciyum pōṉṟa nekiḻporuḷ tāṉāy vaḻitalaiyum vaṭital eṉpar.
vaṭi-vār. orukalattiṉiṉṟu nīrai ūṟṟutalum ōr ulōkattaik kāycci yūṟṟutalum vaṭittal allatu vārttal eṉappaṭum. kaṇṇīrpōlat tāṉāy vaṭital vārtal eṉappaṭum.
vār - vārppu - vārppaṭam = kāycciyurukki vārttuc ceytatu;
vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē  (puṟam. 812)
oru kalattiluḷḷa nīrai ūṟṟumpōtu atiluḷḷa maṇṭiyait taṭuttaṟkuk kalattiṉ vāyil tuṇikaṭṭuvatu. vaṭikaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. itai vaṭārkkāṭṭār vēṭu kaṭṭutal eṉpar. veyilukkut talaiyil tuṇikaṭṭuvataiyum oppumai paṟṟi vēṭukaṭṭutal eṉpatuṇṭu.
xv. vārtal (nīḷal)
vaṭiyum nīrpporuḷ nīṇṭu nērāka viḻumātaliṉ, vaṭitaṟ karuttil nīṭcik karuttum nērpuk karuttum tōṉṟiṉa. kātai nīṭṭi vaḷarttalaik kātu vaṭittal eṉpar.
kuḻaiviravu vaṭikātā  (tēvā. 1091,1)
vaṭi - vari - varicai. vari - variccu - variccal = nīṇṭa kuccu. vaṭi - vār. vārtal = nīḷutal, nērātal, nīḷakkiḻittal.
vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumaiyuñ ceyyum poruḷa  (tol. uriyiyal, 19)
vār - vāri = nīṇṭa peruṅ kampu. vār - vārai = nīṇṭa peruṅkampu. vāraivati = āṟṟiṉ kuṟukkāka vāraiyāl amaikkappaṭṭa vaḻi, pālam. vāraivati - vārāvati.
vār = nīṇṭa tōltuṇṭu.
vār - vāl = nīṇṭavuṟuppu. vāl - vālam = vāl, vāla
mukam = nīṇṭamukam.
vāḷ = nīṇṭa katti, vāḷam = nīṇṭa vār, kampi. vāḷ - vaḷ - vaḷpu = vār.
vār - vāram = colloḻukkamum icaiyoḻukkamumuḷḷa pāṭal.
xvi. vaḻukkai
moṭṭaiyāṉa poruṭkaḷ, ciṟappāka maḻitta talai, vaḻukkai yāyiruppatāl, maḻukkaik karuttiṉiṉṟu vaḻukkaik karuttup piṟantatu.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iḻu - iḻum. iḻumeṉal = vaḻu vaḻuppu.
muḻu - moḻu - moḻukkai - maḻukkai - vaḻukkai. moḻu - maḻu.
maḻu - maḻa - maḻamaḻa - maḻamaḻappu = vaḻavaḻappu. maḻu - vaḻu. maḻa - vaḻa.
vaḻu - vaḻukku - vaḻukkal = vaḻuvaḻuppu. vaḻukkait tēṅkāy.
vaḻu = vaḻukkai. vaḻukkāy = vaḻukkait tēṅkāy.
vaḻu - vaḷukkai = vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa iḷantēṅkāy uḷḷīṭu.
vaḻutalai = vaḻavaḻappāṉa kattirikkāy.
vaḻutuṇai = kattarikkāy.
vaḻuval = tēṅkāy vaḻukkai.
vaḻuvaḻuppu = vaḻavaḻappu, meṉmai.
vaḻu - vaḻuku vaḻukutal = vaḻuvaḻuppāyiruttal.
vaḻu - vāḻai = vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa maram. maram vaḻukum eṉpatu viṭukatai.
vaḻa - vaḻaku = vaḻavaḻappu, meṉmai.

xvii. vaḻukkal
vaḻukkaiyāṉa iṭamum poruḷum vaḻukkum.
(uḻu) - iḻu - iḻukkal = vaḻukkal.
(nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuṅku - naḻuṅkutal = vaḻuvutal; caṟukkutal.
vaḻu - vaḻuku - vaḻukku - vaḻukkal = caṟukkal, caṟukku nilam.
vaḻukkumaram = caṟukkumaram.
vaḻuvaḻuttal = vaḻukkutal. vaḻu - vaḻuvu. vaḻuvutal = caṟukkutal.
vaḻāal = vaḻukkukai.
vaḻūu maruṅkuṭaiya vaḻāa lōmpi  (malaipaṭu. 21)
xviii. naḻuval
vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa poruḷ kaiyaiviṭṭu naḻuvum.
muḻu - maḻu - vaḻu. muḻu - moḻu - moḻuppu. moḻupputal = maḻupputal.
moḻuppu - maḻuppu. maḻupputal = valakkāramāyp pēcik kaṭamaiyiṉiṉṟu naḻuvutal.
vaḻu - vaḻuvu - vaḻuval = naḻuval. vaḻuvāṭi = kāriyattai naḻuvaviṭupavaṉ.
vaḻu - vaḻukku. vaḻukkutal = tapputal.
(nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuvu - naḻuval.
naḻuvu - naḻuvi = piṭikoṭātavaṉ
naḻu - naḻuppu. naḻupputal = maḻupputal.
vaḻuval
neṟiyiṉiṉṟu vaḻukkalum vaḻuvalum tavaṟutalātaliṉ, vaḻukkaṟ karuttu vaḻuvaṟ karuttait taḻuviṟṟu.
(uḻu) - iḻu - iḻukku - iḻukkam = tavaṟu, tīyoḻukkam. iḻukku = tavaṟu.
muḻu - maḻu - vaḻu = tavaṟu. vaḻu - vaḻukku - vaḻukkam = tavaṟu. vaḻukkutal = tavaṟutal. vaḻukku = tavaṟu.
vaḻu - vaḻūu. vaḻu - vaḻāl - vaḻāal = tavaṟukai.
vaḻu - vaḻuvu - vaḻuval = tavaṟu, kēṭu.
vaḻu - vaḻumpu = kuṟṟam.
vaḻavaḻappu
vaḻukkum poruṭkaḷ cila oṭṭuntaṉmaiyaṉavāyiruttaliṉ, vaḻukkalaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu piciṉpōl oṭṭukiṉṟa allatu neyppacaiyuḷḷa cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uḻu - uḻuntu = vaḻavaḻappāṉa payaṟu.
koḻu - koḻuppu.
(nuḻu) - (niḻam) - niṇam. o.nō. taḻal - taṇal.
(muḻu) - maḻu - vaḻu - vaḻumpu = niṇam.
vaḻu - (vaḻal) - vaḻalai = cavukkāram (Soap).
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaṇṭai = vaḻavaḻappāṉa kāy. vaṇṭai - veṇṭai.
xix. kuṭṭaiyātal (ciṟuttal)
muṉai maḻuṅkiṉa poruḷum mēṉmēlum muṉai tēyum poruḷum, kuṭṭaiyākum. kuṟaitalum kuṭṭaiyātalē.
kuḷ - kuḷḷam. kuḷ - kuḷḷai. kuḷ - kuḷḷal.
kuḷ - kuṭṭai - kaṭṭai.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūḷi = kuḷḷam, kuḷḷamāṉatu.
kūḷ - kūḻai = kuḷḷam. kuṭṭai, kuḷḷamāṉatu, kuṭṭaiyāṉatu. kūḻai - kūḻaiyaṉ.
kuḷ - (kuṇ) - kuṇil = kuṟuntaṭi.
kul - ku (kuṟumai muṉṉoṭṭu - diminutive prefix)
e.ṭu. kukkirāmam (va.)
o.nō. nal - na. nappiṉṉai, nakkīraṉ.
xx. taṭṭaiyātal
mikak kuṭṭaiyāṉa poruḷ taṭṭaiyākum. uyaramāṉa poruḷkaḷellām caṟṟuk kuṟukiṉ kuṭṭaiyum, mikak kuṟukiṉ taṭṭaiyum, ām.
caṭṭu eṉpatu taṭṭutalaik kuṟitta oru paḻantamiḻ viṉaiccol. caṭṭuc caṭṭeṉṟu taṭṭukiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. taṭṭum poruḷum taṭṭappaṭum poruḷum taṭṭaiyākum. taṭṭutalum oruvakai muṭṭutalē. muṭṭutal, viṉaimutal muṭṭutalum karuvi muṭṭutalum eṉa iruvakai. ciṉaiviṉai viṉaimutal viṉai yokkum.
(cuttutal) = taṭṭutal. cuttu - cutti - cuttiyal = taṭṭuṅkaruvi. cuttu - cuṭṭu.
(cuṭṭu) caṭṭu - caṭai. caṭaital = aṭikkappaṭum āṇi muṉai maḻuṅkit taṭṭaiyātal.
caṭṭu - caṭṭam = taṭṭaiyāṉa marappaṭṭi.
caṭṭakappai = taṭṭakappai.
caṭṭu - caṭṭuvam - caṭṭukam = caṭṭakappai.
caṭṭuvam = caṭṭakappai, taṭṭaiyāṉa pāṉai.
caṭṭuvam - caruvam.
caṭṭam - taṭṭaiyāṉa paṉaiyōlai. caṭṭam vārutal = ōlaiyiṉ arukukaḷai arintu eḻutuvataṟku ēṟṟatākkutal.
caṭṭam - caṭṭaṉ = ōlaiccuvaṭi payilum māṇavaṉ.
caṭṭaṉ + nampi = caṭṭanampi = māṇavar talaivaṉākiya āciriyaṉ, talaimai māṇavaṉ, caṭṭanampi - caṭṭampi.
caṭṭanampippiḷḷai - caṭṭampippiḷḷai - caṭṭām piḷḷai.
caṭṭam = maraccaṭṭam, kōṭiḻukkum caṭṭappalakai, nērmai, ceppam, muṟaimai, nītiyoḻuṅku, varampu. viti.
māṇavar pārttu oḻuṅkāka eḻutuvataṟku mēl variyil varaiyappaṭṭiruppatu mēlvaric caṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
ōr amaippakattiṉ karuma naṭappiṟkuriya vitiyoḻuṅku muḻuvatum caṭṭa tiṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
caṭṭam = viti, vitikkum atikāram, atikārattāṟ peṟṟa vurimai.
iṭṭamuṭaṉ mutaliyār vāṅki vanta kāḷaitiṉam irupōruṇṇum
caṭṭamuṭaṉ koḷḷuṇṇum ...  (vētanāyakampiḷḷai taṉippāṭal)
caṭṭam - caṭaṅku = matavitippaṭi allatu oḻukka vitippaṭi naṭaipeṟum karaṇam.
caṭṭam = paṭam kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nāṟpuṟamuṅ kōkkap paṭum maraccaṭṭam. caṭṭak kaṭṭil. caṭṭak katavu, caṭṭap parampu. caṭṭa vāḷ mutaliyaṉa nāṟpuṟamuñ caṭṭaṅ kōttavai.
capparam mukaṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc caṭṭaṅkaṭṭutal āyatta viṉaiyāyiruttaliṉ, caṭṭaṅ kaṭṭutal eṉpatu āyattañ ceytal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.
uṭampu uyirukkuc caṭṭam pōṉṟiruppatāl, atuvum caṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
caṭṭam - caṭṭakam (frame, outline).
caṭṭam - caṭṭai = uṭampu, uṭampiṉ mīntōl, atu pōṉṟa meyppai.
mīntōl peyara aṭipaṭṭavaṉai nōkki avaṉ uṭampu caṭṭai caṭṭaiyāyk kaḻaṉṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟum, pāmpu mīntōl kaḻittalaic caṭṭai cuḻaṟṟutal eṉṟum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
caṭṭam - caṭam = uṭampu. o. nō. paṭṭam - paṭam.
caṭam = (caṭal) - caṭalam = uṭampu.
o. nō. paṭam - paṭal - paṭalam (parappu).
caṭalam eṉṉum vaṭivu vaṭamoḻiyilillai. cattiyum caṭalamum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
cappu
cappu eṉpatu, oru nekiḻ poruḷ ētēṉum oṉṟai muṭṭalaik kuṟikkuñ col. cāṇamum kaḷimaṇṇum paḻac cataiyum pōṟ kuḻainta poruḷkaḷai, nilattiṟ pōṭum pōtum cuvaril eṟiyum pōtum, cappu eṉṟa oli kēṭkum. ataṉāl cappeṉṟu viḻuntatu eṉpar. avvoliyeḻap pōṭappaṭum kuḻai poruṭkaḷ taṭṭaiyāṉa vaṭivaṅ koḷḷum. ataṉāl, cappu eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟu taṭṭaiyātal karuttuṇarttum pala coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
(cuppu) - cappu - cappai = taṭṭai, taṭṭaiyāṉatu, taṭṭaiyāṉa toṭaip pakuti.
cappaik kāy, cappaik kāl, cappait tirukkai, cappai mūkku, cappai vāy, cappai yelumpu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.
cappu - cappaṭṭai = taṭṭaiyāṉatu. ciṟaku, cappaṭṭaiyaram = taṭṭaiyaram.
cappu - cappal = cappaṭṭai. cappal - campal = vilait tāḻvu.
cappu - cappaṭi = cappaṭṭai.
cappai = taṭṭaiyāṉa patar. patar pōṟ payaṉaṟṟavaṉ (pataṭi).
cappu - cappaṉ = payaṉaṟṟavaṉ.
cappu - capparai = mūṭaṉ. cappaṭṭai = maṭaiyaṉ.
cappu - cappaṅki = mantaṉ.
cappu - capparam = moṭṭaiyāṉa eṭupputtēr.
cappu - cappaḷi. cappaḷital = cappaiyātal.
cappaḷittal = aṭiyiṟ cappaiyāka uṭkārtal.
cappaḷi - cappaḷam - cappaṇam - cammaṇam.
cappaṇaṅ kūṭṭutal, cammaṇaṅ kūṭṭutal, eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
cappaḷakkaṭṭai = cappaiyāṉa tāḷakkaṭṭai (cappaḷāk kaṭṭai).
cappu - cappatti = taṭṭaiyāṉa muttu.
cappu - cappāttu = (taṭṭaiyāṉa) ceruppu.
cappāttu - cappātti = taṭṭaiyāṉa allatu ceruppup pōṉṟa ilaiyuṭaiya kaḷḷivakai.
vaṭavar taṭṭaiyāṉa appa (roṭṭi) vakaiyaic cappātti eṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
cappu - (cappāḷi) - cappāṇi. o. nō. kaḷavāḷi - kaḷavāṇi.
cappāṇi = nilattiṟ cappaiyāyirukkum muṭavaṉ, avaṉaip pōl kuḻantai amarntiruntu kaitaṭṭum nilai.
taṭṭai eṉpatu uyarattiṟku māṟāṉa taṉmaiyātalāl, atai yuṇarttum cappaiyeṉṉum col potuvākat tāḻvāḷa nilaiyaiyē kuṟikkum.
cappai = taṭṭaiyāṉatu. tāḻntatu, payaṉaṟṟatu, cuvaiyaṟṟatu, keṭṭatu.
cappaittuṇi = maṭṭamāṉa tuṇi. cappai nilam = payaṉaṟṟa nilam, cappai vākku (cappaṭṭai vākku) = payaṉaṟṟa col.
cuvaiyaṟṟataic cappeṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkāta lāl, cappu eṉpatē cappai yeṉṟu tirintiruttal uṇarappaṭum.
coṭṭuc coṭṭeṉṟu coṭṭukiṟatu, cotteṉṟu viḻuntatu, eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmaiyālum, taṭṭuṅ karuvi yoṉṟu cutti eṉappaṭutalālum, caṭṭu cappu mutaliya coṟkaḷum tuvakkattil ukara mutalākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
taṭṭu
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu.
taṭṭutal = kaiyāl allatu karuviyāl koṭṭutal; atāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟai valitāy muṭṭac ceytal.
taṭṭu - (taṭai) - taṟai. taṟaital = caṭaital.
taṭṭu = taṭṭaiyāṉatu.
tulaittaṭṭu, tērttaṭṭu, pūttaṭṭu, veṟṟilait taṭṭu mutaliya vaṟṟai nōkkuka. pātti varicaiyāṉa nilappakutiyum taṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
taṭṭukkūṭai = taṭṭaiyāṉa kūṭai.
taṭṭu - taṭṭam = taṭṭaiyāṉa kiṇṇam.
taṭṭu - taṭṭai = taṭṭaiyāṉatu, aṟivili.
(tuṭṭu) - tiṭṭu (o. ku.). tiṭṭutiṭṭeṉṟu kēṭkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
taṭṭai - taṭai. vāḻaip paṭṭaiyai vāḻaittaṭai eṉpar.
toppu
toppu (o. ku.) oruvaṉ tuṇi tuvaippataik kuṟikkumpōtu, topput toppeṉṟu tuṇiyai aṭikkiṟāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
toppu - tappu - tapputal = tuṇi tuvaittal.
tappu - tappai = aṭi (te.) taṭṭaiyāṉatu. mūṅkiṟpaṟṟai, tappai vaittuk kaṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
tappu - tappaḷam = paṟṟu.
toppu - tuvai. tuvaittal = tuṇiyaṭittal, cammaṭṭiyā laṭittut taṭṭaiyākkutal.
poṭṭu - paṭṭu
paṭṭutal eṉpatu taṭṭutalaik kuṟitta oru paṇṭai viṉaiccol. paṭṭaṭai eṉṉuñ collai nōkkuka.
paṭṭu - paṭṭam = paṭṭaiyāṉa takaṭu, oru talaivaṉuṭaiya ciṟappaip poṟittu avaṉatu neṟṟiyiṟ kaṭṭum takaṭu, attakaṭṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭum ciṟappup peyar, oruvaṉukku makkaḷ pukaḻcciyākavō ikaḻcciyākavō vaḻaṅkum ciṟappup peyar, taṭṭaiyāṉa kāṟṟāṭi, marappaṭṭai, cīrai (maravuri), cīraipōṉṟa tuṇi.
paṭṭaṅ kaṭṭutal, paṭṭañ cūṭṭutal, paṭṭamaḷittal, paṭṭap peyar mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka.
paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi, tuṇiyil eḻutappaṭum ōviyam.
paṭam - paṇam = takaṭāṉa kācu.
paṭam - paṭām = tuṇi, tuṇikkaccu, yāṉaiyiṉ mukapaṭām.
paṭam - paṭakam = tiraiccīlai, paṭamāṭam.
paṭam - paṭaṅku = āṭai, tirai, paṭamāṭam, tuppākkiyiṉ aṭi, pātattiṉ muṟpakuti.
paṭaṅku - paṭaṅkam = paṭamāṭam.
paṭam - paṭavu - paṭaku = pāy kaṭṭiya tōṇi.
paṭam - paṭal = taṭṭipōṉṟa aṭaippu.
paṭal - paṭalam = mēṟkaṭṭi.
paṭal = paṭalikai = pūntaṭṭu.
paṭṭu - paṭṭā = paṭṭaivāḷ, vaṇṭip paṭṭai, āvaṇa ēṭu, āvaṇam.
paṭṭu - paṭṭi = cīlai, takaṭu, ēṭu, ēṭṭileḻutappaṭum aṭṭavaṇai, mūṅkiṟ piḷāccu, taṭṭi, taṭṭiyālamaitta kāl naṭaittoḻu, kāl naṭaippaṭṭiyuḷḷa ciṟṟūr. pakkattuk kollaikaḷiṟ pōy mēyum paṭṭimāṭupōṟ kaṭṭukkāva liṉṟit tiripavaṉ.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiyal = poruḷvilai yaṭṭavaṇai, taṭṭaiyāṉa variccal.
paṭṭi - paṭṭikai = ēṭu, paṭṭayam, cīlai, araikkaccai, mēkalai, yōkappaṭṭai.
paṭṭu - paṭṭai = taṭṭaiyāṉa poruḷ.
marappaṭṭai, vaṇṭippaṭṭai, vāḻaippaṭṭai, poṭip paṭṭai, tōṭpaṭṭai, yōkappaṭṭai mutaliya peyarkaḷaiyum, paṭṭaiyaṭittal, paṭṭai tīrttal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaiyum; paṭṭaiyaram, paṭṭaippuḻu mutaliya vaṟṟaiyum nōkkuka.
paṭṭai - paṭṭaiyam - paṭṭayam = paṭṭaivāḷ, ēṭu, takaṭu, ceppēṭṭāvaṇam.
paṭṭai - paṟṟai - pattai = taṭṭaiyāṉatu.
mūṅkiṟpaṟṟai tēṅkāyppaṟṟai mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
paṭṭam - pāṭṭam. pāṭṭaram = taṭṭaiyaram.
muṭṭu
muṭṭu - maṭṭu - maṭṭai = paṭṭai.
vāḻaippaṭṭaiyai vāḻaimaṭṭaiyeṉṟum poṭip paṭṭaiyaip poṭi maṭṭai eṉṟum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
maṭṭai = taṭṭaiyāṉa ōlaikkāmpu, taṭṭaiyāṉa aṭikaruvi (bat).
paṉaimaṭṭai pantaṭimaṭṭai mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.
maṭal = maṭṭai, itaḻ. maṭal - maṭali - vaṭali = maṭṭai veṭṭappaṭāta iḷampaṉai.
(maṭai) - maṇai = taṭṭup palakai, taṭṭaiyāṉa kaṭṭai.
aṭimaṇai arivāḷmaṇai mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
xxi. paṭartal
taṭṭaiyākum poruḷ paṭarntu paravum.
paṭṭu = ciṟṟūr.
paṭṭu - paṟṟu = nilam, ciṟṟūr, mēkappaṭai, tappaḷam.
paṭam = paṭṭaiyāka akalum pāmpiṉ kaḻuttu.
paṭam - paṇam - paṇi = paṭattaiyuṭaiya nalla pāmpu, pāmpu.
paṭaṅku - paṭaṅkāṉ = akaṉṟa pūrāṉ.
paṭal - paṭalam = parappu, iyaliṉum paranta nūṟpakuti, tiraipōṟ kaṇṇiṟ paṭarum catai.
paṭal - paṭalai = paṭarkai, paranta iṭam, vāyakaṉṟa paṟai.
paṭalai - patalai = vāyakaṉṟa paṟai.
paṭal - paṭar = paravum mēkappaṟṟu.
paṭartal = paravutal, akalutal, cellutal.
paṭṭai - paṭai = parappu, kūṭṭam, cēṉaipparappu. cēṉaip pakuti, mēkappaṟṟu.
paṭākai = akaṉṟa koṭi, nāṭṭiṉ uṭpirivu, kūṭṭam.
paṭākai - patākai = peruṅkoṭi. aintu viralum neruṅkip paranta kai.
pāṭavarai = vāḷavarai.
(paṭa) - para - paravu. para - parappu. para - parattu (pi.vi.)
para - paravai = paranta kaṭal. para - parampu.
parampaṭittal= uḻuta nilattiṟ kaṭṭiyaṭittup paravac ceytal.
parattai = parantu oḻukupavaḷ. parākam = paravum pūttūḷ.
para - pār = parappu, tērttaṭṭu, pāṟai, nilappakuti, ñālam.
pārmutir paṉikkaṭal  (tirumuru. 45)
pār - pāṟai.
pār - pāri. pārittal = paravutal, paruttal, virittal.
para - pā - pāvu. pā - pāy = paranta taṭukku. pāytal = parattal.
palakai - akaṉṟa pāḷam. palāciṉai = parakka naṭutal.
pāḷ = iruppuc caṭṭam. pāḷam = palakai. pāḷai = viriyum maṭal.
pāḻi = akalam. perumai, nakar (pērūr).
akaittal = aṭittal. akalutal = paṭartal, nīṅkutal, cellutal. akalam = mārpu.
akal = akaṉṟa maṇtaṭṭam.
akal - āl = paṭarumaram. ālpōṟ paṭarntu eṉṉum uvamai marapai nōkkuka. āl - ālam.
āli = perumpūtam.
aṭittal = taṭṭutal. aṭartal = taṭṭiyuruvākkutal. 
aitaṭarnta nūṟpeytu  (puṟam. 29).
aṭar = takaṭu; pūvitaḻ.
ataḷ = tōḷ.
avaittal = kuṟṟutal. aval = kuṟṟutalāl taṭṭaiyāṉa arici.
aviḻtal = virital, virintu kaṭṭu viṭutal.
alaittal - aṭittal. alaku = akalam, kattiyalaku.
alaku pākku = taṭṭaiyāṉa pākku.
alacutal = iḻaiyakalutal. alacal = iḻaiyakaṉṟa tuṇi.
alartal = malartal.
alavai - parattaimai.
kalattal = parattal. kalakka naṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
kaṭṭurai kalantakālai  (kampaṉ. kara. 68)
kala - kalavu - kalavam - kalāvam = araippaṭṭikai. mayiṟpīli.
kalai = āṭai.
callā = alacal tuṇi, callāri = alacaṟ cīlai.
callavaṭṭam = kēṭakavakai.
tuvaittal = kuṟṟutal. tukil = tuṇi. tukilikai = tuṇikkoṭi. takaḻi = akal.
takaṇ = puṟpaṟṟai. takaṇ - takaṭu.
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu - taṭṭam - taṭam = akalam, perumai, akaṉṟa kuḷam. taṭam - taṭākam.
taḷam = taṭṭu, parappu, paṭai itaḻ. tāḷam = cālar. 
tālam = taṭṭu, yāṉaikkātu.
nal - (nāl) - nālam - ñālam = paranta ulakam.
parumaiyum akalamum oṉṟē. periya ilai, periya tāḷ, periya taṭṭu, periya tuṇi, peruvaḻi eṉṟu akaṉṟa poruḷkaḷaik kūṟutal kāṇka.
naṉam = akalam. naṉam - naṉavu = akalam.
malartal = virital.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku nēr mūlam akaramutalāyiṉum, aṭimūlam ukaramutala eṉpatu oru talai. itu ippaṭala muḻumaikkum okkum.
xxii. amuṅkutal
amuṅkutalāvatu oru poruḷ aḻunta muṭṭutal.
uṟu - uṟuttu. (uḷ) - (aḷ) - aḻu - aḻuntu - aḻuttu - aḻuttam.
(um) - am - amuṅku - amukku - amukkam.

(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku - nerukku - nerukkam.
(3) kuttal muṟai
kuttal eṉpatu, moṭṭaik karuvi kuttutalum kūrṅkaruvi kuttutalum eṉa iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum.
i. kuttutal
kuḷ - kiḷ, kuḷḷutal - nakattāṟ kiḷḷutal.
kuttu - kuntu.
kuttu - kottu - koṭṭu.
cuḷ. cuḷkaḷ eṉṟu kuttukiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
cuḷ - cur - curukku - cuṟukku (kuttaṟkuṟippu)
tūṇṭutal = kuttutal.
tuḷ - teḷ - teṟu. teṟutal - koṭṭutal.
tuḷ - taḷ - (tay) - tai. taittal = kuttutal. muḷ taikkum eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
nuḷ. muḷ. mucumucuttal = tiṉaveṭuttal.
ii. kuntutal
kuntutalāvatu nilattiṟ kuttuvatupōl uṭkārtal. kuṇṭi kuttutal eṉpatu nākai vaḻakku. kutta vaittal eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kuttu - kuntu. kuntutal = uṭkārtal.
kuttu - kutti - kuti. kutiṅkāl = nilattiṟ kuttum aṭippātam.
kutai = nilattiṟ kuttum viṟkōlaṭi.
kutirtal = kāriyam kuntuvatupōl ēṟpāṭātal.
o.nō. sit - settle. 
kuntu - kuntaṉam = maṇik kallaik kuntavaittāṟ pōṟ patikkun takaṭu. kuttukkal kuttuviḷakku mutaliya peyarkaḷum kunti yiruttal paṟṟi ēṟpaṭṭavaiyē.

muḷ - (muḷku) - muḷkā. muḷkāttal = kuntiyiruttal. 
mucuppōla muḷkāntiruppar  (naṉṉūl virutti, cū. 96)
muḷkā - (uḷkā) - uḷkār - uṭkār.
iṉṟum kallā makkaḷ uḷukkārtal eṉṟē kūṟuvar.
iii. nērātal
kuttuk kallum piṟa kuttum poruḷkaḷum potuvāka nērākak kuttuvatāl, kuttaṟkaruttil nērpuk karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṅku nērpu eṉṟatu vaḷaiyāmaiyai cāyāmaiyai aṉṟu.
ceṅkuttu naṭṭukkuttu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka.
kuttutal = nērātal. kuttu - kutti - (katti) - kati.
katittal = nērātal. nēr kiḻakkākac cellutalaik kiḻakkē katikkac cellutal eṉpar nellai nāṭṭār.
kati - katir = vaḷaiyāmaṟ cellum oḷiyiḻai.
katir - katiravaṉ.

xii. kuṟumporuṭkaḷ
nuṇmaikku iṉamāṉatu kuṟumaiyātaliṉ, nuṇmaik karuttu kuṟumai allatu ciṟumaik karuttait taḻuvum.
(uḷ) - iḷ - iṭṭu. iṭṭitu = ciṟitu. iṭṭu - iṭṭikai = ciṟu ceṅkal.

kuru - kuṟu. kuṟumakaṉ ( - kuṟumāṉ - kuṟumaṉ), kuṟuṅkaṭṭil, kuṟunoy, kuṟumaṇal mutaliya puṇarp peyarkaḷil kuṟu eṉpatu ciṟumaiyuṇarttum.

kaṇṭaṅ kaṇṭamāy naṟukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu vaḻakku. kāynta kaṟittuṇṭu uppuk kaṇṭam eṉappaṭum. kaṇṭam - kāṇṭam = nūṟpakuti.

piḷḷaikkaṟṟāḷai, piḷḷaik kiṇaṟu. piḷḷaik kōṭṭai. piḷḷait takkāḷi, piḷḷaippiṟai, piḷḷaip peṭṭi, piḷḷai viḷātti (kuṭṭi viḷā) mutaliya puṇarp peyarkaḷil. piḷḷai eṉpatu ciṟumaiyaik kuṟikkumēṉum, atu iḷamaip peyariṉ āṭci virivē eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum.  kuṭṭit tolkāppiyam eṉpatil kuṭṭi eṉpatum atuvē.

poḷ - poṭi.

maṇikkayiṟu, maṇikkāṭai, maṇikkuṭal, maṇikkai, maṇittak kāḷi, maṇiccampā, maṇiccuṟā, maṇippayaṟu, maṇippuṟā, mutaliya puṇarppeyarkaḷil, maṇi eṉṉuñ col ciṟumaiyaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
maṇ - māṇ = kuṟaḷ, kuṟaḷaṉ, ciṟuvaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ, maṇamili (piramacāri).
kuṟumāṇoruvaṉ taṟkuṟiyākak koṇṭāṭum  (tēvāram 164, 5)
māṇāki vaiyamaḷantatuvum  (tiv. periyatiru. 8, 10, 8.)
māṇ - māṇi = ciṟiyatu, ciṟuvaṉ, maṇavātāṉ, māṇavaṉ.
karumāṇiyā yiranta kaḷvaṉē  (tiv. iyaṟpā, 2, 61.)
māṇ - māṇaval = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟkuñ ciṟuvaṉ.
māṇavaṉ - māṇavakaṉ - māṇavakam - kalvi.
māṇavakaṉ - māṇākkaṉ.
māṇavaṉaik kuṟikkum vēṟu cila peyarkaḷum ciṟuvaṉ ciṟumiyaik kuṟippaṉavākavē yuḷḷaṉa.
o.nō. piḷḷai = māṇavaṉ, māṇavi. paḷḷip piḷḷai eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
E. pupil, from L. pubillus, pupilla, dims of pupas, a boy; pupa. a girls.
E. pedant, from Gr. pais. paidos, a child.
māṇākkaṉ māṇākki eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷum, māṇavaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivamum, ataṉtiripāṉa māṇi eṉpatum, ivaṟṟukku aṭivaḻiyāṉa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷum, vaṭa moḻiyilillai.
(4) uṟaittal
uṟaittal eṉpatu aḻuttit tākkutal. ālaṅ kaṭṭiyum perumaḻaittuḷiyum nilattil valvēkamāy viḻuvataiyum. oru pēroli kātil aḻuttamāyp paṭuvataiyum, veppam kāram puḷippu mutaliyavai uṭalaiyō nāvaiyō tākkuvataiyum, uṟaittal eṉpar. ivaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu parumait tākkum ēṉaiya  nuṇmait tākkum ākum. ivaiyellām toṭutaliṉ vakai kaḷē. toṭutaleṉpatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟiṉ mēṟpaṭutal. paṇpi paṭutalum paṇpu paṭutalum eṉappaṭutal eṉa iruvakai. aḻuttamāyp paṭutalē uṟaittal.
i. cuṭutal
cuḷḷeṉṟu kuttukiṟatu eṉpatu pōṉṟē, cuḷḷeṉṟu veyilaṭikkiṟatu eṉpatum vaḻakku. kūrṅkaruviyāṟ kuttuvatu pōṉṟatu vemmaittākku.
uṟaittal = veyil uṭampait tākkutal.
cuḷ - cuḷḷāppu = kaṭuveyil.
cuḷ - cuḷḷai - cūḷai = ceṅkal cuṭumiṭam.
cuḷ - (cuṭku) - (caṭkāṉ) - cukkāṉ = aḷaviṟantu cuṭappaṭṭa ceṅkal.
cuḷ - cur - curam = cuṭumpālai. cur - curaṉ - cūraṉ = katiravaṉ.
cur - cuṟu - cuṟukku (cuṭutaṟ kuṟippu).
cuṟu - cuṟīr (cu.ku.).
cuḷ - cuṭu - cuṭal - cuṭalai. cuṭu - cūṭu.
cuṭu - cuṭar - cuṭarōṉ.
tuḷ - teḷ - teṟu. teṟutal = cuṭutal.
nīṅkiṟ ṟeṟūum  (kuṟaḷ. 1104).

iv. nīṟātal

v. kāyccutal
neruppil cuṭappaṭum poruḷkaḷum ulaiyiliṭṭuk kāyccap paṭum poruḷkaḷum kāyum allatu aviyum.
ul - ulai = camaiyaṟkuk kāyccum neruppu.
kollaṉ kaḷariyai ulaikkaḷam ulaittarai eṉṟum camaittaṟku neruppuḷḷa aṭuppiṉ mēl nīriṭṭa kalam ēṟṟuvatai ulaiyēṟṟutal eṉṟum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.

veyililum neruppōrattilum kāyum īrap poruḷkaḷ ularum.

vii. cenniṟam
neruppāṉatu civanta niṟamāyiruppatāl, neruppiṉ peyarkaḷi ṉiṉṟum appeyarkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum cenniṟattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,

eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. erimalar = murukkumalar (cīvaka. 662.)

āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ pukumuṉṉarē tamiḻar civa vaḻipāṭṭi ṉarāyiruntamaiyālum, civaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻc collātalā lum, ataṟkut tamiḻ vaḻiyāyp poruḷ kūṟātu vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyāy naṉmai ceypavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu porun tātu. anti vaṇṇaṉ centī vaṇṇaṉ māṇikkam mutaliya peyarkaḷ civaṉukkup payiṉṟu varutal kāṇka.

viii. terital
viḷaṅkum (oḷirum) poruḷkaḷ teḷivākat teritalāl, viḷakkattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ teḷivākat teritalaik kuṟikkum.
tulaṅkutal = viḷaṅkutal, teḷivākat terital. 
tul - tel - teṉ. teṉpaṭutal = terital, tōṉṟutal, pulap paṭutal.
tuḷaṅkutal = viḷaṅkutal.
tuḷaṅkumiḷam piṟaiyāḷaṉ  (tēvā. 88, 10)
tuḷaṅkoḷi = mikkavoḷi. tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇi = oḷi. (piṅ.).
tuḷ - teḷ. teḷḷutal = viḷaṅkutal. teḷivātal.

ix aṟital

xi. teḷital
ārāycciyāl aiyam nīṅkit teḷivu piṟakkum. ataṉpiṉ teḷiyap paṭṭataṉ mītu nampikkaiyuṇṭām. ataṉāl oru viṉai muyaṟcikku uṟutiyāṉa tīrmāṉañ ceyyappeṟum.
tuṇital = teḷivātal, uṟutiyāka muṭivu ceytal, tuṇivu = uṟuti, cūḻccit teḷivu.
tuṉṉal pōkiya tuṇiviṉōṉ  (puṟam. 23)
tuṇiccal = kaṭumaṉattiṭam.
tul - tel - teṉ - teṉpu = teḷivu. teṉpu - tempu.
teḷḷutal = teḷivātal. teḷ - teḷi. teḷital = aiyanīṅkutal, namputal.
teḷ - teṇ - teṭpu - teṭpam = teḷivu teṭṭavar = teḷintavar.
teri - tēr. tērtal= uṟutikoḷḷutal, muṭivu ceytal.
tēr - tēṟu. tēṟutal = teḷital, tiṭaṅkoḷḷutal, namputal, tuṇital.
tēṟu - tēṟṟu - tēṟṟam = teḷivu, uṟuti.
tēṟṟaṉ = uṇmaiyaṟivuḷḷavaṉ. tēṟṟu tēṟṟaravu = tēṟṟutal, teḷivittal.
teḷivu eṉpatu kalakkattiṟku etirāyiruppatāl, uḷḷam aiyuṟavu nīṅki uṇmaiyaṟivatum,oruvaṉnōyuṇṭanilaiyil 

ēṟpaṭṭa kalakkam nīṅki avaṉ uṭampu valuppeṟuvatum, tērviṟku muṉṉuḷḷa aiyam nīṅkumāṟu māṇavaṉ tērcci kāṭṭuvatum. teḷital allatu tēṟutal eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum.
nīrtteḷivu
nīrt teḷivum kalaṅkal nīkkamē. maṇṇun tūciyum piṟavum kalantirukkum pōtu nīrūṭu pārttal iyalātu. avai nīṅkiya nilaiyil nīr paḷiṅku pōliruppatāl, ataṉ uḷḷum aṭiyum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷ teḷivāyt teriyum. aṅṅaṉam teriyum nīr teṇṇīr.
cilaviṭattu, ūṭu teriyāta nīrpporuḷum ataṉiṉun tiṇṇiya nilaiyuṭaṉ oppu nōkkit teḷivu eṉappaṭum.
tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇi. tuṇital = teḷital.
tuṇinīr mellaval  (maturaik. 283.)
tuḷ - teḷ - teṇ - teṭpu - teṭpam = teḷivu.
teḷ - teḷi - teḷivu.
teṭṭa = teḷinta.
mālkari teṭṭa matappacai  (kampa. carapaṅ. 8).
teḷ - teṭu. tiṇṇamillāta kañciyait teṭuteṭuveṉṟiruk kiṉṟateṉpar.
teḷ - teri - tēr - tēṟu = teḷivu. tēṟutal = teḷital.
tēṟu = nīrait teḷivikkum tēṟṟāṅkoṭṭai.
tēṟupaṭu ciṉṉīr pōla (maṇi. 23, 142).

tēṟṟu - tēṟṟā (tēṟṟāṅkoṭṭai, akkoṭṭai maram).
tēṟṟāviṉuṭaiya vitaiyaik koṇṭu kalattē mellat tēṟṟak
kalaṅkiya nīriṟ citaivu teḷiyumāṟu pōla  (kalit. 142, varai).
xiii. kaḷ teḷivu
kaḷḷum tēṉum potuvāka arittum vaṭikaṭṭiyum teḷivāṉa tāka eṭukkap peṟuvatāl, teḷivu eṉṉum col avviraṇ ṭaiyum ākupeyarāy uṇarttiṟṟu.
iṉimaiyum veṟiviḷaippum kaḷḷukkun tēṉukkum potuviyal pātalāl, kaḷ tēṉ matu maṭṭu mutaliya peyarkaḷ avviraṇ ṭaiyum potuppaṭak kuṟikkum.
iyaṟkaik kaḷḷum ceyaṟkaik kaḷḷum eṉak kaḷ iruvakaip paṭum.
tul - (tel) - teṉ = (teḷivu, kaḷ) iṉimai.
teṉṉicai pāṭum pāṇaṉ  (tiruvālavā. 56, 7).
teṉ - tēṉ = teḷivu, kaḷ, matu. tēṉ - tēm - tīm - tīvu = iṉimai. tēṉ - tēṉi. tēṉittal = iṉittal. tīviya = iṉimaiyāṉa. tīm - (ti) - titti.
tuḷ - teḷ - teḷivu = patanīr, karuppañcāṟu. 
tēṟu - tēṟal = teḷinta kaḷ, tēṉ.
xiv. veṇmai
oḷiyāṉatu veṇmaiyāyiruttalāl, oḷiyai allatu viḷakkattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu (allatu collaṭi kaḷiṉiṉṟu) veṇmaiyaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
neruppu civantateṉṟum, kaṭumaiyāṉa katiravaṉoḷi veṇmai yāṉateṉṟum, potuvākak koḷḷappaṭum.
kuru - kuruku = veṇmai.
tul - tulliyam = veṇmai.
tuḷ - teḷ - teḷi. teḷital = veṇmaiyātal.
pul - pal - pāl = veṇmai.
pāl - vāl = veṇmai.
muḷ - muru - muruntu = veṇmai.
muḷ - viḷ - viḷar. viḷarttal = veṇmaiyātal.
viḷ - veḷ - veṇmai. veḷ - veḷḷai. veḷ - veḷu.
xv. veḷuttal
viḷ - viḷar. viḷarttal = veṇmaiyātal, veṭkutal.

viḷ - veḷ - veḷḷeṉa = viṭiya, veḷḷeṉak kāṭṭi = veḷḷeṅkāṭṭi = viṭiyaṟkālai.
veḷ - veḷu. kiḻakku veḷuttal = viṭiyumuṉ kīḻtticai veḷḷaiyātal.
veḷuttal = veṇṇiṟamātal, vaṇṇāṉ tuṇikaḷai veḷḷaiyākkutal allatu tuppuravākkutal.
veḷ = veḷku. veḷkutal = nāṇattāl mukam veḷuttal. veḷku - veṭku - veṭkam. veḷ - veḷiṟu, veḷiṟutal = ciṟitu veṇṇiṟamātal.
xvi. veṇmaiyāṉa poruṭkaḷ
ul - el - elumpu - eṉpu.

cul - cullu = veḷḷi.

tūlam = tūlakam = parutti.
tuḷ - tuy = pañcu. tuy - tūy - tūcu = pañcu.
tuḷ - tumpu - tumpai - veṇpūc ceṭi vakai.
veḷḷāṭaikkut tumpaip pūvai eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu ulaka vaḻakku.
pul - pal. pal - (paṉ) - paṉṉal = parutti. paṉ - paṉuval = parutti.
pal - pāl. pāl - vāl - vālukam = veṇmaṇal.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷiṅku. paḷ - pāḷ - pāḷitam = cōṟu.
