muḷ - muru - muruntu = mayiliṟakiṉ aṭi elumpu.
muḷ - muḷari = veṇṭāmarai. muḷ - (muṇṭu) - muṇṭakam = veṇṭāmarai.
muḷ - viḷ - viḷa = veṇtōṭṭukkāy maravakai. viḷa - viḷam. viḷa - viḷā - viḷavu - viḷavam. viḷā - viḷātti. viḷa - viḷar - viḷari = viḷā. viḷā = niṇam.
viḷavam - vilvam = viḷāviṟku iṉamāṉa kūviḷam.
karuviḷam = marappaṭṭai karuttuḷḷa viḷāvakai.
viḷ - veḷ - veḷḷil = viḷā.
veḷ - veḷḷam = veḷuppāṉa putupperukku nīr.
veḷ - veḷḷi. veḷ - veḷḷai.
veḷ - veḷicci = veṇṇiṟak koṇṭai mīṉ.
veḷ - veḷir - veḷiṟu = viḷaiyāta veṇmaram. aṟiviṉmai.
kuṟippu : - kuruku eṉpatu kokkai maṭṭuṅ kuṟiyātu nārai kāraṉṉam mutaliya paṟpala nīrppaṟavai vakaikaḷaik kuṟitta lāl, avaṟṟaik kuṟikkumpōtu vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum.
xvii. tūymai
aḻukkellām perumpālum piṟa niṟamāyiruppatālum, aḻukkaṟṟa cāyantōykkāta āṭai veṇmaiyāyiruppa tālum, cuvariṉ aḻukkaip pōkka veṇcuṇṇam pūcuvatālum, veṇmai tūymaik kaṭaiyāḷamām.
tuḷ - tuy - tuyya = tūya. tūy - tu - tuppu - tuppuravu = tūymai.
tuy - tūy - tūymai. tūy - tū.
pul - pal - pāl - vāl. vāṉmai = tūymai. vālāmai = tūymaiyiṉmai. vālaṟivu = tūya aṟivu.
mul - mullai = kaṟpu.
muḷ - veḷ - veḷḷai = tūymai, tūya āṭai, veṇpā. veḷ - veḷi = veṇpā.
ekkāraṇattaiyiṭṭum vēṟṟuttaḷai viravātu taṉṟaḷai koṇṭē iyalum tūymaiyuṭaiya pā veṇpā (veḷ+pā).
veḷḷai = kaḷaṅkamaṟṟavaṉ, cūtuvātillātavaṉ.
veḷḷaikkillai kaḷḷac cintai eṉṟār auvaiyār.
veḷ - veḷḷanti = kaḷḷaṅ kavaṭaṟṟavaṉ.
xviii. veṟumai
tūymai yeṉpatu vēṟṟup poruḷum vēṟṟup poruḷi yalpuṅ kalavātatātaliṉ, tūymaik karuttil taṉimaik karuttum taṉimaik karuttil veṟumaik karuttum tōṉṟum.
taṉikkaruppaic cuttak karuppu eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
veṇmai = tūymai, taṉimai, veṟumai.
veḷḷilai = ilai tavira vēṟu pū kāy oṉṟumillāta koṭiyilai. allatu uṇaviṉṟiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭum ilai.
veṇpāṭṭam = mārāyamillātu veṟumaiyāṉa pāṭṭam.
veṇṇilaik kaṭaṉ = īṭillātu veṟumaiyākak koṭukkap paṭum kaṭaṉ.
veḷ - veṟu - veṟumai = taṉimai, oṉṟumiṉmai. veṟu ilai veṟṟilai (veḷḷilai.)
veṟuñcōṟu = kaṟivakaiyillāt taṉic cōṟu.
māṭṭut tāmpaṇiyil (tāvaṇiyil) eṅku pārttālum veṟum māṭāyirukkum eṉpatu pōṉṟa vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
veṟuvāy = oṉṟumillāta vāy.
veṟu - veṟumam - veṟumaṉ. veṟumaṉē pōy viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu. oṉṟuñ collātu allatu ceyyātu pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatai nōkkuka.
veṟu - veṟṟu. veṟṟiṭam = oṉṟumillāta iṭam. veṟṟu vaṇṭi = veṟu vaṇṭi. veṟṟāḷ = taṉiyāḷ.
veṟu - veṟi. veṟittal = mukilum maḻaiyumiṉṟi vāṉam veṟumaiyātal.
veṟi - veṟittu - veṟikcu = veṟumai.
āḷiruntu pōyviṭṭa veṟṟiṭattaik kaṇṭu. veṟic ceṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpar.
xix. vaṟumai
poruḷillā veṟumaiyē vaṟumai.
veḷ - (veṇku) - veṅku - veṅkaṉ = oṉṟu millātavaṉ.
veḷ - veṟu. veṟumpayal = oṉṟumillāta payal. 
veṟu - veṟṟu. veṟṟukkaṭṭai = oṉṟumillātavaṉ.
veṟṟeṉat toṭuttal = (ciṟanta) poruḷiṉṟic collai yaṭukkutal.
veṟu - vaṟu - vaṟumai = poruḷiṉmai.
vaṟuṅ kūval = nīrillāk kiṇaṟu.
orunāḷum pōkātavaṉ tirunāḷukkup pōṉāṉām. tirunāḷum veṟunāḷāyp pōṉatām eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. veṟunāḷ = (ciṟanta) nikaḻcciyaṟṟa nāḷ.
xx. vīṇmai
payaṉillā veṟumai vīṇmai.
viḷ - vīṇ.
veḷ - veṭṭi. veṭṭi vēlai = payaṉaṟṟa vēlai, vīṇ vēlai.
veṭṭiyāḷ = vēlai ceyyāta āḷ.
veḷ - veṟu. veṟum pēccu = payaṉaṟṟa pēccu.
veṟu - vaṟu - vaṟitu = vīṇāṉatu, vīṇāka.
xxi. veḷi
vāṉaveḷi oṉṟumaṟṟa veṟṟiṭamātalāl, allatu veṟṟiṭa māyt tōṉṟutalāl, atu veṟumai yuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
viḷ - viṇ = ākāyam. viṇ - viṇṭu = ākāyam.
viḷ - (vicu) - vicumpu.
viḷ - veḷ - veḷi = ākāyam.
veḷḷiṭai = veṟumaiyāṉa iṭam.
vaṟitu nilaiiya kāyamum  (puṟam 20)
xxii. veḷippāṭu
veḷippāṭāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟaṉ uḷḷiruntu veḷi varutal.
viḷḷutal = veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
viḷ - viḷampu - viḷamparam.
viḷ - viṭu. viṭutal = veḷivarutal, piñcu viṭutal. inta maram piñcu viṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
viṭuttal = veḷippaṭak kūṟutal.
viṭu - (viṭi) - (viṭicci) - viricci = teyvattāl viḷampappaṭu vatākak karutappeṟum naḷḷirā naṟcol.
pākkattu viricci = paṭaimaṟavar ceṉṟu taṅkiya pakkattu ōrntu kēṭkum viricci.
viṭu - viṭai. viṭaittal = veḷippaṭuttutal.
viḷ - veḷ - veḷi - veḷicci = kātiṟ puṟappaṭum koppuḷam.
koppuḷattiṟkup puṟappāṭu eṉṉum peyaruṇmai nōkkuka.
veḷi - veṭi. veṭittal = veḷivarutal, piñcu viṭutal.
xxiii. cōmpal
cūṭṭiṉāl cōmpal uṇṭākum.
pacicōmpu maituṉaṅ kāṭcinīr vēṭkai
tecikiṉṟa tīkkuṇamō raintu
eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷ.
cuḷ - kaṇaṅku - cuṇakkam = vēlait taḷarcci, tāḻppu, taṭai, cōrvu.
cuṇaṅkutal = cōmpaṟpaṭutal.
cuṇaṅku - cuṇaṅkal = cōmpal, cōmpēṟi.
cuṇaṅku - cuṇaṅki = cōmpēṟi.
cuṇaṅki - cōṇaṅki = cōrvuṟṟavaṉ.
cōṭaṉ = cōmpēṟi. cōṭai = cōrvu.
cum = cōmpal, oṉṟuñ ceyyāmai.
cum - cummā = oṉṟuñ ceyyāmal.
cummāviruttal = vēlaiyoṉṟuñ ceyyātiruttal, ñāṉac cōmpal.
cum - cumpu - cumpaṉ = cōmpēṟi, payaṉaṟṟavaṉ.
cumpu - compu - cōmpal. cōmpu ēṟiyavaṉ, cōmpēṟi.
xxiv. uṟaippu
uṟaippu eṉṉum paṇpup peyar vitantu cuṭṭuvatu kārattaiyē. uṟaikkum poruḷaic cuḷḷeṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpar. kārattai ericcal eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itaṉāl, uṟaittalaic cuṭutalō ṭoppak koṇṭamai pulaṉām.
uṟu - uṟai - uṟaippu.
ul - el - eri - ericcal.
(kuḷ) - kaḷ - kaṭu. kaṭuttal = uṟaittal. kaṭu = kāram.
kaṭu - kāṭṭam = uṟaippu.
kaṭu - kaṭi = kāram.
kaṭi - kari. karittal = uṟaittal.
kari - kār - kārppu. kār - kāram.
cuḷ = uṟaippu. cuḷḷam = uṟaippu. cuḷḷāppu = uṟaippu.
cuḷḷakkāy = miḷakāy. cuḷḷiviṭuvāṉ = miḷaku, miḷakāy.
cuḷ - cur - cūr = kāram, miḷaku.
(nuḷ) - neḷ - neri - neriyal = neruppup pōl eriyum miḷaku.
neriyal - meriyal - miriyal = miḷaku.
nerippu - merippu.
teluṅkar miḷakāyai merappakkāy (merippukkāy) eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
muḷ - muḷaku - miḷaku. muḷakāy - miḷakāy.
muḷaku - muḷaki - miḷaki = miḷakuc campā.
xxv. kaṭum puḷippu
kaṭumpuḷippum kārampōl kāṭṭamāṉatākum. puḷippu mutirnta kaḷ.
tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal  (puṟam. 192)
aravuvekuṇ ṭaṉṉa tēṟal  (puṟam. 176)
pāppuk kaṭuppaṉṉa tōppi  (akam. 348)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
uṟu - uṟai = puḷitta mōr.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu. kaṭuttal = puḷittal. kaṭu - kāṭi = puḷippu, puḷitta kaḷ, puḷitta kañci, ūṟukāy.
kaṭu - kāṭṭam = kaṭumpuḷippu.
cuḷ - cuṭikai = paṉaṅkaḷ. cuḷ - cūḻikai = kaḷ.
cuḷ - cur - curam = kaḷ. cur - curai = cuḷ
cur - cūr = kāṭṭam.
cūrnaṟā vēntiṉāṉ  (paripā 6, 72).
kaḷ - cuṇṭu = kaḷ. cuṇṭucōṟu = kaṭcattuḷḷa cōṟu.
cuṇṭu - cuṇṭam = kaḷ. cuṇṭakaṉ = kaḷḷiṟakkuvōṉ.
cuṇṭu - cuṇṭi. cuṇṭiyuṇṭai = puḷikka vaikkuṅ kuḷikai.
cuṇṭu - cuṇṭai = kaḷ.
cuṇṭi - coṇṭi = cuṇṭiyuṇṭai.
kaṭum puḷippiṟkut tākkuvatu pōṉṟa uṟaikkum tiṟaṉuṇmai paṟṟiyē, pālaip puḷikkavaittalaip pirai kuttutal eṉṟum pirai taittal eṉṟum kūṟuvar. taittal kuttutal.
tai (tay) - tayir = pirai taitta pāl. o. nō. mai - mayir.
xxvi. ciṉattal 
ciṉam tīyaip pōṉṟatātaliṉ, eritalaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ ciṉattaiyum kuṟippaṉavāyiṉa.
ciṉameṉṉuñ cērntāraik kolli iṉameṉṉum
ēmap puṇaiyaic cuṭum.
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar kūṟutal kāṇka.
ul - ulaṟu.    ulaṟutal = ciṉattal.
uruttal = ciṉattal. uruttiram = ciṉam. uru - uruppu - uruppam = ciṉam.
ul eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta aḻalutal aṉalutal aṉṟutal erital mutaliya viṉaikaḷum ciṉattalaik kuṟikkum.
kuruttal = ciṉattal.
kul eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta kaṉalutal kaṉṟutal eṉṉum viṉaikaḷum, kuḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta kāytal eṉṉum viṉaiyum, ciṉattalaik kuṟikkum.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu. kaṭuttal = kōpittal, veṟuttal.
cuḷḷam = kōpam. cuḷḷakkam =kōpam. cuḷittal = kōpittal.
cuṇṭucol = cuṭucol.
toṭutal : toṭutal (allatu muṭṭutal) eṉpatu, uḷḷiruntu toṭutal veḷiyiruntu toṭutal eṉa iru vakaittu. karuppaikkuḷḷiruntu tāy vayiṟṟaik kuḻavi muṭṭutal uḷḷiruntu toṭutal; tāy vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu piṟanta uyirikaḷ tammai aṭuttavaṟṟait toṭuvatu veḷiyiruntu toṭutal. marattuḷḷiruntu tuḷirum nilattuḷḷiruntu muḷaiyum muṇṭi veḷivaruvatu pōla, tāy vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷiruntu kuḻaviyum muṇṭi veḷivaruvatu, ataṟkut tōṟṟam. ākavē tōṟṟamum uḷ tōṟṟam veḷit tōṟṟam eṉa iruvakaittu. muṭṭaikkuḷ kuñcum karuppaikkuḷ kuṭṭiyum tōṉṟuvatu uḷ tōṟṟam, avai piṟappatu veḷit tōṟṟam. ākavē, cilavaṟṟiṟku oru tōṟṟamum cilavaṟṟiṟku iru tōṟṟamum uḷavām.
kūṭal
poruḷkaḷ neruṅkuvatum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭuvatum kūṭalākum. ākavē, kūṭalum toṭṭuk kūṭal toṭātu kūṭal eṉa irutiṟattatu.
(1) kūṭal tuṟai
i. kūṭutal
uṟutal = kūṭutal.
ōrtal = poruntutal, kūṭutal.
kuḻu - kuḻumu.
kuḻu - keḻu. kuḻumu - keḻumu.
kummutal = kūṭutal. kum - kumpu. kumputal = kūṭutal.
kum - kumi - kuvi. kuvital = kūṭutal.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūṇṭu. kūṇṭutal = kūṭutal. kūṇṭu - kūṭu. 
kūṭu - kūṭṭu - kūṭṭal.
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷḷutal = kūṭutal.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceṟi. ceṟital = kūṭutal.
ceṉ - ceru - cēr. cērtal = kūṭutal.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḻu - toku. tokutal = kūṭutal.
toḻutal = kūṭutal, kalattal.
nul - (nura) - nira. nirattal = kalattal, kūṭutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḻi. poḻital = kūṭutal, tiraḷutal.
mul - muṉ - maṉ. muṉṉutal = poruntutal. 
maṉṉutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = kūṭutal.
muḷ - muṭṭu. muṭṭutal = kūṭutal.
ii. tokuti 
kūṭiṉa ovvoru kūṭṭamum oru tokutiyākum.
(uḷ) - iḷ - iṇ - iṇar = kottu.
ōr - ōrai = kūṭṭam, makaḷir kūṭṭam, viṇmīṉ kūṭṭam.
kul - kulai = kāyttokuti. kul - kulam = makkaḷ vakuppu, vaṇṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭam.
kul - kur - kural = payirkkatir.
kul - kuṟṟu - kuttu - kottu.
kuḷ - kuḷakam = oru muṭivu koṇṭa ceyyuḷ tokuti.
kuḻumu - kuḻumam. kuḻu - kuḻumpu. kuḻu - kuḻām.
kum - kumpu - kumpal.
kum - kumu - kumukku = kūṭṭam.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaḷam - kaḷaṉ = avai, kaḷam - kaṇam.
kaḷam - kaḻakam.
kūḷ - kūḷi = kūṭṭam. kūṭu - kūṭṭu - kūṭṭam.
koḷ - koṇṭi = tōṭṭam. koḷ - kōḷ = kulai. kōḷ - kōṭṭi = avai.
cuvaḷ - cuvaṇṭu = poruttam. cuvaḷ - cuvaṭi = ēṭṭukkaṟṟai.
cul - (cōl) - cōlai.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceru - cēr - cēri. ceḷ - ceṇ - ceṇṭu = pūkkaṟṟai. ceṇ = koṇṭai.
cotu - cotai = kuḻām, kūṭṭam.
cēr - cār - cārttu - cāttu = vaṇikak kūṭṭam.
tuḷ - (tuṇ) - tuṇar = kottu.
tuḷ - tuṭu - tuṭuppu = kottu.
tuṭu - tuṭavai - tōṭṭam.
tuval - tuvalai = kūṭṭam.
toḷ - toṇṭi = tōṭṭam. toḷ - tōṭu - tōṭṭam.
tul - (tuṟṟu) - toṟṟu - tottu = pūṅkottu.
toḻu - toḻuti. toḻu - toku - tokuti. toku - tokai.
toku - tokuppu - tōppu.
toḻu - toṟu = mantai.
nul - (nura) - nira - nirai = varicai, mantai.
pul - pol - poru - pōr = paṭappu.
pul - (piḷ) - piṇṭi = kūṭṭam.
poḷ - poḻi - poḻil = cōlai.
poḷ - poy - poytal = kūṭṭam, makaḷir kūṭṭam.
putu - putar. putu - putai - potai. potu - potumpu - potumpar = cōlai, maracceṟivu.
muku - mukai = kūṭṭam. mukai - mūkai = kūṭṭam.
mul - (mal) - maṉ - maṉṟu = avai.
maṉṟu - mantu - mantai.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭal - vaṇṭal = āyam, makaḷir kūṭṭam.
muṭṭu - muṭṭam = ūr.
iii. uṟaiyuḷ
makkaḷum maṟṟa vuyirikaḷum kūṭi vāḻum iṭam allatu poruntiyuṟaiyum iṭam uṟaiyuḷām.
uṟu - uṟai - uṟaiyuḷ.
ul - (ur) - ūr.
kul - kulam = vīṭu, kōyil. tēvakulam = kōyil.
kuḷ - kuṭi = vīṭu, ūr. kuṭiyiruttal = kuṭiyiliruttal, vacittal. kuṭi - kuṭikai - kuṭicai - kuṭi - kuṭil - kuṭilam.
kai il eṉpaṉa kuṟumaip poruḷ vikutikaḷ.
o. nō. kaṉṉi - kaṉṉikai, toṭṭi - toṭṭil.
kumpu - kumpai = cēri. kumpu - kuppu - kuppam = ciṟṟūr.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaḷam - kaḷaṉ. ērkkaḷam pōrkkaḷam avaikkaḷam eṉpavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
koḷ - koṭṭu - koṭṭam. koṭṭu - koṭṭil. 
koṭṭam - koṭṭakai. koṭṭam - koṭṭāram.
koṭṭam - kōṭṭam = toḻuvam, kōyil. araṇmaṉai. koḷ - koṇṭi = toḻuvam.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceru - cēr - cēri.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḻu - toḻuvu - toḻuvam. toḻu - toṟu.
muṉ - maṉ - maṉai. maṉ - maṉṟu - maṉṟam.
maṉṟu - mantu - mantai.
maṉ + tiram = (maṉṟiram) mantiram = maṉai. kōyil, yāṉai kutirait toḻuvam.
tiram eṉpatu oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti.
niṟaintu kūṭal
uṟutal = niṟaital.
kuḷ - kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu. keḻumutal = niṟaital.
kum(kam) - kamam.
kamam niṟaintiyalum  (tol. uri. 57).
kūṭutal = niṟaital.
cul - cōl - cāl. cālutal = niṟaital.
cāla = mika. cāṉṟōr = aṟivu niṟaintōr.
cāṉṟāṇmai = ellā naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum niṟaintu avaṟṟai āḷun taṉmai.
cālpu = cāṉṟāṇmai.

tuvaṉṟu niṟaivākum  (tol. uri. 34).
muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = niṟaital, tiraḷutal.
kālvicain tōṭik kaṭalpuka maṇṭi  (tiruvācakam, 2, 135)
maṇṭu - maṭu. maṭutal = niṟaital. maṭuttal = niṟaittal.
v. niṟaintu kūṭumiṭam
kūṭu - kūṭam = nīṇṭa aṟai allatu maṉai.
cāl - cālai = kūṭam.
māṇavar kalvi payilum paḷḷiyaip paḷḷikkūṭam kalvic cālai pāṭacālai eṉṟum, toḻilāḷar palar kūṭit toḻil ceyyumiṭattait toḻiṟkūṭam toḻiṟcālai eṉṟum, kammiyar vīṭṭil paṇi ceyyum aṟaiyaip paṭṭacālai eṉṟum, ellā illaṅkaḷilum nīṇṭa aṟaiyaik kūṭam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, niṟaital, niṟaintu kūṭutal. maṇṭu = ceṟivu, mikuti.
maṇṭu - maṇṭakam - maṇṭapam. o.nō.vāṇikam - vāṇipam.
maṇṭu - maṇṭi = poruḷkaḷ niṟainta iṭam, carakkaṟai.
maṇṭu - maṭu - maṭam.
tiṟappāṉa periya vīṭṭai maṭam pōlirukkiṟatu eṉpar.
maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, tiraḷutal. maṇṭu - maṇṭi = nīriṉ aṭiyil tiraṇṭirukkum maṇ allatu aḻukku.
maṇṭu - maṇṭal - vaṇṭal = veḷḷattiṉ aṭiyil tiraṇṭu paṭiyum maṇ.
vi. mikutal
mikap pala poruḷkaḷ kūṭiṉa kūṭṭam aḷavil mikum.
uruttal = mikutal.
uṟutal = mikutal. uravu = mikuti.
kul - kal - kaṉ - kaṉa - kaṉam = mikuti. kaṉ - kaṉai = mikuti.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu kaṭuttal = mikutal. kaṭu - kāṭu = mikuti. veḷḷakkāṭu piḷḷaikkāṭu mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka. kaḷ - kay - (kayal) - kañal. kañalutal = ceṟital, niṟaital, mikutal.
kuḷ - kūr. kūrtal = mikutal.
kuḷ - koḷḷai = mikuti.
cum - cummai = mikuti. cum - cuma - cumati = mikuti.
pul - pal - paṉmai. pal - palku - paluku. palu-paru. paruttal = mikutal. paru - peru.
muku - miku - mikuti - mīti.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu = ceṟivu, mikuti.
vii. ceytal
putitākac ceyyappaṭum poruḷ ēṟkeṉavē ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ aḷavai mikuttalāl, mikuttaṟ karuttil ceytaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṅkilattilum make eṉṉum collai L. magnus (great) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirippar. oru taṉippaṭṭavarkkō oru kūṭṭattārkkō uḷḷa poruḷ pōtātapōtu tēvaiyāṉa aḷavu atu perukkappaṭum. viḷaivup perukkam eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. payir vaḷarttalaip payir ceytal eṉṟum, poruḷīṭṭutalaip poruḷ ceytal eṉṟum kūṟuvataiyum nōkkuka.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu - karu.
kaṭuttal = mikutal. karuvi = tokuti (group). karumai = perumai.
āyutattaik kuṟikkum karuvi eṉṉum col tūya teṉ collāta lālum, ataṉōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa karaṇam eṉṉum col ceykaiyeṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalālum, ivviraṇ ṭiṟkum karu eṉpatu pakutiyāyiruttalālum, karu eṉṉumcol 

mikutip poruḷ tarutalālum, mikuttaṟ karuttu ceytaṟ karuttaip piṟappittaṟkēṟṟatātalālum, ceytal eṉap poruḷpaṭum karuttal eṉpatoru viṉai muṉṉoru kālattu vaḻaṅkip piṉṉar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
karu - karumam = ceykai. toḻil. o. nō. paru - parumam.
karu - (karum) - karumam. o.nō. uru - urum - urumam.

kam - kammiyam - kammiyaṉ = kammāḷaṉ.

kammavāru = payir vēlai ceyyum teluṅka vakuppār.
karu - karuvi = ceyyum āyutam.
karu - karaṇam = ceykai, caṭaṅku, maṇac caṭaṅku, uṭaṉpaṭikkai, āvaṇam (deed), āvaṇam eḻutuvōṉ, kaṇakkeḻutuvōṉ.
karaṇam - karaṇattāṉ = kaṇakkaṉ.
karaṇam = ceyyuṅ karuvi. akakkaraṇam puṟakkaraṇam eṉpavai uṭampiṟku akamum puṟamumuḷḷa karuvikaḷākiya uṟuppukkaḷ.
karu - kār. kār - kāraṇam. kār - kāriyam.
karuniṟattaiyum tōṉṟutalaiyuṅ kuṟikkum karu eṉṉum collum, kār eṉṟu tirital kāṇka. kārttal = karuppātal, tōṉṟutal.
aṇam iyam eṉpaṉa vikutikaḷ. o. nō. kaṭṭaṇam, ēraṇam, kaṇṇiyam, vāriyam.
karumam karuvi eṉpaṉa mutaṟkāla vaṭivaṅkaḷ. ataṉāl, avai marapākap paṇṭai nūlkaḷiṟ payiṉṟu varuvaṉa. karaṇam kāraṇam kāriyam eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyarkku muntiṉa iṭaik kāla vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇara (tol. 1088).
ākkan tāṉē kāraṇa mutaṟṟē  (tol. 504)

2. kuvital tuṟai
i.kuvital
malarnta pūviṉ itaḻkaḷ kūṭumpōtu mukai pōṟ kuvitalāl, kūṭutaṟ karuttiṟ kuvitaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
kum - kumiḻ - kumiḻi.
kum - kumu - kumutam = pakaliṟ kuviyum āmpal.
kum - kumi - kuvi. kuvital = kūmputal.
kuvi - kuvam = āmpal.
kumpu - kūmpu. kūmputal = kuvital.
kumpu - kuppu - kuppi = kuvinta ciṟukalam.
kūlaṅkaḷaiyum kāykaṟikaḷaiyum kal maṇ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum kūmpiya vaṭivil tokuttu vaittal kumittal allatu kuvittal eṉappaṭum.

kum - kummal. kum - kumpu - kuppu - kuppal. kuppu - 
kuppai = kuviyal.
kumi - kumiyal.
kuvi - kuviyal, kuvāl, kuvai.

cūmpiṉakai = kūmpi moṇṭiyāṉa kai.
ii. kaikuvital
kummiyaṭittaṟkum kumpiṭuvataṟkum irukaiyaiyum oṉṟu cērkkum pōtu avai kūmpiya vaṭivattai aṭaiyum.
kum - kummi = kai kuvittaṭikkum viḷaiyāṭṭu.
kummi - kommi.
kum - kumpu - kuppu - kuppi - koppi = kummi.

kum - kumpu - kumpiṭu. kumpiṭutal = kaikuvittut toḻutal. kumpu - kūmpu - kūppu. kaikūpputal = kaikuvittal.
tuḷ - toḻu. toḻutal = kai kalattal allatu kūṭṭutal.
iii. kuppuṟutul
pūkkaḷ malarvatu tam mukattait tiṟappatu pōlum, avai kuvivatu atai mūṭuvatu pōlum, iruttalālum; pakalil malarntu iravil kūmpum ilaikaḷum malarkaḷum, perum pālum, malarumpōtu mēṉōkkiyum kūmpum pōtu kīḻ nōkkiyum iruttalālum, māntaṉ mēṉōkkip paṭuttiruttal malarnta nilaikkoppākavum, kīḻ nōkkip paṭuttiruttal kūmpiya nilaikkoppākavum, collappaṭum.
malartal = mukam mēlātal, malarttal = mukattai mēlākkutal.
malar - mallār - mallā. mallāttal = mukam mēlātal. mallāttutal = mukattai mēlākkutal.
mallāntupaṭuttal mallākkappaṭuttal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.
kumpu - kuppu - kuppuṟu. kuppuṟutal = mukaṅkaviḻtal. kuḻantai kuppuṟṟuk koṇṭatu, mukaṅkuppuṟa viḻuntāṉ, eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakku.
kuvi - kavi. kavital = kuppuṟutal. talaikīḻātal.
kavi - kaviḻ. kaviḻtal = kuppuṟutal, talaikīḻātal.

kuṭam kūṭai mutaliyavai potuvāka vāymēṉōkkiyavāṟu vaikkappaṭum. avai talaikīḻātal kaviḻtal eṉṟum, avaṟṟait talaikīḻāka vaittal kaviḻttal eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭum.
kavital = talaikīḻāṉa kūṭaipōl vaḷaital.
kavittal = talaikīḻāṉa kūṭaiyaip piṭittal pōl kuṭai piṭittal, kūṭaiyaik kaviḻttal pōl makuṭattait talaiyi laṇital.
kavi - kavikai = kuṭai.

kavikaṇ = kuṭaṅkaiyāl maṟaikkappaṭṭa kaṇ. mukil kuṭaipōl vaḷaintu paṭutalum kavital eṉappaṭum.

iv. virumpik kākkum talaivaṉ
oru poruḷai virumpukiṟavaṉ ataip pōṟṟik kāppāṉā taliṉ, virumpaṟ karuttu kāttaṟ karuttaiyun taḻuvum.
e.ṭu. pēṇutal = virumputal, pōṟṟutal. tantai tāyp pēṇ eṉṉum auvaiyār kūṟṟai nōkkuka. oru talaivaṉukku avaṉ kīḻppaṭṭavar māṭṭaṉpu iruttal vēṇṭumātalāl, virumputal kuṟitta cila coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu talaimai kuṟitta coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
namputal = virumputal.
nampum mēvum nacaiyā kummē  (tol. uri. 31)
nampu - nampaṉ = talaivaṉ. nampu - nampi = talaivaṉ.
nayattal = virumputal. naya - nāyam - nāyaṉ = talaivaṉ. nāyam - nāyakam - nāyakaṉ = talaivaṉ.
vēṭṭal = virumputal. vēḷ = talaivaṉ. vēḷ - vēḷāṉ = talaivaṉ.
nampu naya eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum orēyaṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyē.
nāyaṉ = talaivaṉ, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ, tantai.
nāyaṉār pōṉanāḷ iṉṟeṉṟu
akattiluḷḷōrellāruṅ kūṟi  (cīvaka. 2097, urai)
eṉṉumiṭattu, nāyaṉār eṉpatu tantaiyaik kuṟittatu. nāyaṉ nāyccumār eṉṉun toṭar talaivaṉ talaiviyaraik kuṟikkum.
iṟaivaṉaṭiyāraiyum talaivarōṭoppak karutum vaḻakka muṇmaiyāl, nāyaṉ nāyaṉār eṉṉum peyarkaḷ aṭiyārai yuṅ kuṟikkum. vaḷḷuvaṉārkku nāyaṉārp paṭṭam vaḻaṅkuvatu avaratu pulamait talaimai paṟṟi yākum.
nāyaṉ - nāyar = cēra (malaiyāḷa) nāṭṭup paṭait talaivar vaḻivanta kulattār. nāyaṉ eṉpatu taṇṭanāyaṉ eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. nāyaṭu nāyuṭu eṉpaṉa teluṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
nāyaṉār - nayiṉār = camaṇarkkum civa kulattārkkum vaḻaṅki varum paṭṭap peyar. mutalāvatu itu talaimai paṟṟi avviru cārāriṉ talaivarkkē vaḻaṅkiyatu. tantaiyaik kuṟikkum nāyaṉā nayiṉā eṉpavai teluṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
nāyaṉ - nāyantai - nayintai = āṇṭāṉ, aṭimai vaittāḷuṅ kulattalaivaṉ, cila kulattār paṭṭappeyar.
nāyantai eṉpatu viḷi vaṭivil nāyantē (nāyaṉtē) eṉṟākum.
nāyaṉ - nāyakaṉ = talaivaṉ, kaṇavaṉ, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ.
nāyakam = talaimai.
nāyakaṉ - nāyakar = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāyakaṉ - nāykaṉ = vaṇikar talaivaṉ.
nāykaṉ - nāykkaṉ = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāyakaṉ - nāyakkaṉ = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāykkaṉ nāyakkaṉ eṉpaṉavum paṭait talaimai kuṟikkum. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutalāvatu kulat talaivarkku vaḻaṅkiyavai.
nāyaṉ - (nāyar) - nāyiṟu = kōḷkaḷuḷ talaimaiyāṉa katiravaṉ ataṟkuriya kiḻamai.
nāyiṟu - ñāyiṟu. nāyiṟu eṉpatē ulaka vaḻakku.
kuṟippu : nāyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tantai peyarākavum kulap paṭṭap peyarākavum vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyilillai. talaivaṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉṟa poruḷilēyē avai aṅku vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. mēlum, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyilillai. nāya nāyaka (nayaka) eṉṟa iru vaṭivaṅkaḷē aṅkuḷa.
ñāyiṟu eṉṉuṅ kiḻamaip peyar toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatu.
nāyakaṉ eṉṉuñ collukku vaṭa moḻi yakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭum vēr nī eṉpatu. atu celuttutalai allatu naṭattutalaik kuṟikkum. accollum nū eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa nī eṉpatē. nīttal = celuttutal. nīyāṉ = kalañceluttu vōṉ.

uttal = poruttutal. uttam = pōr.
cēra nāṭṭārāṉa malaiyāḷiyar pōrkkaḷattai uttāṅkaḷam eṉpar.
um - am - amar. amartal = poruntutal. amar = pōr. amaram = pōr maṟavarkku viṭappaṭṭa māṉiyam.

ix. iṇaiyātal
āṇum peṇṇumāka iruvar allatu iraṇṭu cērntu puṇarvatālum, iyaṅkutiṇai yuyirikaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa āṇum peṇṇumākak kūṭi vāḻtalālum, uḻaviṟkum vaṇṭikkum aṭikkaṭi iru kāḷaikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭuvatālum, kai kāl mutaliya cila vuṟuppukkaḷ iv viraṇṭāyiruppatā lum, āṭai mutaliya poruṭkaḷ naṉkoṭaiyil potuvāka iṇaiyāka vaḻaṅkappeṟuvatālum, iṇaiyaik kuṟittaṟkuk kalattalaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu pala coṟkaḷ piṟap pikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

nīru maṇṇuṅ kalappatu kalaṅkal. atu poruṭkalakkam. pala āṭkaḷ kūṭiyirukkum pōtu kuṟippiṭṭa oruvar yār eṉṟu teriyātu tiṇṭāṭuvatum. pala vaḻikaḷ kūṭumiṭattil cella vēṇṭiya vaḻi etuveṉṟu teriyātu mayaṅkuvatum. oṉṟaic ceyvatu atai viṭṭuviṭuvatu eṉṉum eṇṇaṅkaḷ maṉattil tōṉṟum pōtu oṉṟait tuṇiyātu ūcalāṭuvatum, maṉakkalakkamām.

mākam - nākam - nākar. nākanāṭu = viṇṇulakam.
v. kuṟṟam
uṭampiluḷḷa aḻukku perumpālum karuniṟamāyiruppa tālum, veḷḷāṭaiyiṟpaṭumkaru niṟam matiyiṉ kaṇ maṟup  pōl tōṉṟutalālum, karumai kuṟitta cila coṟkaḷ aḻukkaiyum aḻukkuppōṉṟa kuṟṟattaiyum kuṟikkum.
kari - karil = kuṟṟam. kari - karicu = kuṟṟam, pāvam.
kaḷ - kaḷaṅku - kaḷaṅkam = kuṟṟam.
kaṟu - kaṟai = kuṟṟam.
kāḻ = kācu = kuṟṟam.
mal - malam = aḻukku, pavvī.
maḷ - maru - maṟu = kuṟṟam. maccu = kuṟṟam.
mayal = aḻukku. mayal - mayalai = aḻukku.
māḻ - mācu = kuṟṟam. o.nō. kāḻ - kācu.
mai = kuṟṟam.
vi. maṟaivu
karumaiyilpaṭṭa piṟaniṟamum iruṇṭa iṭattiluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷum kaṇṇiṟkut teriyātu maṟaintiruppatāl, karumaik karuttil maṟaivuk karuttut tōṉṟum.
kaḷ - kaḷḷam = maṟaivu, tiruṭṭu, vañcaṉai. kaḷ - kaḷḷaṉ = tiruṭaṉ.
kaḷ - kaḷvu - kaḷavu = maṟaivu. tiruṭṭu.
kaḷvu - kaḷvaṉ = tiruṭaṉ.
kaḷ - (kar) - kara - karavu - karavaṭam = kaḷavu. karattal = maṟaital, maṟaittal. karavu = vañcakam, kaḷavu.
maṟuttal = illaiyeṉṟu collāl maṟaittal.
maṟu - maṟa. maṟattal = maṉattil maṟaiyap peṟutal.
maṟu - maṟai. maṟai = potumakkaṭku maṟaintirukkum ceyti kaḷaiyum uṇmaikaḷaiyum koṇṭa nūl.
narampiṉmaṟai = icai nūl.
māl - mālam = pācāṅku. māḷ - māy - māyam = maṟaivu. vañcaṉai. māy - māyai - āṉmāviṉ aṟivai maṟaippatu. o.nō. cāy - cāyai.
māyamāṉ = vañcaṉaiyuḷḷa māṉ. māyamāy maṟaintu viṭṭatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. māyavittai māyamālam mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.

(5) kāṇal tuṟai
akak kaṇṇāṟ kāṇalum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇalum eṉak kāṇal iruvakai. muṉṉatu karutal, piṉṉatu nukartal.
kāṇal
i. kaṇṭaṟital
kaṇṭaṟitalāvatu puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇal.
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟi vatellām kaṇṭaṟivē yāyiṉum, aivakai nukarccip poruḷkaḷiṉ vaṭivaiyum kāṇutaṟ ciṟappup paṟṟik kaṇṇiṉ toḻilē kāṭci eṉa vitantu kūṟappaṭum.
aimpulaṉkaḷum poruḷkaḷoṭu poruntuvataṉālēyē nukarcciyum aṟivum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(uḷ) - aḷ - aṟi - aṟivu.
kuḷ - kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = poruntutal.
kaḷ - karu - kari = kaṇṭavaṉ (cāṭci).
kaḷ - kaṇ = kāṇum poṟi. kaṇṇutal = pārttal 
(nāmatīpa nikaṇṭu).
kaṇ - kāṇ - kāṭci = pārvai, aṟivu.
kāṇ - kāṇam = mēṟpārvai.
kāṇ - kāṇi. kāṇittal = mēṟpārttal.
kaṇkāṇi - kaṇkāṇam. kaṇkāṇi = mēṟpārppavaṉ.
tul - tuṉṟu - tōṉṟu. tōṉṟutal = kaṇṇoṭu poruntu tal, terital, urukkoḷal, utittal, piṟattal.
pullutal = poruntutal.
pul - pulam - pulaṉ = poruntiyaṟiyum aṟivu.
pul = aṟivu, aṟivu nūl, ilakkaṇam.
pulam - pulamai. pulam - pulavaṉ.
pulaṉātal = aṟiyappaṭutal. pulappaṭutal = pulaṉoṭu poruntutal.
pulam - pular. pulartal = kaṇṇukkut terital, viṭital.
pular - pulari = viṭiyal.
pular - palar - palār, palāreṉṟu viṭintatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
ii. karutiyaṟital
karuti yaṟitalāvatu akak kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭaṟital.
maṉam oru poruḷoṭu allatu ceytiyoṭu poruntutalē karutalām.
uttal = poruntutal. utti = poruttam poruntumuṟai, porutta mākak koḷḷum ūkam.
uṉṉutal = poruntutal, karututal, tiyāṉittal.
uṉ - uṉṉam = tiyāṉam.
uṉṉittal = poruttamāka ūkittal.
uṟutal = poruntutal, karututal.
ōrtal = poruntutal, karututal; karutiyaṟital.
kuḷ - kaḷ (karu) - karutu - karuttu. cuḷḷutal = kūṭutal, poruntutal.
kaḷ - kaṇ. kaṇṇutal = poruntutal, karututal, matittal.
kaṇ = kaṇṇiyam = matippu.
(cuḷ) - ceḷ - ce. cettal = poruntutal, ottal. karututal. cettal = karututal.
aravu nīruṇal cettu  (kalit. 45).
veppuṭaiyāṭūuc cettaṉeṉ  (patiṟṟap. 86)
cettal = ottal.
veḷḷāṅ kurukiṉ piḷḷai cettu (aiṅ. 151).
ce - cettu - cittu. o. nō. centuram - cinturam.
cittu = karuttu, aṟivu, karutiyatai aṭaiyum tiṟam. cittu - cittaṉ.
muṉṉutal = poruntutal, karututal muṉ - muṉṉam = karuttu, kuṟippu.
muṉ - maṉ. maṉṉutal = poruntutal karututal. 
maṉ - maṉam = karutum akakkaraṇam.
maṉṉuntiṟam mantiram. tiram= tiṟam. ṟakarattiṟku muntiyatu rakaram.
maṉ + tiram = mantiram = karuttu valimai, eṇṇattiṉ tiṇṇam.
uḷḷattai allatu ācaiyai aṭakkiya muṉivaṉ, taṉ karuttu valimaiyāl tāṉ karutiyatai niṟaivēṟṟu moḻiyum, tāṉ kaṇṭupiṭitta tappāta uṇmaikaḷaic collumoḻiyum, mantiramākum.
niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa  (tol. cey. 178)
niṟaimoḻi māntar maṟaimoḻi karuttu valimaiyoṭu kūṭiyatu eṉpatai āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta eṉṉun toṭar kuṟippāy uṇarttum. karuttu valimaiyoṭu kūṭāviṭattu oru moḻiyum oru payaṉum uṟātu. mantiram vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. vāymaiyāṉa moḻi allatu vāykkum moḻi vāy moḻi. tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantiramum nammāḻvār iyaṟṟiya 
tiruvāymoḻiyum, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ iru parattaraic cavitta moḻikaḷum pōlvaṉa. mantiraṅkaḷām. mantirattaik kāṭṭum moḻiyaiyum mantiram eṉṟatu ōr āku peyar.
eṇṇiya eṇṇiyāṅ keytupa eṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 666).
ātalāl, tiṇṇiya vuḷḷattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭatē vāymoḻi eṉa aṟika. maṉattukkaṇ mācilaṉāṉa oruvaṉ, oruvarai vāḻvikkavō cāvikkavō vāy tiṟantu oṉṟaic colla vēṇṭuvatillai. uḷḷattil uḷḷiṉālum pōtum. kaṭavuḷai vēṇṭum maṉṟāṭṭu eṅṅaṉam uraiyiṉṟi uḷḷattilum nikaḻa muṭiyumō, aṅṅaṉamē mantiramum eṉka.
niṟaimoḻi mānta rāṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattiṟkup pērāciriyar uraitta urai varumāṟu:
itu mantirac ceyyuḷuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu itaṉ poruḷ: - niṟaimoḻi māntareṉpatu, colliya colliṉ poruṇmai yāṇṭuṅ kuṟaiviṉṟip payakkac collum āṟṟaluṭaiyār eṉṟavāṟu. avar āṇaiyamaṟ kiḷakkap paṭṭup puṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmaṉ maṟaittuc colluñ coṟṟoṭarellām mantira meṉappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
avai vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka. tāṉē eṉṟu pirittāṉ, ivai tamiḻ mantirameṉṟaṟkum, pāṭṭāki aṅkatameṉappaṭu vaṉavum uḷa, avai nīkkutaṟkumeṉa uṇarka. avai :
āriya taṉṟu tamiḻtī teṉavuraitta
kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉaic - cīriya
vantaṇ potiyi lakattiyaṉā rāṇaiyāṟ
centamiḻē tīrkka cuvā  - eṉavum,
muraṇil potiyiṉ mutaṟputtēḷ vāḻi
paraṇa kapilarum vāḻi - yaraṇiya
lāṉanta vēṭkaiyāṉ vēṭkōk kuyakkōṭa 
ṉāṉantañ cērka cuvā - eṉavum,
ivai teṟkaṇ vāyil tiṟavāta paṭṭimaṇṭapattār poruṭṭu nakkīrar oruvaṉ vāḻavum (oruvaṉ) cāvavum pāṭiya mantiram aṅkatap pāṭṭāyiṉa mēl pāṭṭurai nūl (391) eṉpuḻi aṅkatameṉṟōtiṉāṉ iṉṉa mantirattai. iḵtu oruvaṉai iṉṉavāṟṟāṟ perumpāṉmaiyuñ capittaṟ poruṭṭākaliṉ ap peyarttāyiṟṟu. ik karuttē paṟṟip piṟarum,
niṟaimoḻi māntar perumai nilattu
maṟaimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 2)
eṉṟāreṉka
amaiccaṉ araciyaṟ karumaṅkaḷai eṇṇum allatu cūḻun tiṟaṉum mantiram eṉappaṭum. tiṟamaiyākac cūḻntu tappātu vāykkum vaḻivakaikaḷaic collutalāl, amaiccaṉ aracaṟkuk kūṟum aṟivuraiyum mantiram eṉappaṭṭatu. mantiram kūṟupavaṉ mantiri.
mantirik kaḻaku varumporu ḷuraittal
eṉṟār ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyarum.
iii. karutum māntaṉ
māntaṉai maṟṟa vuyirikaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu maṉṉuntiṟaṉākiya pakuttaṟivē. iyaṅkum uyirikaṭkellām karutut tiṟamiruppiṉum, atu māntaṉiṭattiṟpōl maṟṟa vuyirikaḷiṭattil vaḷarcciyaṭaiyavillai.
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar (1522).
poruḷkaḷai uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai eṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār iru pakuppākap pakuttatum, aṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟ kēṟpa muṉṉarē potumakkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ viṉaikaḷaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷai vevvēṟu vikuti koṭuttuk kūṟi vantatum, avaṟṟiṉ pakuttaṟi vuṇmaiyiṉmai paṟṟiyē maṉṉuntiṟam ciṟantiruttal paṟṟiyē māntaṉ maṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
āṅkilac collārāycciyāḷarum man eṉṉum peyarukku thinking animal eṉṟē poruṭ kāraṇaṉ kāṭṭuvar (E. man, from A.S. munan, to think).
maṉ = maṉṉum (karutum) uyiriyākiya māntaṉ.
maṉpatai = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
maṉṉuyir = makkaṭ kulam.
niṉṉaḷan taṟital maṉṉuyirk karumaiyiṉ  (tirumuru. 276)
eṉṟār nakkīrar.
tammiltam makkaḷ aṟivuṭaimai mānilattu 
maṉṉuyirk kellām iṉitu  (kuṟaḷ, 68)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
maṉṉuyir eṉpataṟku maṉṉāniṉṟa vuyir eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar uraikkum urai poruntātu. maṉṉāniṟṟal = nilaipeṟutal.
maṉ - māṉ - māṉavaṉ - māntaṉ.
maṉ - manu (va.).


(6) poruttal tuṟai
pal uṟuppukkaḷaiyum pakutikaḷaiyum poruttuvatu poruttu.
i. uṟuppup poruttu
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇu. kaṇ - kaṇai. kaṇukkāl = kaṇaikkāl. kaḷ - kaḷam = uṭampaiyum talaiyaiyum poruttum kaḻuttu.
kaḷam - (kaḷattu) - kaḻuttu.
kaḷ - kaṭṭu
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷai = pāṭṭu.
pul - pol - poru - porun - poruntu - poruttu.
puḷ - (pūḷ) - pūṇ - pūṭṭu. puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇar - puṇarppu.
puḷ - poḷ - poṭṭu = neṟṟiyelumpup poruttu.
muḷ - muḷi = muṭṭu, kaṇu.
muḷi - muḻi - moḻi = kaimuṭṭu, karumpukkaṇu.
muḻi + kai = muḻaṅkai. muḻi + kāl = muḻaṅkāl.
muḻi - muḻam = muḻaṅkaiyaḷavu.
muḷ - muṭṭu = kai kāṟ poruttu.
muṭṭu - muṭṭi = muḻaṅkāṟ poruttu, kaikkaṇu, maṭakkiya muṭṭik kaiyāṟ ceyyum maṟpōr.
muṭṭu - muṭu - maṭu - maṭai = poruttu, koḷuttu.
muṭṭu - mūṭṭu = poruttu.
muṇṭu = marakkaṇu, uṭaṟcantu. muṇṭu - muṇṭam = kaṇukkāṟ poruttu.
kaṭṭutal
(uḷ) - iḷ - iḻai - iṇai. (iḷ) - iya - iyai - icai, icaittal = kaṭṭutal.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭu - kaṭṭil.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭaṇam = pāṭai.
puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇai - piṇai.
puṇ - puṇar. puṇarttal = kaṭṭutal.
puṇ - puṇi - piṇi. piṇittal = kaṭṭutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poṭṭu - poṭṭaṇam = kaṭṭu.
poṭṭaṇam - poṭṭalam.
muḷ - muṭṭu - mūṭṭu - muṭṭai.
ii. muṭaital
ul - al. allutal = kūṇṭu muṭaital.
kuḷ - (kuy) - kuyil. kuyilutal = neytal, piṉṉutal.
nuḷ - neḷ - ney - neyavu - necavu.
pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu.
pul - (puṉ) - piṉ.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭai. muṭaital = piṉṉutal.
iii. taittal
uttu - attu. attutal = taittal.
oṭṭutal = taittal.
kuttutal = taittal.
tuṉṉutal = taittal. tuṉ - tuṉṉam = taiyal. tuṉṉakāraṉ - taiyaṟkāraṉ.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - (tay) - tai. taittal = tuṇi palakai mutaliyavaṟṟai icaittal.
tai - (taiccu) - taccu - taccaṉ.
vaṇṭik kuṭattil āraikaḷaip poruttalait taittal eṉpar.
poḷ - poṭṭu - pottu.
pottutal = mūṭṭutal. pollam pottutal eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
muḷ - mūḷ - mūṭṭu - mūṭṭai - mūṭai = mūṭṭappaṭṭa kōṇippai, mūṭṭutal = taittal.


(7) mūṭal tuṟai
mūṭalāvatu. koḷkalaṅkaḷiṉ vāyaiyum pōrppavaṟṟiṉ ōraṅkaḷaiyum porutti maṟaittal.
i. mūṭutal
um - umi - umital = vāy mūṭi eccil umiḻtal. umi - umiḻ.
cuḷ - (cūr) - cārttu - cāttu. cāttutal = mūṭutal.
tum - tumi. tumital = umital. tūrttal = kuḻiyaiyum tuḷaiyaiyum mūṭutal.
potu - pottu. pottutal = mūṭutal, maṟaittal. potu - potukku. potukkutal = maṟaittal.
muḷa - mūḻ. mūḻttal = mūṭutal. mūḻ - mūḻal = mūṭi.
mūḻ - mūṭu - mūṭi. mūṭu - mūṭam - mōṭam = vāṉam mūṭiya mantāram, mantāram pōṉṟa maṭamai.
mūṭu = aṟivili. mūṭam - mūṭaṉ.
mūḻ - mūy. mūytal = mūṭutal, vāymūṭi eccil umiḻtal.
mūy - moy. moyttal = mūṭutal.
ii. mūṭiya poruṭkaḷ
um - umi = nellai mūṭiyuḷḷa toli.
um - ūm = vāypēcāta ūmai.
ūm - ūmai. ūm - ūmaṉ.
vāy pēcātavaṉai vāyai um eṉṟu vaittuk koṇṭiruk kiṟāṉ eṉpar.
kum - kommai = kampu tiṉai kēḻvaraku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ umi.
cum - coṅku = cōḷa umi.
mūḻ - mūku - mūkaṉ = ūmaiyaṉ. mūku - mūṅku = ūmai.
mūṅku - mūṅkai = ūmai.
mūku - mūkā. mūkāttal = ūmaiyāyiruttal.
iii. pōrttal
uṟu - uṟai.
kuḷ - kuṭi = caṭṭai.
kumpu - kuppu - kuppā = tūrippai.
kuppu - kuppāyam = meyppai, caṭṭai. kuppāyam - kuppācam.
cul - (cōl) - cāl - cālvai = pōrvai.
poru - pōr - pōrvai.
poru - potu - poti. potital = pōrttal.
poti - potir. potirtal = pōrttal.


(8) paṟṟal tuṟai
paṟṟalāvatu, kai kāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷaip piṟa poruḷ kaḷōṭu poruntip piṭittal. maṉattil oṉṟaik koḷḷutalum paṟṟutalōṭokkum.
i. piṭittal
viralkaḷaip porutti oru poruḷaip paṟṟutalē piṭittal. piṟavuṟuppukkaḷāl oṉṟaip paṟṟutalum oppumaipaṟṟip piṭittal eṉappaṭum.
kuḷ - koḷ. koḷḷutal = paṟṟutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ēṟukōḷ tīkkōḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
koḷkompu - koḻukompu = paṟṟukkōṭu.
kutu - katu - katuvu. katuvutal = paṟṟutal. katu - kātu = oliyaip paṟṟum cevi.
pul - (puṟṟu) - paṟṟu.
puḷ - (piḷ) - piṇṭi - piṇṭam. piṇṭam = tiraṭci. piṇṭittal kaikkuḷ cōṟṟai allatu māvait tiraṭṭutal, cērttal, piṭittal. piṇṭi - piṭi. o. nō. kaṇṭi - kaṭi. taṇṭi - taṭi.
piḷḷaiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaiyum, poriviḷaṅkāy piṭittal. koḻukkaṭṭai piṭittal, mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaiyum nōkkuka.
piṭittal = kaikkuḷ tiraṭṭutal, kaikkuḷ māttiraḷaiyait tiraṭṭutal pōl oṉṟaip paṟṟutal, kaiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭatu pōl aḷavaik kuṟaittal.
piṭi = piṭikkappaṭum kāmpu allatu aṭip pakuti, kaikkuḷ piṭikkum aḷavu.
caṭṭai mutukiṟ piṭikkiṟatu, cōṟu pāṉaiyaṭiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭatu. vīṭṭil tīppiṭittu viṭṭatu. campaḷattil ōr urupāvaip piṭittu viṭṭārkaḷ. eṉakkup puḻuṅkalaricitāṉ piṭikkum, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻakkukkaḷellām uṇmaiyākavum aṇi vakaiyilum paṟṟaṟ poruḷaiyē kuṟittal kāṇka.
pol - (poṟṟu) - poṟṟi - poṟi = piṭikkuṅ karuvi, cūḻcciyam, iyantiram. elippoṟi pulippoṟi mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka. aimpulavuṟuppuk kaḷum poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉmaiyaip piṭittaliṉ, poṟiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
ii. koḷḷutal
oruvar oṉṟaip peṟṟapiṉ ataik kaiyiṟ koḷḷutalāl, koḷḷutal eṉṉuñ col, vilaiyiṉṟip peṟutalaiyum vilaikku vāṅkutalaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vilaiyiṉṟip peṟutal, vantataip peṟutalum valintu peṟutalum eṉa iru vakaittu.
koḷḷutal = peṟutal, vāṅkutal, vilaikku vāṅkutal.
koḷ - koḷḷai = koḷḷum vilai.
koṇṭāṉ = peṟṟavaṉ. vilaikku vāṅkiṉavaṉ, oruttiyai maṉaiviyākak koṇṭavaṉ.
koḷ - koṇku - koṇkaṉ = kaṇavaṉ.
koḷ - koḷvu - koḷvaṉai.
koḷ - koḷnaṉ - koḻunaṉ = kaṇavaṉ.
koḻunaṉ - koḻuntaṉ = koḻunaṉoṭu kūṭap piṟantavaṉ. koḻunti (pe. pā).
koḷ - koḷḷai = peruvāriyāka valintu koḷvatu (cūṟai), peruvāriyāka uyirkaḷaik koḷḷum nōy.
koḷ - koṇṭi = koḷḷai.
koḷ - kōḷ = koḷḷai. ākōḷ niraikōḷ mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷaik kāṇka.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōṭal. cūṟaikōṭal = koḷḷaiyaṭittal.
koḷḷutal = maṉattiṟkoḷḷutal. karututal, namputal.
koḷ - kōḷ = karuttu. koḷ - koḷkai = karuttu, nampikkai, kōṭpāṭu = kuṟikkōḷ, neṟimuṟai.

koḷuttutalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟaik koḷḷa vaittal; atāvatu paṟṟavaittal.

tīkkōḷ kōḷ mūṭṭutal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.

muḷ - mūḷ - mūṭṭu. tīppaṟṟavaittal tī mūṭṭutal eṉpaṉa vaḻakku. mūṭṭutal = poruttutal.
mūṭṭu - mūṭṭam.

māṭṭu = māṭṭal = talaimuṭiyil māṭṭum aṇi.

iv. kollutal
arimā puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ ōr uyiraik kollaṟkē paṟṟutalāṉum, kollum vilaṅkukaḷum makkaḷum ōr uyiriyaip paṟṟutaluṅ kollutalōṭokkumātalāṉum, paṟṟutaṟ karuttil kollutaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṅkilattilum paṟṟutalaik kuṟikkum seize eṉṉum col. kollutalaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
koḷ - kōḷ = kolai. koḷḷutal = paṟṟutal, kollutal.
kōḷari = kolluñciṅkam.
pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ mutaliya toṭarkaḷ, puliyiṉālum pēyiṉālum kollappaṭṭataik kuṟittal kāṇka.
koḷ - kol - kolai.
kol - kollaṉ = marattaik kolpavaṉ, taccaṉ, kammiyaṉ, iruppukkollaṉ.

oruvaṉ ceyalai oppa iṉṉoruvaṉ ceytalē naṭippām.

naḷiya eṉpatu or uvamavurupu.
nāṭa naḷiya naṭuṅka nanta (tol. 1232).
naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivum ākum  (tol. uri. 25).
naḷi - naḷiṉam = pakaṭi, kōmāḷik kūttu.
naṭittal = pācāṅku ceytal, kōlaṅkoḷḷutal, kūttāṭutal.

vaḷinaṭaṉ melliṇarppūṅkoṭi mēvara nuṭaṅka  (paripā. 22, 43).
naṭam - (naṭal) - naṭalai = pācāṅku, poymmai, vañcaṉai.
o. nō. paṭam paṭal - paṭalai,
naṭal - naṭalam = pācāṅku, ikaḻcci. kūttu.
naṭalamaṭittal - pācāṅku ceytal.
naṭalam - naṭaṉam = pācāṅku, kūttu.
iṉi, naṭi + aṉam = naṭaṉam - naṭalam eṉṟumām.
aṉam oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti. e.ṭu. viḷampu - viḷampaṉam, kaṇṭi - kaṇṭaṉam.
naṭam - naṭṭam - naṭṭuvaṉ = kūttāciriyaṉ.
naṭṭuvaṉ - naṭṭuvam = naṭṭuvaṉ toḻil.
naṭṭuvam - naṭṭu.
naṭṭuvattiṟku mattaḷam ciṟanta tuṇaiyāvatu. ātalāl naṭṭuvaṉōṭu eṉṟum mattaḷakkāraṉ kūṭiyēyiruppāṉ. ataṉāl, naṭṭumuṭṭu, naṭṭuvaṉum muṭṭuvaṉum, eṉṉum iṇaimoḻikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. naṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum collai nōkki muṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum collum muṭṭu eṉṉum collai nōkki naṭṭu eṉṉum collum etukaipaṟṟiyamaintaṉa.
naṭṭu - naṭṭaṇam. naṭṭaṇam. naṭṭu - naṭṭaṇai = naṭippu, kūttu, kōmāḷikkūttu.
naṭi + akam = nāṭakam (mutaṉilai tirintu vikuti peṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar).
āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē, tamiḻar icai nāṭakak kalaikaḷil ciṟantiruntatiṉāl, avar avaṟṟai iyalōṭu cērttut tamiḻai muttamiḻāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. talaikkaḻakat tamiḻ muttamiḻākavē iruntatu.
ellā nūṟkum potuvāṉa col vaḻakkai ulaka vaḻakku ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eṉṟum, poruḷ vaḻakkai ulakiyal vaḻakku nāṭaka vaḻakku eṉṟum, ivviruvakaiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar. ulakiyal vaḻakku uṇmaiyāṉatu; nāṭaka vaḻakku puṉainturai yāṉatu. ivviraṇṭum kalanta akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟittu ataṉpaṭiyē toṉṟutoṭṭup pāṭi vantaṉar muṉṉait tamiḻar.
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam
kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyiru pāṅkiṉum
uriya tākum eṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṟu cārpu nūlārāṉa tolkāppiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. (akattiṇaiyiyal, 53).
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa naṭippup paṟṟiya coṟkaṭkellām vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamākak kuṟikkappaṭuvatu nrutta eṉṉum vaṭivoṭu naṭṭa (va.) eṉṉum vaṭiviṉ ovvāmaiyai eṉpatākum.
viii. aḷavu
ōr aḷaviṉāl orumuṟai aḷakkappaṭṭa poruḷ aḷanta karuviyōṭotta aḷaviṉatāyiruppatāl, oppumaik karuttil aḷavuk karuttup piṟantatu. eṇṇal eṭuttal mukattal nīṭṭal eṉṉum nālvakaiyaḷavuḷ muṉṉatoḻinta ēṉai mūṉṟilum, iṉṉatutāṉ aḷavu karuvi eṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. makkaḷel lārkkum potuvāṉa aḷavu karuvikaḷ eṅkaṇum peru vaḻak kāka vaḻaṅkivariṉum, eṉṟum evarum tam vacatikkēṟpa etaiyum aḷavu karuviyāka vaittuk koḷḷalām. parumaṉilō neṭumaiyilō, oru poruḷukku otta aḷavu iṉṉoru poruḷiṟ koḷḷuvatē aḷattal eṉka. vacatipaṟṟic cila aḷavu karuvikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruppiṉum, uṇmaiyil aḷakkum poruḷukkum aḷakkappaṭum poruḷukkum vēṟupāṭillai. aḷakkappaṭum poruḷaik koṇṭē aḷakkavuñ ceyyalām. tuṇiyāl tuṇiyai aḷappataiyum kāyāl kāyai niṟuppataiyuṅ kāṇka.
ul - al - ala - (alavu) - alaku = aḷavu.
allutal = poruntutal, poruttutal (muṭaital).
ul - (uḷ) - aḷ - aḷa - aḷavu - aḷavai.
aḷḷutal = ceṟital, poruntutal.
puḷ - (paḷ) - paṭu - paṭi. paṭutal = toṭutal, poruntutal, ottal. paṭutal = ottal.
malaipaṭavarintu  (cīvaka. 56)
paṭiyoruva rillāp paṭiyār pōlum  (tēvar. 44. 7)
paṭi = oppu.
paṭi = otta aḷavu, aḷavu. mukattalaḷavu karuvi (nāḻi), eṭutta laḷavu karuvi (paṭikkal), taram, vakai, nāḷ toṟum nāḻiyāl aḷantu koṭukkappaṭum kūlam, nāṭcelavukkācu.
varumpaṭi = varumaḷavu.
paṭippaṭiyāka = aḷavaḷavāka, mellamella.
paṭittaram = nāṭoṟum kōyiṟkaḷantu koṭukkappeṟum oḻuṅku.
paṭimuṟai = mēṉmēlaḷavu.
paṭikkaṭṭu = mēṉmēlaḷavāṉa kaṭṭu.
eṉakku oruvakaiyāy varukiṟatu eṉpatai eṉakku orupaṭiyāy varukiṟatu eṉpar.
nāḻiyaiyum niṟaikallaiyuṅ kuṟikkum paṭi eṉṉum collum, mūlattiṉ oppaik kuṟikkum paṭi eṉṉum collum, oṉṟē. oppumaik karuttaiyuṇarttum paṭi eṉṉum col, pōṉ maiyaik kuṟittu uruvam paṟṟiya coṟkaḷaiyum, aḷavaik kuṟittu aḷavu karuvi paṟṟiya coṟkaḷaiyum piṟappittateṉka.
muṉ - maṉ. maṉṉutal = poruntutal.
maṉ - māṉ. māṉutal = ottal.
māṉ - māṉam = oppu aḷavu, paṭi, (nāḻi, nāḻiyai māṉam eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku).
māṉavaṭṭil = aḷavu vaṭṭil. eṇmāṉam = eṇṇaḷavu.
varumāṉam = varumaḷavu. varumpaṭi.
maṉ - māṉ - mā. o. nō. paṇ - pāṇ - pā.
mā eṉṉum col aḷa eṉṉum poruḷil oru kālattu vaḻaṅkiya ēval viṉai.
mā + tiram = māttiram = aḷavu. mā + tirai = māttirai = aḷavu. tiram tirai eṉpaṉa toḻiṟpeyar vikutikaḷ.
tiram - tirai. o. nō. aṉam - aṉai. (vañcaṉam, vañcaṉai).
‘avaṉ eṉakku emmāttiram? eṉṉum toṭaril, māttiram eṉṉum col aḷavaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
‘evvaḷavu?’ eṉpatai vaṭārkkāṭṭār ‘emmāttam? (emmāttiram?) eṉpar.
māṉam eṉṉuñ col. aḷavu eṉṉum poruḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu oru toḻiṟpeyar vikutiyākavum varum.
e.ṭu. kaṭṭumāṉam = kaṭṭum aḷavu, kaṭṭaṭam.
paṭimāṉam = paṭiyum aḷavu. paṭivu.
aṭaimāṉam cerimāṉam cērmāṉam tīrmāṉam mutaliya toḻiṟpeyarkaḷil, māṉam eṉpatu vikutiyaḷavāka niṉṟatu.
māṉ - māṉi. o. nō. tīrmāṉi - tīrmāṉam.
tīrtal = muṭital. tīrmāṉam = muṭivu. tīrmāṉittal = muṭivu ceytal.

ix. kaṇakku
aḷaviṭutal = kaṇakkiṭutal.
kuḷ - kuṇ - kuṇi. kuṇittal = aḷattal, aḷaviṭutal.

kuṇi - kaṇi. kaṇittal = aḷaviṭutal, kaṇakkiṭutal.
kaṇi = kaṇippavaṉ. kaṇi - kaṇiyaṉ.

kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇa - kaṇaku - kaṇakku - kaṇakkaṉ.
kaṇaku - kaṇakaṉ = kaṇakkaṉ.
kaṇakku = aḷavu, eṇ, tokai, kūṭṭal kaḻittal perukkal vakuttal mutaliya aḷavīṭu.
ataṟkoru kaṇakkillai, kaṇakku vaḻakkaṟṟuk kiṭakkiṟatu eṉṉun toṭarkaḷil, kaṇakku eṉṉuñ col aḷavaik kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkāyaṉ, ūrkkaṇakkaṉ kaṇakkappiḷḷai tirumukakkaṇakku mutaliya patavip peyarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki varupavai. kaṇakkaṉ eṉṉum kuṭippeyaraik koṇṭavar tūya tamiḻ marapiṉar. paḻantamiḻar immik kaṇakkum kīḻmuntiri vāypāṭum payiṉṟavar.
	(10) uṟaḻtal tuṟai

ii. tiraṇṭa aṭi
marañceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ aṭi, avaṟṟiṉ maṟṟap pakutikaḷai viṭap paruttiruppatāl, parumaik karuttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa pala coṟkaḷ aṭiyaik kuṟikkat tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇṭu. aṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, muṭṭutal, poruntutal (kūṭutal), tiraḷutal, paruttal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi - aṭi = paruttatu, parutta aṭi ō.nō. taṇṭu - taṇṭi - taṭi.
aṭi = marattiṉ aṭippakuti, mūlam, āti, paḻaimai marattaṭi pōṉṟa pātam, kāl, ceyyuḷaṭi, aṭiyaḷavu (foot) oṉṟaṉ aṭippākam.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi = uṭampiṉ aṭittuḷai (anus), aṇṭi taḷḷutal = aṭittuḷaip pakuti veḷi varutal (Prolapsus ani).
aṇṭi taḷḷutalai aṇṭu taḷḷutal eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. 
aṇṭimā = paḻattiṉ aṭiyiṟ koṭṭaiyuḷḷa mara muntiri.
aṇṭimāmpaḻam, aṇṭimāṅkoṭṭai aṇṭik koṭṭai eṉpaṉa teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
aṇṭutal eṉṉuñ coṟku mēṟkuṟitta poruḷkaḷuḷḷamai kīḻvarun toṭarkaḷālum mēṟkōḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(1) kiṭṭutal, neruṅkutal. (itu veḷippaṭai).
(2) muṭṭutal. aṇṭai koṭuttal = muṭṭuk koṭuttal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭai - aṭai.
paṭṭaṭai = taṭṭum aṇaikal, aṭaikal.
(3) poruntutal, ottal, takutal, ēṟṟal.
ākāra māmuvamaik kaṇṭātu  (ñāṉavā. mumuṭcu. 27)
aṇṭip piḻaittal = oruvaṉaip paṟṟukkōṭākak koḷḷutal.

ā oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti.

taḷ - taṭṭu = cōḷam karumpu mutaliya payirkaḷiṉ aṭi.

iv. pūppaṭaital
nilaittiṇaiyil. tiraṇṭa allatu parutta mutalum ciṉaiyum mutircciyaṭaiyum. ataṉāl, tiraṭcipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ cila pūppaṭaitalai uṇarttum.
uruttal = mutirtal.
kum - kumar = tiraṇṭavaḷ, kaṉṉi, kaṉṉimai, iḷamai, aḻiviṉmai (eṉṟu miḷamai).
mūppu cākkāṭṭiṟ kētuvātalāl, iḷamai aḻiyāmaiyaik kuṟittatu.
kummal = kūṭutal, kuvital, tiraḷutal kum - kom - kommai = tiraṭci.
pūppaṭaintavaḷait tiraṇṭavaḷ eṉpatu koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku. o. nō.  E. virgin, pom L. virgo, to swell.
kumar - kumari = kaṉṉi kaṉṉiyāṉa kāḷi (turkkai). kaṉṉimai, aḻiviṉmai.
kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ. iḷaiñaṉ, murukaṉ. cēyōṉaip paṇṭaittamiḻar iḷaiñaṉākavē karutiyiruntatāl, avaṉ kumaraṉ eṉṟum murukaṉ eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ. (muruku = iḷaimai. murukaṉ = iḷaiñaṉ).
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷaik kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi makaṉaiyum makaḷaiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiyatu piṟkālattu āriya vaḻakku. tamiḻil iḷaiñaṉ iḷaiñai eṉṟē avai poruḷ tarum.
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum teyvaṅkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻarāl vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu varupavai.
kul - kal - kaṉ. kaṉṉutal = tiraḷutal, paḻuttal. arattaṅ kaṭṭutalai irattaṅ kaṉṉutal eṉpar.
kaṉ - kaṉṉi = tiraṇṭavaḷ, paḻutta iḷaiñai, kumarinilai, iḷamai, maṇamākāmai.
kaṉṉi - kaṉṉikai = iḷaṅkaṉṉi, kai oru kuṟumaip poruḷ vikuti -. o - nō. kuṭi - kuṭikai - kuṭicai.
v. mottam (muḻumai)
mottam eṉpatu oru vakaip poruḷkaḷ allatu palavakaip poruḷkaḷ ellām cērnta muḻuttiraṭci.
puḷ - (piḷ) - (piṇṭu) - piṇṭam = tokuti, muḻumai, uṭampu.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻutu = muḻumai. muḻutu - muḻuvatu.
muḻu - muḻuval - muḻuvaṉ.
muḻu - (mutu) - mutal = uṭampu.
mul - muṟṟu = muḻutu.
mutu - motu - mottu - mottam.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷḷitu = muḻumai. vaḷḷitu - vaḷḷicu.
vi. parumai
tiraṇṭa poruḷ paruttirukkum.
(uru) - iru - irumai = parumai.
iru - iṟu - iṟumpu = mikapperiyatu. viyakkat takkatu.
uṟu = periya. uṟumai = parumai.
kuru = parumai. kuru - kurai = parumai. kuru - karu - karumai = parumai.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu - kaṭā = parumaiyāṉatu, parumaiyāṉa āṇ vilaṅku. kaṭānārattai = perunārattai.
kaḷ - (kay) - kayam = parumai.
kaḷ - (coḷ) - coṇṭu = taṭitta utaṭu.
tuḷ - tūṇ - tūṇi. tūṇittal = paruttal.
tuḷ - (tuṭam) - taṭam = parumai.
taṭa - taṭā - taṭavu. taṭā = perumpāṉai.
tuḷ - toḷ - toṭṭa = periya.
(tum) - tim - timmaṉ = paruttavaṉ, parutta āṇ kuraṅku.
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naḷi = paruvai.
taṭavuṅ kayavum naḷiyum perumai  (tol. uri. 22).
pul - pal - palā = parum paḻamaram. palā - palavu.
pal - paṉ - paṉai = paruṅkoṭṭai maram. allatu pul vakaiyil tiṉai eṉpataṉoṭu etukaiyākavuḷḷa peyaraik koṇṭa perumaram.
tiṉaiyaḷavu pōtāc ciṟupuṉṉīr nīṇṭa
paṉaiyaḷavu kāṭṭum paṭittāl
eṉṟu kapilarum.
tiṉaittuṇai naṉṟi ceyiṉum paṉaittuṇaiyāk
koḷvar payaṉṟeri vār
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvarum (kuṟaḷ. 104). kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
pal - paru - parumam - parumaṉ. paru - parumai.
paru - peru - peruku - perukku - perukkam. perukku - perukkal.
puḷ - (paḷ) - paṭu - pāṭu. paṭupāvi = perumpāvi.
putu - (pūtu) - pūtam = periyatu. perumpēy.
(irumpūtu) - iṟumpūtu = mikapperiyatu, viyappāṉatu.
putu - potu - pottu = pottai = perumiḷakāy.
pottu - pōttu = vilaṅkiṉ āṇ.
pottu - pontu - pontaṉ = taṭittavaṉ.
pontu - ponti. pontittal = paruttal.
ponti - pōnti = vīkkam. pōntikkāl = yāṉaik kāl.
pontu - pōntu = paṇai. pōntu - pōntai = paṉai.
muḷ - maḷ - māḷikai = perumaṉai. māḷikai - maḷikai.
muru - mokku - mokkai - periyatu.
mokkai - makkai. makkaic cōḷam = peruñ cōḷam. makkaiyaṉ = mantaṉ.
mokku - moṅku - moṅkāṉ = periyatu, peruntavaḷai.
mutu - motu - mottu - montu - montaṉ = peruvāḻai. mottaṉ - mantaṉ.
montu - mantu - mantam = taṭittaṉmai, kūriṉmai, aṟiviṉmai, cuṟucuṟup piṉmai, ceriyāmai, oḷiyiṉmai.
mantam - mantāram.
vii. perumai (ciṟappu)
matippiṟkum pukaḻiṟkum ētuvāṉa aṟivāṟṟalatikāra celvaṅ kaḷiṉ parumaiyē perumai.
uravōṉ = periyōṉ.
kuru - perumai, periyōṉ. kuru - kuravu - kuravaṉ = periyōṉ. aracaṉ āciriyaṉ tāy tantai aṇṇaṉ ākiya aivarum aiṅkuravar eṉṟum, tāy tantaiyar iru mutukuravar eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭutalāl; kuru eṉṉum collukkup periyōṉ eṉpatē mūlapporuḷākum. kuravar eṉṉuñcol peṟṟōrai vitantu kuṟittal pōla, kuru eṉṉum col āciriyaṉai vitantu kuṟikkiṉṟa teṉka.
kuru - kurai - perumai.
kul - kal - kaṉ - kaṉa - kaṉam = perumai.
cuḷ - (cūr) - cīr - ciṟa - ciṟappu.
pul - pal - paru - peru - perumai.
muḷ - maḷ - maṇ - māṇ - māṇpu. maṇ = māṭcimai. 
māṇ - māṭci. muku - makam = perumai.
makam - makattu - makantu - māntu - māntaṉ = paṭaippiṟ periyavaṉ.
muṉ - maṉ - māṉ - māṉam = perumai.
kuṟippu : makaṉ eṉṉum peyar makam eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirinta tākavuṅ koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, piḷḷai eṉṉum iḷamaip peyar āṇpiḷḷai peṇpiḷḷai eṉap periyōrkkum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, makaṉ makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ piḷḷaiyarkkum periyōrkkum potuvāyirutta lāṉum, iḷamaipaṟṟiya makaṉ eṉṉum peyarē periyōṉai yuṅ kuṟittatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
aiyaṉ : nerukkam ceṟivu toṭutal poruntal oṉṟal tiraṭci parumai perumai eṉpaṉa, muṟaiyē oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟeḻunta toṭarccik karuttukkaḷ.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇa. aṇṇutal = neruṅkutal.
aḷḷal = nerukkam.
aḷ = ceṟivu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital.
aḷ - aḷa - aḷavu - aḷāvu. aḷavutal = toṭutal, poruntal, kalattal.
aḷ = paṟṟirumpu.

aḷḷutal = cērttal, poruttal, pūṭṭutal.
aḷḷukkaṭṭutal = irumput takaṭṭāl iṟukkutal. 
aḷ = pūṭṭu, vaṇṭivillait tāṅkuṅ kaṭṭai.
aḷ = vaṉmai. vaṉmaik kuṇam tiraṭci parumai perumai tiṇmai mutaliyavaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭuvatu.
aḷ - (ay) - ai = perumai. periyōṉ. talaivaṉ tantai, aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, kaṇavaṉ.
ai - aiyaṉ (ai + aṉ) = periyōṉ, mūttōṉ. uyarntōṉ, talaivaṉ, tantai, aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, muṉivaṉ, (antaṇaṉ), pārppāṉ (kōyiṟkāriyam pārppavaṉ). tēvaṉ, cāttaṉ.
aiyaṉ - aiyai (peṇpāl) = periyōḷ, talaivi. āciriyai, āciriyaṉ maṉaivi, tuṟaviṉi (tavappeṇ). kāḷi, (turkkai), malaimakaḷ (pārvati).
ai allatu aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar, mutalāvatu mūppu kāppu aṟivu tavam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, periyōṉ allatu talaivaṉ eṉṟa poruḷaiyē kuṟittatu. iṉṟum periyōraiyellām aiyā eṉṟē aḻaittal kāṇka.
eṉṉai muṉ nillaṉmiṉ  (eṉṟa kuṟaḷil. 771)
ai talaivaṉaik kuṟittatu.
aiṅkuravar eṉṉum aivakaippaṭṭa periyōruḷ, mutalvaṉ tantai. piṟappai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa āriyakkula muṟaippaṭi, tamiḻaruḷ ēṟṟiḻivupeṟṟa oru cār mēlōrum orucār kīḻōrum, tantaiyai muṟaiyē aiyā eṉṟum aiyaṉ eṉṟum, toṉṟutoṭṭu (āriyar teṉṉāṭu varukaikku muṉpiruntum, tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟiyatiliruntum), aḻaittu varukiṉṟaṉar. aiyā eṉpatu aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi vaṭivam. aṉṉīṟṟup peyar ulaka vaḻakkil uyarvu kuṟiyāmai paṟṟi, aiyā eṉṉum viḷivaṭivamē aiyaṉ eṉṉum eḻuvāy vaṭivattiṟkup patilākavum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
tantaiyai nikarttavaḷ tāy. aiyai eṉṉum peṇ pāṟpeyar. potuvākap periyōḷaik kuṟikkum pōtu tiriyātum, tāyaik kuṟikkumpōtu āy eṉṟu tirittum, varum. āy - āycci (iraṭṭaip peṇpāl), āyaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar iṭaiyaṉaiyē kuṟittalaiyum. āycci eṉṉum peṇpāṟ peyar iṭaicciyaik kuṟittalōṭu tāy pāṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkutalai yum nōkkuka.

aṉṉaiyun tantaiyum muṉṉaṟi teyvamātalālum, tirumaṇa mākumvarai makkaḷ peṟṟōrōṭēyē uṟaitalālum, ētēṉumoru tuṉpaṅkaṇṭu araṟṟum pōtum or iṟumpūtu kaṇṭu viyakkumpōtum; ciṟārum iḷaiñarum peṟṟōrai viḷittal iyalpu. itaṉāl, peṟṟōraik kuṟikkum coṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟu, irakkak kuṟippiṭaic coṟkaḷum viyappuk kuṟippiṭaic coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
peṟṟōr	irakkakkuṟippiṭaic                        viyappukkuṟipiṭaic
   peyar 	col 		col
appaṉ 	appa, appā,  		appa, appā,
		appappa, appappā  	appappa,
					appappā	
accaṉ 	accō 		accō
ammai 	ammā, ammavō 	amma, ammā
		ammakō, 		ammamma
aṉṉai 	aṉṉō 		aṉṉō
iṅṅaṉamē, ai aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟum, aiya, aiyavō - aiyakō, aiyē, aiyō, aiyaiyō mutaliya irakkak kuṟippiṭaic coṟkaḷum, ai, aiya, aiyō mutaliya viyappuk kuṟippiṭaic coṟkaḷum; piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
cuṭṭolik kālattiṟku muntiya kuṟippolik kālattil tōṉṟiya viyappuṇarvolikaḷuḷ oṉṟu āy eṉa neṭiṉ mutalākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. atu piṉṉar āy - ay (ai) eṉak kuṟukiyirukkalām. avvuṇar volik kuṟukkam vēṟu; aḷ eṉṉum aṭip piṟantu tantaiyaik kuṟikkum colliṉiṉṟu tirintu iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum ai (ay) eṉṉum viyappiṭaiccol vēṟu.
ai viyappākum  (tol. 868)
tantaikku aṭuttavaṉ tamaiyaṉ. tam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarē, tamaiyaṉ eṉpatu.
muṉṉiṉṟu moyyavintā reṉṉaiyar  (pu. ve. 8, 23)
eṉpatil, aiyar eṉpatu tamaiyaṉmāraik kuṟittatu.
uṟavumuṟaiyallāta periyōruḷ, tantaikku neruṅkiyavaṉ āciriyaṉ. āciriyar illaṟattārum tuṟavaṟattārumāka iru cārār. ekkulattārāyiṉum illaṟam naṭattum āciriyaraiyum, aiyar eṉṟaḻaippatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku, muṉivarāṉa āciriyar eṅkum aiyar eṉṉum peyarkkuriyar.
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa   (tol.  1091)
eṉpatil, aiyar eṉpatu muṉivaraik kuṟittatu. piṅkalam muṉivar tokutiyai aiyar tokuti eṉa vakuttukkūṟum. muṉivar palavakaiyilum mikap periyār eṉpatu, periyārait tuṇaik kōṭal periyāraip piḻaiyāmai eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷati kāraṅkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻnāṭu pukunta pirāmaṇar iruvakai nilaippaṭṭu āciriyat toḻilaiyē mutalāvatu mēṟkoṇṭamaiyāl, avar aiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. pūcāriya rāṉa purōkitarum orucār āciriya vakuppiṉarē. itu paṟṟiyē, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kiṟittavak kurumārāṉa pātirimārum aiyar eṉa aḻaikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭup pātirimārai nāṭṭaiyar eṉpar. kuru eṉṉum peyar kalaiyāciriyaṉaiyum matavāciri yaṉaiyum potuppaṭak kuṟippatu. avviruvakaiyāriṉ toḻi loppumaiyai uṇarttum.
avar eṉṉum teṉcol teluṅkil vāru eṉat tirivatāl, avarkaḷ eṉṉum uyarvīṟṟiṟ kotta vāru eṉṉuñ colloṭu kūṭi aiyavāru eṉa niṟkum teluṅkup peyar, aiyavāru eṉat tirintu māl neṟiyār (vaiṇavar) āṉa teluṅkup pirāmaṇaraik kuṟikkum aiyakāru eṉpatu, āṅkilattil aiyaṅkār eṉṉum vaṭivaṅ koḷḷum.
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa āṭcit talaivaṉ aracaṉātaliṉ, avaṉum aiyaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
oru peṇṇukku vāḻkkait talaivaṉ avaḷ kaṇavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ avaṭku aiyaṉāvaṉ.
eṉṉaikku mutavātu
eṉpatil, ai eṉpatu kaṇavaṉaik kuṟittatu.
iṉi, ellā vulakaṅkaṭkum oru peruntalaivaṉāṉa iṟaivaṉukku. aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarurimai collāmalē viḷaṅkum. aiyai eṉṉum peyar kāḷiyaiyum, malai makaḷaiyuṅ kuṟittalāl, aiyaṉ eṉpatu civaṉaik kuṟittal tēṟṟam. muḻumutaṟṟeyvam perun teyvam ciṟuteyvam eṉat teyvam muttiṟappaṭutalāl, piṟkālattil civaṉ makaṉākak kūṟappeṟṟa vaṇikat teyvamāṉa cāttaṉ. aiyaṉ eṉṟum aiyaṉār eṉṟum āṇpāl vaṭivilum uyarvup paṉmai vaṭivilum kuṟikkap peṟuvaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam, aiyaṉ eṉṉum col, palvēṟu poruḷ kuṟittut toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ nāṭṭil tirintum tiriyātum vaḻaṅki yiruppavum; aṇuvaḷavum āriyat toṭarpaṟṟa paṟaiyaruḷḷiṭṭa cila tamiḻp paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ aiyaṉ eṉṉum collaiyē tantai peyarākak koṇṭiruppavum; vaḻakkiṟkum varalāṟṟiṟkum moḻi nūṟkum muṟṟum māṟāka, āriyaṉ eṉṉum varuṇap peyariṉ citaivē aiyaṉ eṉum tamiḻc col eṉa, ōriru taṉip paṭṭavar nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiluṅ kuṟittiruppatu. mikamika varuttattakka toṉṟām.
perumaik karuttu tiraṭcik karuttiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa parumaik karuttiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyiruppatāl, nerukkamum ceṟivum puṇarppum vaṉmaiyum kuṟikkum aḷ eṉṉum aṭiccol liṉiṉṟu, muṟkūṟiya muṟaippaṭi, ai eṉṉum collum ataṉvaḻi aiyaṉ aiyai mutaliya coṟkaḷum tirintiruppatu, iyaṟkaikkum ēraṇattiṟkum ēṟṟatē.
aiyaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlam ai yātalāl, atu ārya eṉṉuñ colloṭu poruntāmaiyaiyum kaṇṭu koḷka.
viii. tiṇmai
pala aṇukkaḷ allatu poruḷkaḷ mika neruṅkic cērvatāl tiṇmai uṇṭākum.
uṟu - uṟa - uṟappu. uṟa - uṟai. uṟaital = kaṭṭiyātal.
uṟu - iṟu - iṟuku - iṟukku - iṟukkam.
iṟukkutal = iṟukumāṟu amukkik kāṭṭutal.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi = iṟukiyatu. kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāy. kāḻppu = vairam. kāyttal = uḻaippāl kai iṟukutal, piñcu mutirntu iṟukutal, kāy kāyttal.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceṟi - ceṟivu.
tuḷ - tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṭpu - tiṭpam.
tiṇ - tiṇuku - tiṇuṅku - tiṇukkam. tiṇ - tiṇi.
nuṟu - neṟi. neṟittal = viṟappākutal.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟuku - muṟukal = cūṭṭiṉāl iṟukiyatu.
muṟu - muṟa - muṟamuṟappu = viṟappu.
muṟu - viṟa - viṟa - viṟappu.
viṟu - veṟu - veṟi. veṟittal = ceṟital.
viṟa - viṟai. viṟaittal = kuḷirāl iṟukutal.
muḷ - (moḷ) - moy. moyttal = iṟukutal.
ix. tiṇṇam
tiṇṇam eṉpatu taṭṭaiyāṉa poruḷiṉ parumaṉ. uram = tiṇṇam.
ōlai = tiṇṇiya ilai. melliya takaṭṭai ōlaiyāyiruk kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkamiruppiṉum, ilaiyai viṭa ōlai tiṇṇa māyiruttalaik kavaṉikka.
ōṭu = tiṇṇamāṉa kāyttōl.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi. keṭṭik kāppu = tiṇṇamāṉa kāppu.
tuḷ - teraḷ - tōḷ - tōṭu = tiṇṇamāṉa paḻattōl, ōlai.
tuḷ - (tiṉ) - tiṇ - tiṇṇam.
tul = til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ.
muḷ - muraḷ - muraṇ = tiṇṇamāṉa cippi.
x. kaṉam
tiṇmaiyāṉatum tiṇṇamāṉatum kaṉakkum.
kuru = kaṉam. kuru - kurūu.
pacumaṭ kurūuttiraḷ  (puṟam. 32).
kul - kal - kaṉ - kaṉa - kaṉam.
(puḷ) - (paḷ) - paḷu - paḷuvu.
xi. valimai
kaṉamuḷḷatu valiyatu
uram - uraṉ. uram - uravu - uravōṉ = valiyōṉ.
uṟu - eṟuḻ = vali.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
tul - til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ tiṟal.
tiṟam - tiṟamai.
poru - pōr = valimai.

mal = val - vali - valimai.
val - valu - valuvu.
val - vaṉ - vaṉpu - vaṟpu.
val - valam = valimai, veṟṟi.
valakkai = payiṟciyiṉāl valimai peṟṟa kai.

muḷ - moḷ - moy = valimai. moy - moympu = valimai.
muḷ (maḷ) - vaḷ = valimai.
xii. kaṭiṉam
valiyatu kaṭiṉamāṉatu.
kul - kal = kaṭiṉamāṉatu. kal - kaṉṟu. kaṉṟutal = kāy kiḻaṅku mutaliyaṉa kaṭiṉamātal. kaṉṟu - kaṇṭu.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu - kaṭumai.
kaṭu - kaṭiṉam. kaṭu - kaṭṭam.
vaḷ - (vay) - vayir. vayirttal - vayiraṅ koḷḷutal, ceṟṟaṅ koḷḷutal. vayir = kompu. vayir - vayiram - vairam.
vayir - vayiri. vayirittal = kaṭiṉamātal.
vayir - vayiri - vairi = pakai, pakaivaṉ.
xiii. uṟuti
uram - uraṉ.
uṟu - uṟuti, uṟutalai.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu = uṟuti.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
tul - til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ.
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiṇ - tiṇṇam.
tiṇ - tiṭam.
tiṇ - tiṇṇam - tiṇṇakkam = neñcaḻuttam.
tiṇ - tiṭam - tiṭāri - tiṭārikkam = neñcuram.
ūkārac cuṭṭu - vaḷaital
vērum āṇiyum pōṉṟa nīṇṭa poruṭkaḷ, tām muṭṭiṉa poruḷōṭoṉṟāviṭattuc cāyum. iyaṅku tiṇaiyuyirikaḷ cuvarum malaiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟāl taṭaiyuṇṭaviṭattup pakkamākat tirumpic cellum.
(1) vaḷaital tuṟai
i. kōṇutal
kōṇutalāvatu, nērākac cellum poruḷ oru pakkamākac cāytal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = cāytal.
uṟu - iṟu - iṟa - iṟappu = kūraiccāyvu.
iṟa - iṟavāṇam, iṟavāram = kūraiccāyvu.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ. kōṇutal = cāytal.
kōṇ - kōṭu. kōṭutal = cāytal.
kōṇ - kōṇam = cāyviṉāl uṇṭākum mūlai.
cuḷ - caḷ - caḻi. caḻital - kalamum peṭṭiyum pakkamāka amuṅkic carital.
caḷ - caru - caruvu. caruvutal = cāytal.
caru - cari - carivu. carital = cāytal.
cari = aṭivāram.
caru - cār. cārtal = cāytal.
tūṇil cāyntu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ eṉpatum, tūṇil cārntu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ eṉpatum oṉṟē. cērtalaik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collum, cāytalaik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collum vevvēṟu.
cār - cāral = malaiccarivu, cāyntu peyyum maḻai.
cāralaṉ = cāralnāṭaṉ, malaināṭaṉ.
cāralaṉ - cēralaṉ - cēral - cēraṉ = malai nāṭaṉ, muttamiḻ vēntaruḷ oruvaṉ.
caḷ - cāḷ - cāṭu. cāṭutal = cāytal, cāṭu - cāṭṭam - cāyvu. cāṭu - cāṭai = nēraṉmai, cāyal oppu.
cāḷ - cāy - cāyvu. cāyvu carivu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
cāyuṅkālam - cāyaṅkālam - cāyṅkālam = katiravaṉ cāyum vēḷai (eṟpāṭu).
cāy - cāyai = niḻal. niḻal cāytal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. cāy - cāyal = niḻal, orumaruṅku oppu.
(talai) cāyttal = tūṅkutal. cāy - cayaṉam (va.) 
cāy - cā - cāvu. cātal = cāyntu viḻutalpōl iṟattal.
tul - til - tiru - tirum - tirumpu. tirumputal - cāytal.
uccivēḷaikkuppiṉ uṭampu niḻal cāyvatai, aṭittirumputal eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
mul - (mūl) mūlai = kōṇam.
muḷ - muṭa - muṭaṅku - muṭaṅki = mūlai. mūlai muṭaṅki eṉpatu vaḻakku.
muḷ - (mūḷ) - (māḷ) - māṇ - māṇal = cāyvu, vaḷaivu. kōṇal māṇal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
māḷ - māṭu - māṭai = cāyvu. cāṭaimāṭai eṉpatu vaḻakku. nērākap paḻikkāmal cāyvu pōṉṟa nēral muṟaiyil paḻittal, cāṭaimāṭaiyāyt tiṭṭutal eṉappaṭum.
maḷ - vaḷ - vāḷ- vār. vārtal = cāytal, carital.
vār - vāram = carivāṉa iṭam. aṭivāram = malaic carivu.
tāḻvāram = kūraiccarivu.
vāḷ - vāṭu - vāṭṭam = cāyvu. vāṭṭam cāṭṭam eṉpatu vaḻakku. aṅkaṇam vāṭṭam cāṭṭamāy irukka vēṇṭum eṉpar.
ii. vaḷaital
vaḷaitalāvatu, koṭiyum melliya kampiyum pōṉṟa nīṇṭa tuvaḷporuṭkaḷ vaṭṭamākum varai mēṉmēlum palapaṭiyāyk kōṇutal.
uṟu - iṟu. iṟutal = vaḷaital.
iṟu - iṟa = vaḷainta peruṅkūṉi (prawn)
iṟa - iṟā - iṟavu. iṟā - iṟāl - iṟāṭṭu.
iṟa - iṟai = peṇṭiriṉ vaḷainta muṉkai.
kul - kula - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷaital. kulavu - kulāvu.
kul - kuṉ - kuṉi. kuṉital = vaḷaital.
kuṉ - kūṉ = vaḷainta mutuku. kūṉutal = vaḷaital.
kūṉ - kūṉi = ciṟṟiṟāl.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻiyam = vaḷaitaṭi.
kuḷ - (kuṇ) - kuṇa - kuṇaku - kuṇakku = vaḷaivu. kuṇakutal = vaḷaital.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūḷi = vaḷainta vāḻaippaḻam.
kuḷ - kuru - kuruku = (vaḷaital). vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrp paṟavai yiṉam.
kuḷ - kuṭa - kuṭam = vaḷaivu. kuṭā = vaḷaivu. kuṭantai = vaḷaivu. kuṭa - kuṭakkam = vaḷaivu.
kuru - kura - kuraṅku = vaḷaivu, kokki kuraṅkutal = vaḷaital.
kura - kuṟaḷ - kuṟaṇṭu. kuṟaṇṭutal = vaḷaital.
kuṟaḷ - kuṟaṭu = vaḷainta alakuḷḷa karuvi.
kuḷ - koḷ = vaḷainta kāṇakkāy. koṭpu = vaḷaivu.
koḷ - koṭu - koṭumai = vaḷaivu. koṭuṅkōl = vaḷainta kōl. koṭukkāyppuḷi = vaḷainta kāyuḷḷa maravakai. koṭukku = vaḷainta muḷ.
koṭu - koṭi = vaḷainta taṇṭu. koṭu - koṭiṟu = vaḷainta kuṟaṭu, kuṟaṭu pōṉṟa alaku (jaw).
koḷ - (koṭku) - kokku = vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa paṟavaiyiṉam.
koḷ - kom - kompu = vaḷainta kōṭu, vaḷainta vilaṅkuk kompu.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṇam. kōṇappuḷi = koṭuk kāyppuḷi. kōṇam = kūṉvāḷ.
kōṇ - kōṭu. kōṭutal = vaḷaital, kōṭu - kōṭṭam = vaḷaivu. kōṭu = vaḷainta yāḻttaṇṭi, yāḻttaṇṭi, piṟai vaḷaivu.

iii. vaṇaṅkutal
makkaḷ kaṭavuḷaiyum, tāḻntōr uyarntōraiyum vaṇaṅkum pōtu uṭampu vaḷaitalāl, vaḷaitaṟ karuttil vaṇakkak karuttup piṟantatu.
uṟu - iṟu - iṟa - iṟai - iṟaiñcu. iṟaiñcutal = vaḷaital, vaṇaṅkutal.
kuṭam - kuṭantam = vaḷaivu, vaṇakkam. kuṭantam paṭutal = vaḷaital, vaṇaṅkutal, toḻutal.
kuḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - (vaṇ) - vaṇaṅku - vaṇakku - vaṇakkam. vaṇaṅkutal = vaḷaital, toḻutal.
iv. tirumputal
oru poruḷ vaḷaiyumpōtu atu tāṉ muṉpu puṟappaṭṭa iṭattai allatu ticaiyai nōkkutalāl, vaḷaitaṟ karuttil tirumpaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
tuḷ - (turu) - tiru - tirum - tirumpu.
tiru - tiri. tirital = tirumputal.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟi - maṟi. maṟital - tirumputal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. maṭaṅkutal = tirumputal.
maṭaṅku - maṭaṅkal = iṭaiyiṭai tirumpip pārkkum, atāvatu muṉṉum piṉṉum nōkkic cellum arimā, atupōṉṟa kūṟṟuvaṉ.
v. mīḷtal
mīḷtalāvatu tirumpi varutal.
tuḷ - (turu) - tiru - tirum - tirumpu.
tiru - tiri. tirital = tirumputal.
oṉṟaic ceppiṉai tiritiyeṉṟāṉ  (kampa. aṅkata. 10.)
muḷ - (mūḷ) - mīḷ - mīṭci. mīḷtal = tirumputal. mīṭṭal = tirupputal. aṭaivu vaitta poruḷait tirupputal.
mūḷ - mūṭṭu - mīṭṭu. mīṭṭutal = tirupputal, aṭaivu vaitta poruḷait tirupputal. mīṭṭutal eṉpatē ulaka vaḻakku.
mīḷtal = pakaivar kaiyiṉiṉṟu allatu tuṉpattiṉiṉṟu tirumputal. mīṭṭal = pakaivar kaiyiṉiṉṟu allatu tuṉpat tiṉiṉṟu vilakkik kāttal.
mīḷ - mīṭpu - mīṭpaṉ.
niraimīṭci eṉpatu nirai tirumpi varutalaiyum mīṭkappaṭu talaiyum kuṟittal kāṇka.
vi. maṭaṅkutal
ilaiyum tāḷum tuṇiyum pōṉṟa poruṭkaḷiṉ ōramum, kaikāl mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉaiyum, tirumputalē maṭaṅkutalām.
cuḷ - cur - curi. curital = maṭital, maṭippu viḻutal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku.
muṭu - muṭi - maṭi. maṭital = maṭaṅkutal.
maṭittal = tāḷ tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟai maṭakkutal.
maṭi = maṭikkappaṭṭa cēlai, araiyiṟ kaṭṭiṉa āṭaiyiṉ mēl viḷimpaip paipōl maṭitta pakuti.
maṭaṅku - maṭakku =alakai maṭakkivaikkuṅ katti.
maṭakkutal = maṭittal.
vii. tiraital
tiraitalāvatu maṭippu viḻutal allatu maṭippu viḻuntu curuṅkutal.
cuḷ - cur - curi. curital = maṭippu viḻutal, tiraital.
curittal = tiraital, curuṅkutal.
curitta mūñci = mūppiṉāl tirainta mukam.
curitakam = kalippāviṉ piṟavuṟuppukkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa poruḷaic curukki allatu taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik kūṟum muṭivuṟuppu. curitakam, taravu tāḻicai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭum poruḷai aṭakkik kūṟuvataṉālēyē, aṭakkiyal eṉappaṭṭa teṉṟaṟika.
cuḷ - curu - curuṅku - curukku - curukkam.
kuṭaiyai maṭakkutalaik kuṭaiyaic curukkutal eṉṟum, tirai viḻutalaic curukkuviḻutal eṉṟam kūṟutal kāṇka.
tuḷ - (turu) - tirai. tiraital = maṭippu viḻutal, alaiyeḻutal, curuṅkutal, tiraḷutal.
tiraittal = maṭittuc curukkutal, maṭittut tiraṭṭutal.
tirai - tiraiyal = curuṅkukai, veṟṟilaic curuḷ.
tirai - tiraṅku. tiraṅkutal = tiraintu curuṅkutal, vaṟṟic curuṅkutal, curuḷutal.
tiraṅku - tirakku. tirakkutal = curuṅkutal.
viii. tirupputal
tirupputalāvatu pakkan tiruppip puraṭṭutal.
tirumpu - tiruppu - tiruppi.
tōcai tiruppi = tōcaiyaip puraṭṭuṅ karuvi.
puraḷtal = tirumputal. puraṇṭupaṭuttal = tirumpip paṭuttal.
puraḷ - puraṭṭu. puraṭṭutal = oṉṟait tirupputal.
puraṭṭu - puraṭṭi. tōcai puraṭṭi = tōcai tiruppi.
puraṭṭu - puraṭṭal = puraṭṭic camaikkuṅ kaṟi.
ix. maṭakkutal
maṭakkutalāvatu oṉṟait tiruppi ataṉ celavait taṭuttalum atai amarttutalum.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟi - maṟi. maṟittal = maṭakkutal, amarttutal.

kiṭaimaṟittal = kiṭaiyamarttutal.
maṟi - maṟiyal = taṭuttal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku - maṭakku.
maṭakkutal = tirupputal, taṭuttal, amarttal, vellutal.
maṭaṅkutal = tōṟṟal, aṭaṅkutal.
kiṭai maṭakkutal = kiṭaiyamarttutal.
muṭaṅku - muṭakku. muṭakkutal = taṭuttal.
x. muṭaṅkik kiṭattal
muṭaṅkik kiṭattalāvatu cōmputalum payaṉpaṭātu ōriṭattut taṅkutalum.
iyaṅku tiṇaiyuyirikaḷ tūṅkumpōtu potuvāyk kaikāl muṭakkik koḷvatāl, muṭaṅkaṟ karuttil maṭimaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
iṉi, maṭakkuṇṭu kiṭattalē muṭaṅkik kiṭattal eṉiṉumām.
muṭaṅkutal = tūṅkutal, vaḻaṅkātu ōriṭattamartal.
paṇa muṭakkam = paṇam vaḻaṅkātu ōriṭattut taṅkutal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭi - maṭi. maṭital = tūṅkutal, cōmputal. maṭi = cōmpal, kaṭṭukkaṭaic carakku.
maṭivīcutal = kaṭṭukkaṭaiccarakku nāṟutal.
xi. paṉmuṟai kuṟittal
oṉṟait tirumpat tirumpac ceytal ataip paṉmuṟai ceytalātalāl. tirumpaṟ karuttuc col paṉmuṟaik karuttuṇarttum.

tiruppi, tiruppit tiruppi, tiruppiyum.
tiriya, tiriyavum.

maṭakki, maṭakki maṭakki.

maṟittu, maṟittum.
(muṟu - maṟu) maṟukka, maṟuttu, maṟuttum.

vaḷaiya, vaḷaiya vaḷaiya, vaḷaittu, vaḷaittu vaḷaittu.
kuṟippu : iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ maṭakku eṉṉuñ colloṉṟē peyar; piṟavellām iṭaiccol.
xii. vēṟātal
oṉṟait tirumpac ceyvatu vēṟoru muṟai ceyvatum, oṉṟiṉiṉṟu tirital ataṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭutalumātalāl, tirumpaṟ karuttuc col vēṟātaṟ karuttait taḻuviṟṟu.
maṟu = vēṟu. maṟu piṟavi = vēṟu piṟappu.
maṟupaṭi = vēṟoru muṟai. maṟunāḷ = aṭutta nāḷ.
maṟu - maṟṟu =vēṟu, tirumpavum. vēṟāka.
maṟṟu - maṟṟa - maṟṟai (peyareccam)
maṟṟoṉṟu = vēṟoṉṟu.
maṟṟappaṭi = vēṟuvakaiyil.
maṟṟavaṉ = vēṟoruvaṉ, piṟaṉ, aṭuttavaṉ.
xiii. māṟutal
vēṟātaṟ karuttu māṟutaṟ karuttait taḻuvum.
tirumpu - tiruppu. tirupputal = moḻipeyarttal.
collait tirupputal = collai māṟṟutal.
tirumpu - tiṟampu. tiṟamputal = vēṟupaṭutal, mīṟutal.
tirital = vēṟupaṭal, māṟutal, tirittal = vēṟupaṭuttal.
tiri - tirivu - tiripu.
tiricol = iyaṟcolliṉiṉṟu tirinta col.
puḷ - puru - puraḷ. puraḷtal = col māṟutal. puraḷ - puraḷi = meymmāṟṟu, poy. puraḷ - puraṭṭu - puraṭṭaṉ. puraḷ - piṟaḷ - piṟaḻcci. puraḷ - puraṭci.
muṟu - maṟu - maṟuttal = māṟṟutal. maṟu - maṟuppu.
maṟu - maṟai = maṟuppu. etirttu - etirmaṟai.
maṟukkaḷittal = paḻaiya koḷkaikku māṟutal.
maṟutalittal = paḻaiya koḷkaikku māṟutal.
maṟu - māṟu - māṟṟu - māṟṟam.

xiv. maṭaṅkaḷavu
oṉṟait tirumpat tirumpac ceyvatu ataip paṉmaṭaṅku perukkuvatāyiruttalāl, tirumpaṟ karuttuc col maṭaṅ kaḷavaik kuṟittatu.
āṅkilattil turn (tirumpa) eṉṉuñ col muṟaiyaiyum fold (maṭi) eṉṉuñ col maṭaṅkaiyum, kuṟittal kāṇka.
e.ṭu. Five turns, ten fold.
(tuṭam) - taṭam = vaḷaivu. taṭam - taram = muṟai.
ṭa - ra, pōli. o. nō. paṭavar - paravar.
taṭam - taṭavai = muṟai.
tirumpu - tiruppu = taṭavai.
muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. irumaṭaṅku = irumuṟaiyaḷavu.
muṭi - maṭi = maṭaṅku. irumaṭiyāku peyar, mummaṭic cōḻaṉ, nāṉmaṭit tolaivari (tanti) mutaliya toṭarkaḷai nōkkuka.
muṟu - muṟai = vaḷaivu, taṭavai.
muḷ - (mūḷ) - (māḷ) - māṇ = maṭaṅku.
paṉmāṇ  (paripā. 13, 62)
vaḷ - (vāḷ) - vāṭṭi = taṭavai.
cuṟṟu vaṭṭam vaḷaiyam mutaliya coṟkaḷ muḻu vaḷaivu kuṟittavai.
xv. varutal
ōriṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟavaṉ tirumputalē varutalām. iṉṟu ceṉṉaikkuc ceṉṟu nāḷaikkut tirumpuvēṉ eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟil, tirumpuvēṉ eṉpatu tirumpivaruvēṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
cellutal varutal eṉpaṉa ticainōkki vēṟupaṭṭaṉa vēyaṉṟi, viṉaivaṭivil vēṟupaṭṭaṉavalla. oruvaṉ ōriṭatti ṉiṉṟu maṟṟōriṭattiṟkuc cellumpōtu, avviṉai puṟappaṭṭa iṭattai nōkkic cellutal eṉṟum, pukum iṭattai nōkki varutal eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ātalāl, oruvaṉ ōriṭattiṟkup putitāy variṉum mīṇṭuvariṉum, ticaipaṟṟi iraṇṭum oṉṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
vaḷ = vaḷaivu. vaḷ - varu - vār - vā - va.
o. nō. taḷ - taru - tār - tā - ta.
varu : varukiṟāṉ, varukai, varuvāy, varavu.
vār : vārāṉai (varukai), vārum, vāruṅkaḷ (ēvaṟ paṉmai).
vār, vā (ēval orumai).
va; vantāṉ, vammiṉ (ēvaṟpaṉmai).
varukiṟāṉ varuvāṉ eṉṉum nikaḻkāla etirkāla muṟṟu vaṭivukaḷilum. varukai eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarilum, varu eṉpatu pakutiyāyiruttalāṉum; vār eṉṉum orumai yēvalilum, vārum vāruṅkaḷ eṉṉum paṉmaiyēvalilum, vārtal vārāṉai eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkaḷilum, vār eṉpatu pakutiyāyirutta lāṉum; vā eṉṉum ēvalorumai vaṭiviṉiṉṟu vātal vāvu eṉa ētēṉum oru toḻiṟpeyar piṟavāmaiyāṉum, varutal eṉṉum viṉaikku rukaraṅkūṭiya varu eṉpatē pakutiyām. vaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu mutalāvatu tiriyak kūṭiyavai var - varu - eṉpavaiyē.
iṅku varutal viṉaikkuk kūṟiyatait tarutal viṉaikkuṅ koḷka. kōr eṉṉum col kō eṉak kuṟaintatupōla, varu eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa vār eṉṉuñ collum vā eṉak kuṟainta teṉka. nō eṉṉum viṉai iṟantakālattilum ēvalilum no (nontāṉ, nommāṭā) eṉak kuṟukiyatupōla, vā tā eṉpaṉavum va ta eṉak kuṟukiṉa.
vār = vā (ēvalorumai).
vantikka vāreṉa  (paripā. 20, 70)
lāraṭā vuṉakkiyātu tāṉartam makaḷaṭukkimō  (pārata. vēttira. 12).
xvi. varaital
varaitalāvatu eḻututal. eḻututal eṉpatu, eḻuttu eḻutu talaiyum paṭam varaitalaiyum potuppaṭak kuṟikkum. muṟ kālattil paṭaveḻuttē (Heiroglyphic or picture-writing) tamiḻakat til vaḻaṅkiyatāl, eḻuttu eṉṉum col paṭattaiyum varivaṭivaiyum oruṅkē uṇarttiṟṟu.
paṭamum eḻuttum perumpālum vaḷaikōṭukaḷālāvatāl, vaḷaitaṟ karuttil varaitaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
irutiṇaiyuyirikaḷiṉ uṭampilum iyalpākak kāṇappaṭum kōṭukaḷ potuvāka vaḷaintēyiruttaliṉ, kōṭu eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu vaḷaikōṭṭaiyē kuṟittup piṉpu oppumai paṟṟi nēr kōṭṭaiyum kuṟittatu.
kōṭutal = vaḷaital. kōṭu = vaḷaikōṭu, vari (kōṭu). 
varikkutirai varippuli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kōṭukaḷai nōkkuka.

vaḷ - var - vari. varital = vaḷaital, vaḷaittu allatu cuṟṟikkaṭṭutal.
vari = vaḷaikōṭu, kōṭu, kōṭṭuvaṭivāṉa eḻuttu, varaivu, varaṇaṉai, varaṇikkum pāṭṭu allatu kātaṟ pāṭṭu, kaṭṭu.
āṟṟuvari kāṉalvari mutaliya icaippāṭṭu vakaikaḷai nōkkuka.
varittal = eḻututal, varaital, pūcutal, kātaliyiṉ tōḷilum mārpilum varaital, avaḷai maṇattal, vaḷaittu allatu cuṟṟikkaṭṭutal.
vari - varaṉ = maṇavāḷaṉ.
vari - varaṇam (vari + aṇam) = eḻuttu, varaivu, pūccu, niṟam, kulam, vakai, ōcaivakai, paṇ, pāṭṭu.
varaṇam (va.) - varaṇi (va.). varaṇittal = varaital, collāl varaital.
varaṇi - varaṇaṉai (varaṇi + aṉai). varaṇam varaṇi varaṇaṉai eṉpaṉa teṉ collaṭiyāyp piṟanta vaṭanāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ.
vaḷ - vaṇ - vaṇṇam = eḻuttu, varaivu, pūccu, niṟam, vakai, ceyyuḷ, ōcaivakai.
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam = arākam eṉṉum vaṇṇavuṟuppu. vaṇṇaka vottāḻicaikkali toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻc ceyyuḷ vakai.
vaṇṇattāṉ - vaṇṇāṉ = āṭaikku niṟamūṭṭupavaṉ. 
vaṇṇattuppūcci - vaṇṇāttippūcci.
vaṇṇam - (vaṇṇakaṉ) - vaṇṇakkaṉ = kaṭṭi nāṇayattiṉ vaṇṇattai (uraittu) nōṭṭañ ceypavaṉ.
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇi. vaṇṇittal = varaṇittal.
vaṇṇi - vaṇṇakam = varaṇittup pukaḻkai.
o. nō. cuḷ - cuṇ - cuṇṇam - cuṇṇakam. cuṇṇittal = nīṟākkal.
vari - varuvu. varuvutal = kōṭiṭutal. varuvūci = kōṭiṭum ūci.
vari - varai = kōṭu. kīṟal. varaital = ōviyam varaital, eḻuttu eḻututal, kaṭṭurai allatu nūl eḻututal, kātaliyiṉ tōḷilum mārpilum toyyil varaital, avaḷai maṇattal.
kuṟippu : - vaṇṇam vaṇṇakam eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷātalāṉum, avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭuvaṉa taṉittamiḻ yāppu vakaiḷātalāṉum, akkuṟiyīṭukaṭku mūlam taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷē. 
iṉi, vaṇṇam eṉṉum col, vakai eṉṉum poruḷil, avvaṇṇam ivvaṇṇam evvaṇṇam eṉa iruvakai vaḻakkilum peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutalaiyum nōkkuka. 
niṟam eṉṉum varaṇap peyarum tiṟam eṉṉum vakaip peyarum icaivakaiyaik kuṟittal pōṉṟē. avviraṇṭaiyum kuṟikkum vaṇṇam eṉṉum peyarum icai vakaiyaik kuṟitta teṉka. tolkāppiyattil 20 vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, avinayattil 100 vaṇṇaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
vaṇṇan tāṉē nālain teṉpa
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (cey. 210) vaḻinūṉmuṟaiyiṟ kūṟuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
xvii. varampu
ōriṭattiṉ ellai kōṭṭiṉāl kuṟikkappaṭuvatu vaḻakka mātaliṉ, kōṭiṭutal ellai kuṟittalaiyuṇarttiṟṟu. 
varaital =kōṭiṭutal, ellai kuṟittal, varaiyaṟuttal, vilakkutal.
varai = kōṭu, ellaikkōṭu, aḷavu, ellai.
varaiyaṟuttal, aṭimutal muṭivarai, ituvaraiyum, itu varaikkum mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷaik kāṇka.
varai - varampu = kōṭu, ellai, aḷavu, tāṇṭak kūṭāta ellaipōṉṟa aṟaviti, caṭṭam.
varampu - varappu - vayalellaiyākiya cuṟṟuttiṭar.

(2) iyaṅkal tuṟai
i. acaital
acaital eṉpatu, ētēṉumoru poruḷ iṭavalamākavēṉum muṉ piṉṉākavēṉum cāytal allatu vaḷaitalōyātaliṉ, vaḷaital karuttil acaitaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
ul - ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulāvutal = acaital.
ul - al - alai; alaital = acaital. alai - acai.
al - āl - āṭu. āṭutal = acaital.
ul - ulu - uluṅku - ulukku. uluṅkutal = acaital. 
ulukkutal = acaittal.
uluṅku - aluṅku - aṉuṅku - aṉukku.
ulukku - alukku = kamakam. alukkutal = kuralai iṉimaipaṭa vaḷaittal allatu acaittal.
aluṅku - alaṅku - alaṅkal = acaital.
ulu - uluppu. ulupputal = acaittal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = vaḷaital, acaital.
uḷ - (uy) - uyal. uyalutal = acaital.
(uy) - (uya) - iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam = acaivu, kiḷarcci. iyaṅkutal = acaital.
kul - kulu - kuluṅku - kulukku. kuluṅkutal = acaital,
(cul) - cal - cali. calittal = acaital, acaittal. acaittut teḷḷutal. caliyaṭai = callaṭai.
tuḷ - tuḷa - tuḷaṅku - tuḷakku - tuḷakkam.
tuḷaṅkutal = acaital.
tuḷ - (tuy) - tuyal. tuyalutal = vaḷaital, acaital.
nuḷ - nuṇ - nuṇaṅku. nuṇaṅkutal = vaḷaital, acaital.
nuḷ - nuṭa - nuṭaṅku. nuṭaṅkutal = vaḷaital, acaital.
ii. iyaṅkutal
oru poruḷ acaivatiṉāl atu caṟṟu iṭam peyarkiṉṟatu. ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkup peyartalum acaiviṉ pāṟpaṭum. āṅkilattilum move eṉṉuñ col, acaitalaiyum iṭam peyartalaiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka.
ul - ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulāvutal = iyaṅkutal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = naṭattal.
olku - oḻuku - oḻukku - oḻukkam = naṭattai.
oḻukutal = naṭattal, mukkaraṇattāl naṭattal.
ul - al - alai - acai. acaital = naṭattal, mella naṭattal.
uḷ - (uy) - uya - iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam = celavu. iyaṅkutal = cellutal.
uya - uyavu. uyavu ney = vaṇṭi cellutaṟku vēṇṭum macaku.
uya - uyal - iyal. iyalutal = naṭattal, nikaḻtal, nikaḻa muṭital, cellutal.
iya - iyavuḷ = celuttuvōṉ, kaṭavuḷ.
iyal = naṭattai, taṉmai.
iyal - iyalpu = taṉmai.
iyal - iyaṟkai = taṉmai, taṉmaiyāka allatu tāṉāka naṭappatu.
cul - cel - celavu.
iii. varuntutal
acaiviṉālum celaviṉālum uṭampiṟkut taḷarcciyum varuttamum piṟakkum. ataṉāl, acaivum celavum kuṟitta coṟkaḷ taḷarcciyum varuttamuṅ kuṟikkum. uḷḷattiṉ acaivu tuṉpam, uṭaliṉ acaivu varuttam.
ul - al - allal = tuṉpam.
al - allā. allāttal = tuṉpuṟutal.
alacutal = acaital, varuntutal, alacaṭi = tuṉpam.
alaital = acaital, varuntutal. alaittal = varuttutal.
āṟalaittal = vaḻippōkkarai varuttip paṟittal.
alaiccal = tirital, varuttam.
alai - acai - acaivu = taḷarcci, varuttam, tuṉpam.
cul - cal - cali - calippu. calittal = taḷartal, varuntutal.
cil - cel - cellal = tuṉpam.
cellal iṉṉal iṉṉā maiyē  (tol. uri. 6)
tuḷaṅkutal = acaital, varuntutal.

(3) muṟital tuṟai
muṟital
muṟitalāvatu, viṟappāṉa nīḷ poruḷkaḷ oṉṟai muṭṭic cāyumiṭattu allatu vaḷaiyumpōtu oṭital, ivviyaṟkai vitiyaṟintē, variccaiyum karumpaiyum oṭikka vēṇṭiyaviṭattu muḻaṅkālai muṭṭavaittuc cāyppar.
uṭu - oṭu - oṭi. oṭu = vaḷaivu, neḷivu. oṭu - oṭukku, ulōkak kalaṅkaḷiṉ neḷivai oṭu allatu oṭukku eṉpar. atai nīkkutaṟku oṭuttaṭṭutal allatu oṭukkeṭuttal eṉṟu peyar.
oṭital = muṟital. oṭi - oci.
uṟu - iṟu. iṟutal = vaḷaital, muṟital.
nuḷ - noḷ - noṭi. noṭittal = vaḷaital, oṭital.
nuḷ - neḷ - neru - neri - nerical = neruṅki muṭṭutal, uṭaital.
kaṇṇāṭi maṭkalam mutaliya poruḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭi nerintupōvatu, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ cāyntu muṟitalē.
nerital = vaḷaital, muṟital.
neṟital = vaḷaital. neṟittal = puṟavitaḻoṭittal. 
muḷ - muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = vaḷaital, oṭital.
muṟi = muṟinta tuṇṭu, cīṭṭu.

(4) tirukal tuṟai
tirukal
oru nīṇṭa poruḷ muḻu vaṭṭamākātu mēlum mēlum pala neḷivukaḷāka allatu vaḷaivukaḷākat toṭartal tirukalām.
cuḷ - curu - curi. curiyāṇi = muṟukkāṇi.
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiru - tiruku - tirukal. tiruku - tirukku = tiruki vaikkum aṇi allatu āṇi, muṟukku, māṟupāṭu, vañcaṉai.
tirukum āṇi tirukāṇi, tirukalāṉa kaḷḷi tiruku kaḷḷi.
tiruku = koṇṭait tirukupōl tirukivaikkum aṇi.
tiruki = tēṅkāy tiruki pōṉṟa karuvi.
tiru - tiri. tirital = muṟukutal. tirittal = muṟukkutal.
tiricaṭai tiritāṭi tirimaruppu mutaliyaṉa muṟukkuṇṭa poruḷ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ.
puḷ - puru - puraḷ - puraḷai - puraṭai - piraṭai - piruṭai = yāḻ muṟukkāṇi, tirittuk kūṟum poy.
puru - puri = muṟukkuṇṭa nūl, kayiṟu, purital = muṟukutal.
puru - puraḷ - puraṇṭai - piraṇṭai = puraṇṭirukkum allatu muṟukkuṇṭirukkum koṭi.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟuku - muṟukal. muṟuku - muṟukku = tirukalāka vuḷḷa palakāram, muṟukkum āṇi muṟukkāṇi.
tirukal murukal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷai = caṅku (muṟukiyiruppatu).
(5) vaṭṭat tuṟai
vaḷaitta poruḷiṉ irumuṉaiyum toṭumāṟu muḻu vaḷaivāṉatē vaṭṭam.
i. vaṭṭamāṉavai
uṟu - iṟu - iṟāl = vaṭṭamāṉa tēṉkūṭu. iṟattal = vaḷaital.
uḷ - (aḷ) - (āḷ) - āḻi = vaṭṭam, mōtiram, cakkaram.
kul - *(kula) - kulavai - kuravai = vaṭṭamāka niṉṟāṭuṅ kūttu.
kuḷ - (kuru) - kuruku = vaḷaiyal.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, cuṉṉam.
kuḷ - kuṭa - kuṭam - (kuṭakam) - kaṭakam = vaḷaiyal, vaḷainta matil, vaṭṭamāṉa peṭṭi.
(kuṭuku) - kiṭuku = vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam, vaṭṭamāṉa aṟai, kēṭakam pōṉṟa teṉṉantaṭṭi.
kiṭuku - kiṭukam - kēṭakam.
kuṇa - kuṇaku - kuṇakku - kuṇukku = kaṉatta kātu vaḷaiyam.
kuḷ - koḷ = kōḷ - kōṭu -kōṭṭam= vaṭṭam, māvaṭṭam. kōram = vaṭṭil.
cul - cil = cakkaram.
cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam = cuḻi, vaṭṭam. cuṉ - cuṉṉai = cuḻi.
cuḷ - cuḻi = vaṭṭam. cuḻittal = vaṭṭam varaital.
cuḷ - cuṭṭi = vaṭṭamāṉa aṇi, vaṭṭamāṉa poṟi. cuṭṭittalai = caṭṭiyuḷḷa talai.
cuvaḷ - cavaḷ - (cakaḷ) - cakaṇṭai = vaṭṭamāṉa paṟai.
cakaṇṭai - cakaṭai = cakkaram, cakkaramuḷḷa vaṇṭi, paṟai.
cakaṭai - cakaṭu - cakaṭam = vaṇṭi, vaṭṭil.
cakaṭu - cakaṭi. cakaṭu - cākāṭu. cakaṭu - cāṭu.
cakaṭai - cakkaṭai - cakkaṭā. 
cuḷ - (caḷ) - caru - cari = vaḷaiyal vakai.
cuḷ - cuḷai - cuḷaiyam - coḷaiyam = cuṉṉam.
cuḷ - (cur) - cūr - cūrppu = kaikkaṭakam.
puḷ - puru - puri - pari - pariti = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa katiravaṉ. pariti - paruti.
pari - paricai - vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam.
pari - paricu - parical = vaṭṭamāṉa kūṭaittōṇi.
puḷ - (paḷ) - paṇṭi = cakkaram, cakkaramuḷḷa vaṇṭi.
paṇṭi - pāṇṭi - pāṇṭil = vaṭṭam. akal, vaṭṭamāṉa kiṇṇam, cālar.
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭai = cuḻi, cuṉṉam.
muḷ - (maḷ) - (maṇ) - maṇi = vaṭṭamāṉa veṇkalam.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kālai maṭittal allatu maṭakkutal.
maṇṭutal = vaḷaital, maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭakaiyāṉ nāṭṭuppirivu, nāṟpāṉ nāḷ vaṭṭam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali, maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamātal, muḻumai yātal, niramputal.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa kōḷ, (katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa), vaṭṭamāṉa kaṇṇāṭi.
maṇṭila yāppu, maṇṭalittal, nilaimaṟi maṇṭilam, aṭimaṟi maṇṭilam mutaliyaṉa toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varum tamiḻ yāppilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ. aṭinirampi varutal maṇṭila yāppākum. vaṭṭaravuk karuttu muḻumai allatu niṟaivuk karuttait taḻuvum.
(maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷḷam = vaṭṭamāṉa kalam.
vaḷ - vaḷḷi - vaḷaiyal.
vaḷ - vaḷai. vaḷai - vaḷaiyam. vaḷai - vaḷaiyal, vaḷaivi.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa karuppukkaṭṭi, vaṭṭamāṉa ōṭu.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭi = vaṭṭamāṉa peṭṭi.
vaṭṭi - vaṭṭil = vaṭṭamāṉa kalam.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam - vaṭṭakai, vaṭṭāram.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇam = vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam. vaṭṭaṇam - vaṭṭaṇi. vaṭṭaṇittal = vaṭṭamātal, vaṭṭamākkutal.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇai = vaṭṭam, kēṭakam, cālar.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaravu = vaṭṭa vaṭivu.
vaḷ - vaṇṭu = vaḷaiyal, vaṭṭamāṉa pūcci.
vaṇṭu - vaṇṭi = cakkaram, cakkarattaiyuṭaiya ūrti. vaṇṭi - vaṇṭil.
vaḷ - vāḷ - vār - vārppu = vaḷaiyal.
vāḷ - vāḷi = mūkkilum kātilum aṇiyum kampi vaḷaiyam.

iv. cuṟṟutal
cuṟṟutalāvatu, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟaic cuṟṟivarutal allatu vaṭṭamiṭutal.

āl - ālatti = kaṇṇeccil kaḻittaṟku viḷakkaiyāvatu, mañcaḷ nīraiyāvatu oruvar talaiyaic cuṟṟiyeṭuttal.

v. kaṭṭutal
oru poruḷai nūlālum kayiṟṟālum cuṟṟik kaṭṭuvatāl, cuṟṟutaṟ karuttiṟ kaṭṭutaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
cuṟṟutal = āṭaiyai araiyaic cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal.
puri - pari - vari. varital = kaṭṭutal. varittal = kaṭṭutal. vari = kaṭṭum araciṟai. uḻavar kaṇṭu mutalil āṟiloru pakutiyai aḷantu cākkiṟ kaṭṭiyataṉāl, araciṟai vari eṉappaṭṭatu. arici piṭittal eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam ariciyaic cākkiṟ piṭittu vāṅkutalaik kuṟikkumō, aṅṅaṉamē vari kaṭṭutaleṉpatum varip pakutiyaic cākkiṟ kaṭṭi varittaṇṭalāḷariṭam oppu vittalaik kuṟikkum.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭi - muṭiccu = vaḷaittuk kaṭṭiya kaṭṭu, muṭiccup pōṉṟa marakkaṇu.
muṭi = vaḷaittuk kaṭṭiya kaṭṭu. muṭital = vaḷaittuk kaṭṭutal.
muṭi - muṭippu = muṭiyappaṭṭa poruḷ.
vi. cuḻalutal

kuḷ - kuṟu - (kaṟu) - kaṟa - kaṟaṅku = kāṟṟāṭi. kaṟaṅkutal = cuṟṟutal, cuḻalutal.
kuṟu - kiṟu - kiṟukiṟu = kiṟukiṟuppu = talaiccuṟṟu.
cuṟṟum poruḷai nōkkik kiṟukiṟuveṉṟu cuṟṟukiṟatu eṉpataiyum, kiṟukiṟu vāṇam kiṉṉaṟu vāṇam eṉṟu collik koṇṭu ciṟuvar cuṟṟiyāṭi viḷaiyāṭuvataiyum, talai valikkumpōtu kiṟukiṟu veṉṟu varukiṟatu eṉṟu col vataiyum nōkkuka.
kiṟu - kiṟukku = talaiccuṟṟu, mūḷaikkōḷāṟu, paittiyam. oruvar nīṇṭanēram cuṟṟiyāṭuvatāl talaimayakkam uṇṭātal kāṇka.
kiṟukiṟuttal = talai cuṟṟutal, mayakkamātal.
talaicuḻaṉṟu kiṟukiṟuttu  (pirapōta. 18, 54)

kuḷ - koḷ - koṭpu = cuḻaṟci. koḷ - kōḷ = katiravaṉaic cuṟṟum viṇmīṉ.

vii. alaital
oru poruḷ cuṟṟumpōtum cuḻalumpōtum pala iṭattaic cārtalāl, cuṟṟutalaiyum cuḻalutalaiyuṅ kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, aṅkumiṅkum alaitalaik kuṟikkum.
