(6) uruṭcit tuṟai
uruṭci eṉpatu kaṉavaṭṭam. parappu maṭṭum uḷḷatu vaṭṭam, parappum kaṉamum uḷḷatu kaṉavaṭṭam.
kuṇṭuruṭci nīḷuruṭci eṉa uruṭci iruvakaippaṭum.
i. uruḷtal
uruḷtalāvatu, uruṭcip poruḷkaḷ puraṇṭu puraṇṭōṭutal.
ul - al - alai. alaital = uruḷtal, alaittal = uruṭṭutal.
tēṟal kallalait toḻukum  (puṟam. 115)
alai = uruḷum tirai.
uruḷ - uruḷi = uruḷum cakkaram.
uruḷ - uruṭci. uruḷ - uruṭṭu - uruṭṭal. uruṭṭu - uruṭṭi.
ii. kuṇṭuruṭci (uruṇṭai)
pantupōṉṟatu kuṇṭuruṭciyākum.
ul - ula - ulaku = uruṇṭaiyāṉa ñālam, atu pōṉṟa kōḷ.
ulaku - ulakam.
uruḷ - uruṇṭai - uṇṭai.
kuḷ - kuḷikai = uruṇṭa māttirai.
kuḷi - kuḷiyam = uruṇṭai, maruntu (māttirai).
kuḷiyam - kuḻiyam = vācaṉaiyuruṇṭai.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = uruṇṭai.
kul - kōl - kōli = uruṇṭai, ciṟṟuruṇṭai.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam = uruṇṭai.
kōḷ - kōḷā = uruṇṭaikkaṟi.
cuḷ - cuḻi - cuḻiyam = uruṇṭaip palakāram.
puḷ - (poṇṭu) - pōṇṭā = uruṇṭaip palakāram.
muḷ - muḻi - viḻi = kaṇṇiṉ karuviḻi, viḻi.
mūkkum muḻiyum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
muḷ - (muṭam) - muṭal - muṭalai = uruṇṭai.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇai = uruṇṭai.
(vaṭam) - vaṭakam = tāḷikkum uruṇṭai.
iii. uruṇṭa tiraṭci
uruṇṭa tiraṭciyāvatu kuṇṭāyiruntum muḻuvuruṇṭai yallātatu.
ul - ulam = uruṇṭa tiraṭci, uruṇṭa kal.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭatu.
kuṇṭukkal, kuṇṭuk kōtumai, kuṇṭuc campā, kuṇṭumaṇi, kuṇṭu mallikai, kuṇṭūci mutaliya peyarkaḷ, uruṇṭa tiraṭcip poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikkum.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kuṇṭu - kaṇṭu = nūṟpantu.
kuṇṭu - kuṭṭu = muṭṭai (te.)
kuṭam = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kaṭṭai allatu kalam.
kum - kumpu - kumpam = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kumpaccurai = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa curai.
kumpu - kumpā = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam - kōcam = muṭṭai.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṭṭai = uruṇṭai talaiyaṇai. 
cul - cūl = muṭṭai.
ñamali tanta maṉavuccū luṭumpiṉ  (perumpāṇ. 132).
cul - cuṉ - (cuṉai) - ciṉai = muṭṭai.
cuḷ - (coḷ) - cōḷam = uruṇṭa kūlam.
muḷ - muṭṭai.
muḷ - muṭṭu - muttu = uruṇṭa kal allatu vitai.
āmaṇakku muttu, kurukku muttu, puḷiya muttu, vēppamuttu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ - maṇi = uruṇṭa kūlam allatu vitai, uruṇṭa pāci.
neṉmaṇi, kuṉṟimaṇi mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.

iv. nīḷuruṭci
uruḷai pōṉṟatu nīḷuruṭciyām.
ul - ulam - ulakkai.
uruḷ - uruḷai - uruṭai (-rōtai).
muṉpu kūṭaliyal - tiraḷal tuṟaiyil kuṟikkap peṟṟa ulakkai kuṇṭalam mutaliya orucila coṟkaḷ tiraṭcikkarut toṭu uruṭcik karuttum koṇṭavaiyeṉa aṟika.

(9) ūkāraccuṭṭu 
tuḷaittal
oṉṟai valitāyt toṭṭaporuḷ kūrnuṉiyuḷḷa tāyirup piṉ, atu vaḷaiyāviṭattu ataṉāl muṭṭappaṭṭa poruḷait tuḷaikkum.
vēr maṇṇaiyum, ūci tuṇiyaiyum, pūcci ēṭṭaiyum, āṇi marattaiyum, ampu uṭampaiyum, kampi nilattaiyum, piṟa piṟavaṟṟaiyum tuḷaikkum.
oru poruḷai viṉaimutal (karuttā) tuḷaippilum, viṉai mutalāṟ kaiyāḷap peṟum karuvi tuḷaippiṉum, collākka neṟikku iraṇṭum oṉṟē.
valitāyt toṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, kūr nuṉiyuḷḷavai caṟṟu uḷḷiṟaṅku mātalāl, ataṉaiyē tuḷaittaliṉ tuvakka nilaiyākak koḷḷalvēṇṭum. kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷait tuḷaippaṉavellām kūrnuṉi yuḷḷaṉavākavē irukkum.
(1) kuḻittal tuṟai
kuḻittalāvatu paḷḷam uṇṭupaṇṇutal.
i. kuttal
kuttal eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uḷḷiṟaṅkumāṟu kūrpporuḷāṟ kuttutal. kuttu - kutti - katti.
toṭutaliyaliṟ kūṟiya kuttal kuttit toṭutal eṉṟum, iṅkuk kūṟiyatu kuttit tuḷaittal eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika.
(ur) - ar - ara - araṅku. araṅkutal = ampu mutaliyaṉa taittal.
kuttu - kutti = kuttuṅ karuvi, koḻuvoṭu cērnta kalappaip pakuti.
kuttu - kottu - koṭṭu. kaḷaikkottukaḷaik koṭṭu mutaliya peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
(tuḷ) - (taḷ) - (tay) - tai. muḷ taittal = muḷ uḷḷiṟaṅkutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = uḷiyāṟ kottutal.
poḷ - po. pottal = kuttit tuḷaittal.
poḷ - potu. potuttal = muṉ mutaliyaṉa pāytal.
potu - potir. potirttal = kuttutal.
ii. kīṟutal
kīṟutalāvatu kūrṅ karuviyāṟ kutti nīḷa iḻuttal.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḷ. kīḷtal = kiḻittal, piḷattal.
kiḷ - kiḻi, kīḷ - kīḻ, kīḻtal = kiḻittal = piḷattal.
kuḷ - kuṟu - kiṟu - kiṟukku. kiṟukkutal = kīṟivaraital, kōṭu kīṟutal.
kiṟu - kīṟu - kīṟal.
kīṟu - kīṟṟu = kīṟiyavarai, kīṟiya tuṇṭu, tuṇṭu, taṭṭit tuṇṭu.
kīṟṟu - kīccu. kīccutal = kōṭu kiḻittal.
kuḷ - (kuṟu) - kūṟu = pakuti. tuṇṭu.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi - poḷittal = kiḻittal.
puḷ - (puṟu) - pūṟu - pīṟu. pīṟutal = nakattāṟ kīṟutal.
iii. poṟittal
poṟittalāvatu, kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷiṟ kuḻitteḻutul.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻittal = kuḻitteḻututal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = veṭṭutal, eḻuttu veṭṭutal.
poḷi - poṟi - poṟittal = kuḻit teḻututal.
puḷ - piḷ - viḷ - veṭṭu.
iv. kiṇṭutal
kiṇṭutalāvatu poruḷkaḷaik kiḷaittal allatu kuṭaital.
uḷartal = talaimayiraik kiṇṭutal, arumpaik kuṭaital.
uḻutal = kiṇṭutal, mayiraik kōtutal.
kuṭaital = kiṇṭutal. kuṭai - kaṭai.
neykuṭai tayiriṉ  (paripā. 16, 3)
kaṭai - kaṭalai = kaṭaiyappaṭum payaṟu.
kaṭai - kaṭaiccal.
kuḷ - koḻu - kōḻi = nilattaiyum kuppaiyaiyum kiḷaikkum paṟavai. koḻu = nilattai uḻum kāṟu.
koḻu - koḻutu. koḻututal = mayir kuṭaital.
koḻutu - kōtu.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiḷar - kiḷaṟu - kiḷāṟu. kiḷāṟutal = kiṇṭutal.
kiḷ - kiḷai. kiḷaittal = kiṇṭutal, kiṇṭiyatu pōṟ pirital.


kiḷ - kēḷ - kēḷvi. kēṭṭal = kiṇṭutalpōl viṉavutal, viṉaviyataṟku oruvar viṭukkum viṭaiyaic cevikoṭuttal, cevimaṭuttal.
kiḷ - kiṇṭu - keṇṭu. keṇṭutal = kiḷaittal. tōṇṭutal.
keṇṭu - keṇṭi = nīruṇkalattiṉ kiḷai pōṉṟa uṟiñci, atu uḷḷa kalam.
kiḷ - keḷ - kēḷ - kēḻal = nilattaik kiṇṭum paṉṟi, atai yotta yāṉai.
tul = tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = uḻutal. kiṇṭutal, tuṉṉūci = kalappaik kutti.
tuḷ - tuḻa - tuḻavu. tuḻa - tuḻā - tuḻāvu. tuḻāvutal = kiṇṭutal.
tuḷ - toḷ - toy. toytal = uḻutal.
nuḷ - naḷ - naṇṭu. naṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
naṇṭaṟcōṟu = taippoṅkaliṟ kiṇṭic camaitta cōṟu.
naṇṭal = kiṇṭik kuḻainta cōṟu. naṇṭal kiṇṭip paṭaikkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
naṇṭal piṇṭal = kūḻuṅ kaṭṭiyumāy ākkiya cōṟu. cōṟṟai naṇṭal piṇṭalākki viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
naṇṭu = nilattaik kiṇṭic cellum nīruyiri.
naṇṭu - ñaṇṭu.
nuḷ - neḷ - neṇṭu. neṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
neṇṭu = naṇṭu. neṇṭu - ñeṇṭu = naṇṭu. ñeṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭutal = paṉṟi mukattāṟ kiḷaittal.

7. aṟuttal
aṟuttalāvatu, vāḷāl nīṭṭuvākil palamuṟai tuḷaittuk kīṟutal allatu tuḷaittut tuṇittal.
(ur) - ar - araṅku. araṅkutal = ampu mutaliyaṉa taittal. (tuḷaittu aṟuttal)
araṅku - arakka. arakkutal = kiḷaitaṟittal, veṭṭutal.
tārun tōḷumarakki  (vināyaka. pu. 42, 4).
araṅku = aṟukkappaṭṭa aṟai. vaṭṭāṭṭiṟkum cūtāṭṭiṟkum vakutta kaṭṭam, naṭattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum vakutta nilam allatu mēṭai.

araṅkiṉṟi vāṭṭāṭi yaṟṟē nirampiya
nūliṉṟik kōṭṭi koḷal  (kuṟaḷ. 401).
araṅkēṟṟam = mēṭaiyēṟik kalvittiṟamum kalaittiṟamum kāṭṭal.
oru vīṭu pala aṟaikaḷ koṇṭiruppatai araṅkaraṅkāyirukkiṟatu eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭakavaraṅku, cūtāṭumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, nīrāl aṟukkappaṭṭa āṟṟiṭaikkuṟai, kāvirikkum koḷḷi ṭattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa āṟṟiṭaik kuṟaiyaic cērnta tiruvaraṅkam.
araṅkam - araṅkaṉ = tiruvaraṅkattil paḷḷikoṇṭirukkum tirumāl.
araṅkam tiruvaraṅkam araṅkaṉ eṉpaṉavē toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum vaṭivaṅkaḷ. raṅkam srīraṅkam raṅkaṉ eṉpaṉa iṭaikkālattil mutaṟkuṟaiyākat tirinta vaṭa coṟkaḷākum.
araṅku allatu araṅkam eṉṉum coṟku aṟukkappaṭṭatu eṉpatē mūlapporuḷ; ar(ur) eṉpatē mūlam. raṅkam eṉṉum talaiyaṟṟa coṟku mūlamumillai; mūlap poruḷu millai.
ar - aṟu - aṟuppu.
aṟu - aṟuvu - aṟuvaṭai.
aṟu - aṟuvai - aṟukkappaṭum tuṇi, tuṇi.
aṟu - aṟai = tuṇṭam, kaṭṭiṭappakuti, aṟukkappaṭṭa allatu vakukkappaṭṭa iṭam, araṅku, cūtāṭum kaṭṭam, pātti.
araṅku aṟai eṉṉum iru coṟkaṭkum uriya poruḷkaḷ ēṟattāḻa oṉṟāyiruppatālum, av iru coṟkaṭkum ar eṉpatē mūlamātalālum, aṟaiyeṉṉum col taṉittamiḻc col eṉpatu veḷḷiṭaimalai yātalālum, araṅku allatu araṅkam eṉṉum collum teṉ collēyeṉṟu teḷika.
vi. piḷattal
piḷattalāvatu oru poruḷai orē muṟaiyil veṭṭit tiṟattal allatu tuṇittal. tāṉāyt tiṟattalum piḷattalē.
uṭai - uṭaippu, kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇi - kaṇicci = piḷakkuṅ kōṭari.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiṟu - kīṟu - kīṟal.
tuḷ - tuṟa - tuṟappu = tiṟattal.
tuṟa - tuṟavu = tiṟavu. tuṟa - tiṟa - tiṟavu.
tiṟa - tiṟappu - tuṟappuk kuccu = tiṟavukōl.
tuṟa - tuṟavai = tiṟanta veḷiyiṭam.
maṇṭai piḷattalai maṇṭai tiṟattal eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakku.
tiṟa - teḷi. teṟittal = piḷattal, uṭaital.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = veṭṭutal, piḷattal.
poḷ - pōḻ. pōḻtal = piḷattal. pōḻ = tuṇṭu.
puḷ - piḷ - piṭu. piṭutal = kaiyāṟ piḷattal.
piḷ - piḷavu = tuṇṭu.
piṭu - pitu - pitir. pitirtal = piṇṭu utirtal.
piḷ - viḷ - viṭu. viṭutal = piḷattal. viḷḷutal = piḷattal.
vii. veṭittal
veṭikkum poruḷ oṉṟaip piḷantukoṇṭu veḷivaru vatāl, piḷattaṟkaruttil veṭittaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
puḷ - piḷ - piṭu - piṭuṅku - piṭāṅku = piṭuṅkum vēṭṭu.
piṭāṅku - pīraṅki.
aṭaittiruntatu tiṭumeṉat tāṉē tiṟantuviṭṭāl, piṭuṅkiviṭṭateṉpar. piṭāṅku vēṭṭaip pīraṅki eṉpatu tañcai vaḻakku.
piḷ - viḷ - viṭu - veṭu - veṭi.
viḷ - (veḷ) - veṭṭu - vēṭṭu.
viii. virital
piḷanta poruḷ viriyum.
noḷ - noku - nokku = veṭippu.
kum - kam - kamar = veṭippu.
puḷ - piḷ - viḷ.
viḷḷutal = piḷattal, virital.
viḷ - viḷa - viḷavu. viḷavutal = kamarātal.
viḷ - viri - virivu. virital = piḷattal, veṭittal. pittac cūṭṭiṉāṟ pātattil veṭikkum veṭippaip pitta virivu eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
viri - viral = kaiyiṉiṉṟu virintatu.
viḷ - viru - viruvu = nilacciṟu veṭippu.
viḷ - viṭu - viṭar = malai veṭippu, kukai.
viṭutal = piḷattal, virital. viṭu - viṭavu = viṭar.
viḷ - (viy) - viyam - viyaṉ - viyal = virivu, akaṟci, parappu.
viyal - (viyalaṉ) - viyāḻaṉ = katiravaṉaic cuṟṟum kōḷkaḷellā vaṟṟilum periyatu.
viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē  (tol. uriyiyal, 66.)
viḷ - veḷ - veṭi - veṭippu. veṭittal = piḷattal.
ix. vāy virital
piḷ - piḷā - piḻā = vāyakaṉṟa ōlaik koṭṭāṉ.
piḻā - piṭā = vāyakaṉṟa nārppeṭṭi.
piṭā - piṭaku - piṭakam = taṭṭu, peṭṭi.
piḷ - (peḷ) - peṭṭi = ōlai nār maram mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vāyakaṉṟa ēṉam.
peṭṭi - peṭṭakam.
(peḷ) - pēḷ - pēḻ = piḷavu, virivu, akaṟci, pēḻvāy =akaṉṟa vāy.
pēḻ - pēḻai = vāyakaṉṟa ceppu allatu marakkalam.
x. pirital
piḷanta poruḷ vēṟākap piriyum.
pul - pil (vil) - vilaku - vilakku - vilakkam.
vilaku - vilaṅku. vilaṅkutal - vilakutal.
puḷ - puy. puyttal = pirittal, piṭuṅkutal, puy - piy.
piy - (pey) - peyar. peyartal = pirital, vilakutal. peyar - pēr.
puḷ - piḷ - piri - pirivu - piriviṉai.
piḷ - piṭu - piṭuṅku. piṭuṅkutal = peyarttal, paṟittal.
piṭu - (pitu) - pituṅku - pitukku - pitukkam.
pituṅkutal = peyartal, peyarttu veḷivarutal.
(pitu) - pitir. pitirtal = peyartal, pituṅkutal.
piḷ - viḷ. viḷḷutal = vilakutal.
viḷ - viṭu - vīṭu = viṭutal, nīṅkutal, celavu viṭṭirukkum iṭam (illam), paṟṟuviṭukai, pēriṉpa nilaiyam.
viṭu - viṭuti. viṭu - viṭutalai.
viṭutal = pirital, nīṅkutal, viṭṭicaittal = pirintolittal.
xi. viṭuttal
viṭuttalāvatu cikkalāṉa poruḷai viṭuvittal.
puḷ - piḷ - piy - (piyi) - pici = piyppatupōl viṭukkum viṭukatai.
piḷ - piṭu - pitu - pitir = viṭukatai. pitir - putir.
piḷ - viḷ. viḷḷutal = pitir viṭuttal. viḷ - viṭu viṭuttal = piṭṭuc collutal, viṭuvittal. viṭu - viṭai = pitirpōṉṟa aṟiyā viṉāvai viṭuvittalpōṟ kūṟum maṟumoḻi.
xii. cēy pirital
piḷ - piṟa - piṟappu. piṟa - piṟavi. piṟa - piṟantai = piṟavī,
iṉam (genus).
tāyum piḷḷaiyum vēṟuvēṟu pirintuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu pēṟukālattiṟ peṇṭirāṟ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. tāy vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu cēy piritalē piṟattalām.
xiii. malam pirital
(pul) - (pil) - pēl.
puḷ - puḻukkai - piḻukkai.
(puḷ) - piḷ - (piy) - pī.
piḷ - pīḷ - pīḷai - pūḷai = kaṇ malam.
piḷ - (viḷ) - viṭṭu - viṭṭai.
xiv. nīrpporuḷ pirital
(pul) - pil - piliṟṟu. piliṟṟutal = cintutal, pīccutal.
pil - pīl - pīr = oru pīccu.
pīl - pīṟṟu - pīccu. o-nō: kīṟṟu - kīccu.
piḷ - piḻi - piḻiyal.
piḷ - piy - pey - peyal.
xv. pakutal
poruḷkaḷ pala pakutikaḷāyp piritalē pakutalām.
puḷ - (paḷ) - (paḻu) - paku. o. nō. toḻu - toku.
pakutal = pirital. pakkicaittal = pirittolittal.
paku - pakuti = pirivu. kūṟu, kaṇṭumutalil āṟiloru kūṟāṉa araciṟai, pakucolluṟuppāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāṉa mutaṉilai.
pakuti vikuti eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷum, prakruti vikruti eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷum, vevvēṟām. pakuti = kūṟu. vikuti = īṟu. vikutal = muṭital. prakruti = iyaṟkai (muṉ ceyyappaṭṭatu), iyalpu. vikruti = vikāram. pakutiyum tiripai (vikārattai) aṭaiyak kūṭumātalālum, canticāriyai iṭainilai eṉpavum tiripai yuṇṭu paṇṇutalālum, vikutiyeṉṉum uṟuppiṟkut tiripu eṉṉum poruḷ poruntātu. pakuti vikutikaḷ muṟaiyē mutaṉilai iṟutinilaiyeṉṟu peyar peṟutalālum, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟu īṟu eṉpaṉavē coṟporuḷ eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. kaḷattil mutalāvatu aracaṉukkuc celuttappaṭum pakuti ciṟappākap pakuti eṉṟē peyar peṟṟatu pōl, pakucolluṟuppukkaḷuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉatum pakuti yeṉṟē peyar peṟṟa teṉka. ākavē, pakuti vikutiyeṉpaṉa  prakruti vikruti eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripallaveṉṟu teḷintu koḷka. pakuti vikutiyeṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē prakruti vikruti eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷait tirittuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkup poruntap puḷukalāka vēṟu poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar vaṭavar eṉka.
paku - pakam = pakuti, āṟu eṉṉum tokai.
vēḷāḷaṉviḷaivu, aracaṉ teṉpulattār teyvam viruntu okkal tāṉ eṉṉum aṟuvarkkup pakirntaḷikkappaṭṭata ṉālum, pakucol pakuti vikuti canti cāriyai iṭainilai vēṟupāṭu (vikāram) eṉṉum āṟuṟuppukkaḷāyp pakukkap paṭṭataṉālumē, pakam eṉṉum coṟku āṟu eṉṉum tokaipporuḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
paku - pakam - pakavaṉ = pakuttaḷittuk kāppavaṉ, palarkkum paṭiyaḷappavaṉ, āṇṭavaṉ.
vēḷāḷaṉ aṟu cārārkkam, aracaṉ araciyal viṉaiñarkkum, āṇṭai aṭimaiyarkkum, paṭiyaḷappatu pōla; āṇṭavaṉum palarkkum uṇavaip pakuttaḷittuk kāppavaṉ eṉṉum karuttil, avaṉaip pakavaṉ eṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum āṇṭavaṉaip paṭiyaḷakkiṟavaṉ eṉṟu potumakkaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. Lord eṉṉum āṅkilac collum itē karuttup paṟṟiyeḻun tiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
E. Lord. O.E. hlaford (loafward) = bread. keeper.
iṉi, avaravarkkuriya naṉmai tīmaikaḷai vakuppavaṉ eṉṟumām.
pālvarai teyvam  (col. 53)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatai nōkkuka.
iṉi, paku - pakavu - pakavaṉ eṉṟumām, eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum poruḷ oṉṟē.
paku - pakuppu. paku - pakal - pāl. paku - pakai.
paku - pakku - paṅku. pakku - pakkam - pakkal.
paku - pakir - pakirvu. paku - pakar. pakartal = poruḷkaḷaip pakuttu vilai kūṟutal.
paku - pāku = pakuti, pakkam, pakkamāyiruntu pēṇupavaṉ (pākaṉ).
pāku - pākaṉ. pāku - pākam. pāku - pākai.
pāku - pākku = pakuti, kūṟu. pākku - pākkiyam = kūṟu, naṟkūṟu, pēṟu.
alareḻa āruyir niṟkum ataṉaip 
palaraṟiyār pākkiyat tāl.  (kuṟaḷ. 1141)
pālvāykkuḻavi eṉṉum cilappatikārat toṭarukku (15:23). mēṟpeṟakkaṭava nalla pakutiyait taṉṉiṭattē yuṭaiya kuḻavi eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟutal kāṇka.
pākku eṉṉum collai bhag eṉa eṭuttolippataṉālēyē atu vaṭacoṟpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. iyam eṉpatu ōr īṟu. o. nō. kaṇ - kaṇṇiyam, paṇ - paṇṇiyam.
pākku - pākkam = pakkam, pakkamāṉa iṭam, paṭṭiṉap pakuti, pākkam - vākkam.
pākku - pāṅku - pāṅkar. pāṅku - pāṅkaṉ.
paku - pā - pātti. pā - pātu. pātiṭu - pātīṭu.
paku - vaku - vakuti. vaku - vakuppu.
vaku - vakai. vaku - vakuntu = vakai, vakutta vaḻi. vaḻivakuttal, vaḻivakai eṉpaṉa marapu.
vaku - vakku = vakai, vaḻi.
vaku - vakir - vakiṭu = vakirnta ucci, uccikkōṭu.
vaku - vāku - vākku = pākkam, ticai.
iṭakkai vākku, kāṟṟuvākku mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷaik kāṇka.
paku eṉṉum col teṉcol eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ :
(1) vaṭamoḻiyil paku eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭap paṭum bhaj eṉpataṟku āṇivērēṉum varalāṟēṉum illai.
atōṭu bhaj (to divide) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭatāka bhanj (to break) eṉṟoru mūlam kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmaiyil iraṇṭum oṉṟē. tamiḻp pakutiyiṉ tirinta vaṭivukaḷē vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamākak kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. pakutikkum, muntiyatu mūlam allatu vēr.
(2) Bhaj eṉṉum mūlattiṉ tirivukaḷāka, bhakti, bhaga, bhagavan, bhaga, bhagya mutaliya cila coṟkaḷē vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō nāṟpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa coṟkaḷ paku eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷaṉa.
(3) pakuti pakkam pākam mutaliya pala coṟkaḷum paku eṉṉum orē pakutiyiṉiṉṟu tirintirukkavum, avaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷāṉa prakruti pakṣam bhaga mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ vevvēṟeḻuttuk kaḷaik koṇṭaṉavāy vevvēṟu mūlattaṉavākak kāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
xvi. tōṇṭutal
tōṇṭutalāvatu oru poruḷiṉ parappu paḷḷamākak kuṭaiyap paṭal. uḷ - aḷ - (aḻu) - aku - akaḻ - akaḻi.
kul - kal. kallutal = tōṇṭutal. kal - kalam = tōṇṭappaṭṭa ēṉam. kal - kaṉ, kaṉṉutal = tōṇṭutal. kaṉ - kaṉṉam = tōṇṭutal. tuḷaittal, tuḷai, tuḷaiyāṉa kātu. kaṉṉakkōl = tōṇṭuṅ karuvi.
kaṉ - kaṉi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa curaṅkam.
kul - kil. kil - kīḷ = kīḻ. kīḻtal = tōṇṭutal.
kuḷ - kuḻai - kuṭai. kuṭaital = tōṇṭutal.
kuḷ - (kuy) - kuyil. kuyilutal = tōṇṭutal. kuyil - kuyiluvam = marattaik kuṭaintu ceyyappaṭṭa icaik karuvi.
cul - cūl. cūlutal = tōṇṭutal.
tuḷ - (tūḷ) - tūṇi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa kalam, ōraḷavu. 
tuḷ - turu - tūri - tūriyam = kuṭaintu ceyyappaṭṭa icaikkaruvi.
tuḷ - toḷ - toṭu. toṭutal = tōṇṭutal.
toḷ - tōḷ.
kēṭpiṉuṅ kēḷāt takaiyavē kēḷviyāl
tōṭkap paṭāta cevi. (kuṟaḷ. 418)
tōḷ - tōṇṭu. tōṇṭu - tōṇṭi.
tōḷ - tōṇ - tōṇi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa marakkalam.
nuḷ - noḷ - nōḷ - nōṇṭu. nōṇṭutal = tōṇṭutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poy. poytal = tuḷaittal, tōṇṭutal.
xvii. tēṭutal
nilattiṟkuḷḷirukkuṅ kiḻaṅkaiyum, eṟumpukaḷ cērttu vaittirukkum aṭippullaiyum, poṉṉaiyum, putaiyalaiyum, makkaḷ tōṇṭiyeṭuppa taṉāl,tōṇṭaṟ karuttil tōṇṭu taṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. kiṇṭutalum tōṇṭutaliṉ pāṟpaṭum,
tuḷ - tuḻa - tuḻavu - tuḻāvu. tuḻāvutal = tēṭutal.
tuḷ - tura - turappu.turapputal = tuḷaittal, tēṭutal.
tura - tira - tirakku. tirakkutal = tēṭutal.
tuḷ - toḷ - tōḷ - tōṇṭu - tēṇṭu - tēṭu - tēṭṭu - tēṭṭam.
nuḷ - noḷ - nōḷ - nōṇṭu - nēṇṭu - nēṭu. nēṭutal = tēṭutal.
nōṇṭu - nōṭu. nōṭutal = tēṭutal, āytal, ārāytal, pārttal.
nōṭu - nōṭṭam = āyvu, ārāycci.
nōṭu - nāṭu, nāṭutal = tēṭutal, virumputal, āytal.
nāṭu - nāṭṭam = tēṭṭam, viruppam, tēṭum pārvai, pārvai, kaṇ.
nāṭṭa miraṇṭum matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yuraikkuṅ kuṟippurai yākum  (tol. 1042)
xviii. paḷḷamuṅ kuḻiyum
tōṇṭiya iṭam paḷḷamuṅ kuḻiyumākum. avai iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyum eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. paḷḷattiṉum āḻntatu kuḻi.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻivu. kuḷ - kuṇṭu. kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyum eṉpatu vaḻakku. kuḻi - kuḻici - kuḻinta pāṉai.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭam = paḷḷam, āḻam.
kuḷ - (kaḷ) = (kay) - kayam = paḷḷam.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḷ - kīḻ = paḷḷam. kīḻ - (kiḻaṅku) - kiṭaṅku.
cuḻittal = kaṇ kuḻital.
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷḷal = kaṭkuḻivu. noḷ - noḷḷai.
noḷ - neḷ - ñeḷ - ñeḷḷal = paḷḷam.
noḷ - noṭi = paḷḷam.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷam. paḷ - paḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa maruta nilattil vāḻpavaṉ. uḻavaṉ.
paḷ - (pay) - payam - payampu = paḷḷam.
paḷ - paṇ - paṇṇai = paḷḷam, kuḻi, neṟkuttum paḷḷam, vayal.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭukar = paḷḷam, paḷḷamāṉa vayal, maruta nilam.
puḷ - poḷ - poy = kuḻi.
muḷ - maḷ - maṭu = paḷḷam.
maḷ - maḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa marutanilattil vāḻpavaṉ, uḻavaṉ.
paḷḷaṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkum maḷḷaṉ eṉpatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkumākum.
maḷḷa ruḻupaka ṭurappuvār  (kamparā. nāṭ. 18).
xix. nīrnilai
paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil nīr taṅkuvataṉāl, paḷḷattaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, nīrnilaiyaik kuṟittaṉa. nīr nilaiyaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, nīraiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kuḷ - kuḷam.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = ciṟu kuṭṭai.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭai = ciṟu kuḷam.
kuḷ - (kaḷ) - (kay) - kayam = kuḷam.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḻuvai = maṭu.
toḷ - tōḷ - tōy - tōyam = nīr, kaṭal.
puḷ - poḷ - poy - poykai = kuḷam.
puḷ - paḷ - pāḻi = ciṟu kuḷam.
paḷ - paṭu = nīrnilai.
paṭu - paṭukai = nīrnilai, āṟṟōrattu nilam.
paṭukai - paṭukar = nīrnilai.
paḷ(pal) - payam = nīr; payam - payampu = nīr, nīrnilai.
paḷ - paṇ = nīrnilai. paṇ - pāṇi = nīr.
muḷ - maḷ - maṭu = nīrkkiṭaṅku.
xx. tōṇṭappaṭṭa nīrnilai
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiṇaṟu. kiḷ - keḷ - kēḷ - kēṇi.
tuḷ - tura - turavu = kiṇaṟu.
xxi. kīḻmai
paḷḷam nilamaṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻāyiruppatāl, paḷḷattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum collaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum kīḻmaiyaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. kīḻmai nilam (iṭam) paṟṟiyatum nilaiyaip paṟṟiyatum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. nilam paṟṟiyatum, nilattiṟku uṭpaṭṭatum mēṟpaṭṭatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḻ - kīḻmai.
kīḻ - kiḻakku = kīḻ, kīḻiṭam, iḻivu.
kāṇiṟ kiḻakkān talai  (kuṟaḷ. 488)
kiḷaiiya kuralē kiḻakkuvīḻn taṉavē  (kuṟun. 337)
kiḻakkiṭu poruḷō ṭaintu mākum  (tol. 1226)
kuḷ - (kaḷ) - (kay) - kayam - kayamai = kīḻmai.
kīḻ eṉpatu mutalāvatu oṉṟaṉ uṭpakutiyaiyē kuṟittatu. nilattiṟ kuḷḷiruppatu nilattiṉ mēluḷḷa makkaṭku aṭippuṟat tiliruppatāl, kīḻ eṉṉuñ col piṉpu nilattiṟku mēluḷḷa veḷiyiṭattilum aṭitticaiyaik kuṟippatā yiṟṟu.
xxii. tāḻtal
tāḻvu eṉpatu kīḻnilai.
uḷ - (iḷ) - (iḻu) - iru. irutal = tāḻtal. tāḻntu viḻutal.
ikuttal = tāḻtal.
māriyi ṉikutaru villumiḻ kaṭuṅkaṇai  (malaipaṭu. 226)
kaṇaṅko ṭōkaiyiṟ katuppikut tacaii  (malaipaṭu. 44)
kuḷ - kuṇ - kuṇam = tāḻnta kīḻtticai. kuṇam - kuṇakku = kiḻakku.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḻ = kiḻakku. kīḻ - kīḻkku - kiḻakku.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - taḻu - tāḻ - tāḻvu; tāḻ - tāḻmai.
tāḻ - tāḻppu = tāḻttal, kālan tāḻttal, tāḻ - tāḻam.
taḻu - taku - takku = tāḻvu. tāḻkural toṇṭaiyait takkut toṇṭai eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
takku - takkaṇam = tāḻnta teṉ ticai.
teṟkilirunta kumari malaiyum nilamum muḻuki, vaṭakkil kaṭalirunta iṭattil paṉimalai yeḻuntatāl, teṉticai tāḻntu vaṭaticai uyarntatu. itaṉāl, teṉticai takkaṇam eṉṟum vaṭaticai uttaram eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. takkaṇam tāḻvu; uttaram uyarvu. tamiḻakap parappiṉpaṭi, nilamaṭṭattil tāḻntatum uyarntatumāṉa kīḻtticai mēṟṟicaikaḷum, muṟaiyē, kīḻmēl allatu kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṟum, kuṇam kuṭam allatu kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉṟum kūṟuppaṭutal kāṇka.
taṉ - taṇ - taṇa. taṇattal = tāḻttal, kālantāḻttal.
taḷ - taṇ - taṇi - taṇivu. tāḻttal, kālan tāḻttal.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷai - kīḻmaṭṭamāṉa (kuṭṭaiyāṉa) āṭu.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭai = kīḻmaṭṭam.
paṭai - pāṭai = kīḻmaṭṭamāṉa (tāḻvāṉa) kaṭṭil.

paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = tāḻvāṉa kūraiyuḷḷa kuṭiyiruppu allatu pācaṟai.
paḷ - paṇ - pāṇi. pāṇittal = tāḻttal.
xxiii. taṭpam
kuḷircci cūṭṭait taṇippatāl taṇittaṟ karuttil kuḷirccik karuttup piṟantatu. veppa nāṭṭil cūṭu taṇivu mika mutaṉmai yākak karutappaṭum.
tuḷ - taḷ - taṇ - taṇam, taṇ - taṇmai.
taṇ - taṭpu - taṭpam.
taṇ - taṭu = taṭumam = kuḷircci, nīrkkōvai.
xxiv. kīḻātal
kīḻātalāvatu aṭitticaiyaṭaital. viḻutalum, paṭuttalum, nilattiṟ kiṭattalum, kīḻātalām.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iru. iruttal = kīḻamartal, amartal, (iḷ) - iṭu. iṭutal = kīḻiṭutal, koṭuttal. iṭu - iṭam.
ul - (il) - (īl) - īṉ - īṉutal = kīḻiṭutal, peṟutal. 
maṇaṟkīṉṟa veṇmuttam
īṉ - ī. ītal = iṭutal.
kuḷ - (kiḷ) - kiṭa - kiṭakkai. kiṭattal = nilattiliruttal, paṭuttiruttal.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷi - paṭukkai, paṭukkaiyaṟai, paṭukkum vīṭu, vīṭu, kōyil, iṭam, vīṭukaḷ cērnta ūr.
paḷḷi koḷḷutal = paṭuttal. paḷḷiyeḻucci = paṭukkai viṭṭeḻun tiruttal. makkaḷ perumpālum vīṭṭiṟku veḷiyē vēlai ceytalālum, iraviṟ paṭuttaṟkē vīṭṭait tēṭutalālum, paṭukkaiyaik kuṟikkum paḷḷiyeṉṉum col, vīṭṭaiyum pēriṉpa vīṭākiya kōyilaiyum, aṭiyār paḷḷikoḷḷum maṭattaiyum, vīṭukaḷ cērnta ūraiyum, kuṟitta teṉṟaṟika.
paḷ - paṭu. paṭuttal= kīḻātal, viḻutal, kiṭattal, tūṅkutal, paṭutal = viḻutal, pōrkkaḷattil viḻuntiṟattal, iṟattal.
paṭu - paṭukkai.
paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = viḻutal.
niṉṟāṉ iruntāṉ kiṭantāṉ taṉ kēḷalaṟac 
ceṉṟāṉ eṉappaṭuta lāl

eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷāl, iruttal eṉpatu kīḻamar talaik kuṟittal peṟappaṭum.
xxv. iṟaṅkutal
iṟaṅkutalāvatu, mēṟṟicaip poruḷ kīḻtticaiyaṭaital. av iṟakkam uḷḷiṭattatum veḷiyiṭattatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. muṉ kālil taippatu uḷḷiṟakkamum, paṟavai viṇṇiliruntu maṇṇiṟku varuvatu veḷiyiṟakkamum ākum.
uḷ - iḷ - iḷi - iḻi - iḻivu. iḻital = iṟaṅkutal.
iḷ - (iṟa) - iṟaṅku - iṟakku - iṟakkam.
kum - kumuṅku. kumuṅkutal = iṟaṅkutal.
puḷ - puḻu - puku. pukutal = iṟaṅkutal.
iḷi, iḻi, iṟa, iṟaṅku mutaliya coṟkaḷ ukara mutalavāy mūlavaṭi viliruppiṉ vēṟu poruṉpaṭumātaliṉ, ammayakkattai nīkkutaṟkē ikara mutalavāyiṉa veṉṟaṟika. collākka neṟimuṟaikaḷuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉavaṟṟuḷ immayakkoḻippum oṉṟākum.
xxvi. paḻittal
paḻittalāvatu, matippil iṟakkutal. oruvaraip paḻittal, mēliruntu kīḻum mēṭṭiliruntu paḷḷattiṟkum iṟakkutal pōṉṟatām.
uḷ - (iḷa) - iḻu - iku - ikaḻ - ikaḻcci.
iḷ - iḷi - iḻi - iḻippu. iḷi - iḷivu.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - taḻu - tāḻ - tāḻcci.
puḷ - paḷ - paḻi - paḻippu.
paḻi - (vaḻi) - (vayi) - (vay) - vai.
vay - vayavu - vacavu. vay - vayai - vacai.
xxvii. patital
oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟiṟ paṭitalē patitalām.
paṭital = viḻutal, patital, uṟuttutal = patittal.
paṭi - pati - pativu. pati - patippu.
patital = paṭital, taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal.
ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruttalaip pativāyiruttal eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
pati = pativāyirukkum iṭam, vīṭu, ūr, nakar.
pati - vati - vaci - vācam. (va).
vatital = taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal.
pati = pātam = nilattiṟ patiyum uṟuppu allatu pākam. patiyum pātam pati pāta mūlam pati pātamūlap paṟṟuṭaiyāṉ eṉṟu iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭi kuṟittu varum kalveṭṭut toṭarkaḷai nōkkuka.
pātam - pātai = pātam paṭṭu uṇṭākum vaḻi.
xxviii. paṇital
paṇitalāvatu oruvarukkuk kīḻppaṭutal allatu kīḻppaṭital.
paḷ - paṇ - paṇi - paṇivu = paṇiviṭai. paṇi = toṇṭu. paṇittal = kīḻppaṭivittal, paṇi ceyvittal, kaṭṭaḷai yiṭutal.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = paṇital. kīḻ + paṭi = kīḻppaṭi.
xxix. puḻai
puḻai yeṉpatu pakkavāṭṭilum oru poruḷilumuḷḷa āḻnta paḷḷam.
kuḷ - kuḻai - kuṭai = puṭai, kavikai. vilāp puṭaiyai vilākkuṭai eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
viritta kavikai puṭaiyuḷḷatāyiruppatāl, kavikai kuṭai yeṉṉappaṭṭatu. kuṭai - kūṭai = kuṭaiyuḷḷa olai nārp peṭṭi.
kuḻai - kukai - kuvai.
pul - (pil) = pilam = nilak kukai.
puḷ - puḻai - puṭai - puṭaṅku.
puḷ - puḻai - puṭai - puṭaṅku.
puḷ - piḷ - viḷ - viṭu - viṭar = kukai.
muḷ - muḻai = kukai. muḻai - muḻaiñcu. muḻai - mukai = kukai.
xxx. paḻaimai
paḻaimai kālattiṉ kīḻnilaiyaip pōṉṟiruttalāl, kīḻmaiyai allatu paḷḷattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ cila paḻaimaiyaik kuṟittaṟ kēṟṟa vāyiṉa.
kīḻ = muṟkālam
kīḻc ceyta tavattāṟ kiḻiyīṭu nērpaṭṭu  (tiruvācakam, 5, 46.)
kīḻkkaṭai = kaṭantupōṉa nāṭkaḷ.
kīḻ - kiḻa = kiḻamai. kiḻa - kiḻavu.
tol - tollai = paḻamai. tol - toṉmai, tol - toṉṟu = paḻaimai.
tol - tōl = paḻaimai paḻamporuḷpaṟṟiya nūl, paḻaimaiyāṉa pukaḻ.
toḷ - toṇṭu = paḻaimai.
puḷ - paḷ - paṇṭu - paḻaimai.
paḷ - paḻa - paḻamai - paḻaimai. paḻaiyaṉ = mutiyaṉ.
xxxi. paḻakutal
oruvaroṭu allatu oru toḻilil palanāṭ paḻakuvatāl, putumai nīṅkip paḻaimai ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam paḻaimai yātalē paḻakutal. oru poruḷaip palanāṭ kaiyāḷutalum paḻakutalē.
paḷ - (pay) - payil - payiṟci.
paḻa - paḻaku - paḻakku - paḻakkam.
paḻaku - paḻaṅku. paḻaṅkutal = oru putupporuḷaip palanāṭ payaṉpaṭuttip paḻaimaiyākkutal, payaṉpaṭuttal.
paḻaṅku - puḻaṅku - puḻakkam = oru poruḷaip palakāṟ payaṉpaṭuttal, ōriṭattiṟ paḻakutal, naṭamāṭṭam. paṇappuḻakkam makkaṭpuḻakkam, eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
xxxii. vaḻaṅkutal
vaḻaṅkutalāvatu nīḷap paḻakutal allatu payaṉpaṭuttal.
paḻa - vaḻa - vaḻamai = vaḻakkam. vaḻa - vaḻappu - vaḻappam = vaḻakkam.
(vaḻa - vāṭu = vaḻakkam). toḻuvāṭu = toḻil vaḻakkam allatu kula vaḻakkam, vaḻakkam.
vāṭu - vāṭukkai - vāṭikkai = vaḻakkam.
vaḻa - vaḻaṅku - vaḻakku - vaḻakkam.
vaḻaṅkutal = paḻakutal, toṭartal, naṭamāṭutal, payaṉpaṭuttal, payaṉpaṭutal.
vaḻakku - vaḻakkāṟu.
xxxiii. vāḻtal
ōriṭattu nīṭittup paḻakutalē vāḻtal.
vaḻa - vāḻ - vāḻkkai. vāḻ - vāḻcci. vāḻ - vāḻvu.
vāḻ - (vāḻakai) - vāṭakai = kuṭikkūli, atai yotta piṟakūli.
akai eṉpatu ōr īṟu.
vāḻ - vāḻttu - vaḻuttu. vaḻuttutal = tutittal. kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttaik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. vaḻuttu - paḻiccu.
(2) tuḷaittal tuṟai
tuḷaittalāvatu oru poruḷait turuvumāṟu kuṭaital.
i. tuḷaittal
uḷ - uḷu = tuḷaikkum puḻu. uḷuttal = puḻu marattait tuḷaittal. uḷu - ucu.
kuḷ - kuḻai - kuṭai. kuṭaital = tuḷaittal.
tuḷ - tuḷai. tuḷ - tura - turattal = tuḷaittal, kuṭaital. tuḷ - toḷ - toḷku, toḷkal = tuḷaittal.
puḷ - puḻu = tuḷaikkum pūcci. puḻuttal = puḻu marattait tuḷaittal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷḷutal = tuḷaittal. poḷital = tuḷaittal. poḷittal = tuḷaittal. poḷ - poy. poytal = tuḷaikkappaṭutal.
poḷ - potu. potuttal = tuḷaittal.
ii. tuḷai
ul - (il) - illi.
(uḷ) - aḷ - aḷai = vaḷai.
uḷ - oḷ - (oṭṭai) - ōṭṭai.
kuḷ - kuḻai = tuḷai. kuḻai - kuṭai - kuṭaivu.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūṇṭu - kūṭu.
cuḷ - cura - curaṅkam = kuṭaipātai, kuṭaivu, kaṉi.
cura - curai = tuḷai.
cuḷ - curu - curuṅkai = kuṭaipātai.
tul - tuṉ = vaḷai. tuṉ - tum - tumpu - tūmpu - tūmpā.
tuḷ - tuḷai. tuḷ - tura - turappu = kuṭaipātai. turappu - turappaṇam = tuḷaikkuṅ karuvi.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḷḷai - toḷai.
toḷ - (toṇṭu) - toṇṭi.
nuḷ - nuḻai - nūḻai = tuḷai, vāyil, palakaṇi, curuṅkai.
nuḷ - nūḻ - nūḻil = tuḷai.

pul - (pullam) - pollam = ōṭṭai, tuḷai.
pul - purai. pul - puṟṟu.
puḷ - puḻu - puḻal. puḷ - puḻai - pūḻai = tuḷai, kaṇavāy.
puḻal - pol.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷḷal = tuḷai. poḷḷai = tuḷai.
iii. tuḷaiyuḷḷa poruṭkaḷ
tuḷai ūṭuruviyatum uruvātatum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. 
kuḻal - kuṭal.
kuḻai - kuḻāy.
kuṭu - kuṭuvai. kuṭu - kuṭukkai. kuṭu - kuṭal - kuṭali, kuṭalai.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūṇṭu - kūṭu.
curai = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa kāy.
uṭṭuḷai muṟṟiya curaiyiṉ kuṭukkaiyiluḷḷatu.
tumpu - tumpi = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa uṟiñciyaik koṇṭa īvakai, uṭṭuḷai yuḷḷa kaiyaiyuṭaiya yāṉai. tumpu - tūmpu = nīrkkuḻāy. tūmpu - tūmpā.
tumpu - tompai = kuntāṇi, paṟai.
nul - (nal) - nalli = mūḷaiyelumpu.
nuḷ - nūḻil = tuḷaiyuḷḷa cekku.
nūḻilāṭṭu = mikunta eḷḷaic cekkilāṭṭuvatu pōl ērāḷamāṉa pēraik kollutal, ataik kūṟum puṟattuṟai.
nuḷ - (nūḷ) - nāḷ - nāḷam = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa taṇṭu, arattak kuḻāy, atupōṉṟa narampu.
nāḷ - nāḷi = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa mūṅkil, mūṅkiṟpaṭi.
nāḷi - nāḻi = paṭi. nāḻi - nāḻikai = nāḻikai vaṭṭil, nāḻikai nēram, aṟai.
uṇṇāḻikai = karuppak kirukam.
nāḷi - nāṭi = arattak kuḻāy, atupōṉṟa narampu.
nāṭi - nāṭā = narampu pōl nīṇṭa paṭṭi.
nāḷam - (naḷam) - naram - narampu.
naram - nār = narampupōṉṟa maraiḻai.
nāḷ - nāṇ - nāṇal = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa taṭṭai.
pul = uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa payirvakai.
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa  (tol. marapiyal, 86)
puḷ - (puḻu) - puḻal - puḻalai - puṭalai.
puḷ - puṭṭi - puṭṭil.
puḷ - poḷ - poy - (pay) - pai.
muḷ - muṭṭi.
iv. patar (uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu)
tuḷaiyuḷḷatu uḷḷīṭaṟṟatām. oru ceytiyiṉ uṇmai ataṟku uḷḷīṭupōṉṟiruppatāl, poyyāṉatu uḷḷīṭaṟṟatākak karutappaṭum.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūṇṭu - kūṭu = patar.
cuḷ - coḷ - coṇṭu = patar miḷakāy. coḷ - coṭṭai - cottai - cūttai = patarāṉa kāykaṉi.
poḷ - poḻu - (poku) - pokku = patar, poy.
pokku - pokkai = pallillā vāy.
poḷ - poy = patar, meyyallātatu.
v. kuṟṟam
tuḷaiyuḷḷa cila poruṭkaḷ kuṟṟamuḷḷaṉavākak karutappaṭu vatāl, tuḷaippeyar kuṟṟattaik kuṟikkum.
uḷ - oḷ - (oṭṭai) - ōṭṭai = kuṟṟam.

nuḷ - (noḷ) - noṭṭai = kuṟṟam.
puḷ - (pura) - purai = kuṟṟam.
vi. uḷḷiṭam
uḷ - uḷḷam - uḷam

akam = uḷ, uḷḷiṭam, maṉam, vīṭu, uḷnāṭākiya marutam, akapporuḷ.
ālaikkarumpi ṉakanāṭaṇaintāṉ  (cīvaka. 1618.)
āṭukaḷaṅ kaṭukku makanāṭṭaiyē  (puṟam. 28.)
akam - akaram = marutanilattūr.
uḷ - uṭu - ūṭu.
aku - akaṇ - akaṇi. akaṇ - akaṭu = uḷ. naṭu vayiṟu.
nuḷ - naḷ - naṭu - naṭuvu - naṭuvaṇ - nāppaṇ.
vii. uḷḷaṟital

tuḷ - tura - turavu. tuḷ - (tuṭpu) - tuppu.
tupputturavu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
viii. oḻukutal
uḷḷiruntu allatu tuḷaiyiṉiṉṟu viḻutalē oḻukutal.
uḷ - oḷ - oḻuku - oḻukku - oḻukkam.
uḷ - uṟu - ūṟu.
kuḷ - kuṟu - kuṟa. kuṟattal = vārttal.
kuṟa - kaṟa - kaṟavai.
cuḷ - (cur) - cura. curattal = oḻukutal.
cura - curai = curakkum pālmaṭi.
cuḷ - (cuṉ) - cuṉai = curakkum nīrnilai.
muḷ - mōḷ. mōḷutal = ciṟunīr viṭutal.
mōḷ - mōṭṭiram - mōttiram - mūttiram.
vaṭamoḻiyil mōḷ eṉṉum pakuti allatu viṉai illai. mūttiram eṉṉum viṉaippeyarē uḷḷatu. viṉaip pakuti yiṉiṉṟu viṉaippeyar amaiyumēyaṉṟi, viṉaippeyariṉiṉṟu viṉaippakuti amaiyātu.

(3) taḷartal tuṟai
oruporuḷ tuḷai viḻuvatāl kaṭṭuviṭṭut taḷarcciyaṭai kiṉṟatu.

ii. kuḻaital
tuḷaiviḻunta poruḷpōl cuṭṭuviṭutal kuḻaital.

aḷ - aḷḷi = veṇṇey.

uḷ - iḷ = iḻutu = ney.
kum - kumai - kumaital = kuḻaital, kuḻaiya vēkutal.
kuḷ - kuḷu - kuḷukuḷu. kuḷ - kuḻai - kuḻaivu.

kuḷ - koḷ - koḷa - koḷakoḷa - koḷa koḷappu.

cuḷ - caḷ - caḷḷal = cēṟu.
caḷ - caḻu - caḻuṅku - caḻukkam = nekiḻcci.
tuḷ - taḷa - taḷar - taḷarcci.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḷku = cēṟu. toḷḷutal = nekiḻtal.

nuḷ - noḷ - noḷa - noḷanoḷa. noḷa noḷattal = nekiḻtal.

noḷ - noḷil = cēṟu.
noḷ - neḷ - neḷu - neḷuneḷu. neḷuneḷuttal = nekiḻtal, kuḻaital.
neḷ - ney. neḷu - neku - nekiḻ.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷapoḷa. poḷapoḷattal = nekiḻtal, oḻukutal.

moḻu - moḻuku - meḻuku = nekiḻnta neyp poruḷ.

xii. nilaitaḷartal (vaṟumaiyaṭaital)

mel - vel. vellutal = melittal, tōṟkaṭittal.

vel - veṟṟi.
metu - mettu. mettutal = melittal, vellutal.

(4) nīḷal tuṟai
nekiḻum poruḷkaḷ nīḷutalāl, nekiḻccik karuttil nīṭcik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ceṅkuttu paṭukkai eṉṉum iruvakai vākilum nīṭci nikaḻum.
i. nīḷutal
nekiḻ - nīḷ - nīḷam. nīḷ - nīṭci.
nīḷ - nīr = nekiḻum (nīḷum) poruḷ.
nīr - nīl = nīlam = kaṭal nīriṉ niṟam.
nīḷ - nīṭṭu = nīṭṭappaṭum olai.
nīṭṭu - nīṭṭam.
nīḷ - nīṭu.
nīḷ - neḷ - neṭu - neṭṭu - neṭṭai.
neṭu - neṭil, neṭṭu - neṭṭam.
neṭṭu = neṭumai, neṭuntūram, neṭṭam = neṭumai, ceṅkuttu.
nīṭci eṉpatu ceṅkuttu vākilum koḷḷappaṭutal.
uruvattāl nīṇṭa uyarmara mellām
paruvattā laṉṟip paḻā
eṉṉum auvaiyār kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
keṭṭu - naṭṭu - naṭṭam;
naṭṭamāy niṟkiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
ii. niṟṟal
niṟṟalāvatu oru poruḷ ceṅkuttāka nīṇṭiruttal.
nīḷ - (niḷ) - nil.
nil - nilai - nilaiyam.
nilai - nilaippu = nilaivaram.
nil - nilavu. nilavutal = nilaittal.
nil - nilu - niluvai.

iii. niṟuttal
niṟpittalum tulaiyai eṭuttu eṭaiyaṟitalum ceṅkuttāka ūṉṟutalum, niṟuttal.
nil - niṟpu - niṟpāṭṭu. nil - nilu - niluttu. nilu - niṟu - niṟuttu - niṟuttam.
niṟu - niṟai. niṟu - niṟuvai.
niṟu - niṟuvu - niṟuvaṉam (tāpaṉam)
neṭu - naṭu. neṭu - neṭṭu - naṭṭu - nāṭṭu.
(5) pukutal tuṟai
pukutalāvatu tuḷaikkuṭ cellutal kūrmaiyāṉa allatu kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷ oṉṟaṟkuḷ putitāyt tuḷaittuc cellutalum pukutalē.
i. uṭpukutal
uḷ - oḷi - (oḷ) - oṭṭu - oṭṭutal = oṉṟait tuḷaikkuṭ pukuttutal.
tuḷ - turu - tūr. tūrtal = pukutal.
pukutalait tūrtal eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku. 
tūr - tūri = mīṉ pukum poṟi.
nuḷ - nuḻu - nuḻuntu - noḻuntu. noḻuntutal = talaiyai uḷ nuḻaittal.
nuḻu - nuḻai.
puḷ - puḻu - puku - pukuti = maṉaivāyil, vaḻi, varuvāy. puku - pukutu.
pukuti - pukuṭi = vāyil.
puku - pukur. pukurtal = pukutal. pukur - pūr. pūrtal = pukutal.
puku - pō - pōku - pōkku. pō - pōtu. 
muḷ - (muḻu) - muḻai.
ii. uṭkoḷḷutal
uṭkoḷḷutalāvatu vāykkuṭ pukuttutal allatu uṇṇutal.
uḷ - uṇ - uṇa - uṇā - uṇavu.
uṇ - ūṇ - ūṭṭu - ūṭṭam.
ūṭṭu - ūṭṭi = ūṭṭappaṭum paṉṟi.
uḷ - uṟi - uṟiñcu (olikkuṟippōṭu kūṭiyatu) uṟiñcutal = nīrai uḷḷiḻuttal.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṇṭi = uṇavu.
cumpu - cūmpu - cūppu (olikkuṟippōṭu kūṭiyatu) cūpputal = cārattai allatu cattai uḷḷiḻuttal.
tul - tuṉ - tuṟṟu = uṇavu. tuṟṟutal = uṇṇutal.
tuṟṟu - tuṟṟi = uṇavu.
tuṉ - tiṉ - tīṉ - tīṉi.
tiṉ - tiṉṟi - tiṟṟi = uṇavu.
tīṉ - tīṟṟu. tīṟṟutal = ūṭṭutal.
tuḷ - tuy. tuyttal = uṇṇutal, nukartal.
tum - tu. tuttal = uṇṇutal. tu = uṇavu.
tu - tuppu = uṇavu.
nuḷ - nuḻu - (nuku) - nukar. nukartal = uṇṇutal tuyttal (aṉupavittal).
nuḷ - noḷ - noṇṭu - noṇṭal = nukarkai.
puḷ - puḻu - puku - pukā = cōṟu. pukā - pukavu = uṇavu.
puḷ - puci - poci. pocittal = uṇṇutal.
muḷ - muṭu - maṭu. maṭuttal = vāyiliṭal, uṇṇutal.
maṭu - maṭai = uṇavu, cōṟu.
muḷ - (muci) - moci. mocittal = tiṉṉutal.
iii. moḷḷutal
moḷḷutalāvatu kalattai nīruṭpukutti nīr koḷḷutal.
kul - kōl. kōlutal = moḷḷutal. kōl - (kōlakai) - kōrakai = akappai, kōrakai - kōrikkai.
kuḷ - koḷ. koḷḷutal = mukattal.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōy = kaḷmukakkuṅ kalam.
nuḷ - noḷ. noḷḷutal = mukattal.
muḷ - muḻu - (muku) - muka. mukattal = moḷḷutal, moṇṭa ḷattal, muka - mukavai.
mul - (muḻai) - mūḻai - akappai.
muḷ - moḷ. moḷ - moṇṭai - montai = kaḷ mukakkuṅ kalam, montai pōṉṟa tōṟkaruvi. (icai).
moṇṭai - maṇṭai = nīrmukakkuṅ kalam, irappōr kalam, atupōṉṟa talaiyōṭu, talaiyiṉ mēṟpakuti, maṇṭai pōṉṟa tōṟkaruvi (icai).

iv. muḻukutal
muḻukutalāvatu oru poruḷ, iṉṉoṉṟiṟkuḷ pukuntu amiḻtal.
um - am - amiḻ - āḻ. āḻ - āḻam. āḻ - āḻi. 
kuḷ - kuḷi. kuḷittal = uṭpukutal, muḻukutal, nīruṭ pukutal.
koṭiyāṉ kūrṅkaṇai kuḷippa  (pu.ve. 10:10, koḷu).
eṅku marumat tiṭaik kuḷippa  (pu.ve. 7:23).
kaṭaṟpaṭai kuḷippa maṇṭi  (puṟam. 6).
muttukkuḷittal = muttukkaḷai nīruḷ mūḻkiyeṭuttal.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ. maṇṇutal = kuḷittal, muḻukutal.
mutūr vāyiṟ paṉikkaya maṇṇi  (puṟam. 79)
maṇṇumaṅkalam = aracaṉ nīrāṭṭu viḻā.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻuku - muḻukku. muḻuku - mūḻku.
KGF - (KG§F) - K§F (j.É.); muḻukku - mukku - (pi.vi.)
v. viḻuṅkutal
viḻuṅkutalāvatu, ōr uṇavupporuḷai muḻukkutal pōl tiṭumeṉa vāyvaḻi uṭpukuttutal.
kuḷ - kuṭi.
nuḷ - noḷ. noḷḷutal = viḻuṅkutal.
nuḷ - nuḻu - (nuḻuṅku) - nuṅku, nuṅkutal = viḻuṅkutal.
nuṅku - noṅku - nokku. noṅkutal = viḻuṅkutal. nokkutal - uṇṭu kuṟaiyac ceytal.
puḷ - paḷ - (paru) - paruku.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
muḻuṅku - (muḻukku) - muṭukku = orumuṟai viḻuṅkum nīr aḷavu. muṭukku - maṭakku.
viḻuṅku - (viḻukku) - viṭukku = maṭakku.
muḻukku - mukku. mukkutal = oṉṟai vāynīruḷ muḻukkit tiṉṉutal.
avalai mukkittiṉ, eḷḷai nakkittiṉ
eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
pācavaṉ mukkit taṇpuṉaṟ pāyum  (puṟam. 63).
mukku - mokku. mokkutal = vāynīruḷ nirampa muḻukkit tiṉṉutal.
vi. oḷittal
oḷittalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟuḷ pukuntu maṟaital.
uḷ - oḷ - oḷi.
kuḷ - kuḷi. kuḷittal = maṟaital.
yāṉaṟital añcik kuḷittu  (kalit. 98)
puḷ - puy. puytal = maṟaital.
kōlap pakaṟkaḷiṟoṉṟukaṟ puyya (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 40)
puḷ - paḷ - paṭu - (patu) - patuṅku - patukku - patukkam.

(6) turuval tuṟai
turuval
turuvutalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟuṭ pukuntu ūṭuruvic cellutal.
uḷ - uru - uruvu.
uḷ - (uṭu) - ūṭu - ūṭai = ūṭucellum iḻai.
kuḷ - (kuru) - kōr - kō. kōrvai - kōvai. kōrttal = nūlait tuḷaiyiṉ ūṭu uruvac ceytal.
tuḷ - tuḷai. tuḷaittal = turuvutal.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu.
mul - (mūl) - mūlam = vāyil, ūṭu.
ōr aṭaippiṭattiṉ ūṭu ceṉṟu kaṭattal eṅṅaṉam turuvutal eṉappaṭumō, aṅṅaṉamē oru tiṟappiṭattiṉ ūṭuceṉṟu kaṭattalum turuvutal eṉappaṭum. oru tuḷaiyūṭu cellutalum oru nāṭṭūṭu cellutalum turuvutal eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci
ūkāraccuṭṭu, muṟkūṟiyapaṭi mutalāvatu muṉmaiyaik kuṟikkum. muṉmaiyeṉpatu, kālamuṉ, iṭamuṉ, muṉṉilai, muṉṉuṟuppu, muṟpakuti, muṉi (nuṉi) mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷait taḻuvum.
tōṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟpaṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru marattil tōṉṟiya taḷir muṉṉāl nīṇṭu vaḷarvatum, ōr uṭampil tōṉṟiya taḷir muṉṉāl nīṇṭu vaḷarvatum, ōr uṭampil tōṉṟiya uṟuppum koppuḷamum muṉṉukkut taḷḷiyum puṭaittum varuvatum, ivai pōlvaṉa piṟavum, muṟpaṭalākum.

muṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟcelavuk karuttup piṟakkum. iṭampeyarum irutiṇaiyuyirikaḷum ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkuc celvatellām muṟcelavē. piṟcela vellām, perumpālum, uyirikaḷ ciṟitu vaḷarcciyuṟṟa piṉpum kuṟuntolaiviṟkum vēṇṭumeṉṟē nikaḻttap peṟuvatāl, iyaṟkaiyāṉa celavellām potuvāka muṟcelaveṉṟēyaṟika. kīḻiruntu mēṟcelvatum oruvakai muṟcelavātaliṉ, muṟcelavu eṉpatu eḻutal allatu uyartalaiyun taḻuvum.
muṟcelavuk karuttiṉiṉṟu neruṅkaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru marakkiḷai nīṇṭu vaḷarntu iṉṉoru kiḷaiyai aṭuppatum, irutiṇaiyiyaṅkuyirikaḷum muṉ ceṉṟu tāntām virumpum iṭattaiyum poruḷaiyuṅ kiṭṭuvatum, neruṅkalākum.
neruṅkaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu toṭutaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru poruḷ etticaiyilāyiṉum mēṉmēlum ceṉṟu koṇṭē yiruppiṉ, ētēṉumoru poruḷait toṭṭē tīral vēṇṭum. muṉṉōkkic cellum uyirikaḷum tām virumpiya poruḷaiyum iṭattaiyum toṭṭaṭaiyum. toṭutal eṉpatu, melitāyt toṭutal valitāyt toṭutal eṉa irupāṟpaṭṭu, tīṇṭutal, taḻuvutal, muṭṭutal, kuttutal, uṟaittal mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷait taḻuvum.
toṭutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kūṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. kūṭal eṉpatu, oṉṟiya kūṭal, oṉṟāk kūṭal eṉa iru vakai. maṇṇoṭu maṇṇum nīroṭu nīruṅ kūṭuvatu oṉṟiya kūṭal; uṭaloṭu uṭalum kūlattōṭu kūlamuṅ kūṭuvatu oṉṟāk kūṭal. oṉṟāk kūṭal mīṇṭum toṭṭuk kūṭal toṭātu kūṭal eṉa iru tiṟattatu.
kūṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu vaḷaitaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oṉṟāk kūṭaliṉ cila poruḷkaḷ vaḷaivatuṇṭu. oṉṟaiyoṉṟu muṭṭiya iruporuḷkaḷ oṉṟātavaiyāyiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ meliyatu kampipōl nīṇṭiruppiṉ vaḷaiyum. kallaimuṭṭiya vērum kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷai muṭṭiya āṇiyum vaḷaital kāṇka. vaḻiccelvōṉ teruvaṭaitta cuvarai muṭṭit tirumpuvatum, vēṟṟiṭañ ceṉṟavaṉ viṉaimuṟṟi mīḷvatum, aṇivakaiyil vaḷaitaliṉ pāṟpaṭum. vaḷaital eṉpatu, cāytal, kōṇal, tirukal vaṭṭam, cuṟṟal, cūḻtal, uruṭci cuḻaṟci mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷait taḻuvum.
kūṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum maṟṟoru karuttu tuḷaittaṟ karuttākum. muṭṭiya piṉ vaḷaiyāta valiya poruḷ, tāṉ muṭṭiyatait tuḷaittum cellum. āṇi caṭṭattaiyum, vaṇṭu marattaiyum, pūcci puttakattaiyum. māntaṉ malaiyaiyum tuḷaikkalām tuḷaittal eṉpatu, kuḻittal, tōṇṭutal, tuḷaiyiṭutal, pukutal, turuvutal ākiya pala karuttukkaḷait taḻuvum. tuḷaittup pukutalum, tuḷaikkuṭ pukutalum eṉap pukutal iru vakaittu. uyirikaḷ tattam uṟaiviṭattiṟkuṭ pukutal tuḷaikkuṭ pukutalākum. oṉṟait turuviya poruḷ iṟutiyil veḷippaṭum. atu tōṉṟal allatu muṉvarutal pōṉṟatākum. ataṉpiṉ nikaḻakkūṭiyavai. tōṉṟal mutal turuval varai kūṟiyuḷḷa pala nikaḻccikaḷē. ivai oru poru ḷiṉ kālamellām mīṇṭum mīṇṭum nikaḻntu koṇṭē yirukkumātaliṉ, ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci muṟṟuṟum ellai turuvaṟkaruttē. ākavē, tōṉṟal mutal turuvalvarai yuḷḷa karuttukkaḷellām oru cuḻal cakkaramātal kāṇka.
ūkārac cuṭṭāl muṟaiyē uṇarttappeṟuṅ karuttukkaḷil, mutaṉmaiyāṉavaiyē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa.  (mu.tā.)
ūṅku
ū - ūṅku = muṉ.
ūṅkutal = muṉ cellutal
ūkkutal = muṟceluttutal, uḷḷattai viṉaiyil muṉ cellat tūṇṭutal
ūṅku - ūkku - ūkkam.  (ti.ma. 338)
ūci
ūci - va. cūci (i. vē.) su#ci. 
uḷ - aḷ = kūrmai. uḷ - uḷi = kūriya veṭṭuk karuvi. 
uḷi - ukir = kūriya viraluṟuppu.
uḷi - uci - ūci = kūrmai (patiṟṟup. 70:7), kūriya kuttuk karuvi allatu taiyaṟkaruvi (piṅ), ciṟumai.
o. nō: iḷi - ici, vāḷi - vāci - vācikai.
ūcik kaṇavāy, ūcikkaḻutti, ūcikkaḷā, ūcikkāy, ūcikkār, ūciccampā, ūcittakarai, ūcittūṟal, ūcit toṇṭai, ūcippālai, ūcippuḻu, ūcimallikai, ūci miḷakāy, ūci mullai, ūcivēr eṉpaṉa kūrmai allatu ciṟumai paṟṟit toṉṟu toṭṭup palvēṟu poruḷkaṭku vaḻaṅkivaruñ coṟkaḷām.

eḻuttūci, kuttūci, tuṉṉūci, taiyalūci eṉpaṉavum attakai yaṉavē. eḻuttūci = eḻuttāṇi. maiyeḻut tūciyiṉ ... eḻut tiṭṭāḷ (cīvaka. 1767).
mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ  (patiṟṟup. 42: 2-4)
eṉpatāl, puṇtaikkum ūciyum paṇṭait tamiḻakat tiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
kollat teruvil ūci viṟpatā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
matiṟṟalaiyaik kaippaṟṟum pakaivar kaiyaip potukkum ūcip poṟikaḷum, akkālattuk kōṭṭai matilmēl vaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
aiyavit tulāmuṅ kaipeya rūciyum  (cilap. 15: 213).
vaṭamoḻiyil cūci eṉṉum coṟku mūlamillai. taiyalaik kuṟikkum civ eṉṉum collai mūlamākakkāṭṭa virumpuvar.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum, cūci eṉpatiṉiṉṟu ūci eṉpatu tirintuḷḷa tāka, ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka tamiḻ, akara mutaliyiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatu ettuṇai neñcaḻuttam!  (va.va.96-97).
ūṭṭi
caṅkiṉāl kuḻantaikaḷukkup pāl ūṭṭuvatāl ataṟku ūṭṭi eṉṟu peyar. makkaḷiṉ kaḻuttil muṉpuṟattuḷḷa caṅku pōṉṟa puṭaippum ūṭṭi eṉappaṭum. caṅku eṉavumpaṭum  (col. 13.)
ūtā maṇi viḷaiyāṭṭu
pala piḷḷaikaḷuḷ mūvar kaikōttuc cāṭ pūṭ tiri eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu mūṉṟutaram kai acaittut tiri eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ kaiyai yuruvik kaimēṟkai vaippar. mēṟkai, akaṅkai mēṉōkkiyiruntāl veḷḷai eṉṟum, puṟaṅkai mēṉōkkiyiruntāl karuppu eṉṟum, collappaṭum.  iv viraṇṭil evvakaiyilēṉum, taṉiyāka vaitta piḷḷai vilakiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, ēṉaiyiruvarum mutaliṟ cērāta vēṟoru piḷḷaiyuṭaṉ kaikōttu, muṉpōṉṟē oru piḷḷaiyaip pirikka vēṇṭum. mūvarum orē vakaiyāyk kai vaittiruntāl, mīṇṭuṅ kōkkavēṇṭum iṅṅaṉam mummūva rāyk kaikōttu ovvoruttiyaip pirittapiṉ, iṟutiyil iruvar iruppar. avviruvaruṭaṉ, mutaṉmutaṟ pirinta piḷḷai oppukku eṉṟu collik kaikōkkum. muṉpu piriyāta iruvaruḷ yārēṉum orutti taṉivakaiyiṟ kaivaippiṉ, avaḷ vilakiviṭa, aṭuttavaḷ akappaṭṭukkoḷvāḷ.
akappaṭṭukkoṇṭavaḷ caṟṟut tolaiviṟpōy niṟka, avaḷukku muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṇimālai allatu vēṟōr aṇi, oru piḷḷaiyiṭam oḷittu vaikkappaṭum. tolaivil niṟpavaḷ, varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ vantu, maṇimālai vaittirukkiṟavaḷ eṉṟu oru piḷḷaiyai ūkippāyc cuṭṭikkāṭṭum. illātavaḷ eḻuntu niṟpāḷ. aiyattiṟkiṭamāyiruppiṉ, avaḷ maṭi mutaliya vaṟṟaip piṭittup pārkkalām. orumuṟai kaṇṭupiṭikkat tavaṟi ṉavaḷ maṟumuṟaiyuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. maṇimālai vaittiruppavaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭiṉ, maṟṟellāp piḷḷaikaḷuñ cērntu, ūtāmaṇiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittāl kai taṭṭuvōm eṉṟu pāṭik kaitaṭṭi makiḻvar. itu teruvilum ūrppoṭṭalilum viḷaiyāṭap peṟum.

ūtu eṉṉum olik kuṟippuc col
ū - ūtu.
māntaṉ moḻi iyaṟkai moḻi (Natural Language), ceyaṟkai moḻi (Artificial Language) eṉa irunilaippaṭum. cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭku muntiyatu iyaṟkai moḻi; pintiyatu ceyaṟkai moḻi. ivai muṟaiyē muḻaittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech), iḻaittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉavum paṭum.
iyaṟkai moḻiyaic cērnta eḻuvakai yolikaḷuḷ, oppoli (Imitative) eṉpatu oṉṟākum. aḵtu aḵṟiṇai yoliyum uyartiṇai yoliyum eṉa iruvakai.
e-ṭu: 
aḵṟiṇaiyoli :	kū, kūvu (coo), karai (cry, crow), ūḷ -ūḷai 				(howl), piḷiṟu (blare), im - imir (hum).
uyartiṇaiyoli :	cappu (sup, sip), tuppu (spit), mukku, vikku 					(hiccup), ūm (hum), ām (haw), mūcu, ūtu.
uyartiṇaiyolikaḷ, vāyoli, mūkkoli, mūccuk kāṟṟoli eṉa mūvakaiyām. capputalum tupputalum vāyoliyuṭaṉum, mukkutalum ūṅkoṭṭutalum mūkkoliyuṭaṉum, mūcutalum ūtutalum mūccuk kāṟṟoliyuṭaṉum nikaḻum.
mūccuk kāṟṟum mūkkuvaḻi varuvatum vāyvaḻi varuvatum eṉa iruvakaittu. mūcutal mūkkuvaḻik kāṟṟālum, ūtutal vāyvaḻik kāṟṟālum nikaḻum. mūcutalāvatu, ceviyuṟumāṟu kāṟṟai veḷiviṭṭum uḷḷiḻuttum urakka mūccu viṭutal. mūcumūceṉṟu iḷaikkiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. 
mūcu - mūccu. o.nō: pēcu - pēccu.
ūtutalāvatu, nerupperittalum viḷakkaṇaittalum cūṭāṟṟu talum tukaḷaip pōkkutalum mutaliya ceyalkaṭku, vāyvaḻik kāṟṟai veḷiviṭutal.
kāṟṟu veḷivarum ōcai, perumpālum cakara voliyālum, ciṟupāṉmai cakarattiṟku orupuṭaiyiṉamāṉa takara voliyālum, kuṟikkappaṭum. mūcu eṉṉum collum, kāṟpantiṟkuk kāṟṟaṭip pataik kuṟikkum pucukku eṉṉum olik kuṟippum, cakarattai yuṭaiyaṉa. Gas eṉṉum āṅkilac collilum cakaramiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. vāṉ elmāntu (Van Helmo#nt) eṉpavar gas eṉṉum collai, khaos (chaos) eṉṉum kirēkkac colli ṉiṉṟu tirittār eṉpatu, attuṇaip porutta muṭaittaṉṟu. kāṟṟiṉ toḻilaik kuṟikkum vīcu, viciṟu, vicukku (whisk) eṉṉum coṟkaḷum cakarattaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
nerupperittaṟkuk kāṟṟai ūtumpōtu, ūkārattaiyē olittaṟkēṟṟa itaḻ kuvivum, cakara takaram pōṉṟa uracoli kaḷaiyē (fricatives) olittaṟkēṟṟa vāynilaiyum, amain tiruttalāl, annilaiyil vāyvaḻik kāṟṟai veḷiviṭuñ ceyalai, ūcu, ūtu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē kuṟikka muṭiyum. rakaram uruḷoliyātalāl (trill) attuṇaip poruttamāṉa taṉṟu.
kāṟṟai veḷiviṭumpōtum uḷḷiḻukkumpōtum amaiyum vāynilai, ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē. ataṉāl veyilil alaintavaṉ iḷaippāṟa uṭkārumpōtu vāyvaḻik kāṟṟai veḷi viṭutalaiyum, uṟaippāṉa uṇavai yuṇpavaṉ vāyvaḻik kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuttalaiyum, ūcu eṉṉum orē col kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu.
ūceṉṟu uṭkāra nēraṅ kiṭaiyātu, ūcu ūceṉṟu avvaḷavu cōṟṟaiyum uṇṭuviṭṭāṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷai nōkkuka.
nerupperittal pōṉṟa ceyaliṟ kāṟṟai veḷiviṭutalai, ūtu eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu. cakara takaram oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu pōliyāka varum.
e-ṭu: ōcai - ōtai, nattu - naccu.
ūtutal = 1. nerupperikka ūtutal, ūtu - ūttu, (to.pe.)
2.	viḷakkavikka ūtutal.
3.	cūṭāṟṟa ūtutal.
4.	nōvu tīra ūtutal.
5.	tukaḷ nīkka ūtutal.
	ūtippōṭutal = eḷitil vellutal.
	uṉṉaik kacakki ūtiviṭuvēṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
6.	ūtāṅkuḻa lūtutal.
	ūtāṅkuḻal, ūtāṅkucci eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
7.	icaikkuḻa lūtutal.
	ūti, ūtili (makuṭi), ūtu kompu mutaliyaṉa tuḷaiyuḷḷa icaik karuvi vakaikaḷ. ūtal eṉṉum col, ekkāḷam, tārai, kompu mutaliyaṉa ūticcellum ūrvalam paṟṟi āravārattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. o. neh.: E. fanfare - fonfaronade.
8. 	taṭṭāṉ poṉṉai ūtip puṭamiṭutal.
	ūtukaṭṭi, ūtuveḷḷi eṉpaṉa cokkaveḷḷiyaik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ.
9. 	ulaitturutti allatu icaitturutti yūtutal.
	icaitturuttiyiṉ peyar piṉvarumāṟu tiriyum.
	turutti - tutti - titti.
10. 	ūttāmpai yūtutal.
	ūttāmpai ūttām peṭṭi, ūttām pēḻai eṉṟum collap peṟum.
11. 	kāykaḷai mūṭṭampōṭṭu ūtip paḻukka vaittal.
12. 	nōyāḷi yuṭampiṟkuḷ ūti mantirittal.
13. 	vaṇṭu olittal. cēvaṭikkē ceṉṟūtāy kōttumpī (tiruvā. 10:1)
14.	vaṇṭu ūtit tēṉai nukartal.
15.	vaṇṭu ūti marattait tuḷaittal.
16. 	uṭal vīṅkutal.
	ūttam = vīkkam. te. ūta. ūtal = vīkkam.
	ūtai = vaḷinōy (vātam). ūttu = vaḷinōy.
	ūtukaṇai, ūtukarappāṉ, ūtukāmālai, ūtumāntam eṉpaṉa ūtunōy vakaikaḷ.
	ūtucuruṭṭai, ūtuvaḻalai, ūtuviriyaṉ eṉpaṉa uṭampai ūtacceyyum naccuppāmpu vakaikaḷ.
	  17.  	mikutal.
	ūttappam, ūtumākkūḻ eṉpaṉa eḻumpum ciṟṟuṇṭi vakaikaḷ.
ūtu - ūtiyam = 1. celaviṟkumēl mikunta varavu. mutalilārk kūtiya millai (kuṟaḷ. 449)
2. payaṉ ūtiyaṅ karutiya vorutiṟan tāṉum (tol. po. 41)
ūtiyam vēṟu; campaḷam vēṟu.
ūtiya iḻappu - va. lāpa naṭṭam.
ūtu - ūtāri = tukaḷai ūtip pōkkuvatupōl celvattaiyellām vīṇ celavu ceytaḻippavaṉ.
koṭaiyilāta vūtāri  (tiruppukaḻ)
ūtāri paṭutal = kēṭuṟutal.
uṭalūtāripaṭ ṭoḻi  (tiruppu. 904)
ūtāri eṉṉuñ collai, utāra eṉṉum vaṭacolli ṉiṉṟu tirintatākac ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali kāṭṭiyiruppatu, uṇmaikku māṟāṉatum muṟṟum porutta maṟṟatumām. utāra eṉṉum vaṭacol īkait taṉmaiyaik kuṟippatākum, ūtāri eṉṉum teṉ collō aḻippāḷiyaik kuṟippatākum. aḻippāḷi vīṇcelavu ceypavaṉēyaṉṟi aḷippāḷiyallaṉ. tamiḻukku immiyum utavātu piṟamoḻi vaḷarccikku ērāḷamāy vārik koṭukkum tamiḻa aracaṉai allatu celvaṉai, ūtāriyeṉiṉ, atu mikap poruttamē.
CJ - k., f., bj., tu. ūtu.
ūtu - ūttu - ma. ūttu = ūtukai.
ūtu - ūttam - te. ūta = vīkkam.
ūtu - ūtai - 1. kāṟṟu (tivā.) 2. vāṭaik kāṟṟu.
paṉippular pāṭi ... ūtaiyūrtara  (paripā. 11: 84)

talaivali paṉṉamaicca lūtai  (tailava. taila. 7)
ūtai - Skt. vata (vāta.)
ūcu, ūtu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiluḷḷa cakara takaraṅkaḷ vāyvaḻik kāṟṟup paṟitalai uṇarttutalāl, avai āriya moḻikaḷilum vaṭa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa c, t eṉpaṉa pōl veṭi(pput) takaippolikaḷ (Plosive Stops) ākāveṉṟum, iraṭṭitta viṭattē aṅṅaṉamākumeṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya toṉmūtiyaṉ moḻiyātaliṉ, annilamirunta iṭattai yoṭṭiya teṉṉāṭṭut teṉkōṭi yil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻolikaḷai, tamiḻ vāyilākattāṉ aṟiya  muṭiyumē yaṉṟip piṟa vaṭanilat tirimoḻikaḷ vāyilāy aṟiya muṭiyāteṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam aṟiya muyalvār kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kuruṭarpōla iṭarppaṭuvāreṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
 hoot, v.i. & t., & n, l. Make loud sounds ... (of steam whistle or motor car or driver) Sound (intr) ... ME huten, imit.
“hooter, n. In vbl senses, esp. siren, steam whistle, esp. as signal for work to begin or cease, [ER]”
eṉṟu erutantuṟai naṭappu āṅkilac ciṟṟakaramutali  (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English) kūṟuvatāl, ūtu eṉṉuñ colloṭu hoot eṉṉum coṟku orumaruṅku toṭarpirukka lāmō eṉa aiyuṟa iṭamēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
ūtu - pūtu - pūttu. pūttup pūtteṉṟu aṭuppūtik koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
ūtu - ōtu. ōtutal = 1. kātiṟkuḷ ūtuvatu pōl, camaya kuravaṉ arumaṟai mantirattaic camainta māṇavaṉukkuc collutal.
2. marumac ceytiyai oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.
ūtai (vātam) mutaliyaṉa
mūccum pēccum uṭporuḷ iṭamāṟṟamum veḷiyēṟṟamum taṉittum piṟatātukkaḷōṭu kūṭiyum nikaḻttuvatu ūtaiyiṉ toḻilkaḷ. uṇṭataṉ cerimāṉattiṟku utavuvatu pittanīr, tacai nārkaḷiṉ maḻamaḻappāṉa iyakkattiṟku uyavuney pōṟ payaṉpaṭuvatu kōḻai. ai. allatu aiyam eṉpatum kōḻaik koru peyar.  (ti.ma. 941)
ūr
ūr - va. rōh (i.vē.)
ur - uṟu - uṟi = uyarat toṅkiviṭum tūkku.
uṟu - uṟai = uyaram (piṅ.)
ur - ūr. ūrtal = 1. ēṟutal. ūrpiḻipu ... vantaṉṟu (aiṅ. 101). 2.ēṟiccellutal.
civikai poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntāṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 37)
vaṭamoḻiyār kāṭṭum ruh (to ascend) eṉṉum mūlam ūr eṉpatiṉ muṟaimāṟṟut tiripē (metathesis)..
ūr - ūrttam. o. nō: nēr - nērttam. ūrttam - ūrtva (urdhva) uyarnta, tūkkiya (i.vē.).  (va.va. 98)
ūrppeyar
ūrppeyarkaḷum ūrppeyar īṟukaḷum vevvēṟu kāraṇam paṟṟiyavai:
āṟai eṉpatu āṟṟūr;
puttūr eṉpatu putiya ūr;
mūtūr eṉpatu paḻaiya ūr;
pērūr eṉpatu mānakar.
paṭṭi eṉpatu kālnaṭait toḻuvamuḷḷa ciṟṟūr.
paṟṟu eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭavarkku allatu oru cārārkku uriya ciṟṟūr allatu ciṟṟūrt tokuti.
aṭaṅkāppaṟṟu eṉpatu aracaṉ āṇaikku aṭaṅkātavar vacikkum ūr.
paḷḷi eṉpatu pautta camaṇa maṭamuḷḷa ūr.
pāḷaiyam eṉpatu paṭaiyirukkum ūr. 
paṭṭu eṉpatu pāḷaiyat talaivarāṉa ciṟṟūr aracarkku viṭappaṭṭa ciṟṟūr allatu ciṟṟūrt tokuti.  (col)
ūrppeyar
maṅkalam eṉpatu pārppaṉar irukkum ūr.
vāṭai eṉpatu vēṭṭuvar allatu iṭaiyar irukkum ūr.
paṇṭāra vāṭai eṉpatu kuṭikaḷukkuriya ūr.
nattam eṉpatu pārppaṉar allātār vāḻum ūr.
kuṭi eṉpatu oru kuṭumpattārē allatu kulattārē vacikkum ūr.
kuṭikkāṭu eṉpatu kuṭikaḷ vacikkum kāṭṭūr.
kuṭiyēṟṟam eṉpatu makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya ūr. (kuṭiyēṟṟam eṉpatu iṉṟu kuṭiyāttam eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkum).
kallāṅkuttu eṉpatu kaṭiṉanilattūr
murampu eṉpatu kaṟpāṅkāṉa mēṭṭu nilattūr
pēṭṭai eṉpatu cantai kūṭum ūr.
eyil eṉpatu matil cūḻnta ūr.
puram puri eṉpaṉa aracar talainakar
viṇṇakaram eṉpatu tirumālkōyil uḷḷa ūr  (viṇṭu + nakaram = viṇṇakaram; viṇṭu - viṣṇu. nakar, nakaram - maṉai, kōyil) ēri, kuḷam, kōṭṭai, kōyil mutaliya īṟukaḷil muṭiyum pērkaḷaik koṇṭa ūrkaḷ, avvav vīṟukaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭum iṭattaik koṇṭavai eṉpatai evarum collāmalē aṟintu koḷḷalām.
ēri eṉṟu muṭiyum pēraik koṇṭa ōr ūril ēri illā viṭiṉ atu oru kālattil iruntu piṉṉart tūrntu pōyiṟ ṟeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
puram paṭṭu mutaliya cilapeyarkaḷum ikkālattil ciṟappup poruḷ iḻantu potup peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl ikkālap puttūrp peyarkaḷ varalāṟṟuc ceyti yoṉṟum uṇarttā.  (col. 29).
ūruṇi
ūruṇpatu ūruṇi, itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku  (ti.ma. 131).
ūḻ
paḻam piṟappukaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa iruviṉaippayaṉ ceyta vaṉaiyē ceyta muṟaippaṭi ceṉṟaṭaiyum iyaṟkai yoḻuṅku. itu muṟaippaṭi varuvatāl muṟai eṉṟum ūḻ eṉṟum, avaravarkkuriya iṉpa tuṉpap pakutikaḷai vakuppatāl pāl eṉṟum, vakuttāṉ eṉṟum, teyva ēṟpāṭu pōliruppatāl teyvam eṉṟum, pālvarai teyvam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. iṉi māṟā iyalpāy iruppatāl iyaṟkai eṉṟum peyar peṟuvatām.  (ti.ma. 214).
ūṟu
uṟuvatu ūṟu. uṟutalāvatu uṭampiṉ uḷḷum puṟampum paṭutal allatu toṭutal.  (ti.ma. 51).
ūṟu eṉpatu nērcci. uṟuvatu ūṟu; paṭuvatu pāṭu eṉpatu pōla.  (ti. ma. 945).
ekara okara iyaṟkai
tolkāppiyaṉār tamatu nūlil, akkālattil vaḻaṅki vanta eḻuttukaḷ evvitam varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉ pataik kuṟippiṭavillai : āyiṉum cila eḻuttukaḷ puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟpatai māttiram kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. av veḻuttukaḷ kuṟṟiya lukaram, kuṟṟiyalikaram, makarakkuṟukkam, āytam, meyyeḻuttu, ekaram, okaram eṉpaṉa.
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ a,i,u,e,o ākiya aintum tamakku iṉamāṉa neṭilkaḷai uṭaiyaṉa. a,i,u ākiya mūṉṟukkum iṉamākiya neṭilait terivikka ak kuṟil uruvattukku ōr atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl ekaram okaram ākiya iraṇṭiṉ vakaiyil kuṟilaik kuṟikkumpoḻutu atikappaṭiyāṉa puḷḷi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiliṉ nīṭciyē neṭil. ātaliṉ neṭiliṉ vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅkak kuṟiluruvattukku atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟiyiṭṭu neṭilai uṇarttuvatē iyaṟkai. ekara okaraṅkaḷ antap potuvitikku māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āciriyarum a,i,u ākiyavaṟṟukkuriya neṭiluruvattaik kuṟippiṭāmal, iv viraṇṭai maṭṭum ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟār.
ātaliṉ, ekara okaraṅkaḷukkuk kuṟil illāmal neṭilē vaḻaṅkum oruvakaiyiṉiṉṟum ivveḻuttukaḷiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷaip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟē nām eṇṇavēṇṭi yirukkiṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyil ekara okaraṅkaḷ neṭilaiyē kuṟikkum. avaṟṟiṟku vaṭanāṭṭār iṭṭa kuṟikaḷai avvāṟē tamiḻilum neṭilukku mēṟkoṇṭu, an nāṭṭārāl vaḻaṅkappaṭāta kuṟilai yuṇartta vēṟu kuṟikaḷai amaikka vēṇṭi vantatu pōlum. puḷḷiyiṉāl māttiraik kuṟaivai aṟivittalaip piṟa puḷḷiyiṭṭa eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyalām. ataip pōlavē ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ tiṟattilum neṭili ṉiṉṟum kuṟainta kuṟilai uṇarttap puḷḷiyiṭṭaṉar eṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār tam paṇṭait tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ eṉṉum cuvaṭiyil (pak. 86-7) varaintuḷḷār.
ilakkaṇa nūlār eḻuttukaḷiṉ varivaṭivukaḷaik kuṟippatē yaṉṟi, avaṟṟai varaṇikkum vaḻakkam eṅkumillai; avaṟṟai varaṇikkavum muṭiyātu. ṭakara pakaram pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru nērkōṭṭu varivaṭivukaḷai maṭṭum ōraḷavu vaṇṇikkalām. āyiṉ, ataṉāṟ peṟum payaṉillai. eḻuttukaḷai eḻututalē, avaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivaik kāṭṭutalaṉṟi vēṟākātu.
puḷḷiyeṉpatu eḻuttukaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭuk kuṟiyēyaṉṟi uṇmaiyāṉa varivaṭivākātu. itai yuṇarātu, perumpulavarum ciṟanta uraiyāciriyarumāṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, makara vaṭivaip paṟṟi vaḻuppaṭa vuraittuviṭṭār.
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē  (tol. eḻuttu. 14)
makara varivaṭiviṉ aṭaippuḷḷiṭṭa puḷḷi, makarak kuṟukkattiṉ vaṭivām eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ.
makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu. teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa vaṇṇamālaikaḷil iraṇṭām ṭakarattiṟkuḷḷa varivaṭivai orupuṭai yottatu. tamiḻ makara vaṭivu iṉṟu pōṉṟē aṉṟum iruntatu. mēṟ kuṟitta nūṟpā makarak kuṟukka varivaṭivu paṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi, makara varivaṭivu paṟṟiyataṉṟu. ataip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, pakara varivaṭivuḷ ḷiṭṭa puḷḷiyē makara varivaṭivam eṉṟu uraittatoṭu. atai varaintum kāṭṭiviṭṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. avar urai varumāṟu :
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē  (nūṟpā)
itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceykiṉṟatu.
makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ : uṭpeṟupuḷḷi - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum - makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭkuk kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcip poruḷai uṇartti niṉṟatu.
utāraṇam; kappi kappi (kammi) eṉa varum iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal.
iṉi, uyirmey vaṭivu paṟṟiya puḷḷi yillā ........... āṟē (17) eṉṉum nūṟpāvuraiyilum.
arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyai ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyai vaḷaitteḻutiṉār eṉṟu tam vaḻuvaik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiviṭṭār. uruvutirin tuyirttal eṉpatu uyirmey vaṭivukaṭkuriyatē yaṉṟi, makara vaṭiviṟ kuriyataṉṟu.
meyyiṉ aḷavē araiyeṉa moḻipa  (eḻuttu 11)
avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē  (eḻuttu 12)
araiyaḷavu kuṟukal makaram uṭaittē
icaiyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅ kālai  (eḻuttu 13)
eṉṟu muṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷaiyum.
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal  (eḻuttu 15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē  (eḻuttu 16)
eṉap piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷaiyum, puḷḷipeṟutalaṉṟi ōreḻuttiṟkum tolkāppiyar vaṭivu kūṟāmaiyaiyum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nōkkiyilar.
mēlum, makaravaṭiviṟku akattum puṟattum puḷḷiyiṭuvatu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟku iṭarppāṭāṉa teṉpataiyum, pakara vuṭ puḷḷiyai vaḷaitteḻutum pakuttaṟivu kūṭap paṇṭait tamiḻaṟiñarkku illātiruntirukkātu eṉpataiyum avar karutiyilar.
ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu kūṟum nūṟpā veṉṟu kaṇṭapōtu, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṇātu pōṉatu āṉai yaṭiccaṟukkalē.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vaḻuvurai. tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ ēṟkeṉavē paṉmuṟai vaṭivu māṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟum eḻuttumāṟṟakkārarukku, oru pōlic cāṉṟākavum vāyttuviṭṭatu.
makarak kuṟukkam paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum ataṟku iḷampūraṇar uraitta vaḻuvuraiyum varumāṟu:
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē  (tol. eḻuttu 14)
itu. pakarattiṉ makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) uḷpeṟu puḷḷi uruvu ākum - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi makarattiṟku vaṭivām. (aḵtiṉmai pakarattiṟku vaṭivām).
e-ṭu: p, (p) eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. kappi, kappi (kampi) eṉavarum.
ivvuraiyaik kaṇṭē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum mayaṅkiṉār pōlum! iṉṟuḷḷapaṭiyē makara vaṭivam muṉpum iruntatu. makaraṅ kuṟukum pōtē uṭpuḷḷi peṟum. e-ṭu: m.
makarak kuṟai vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyum oru puḷḷiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
kīḻaiyāriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 2000. avarkkaṉṟu ilakkiyamum illai; eḻuttumillai; tamiḻarō ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ talaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē muttamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭavar. mēlum, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakaiyeḻuttuk koṇṭa neṭuṅkaṇakku mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉi, camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya vētamoḻi yuṭpaṭa āriya moḻikaṭkellām tamiḻ muntiyatum mūlamumāṉa moḻiyeṉṟu, ataṉ muccuṭṭeḻut tukaḷē muḻucceviṭaṉukkuṅ kēṭkumāṟu muḻaṅkip paṟaiyaṟai kiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, kiranta eḻuttum tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu, ūṉṟi nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākāmaṟ pōkātu. innilaiyil, tamiḻ ēkāra ōkāra eḻuttukaḷai vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭateṉpatu, pāṭṭaṉ tirumaṇattaip pēraṉē naṭatti vaittāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē am moḻikkuriya eḻuttukaḷ amaikkappaṭum. ayaṉmoḻiyiluḷḷa ciṟap peḻuttuc coṟkaḷ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭiṉum, ac coṟkaḷē yaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappeḻuttukaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vaḻakkup peṟā. ēkāra ōkāra vaṭivukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantaṉaveṉiṉ. av vīreḻuttukaḷaiyum, uyirākavō uyirmeyk kūṭṭilō, mutalākavō iṭaiyākavō kaṭaiyākavō koṇṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil illātiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
ē, ēkkam, ēṅku, ēcu, ēṭāci, ēṭu, ēṇi, ēṇai, ētu (viṉā), ēntu,  ēppam, ēmā, ēmāḷi, ēy, ēr, ērāḷam, ēri, ēl, ēla, ēlam, ēvu, ēḻu, ēḻai, ēṟpāṭu, ēṟṟam, ēṟṟu, ēṟu, ēṉ mutaliya ēkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum.
ō, ōkkāḷam, ōṭu (peyar), ōṭu (viṉai), ōṭi, ōṅku, ōcai, ōṭṭam, ōṭṭu, ōṭṭi, ōṭṭai, ōṭam, ōṇāṉ, ōtam, ōtu, ōmal, ōytal, ōrpaṭiyāḷ, ōram, ōri, ōlai, ōviyam mutaliya ōkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum.
av vīruyirum meyyōṭukūṭi mūviṭattum varum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷum, kallā makkaḷum vaḻaṅkum aṭippaṭai yulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷāyiruppatai nōkkumpōtu, ti.nā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉāriṉ aṟiyāmai ettuṇai aḷavaṟṟa teṉṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
mutaṟkālattil māntaṉ vāyil mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya neṭi luyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilkaḷākak kuṟukiya piṉpē moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruppatāl, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷai iruvakai yilum mutalilum mūviṭattuṅ koṇṭa iruvakai vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, āyirakkaṇakkākap perukiyuḷḷaṉa. ēkāra ōkāra neṭiṟcoṟkaḷiṉum, ekara okarak kuṟiṟcoṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa irumaṭaṅku perukiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ā ī ū eṉṉum neṭilkaḷaiyum a i u eṉṉum avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷaiyum amaikkat terinta tamiḻarkku ē ō eṉṉum neṭilkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ e o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷaiyum amaikkat teriyavillai yeṉpatu. muṉ piṟanta mummakavaiyum vaḷartta peṟṟōrkku, piṉpiṟanta irumakavai vaḷarkkat teriyātu pōyiṟṟeṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
tolkāppiya mutalatikāra mutaliyalākiya nūṉmarapiluḷḷa 33 kuṟu nūṟpāviṟkuḷḷēyē. ekara ēkāra okara ōkārac coṟkaḷ eṅṅaṉam iyalpāka vantuḷḷaṉa eṉpataip piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷālum aṭikaḷālum uṇarntu koḷḷalām.

aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa icaiyoṭu civaṇiya 
narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar  (33)
akara ākāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum ikara īkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum ukara ūkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum pōṉṟu attuṇai ērāḷamāka illāviṭiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka ekara ēkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum okara ōkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum. nūṟṟukkaṇak kiṉavum iyaṟkaiyāṉavum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavum piṟkāla nūlkaḷiṟ pōṉṟē tolkāppiyattilum payiṉṟu varuvaṉa vākavum irukkumpōtu, ekara okarak kuṟilkaṭku maṭṭum ēṉ atikappaṭiyāṉa puḷḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkavillai.
piṟamoḻiyāḷar kāṇāta poruḷilakkaṇam kaṇṭum iya loṭu icai nāṭakaṅkaḷai iṇaittu muttamiḻ puṇarttum, eḻunilac ceyyuḷ yāttum, eḻuttiṉaṅkaṭku uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟu peyariṭumaḷavu meyp poruḷaṟivu viñciyum, āmpal tāmarai veḷḷam eṉṟu aṭukkiya kōṭiyum muntiri kīḻmuntiri immi yeṉṟu nuṇukkiya piṉṉamuṅ kaṇittum, ulaka muḻutum vaḻaṅkumāṟu eḻunāṭ kiḻamai vakuttum, ēraṇamum maṟaiyum iyaṟṟiyum, mukkaraṇamuṅ kaṭanta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷaik kaṇṭum, tam teyvap pulamaiyai veḷippaṭuttiya kumarit tamiḻarkku, ā ī ū pōṉṟē ē ō neṭilkaṭku atikap paṭiyāṉa vari vaṭivamaikkum aṟivillātu pōyiṟṟeṉpatu. immiyum nampat takkataṉṟu.
cūracēṉi mākati mutaliya nāṭṭup pirākiruta moḻiyāḷar, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ekara okaramākak kuṟukumuṉ kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vaṭapāṟ ceṉṟō, ēṉ(eṉ) nilam. ōṉ (uṉ-oṉ) vīṭu eṉṟu kuṟilkaḷai neṭilākkip pēciṉatiṉālō, ekara okaram illāta allatu vaḻaṅkāta moḻikaḷaip pēci vantaṉar. mēlai yāciyāviṉiṉṟu ki.mu. 2000iṟkuppiṉ intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta ciṟupāṉmaik kūṭṭamāṉa vēta āriyariṉ moḻi, vaḻakkaṟṟup perumpāṉmaip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ pirākiruta moḻikaḷuṭaṉ kalantu pōṉatiṉāl, avaratu vētamoḻiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻiyum ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ illātatāyiṟṟu. mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilellām ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatāl, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr moḻiyilum avai vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
neṭilukkuriya atikappaṭi kuṟiyai ekara okarak kuṟilkaṭku iṭṭatu pōṉṟē, ēṉai munneṭilkaṭkup pōṉṟu ēkāra ōkāra neṭilkaṭkuc cuḻikkuṟiyiṭāmaiyum; iyaṟkaikku māṟākat tōṉṟuvatāl.
ēkāra ōkārat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyataip piṟkālattār ekara okaratti yaṟkaiyum aṟṟē eṉṟu tirittuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvataṟkum iṭamillai.
puḷḷiyeṉpatu kuttu eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatu. puḷḷutal kuttutal. alakāṟ kutti (kotti)t tiṉpataṉālēyē paṟavai puḷ eṉappaṭṭatu.
eḻuttāṇiyāl ēṭṭiṟ kuttiṉāl tuḷai viḻum. ataṉāl, ēṭṭuppuḷḷi yellām ciṟu cuḻiyākavēyirukkum. ic cuḻi ākāra īkārattiṟkuppōl iṉṟu ōkāra vaṭiviṟku iṟutiyil iṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paṇṭai nāḷil īkāra vaṭivum iṟutiyiṟ cuḻi peṟṟa ikara vaṭivākavēyiruntatu ataip putuppittal vēṇṭum.
tēvanākari tōṉṟumuṉ vaṭamoḻikkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kiranta veḻuttil uḷḷa ōkāra vaṭivu, iṉṟu tamiḻil uḷḷatu pōṉṟatē. kiranta veḻuttu, tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintatu.
tolkāppiyar ekara okarattiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē eṉṟu avar cēranāṭṭu vaḻakkaiyē kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar vāḻntatum cēranāṭē. avar kālattil aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉum, kālamellām atu toṭara vēṇṭumeṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. avar kālamākiya ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ iṉṟu 24 nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭantuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tavaṟāṉa allatu paḻaimaipaṭṭa karuttukaḷait tiruttuvataṟku, atikāramuḷḷa nūlāciriyaṉukku ekkālattum urimaiyuṇṭu. ekara okaram paṟṟiya tolkāppiyar kūṟṟaiyum naṉṉūlārkku muṉpē oruvar māṟṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉaiyē.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi  (naṉ. 98)
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ, ellā eḻuttukaḷiṉ vaṭivum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavē. āyiṉ, akkālattil (paṇṭaik kālat til) ekara okara uyirkaḷum avaṟṟaik koṇṭa uyirmey yeḻuttukaḷum puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa; eṉṟē teḷḷat teḷivākak koḷḷak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl. āṇṭu ... puḷḷi eṉpatu mikaipaṭak kūṟalākavum, ciṟitum payaṉaṟṟa tākavum iruttal kāṇka. tollai vaṭiviṉa ellām eṉpatilēyē, ekara okara mey puḷḷipeṟṟamai aṭaṅkiyirukka ataṉaiyēṉ mīṇṭum eṭuttuk kūṟavēṇṭum! ākavē, tollai vaḻakkiṟkum uḷḷa vaḻakkiṟkum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭavē, piṟkūṟṟeḻunta teṉka.
itai nōkkātu, ellā veḻuttukaḷum palvēṟu vakaippaṭa eḻuti vaḻaṅkum paḻaiya vaṭiviṉaiyuṭaiyavām. av vaṭivai yuṭaiyaṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, ekaramum okaramum meykaḷum puḷḷiyaip peṟum eṉṟuraittār āṟumuka nāvalar. (19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu).
itaṉaiyē oṭṭiyuraittār vai.mu. caṭakōparāmāṉucāc cāriyār.
mayilainātarum, ellāveḻuttum paḻaiyatāka varukiṉṟa vari vaṭiviṉavē yām: avviṭattu ekaramum okaramum meykaḷum puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟpaṉavām eṉṟē kūṟi.
āṇṭu eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē, tātu, ētu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu āriya moḻikaḷum; eṭṭu, koṭṭu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup potumoḻikaḷum; kuṉṟiyātu. nāṭiyātu. eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup puṇarmoḻip poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kaḷum, aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukkumēṟ puḷḷi koṭuppārum uḷareṉak koḷka eṉṟu ciṟappuk kuṟippu varaintār.
iṉi, caṅkara namaccivāyarō, ellā veḻuttum palvēṟu vakaippaṭa varaintu vaḻaṅkum paḻaiya vaṭiviṉaiyuṭaiyavām. avvaṭiviṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, taṉittum uṭampūrntum varum ekaramum okaramun taṉimeykaḷum, iyalpāya puḷḷiyaip  piṟkālattu oḻittu varaintu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷōṭum uyirmeykaḷōṭum aiyappaṭa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉṟi, tuṇiyappaṭun tollai vaṭiviṉatu uṟuppāya puḷḷiyaip peṟum, eṉṟu nūṟpāviṉ piṟkūṟṟai viḷakkuvār pōṉṟu iṭaikkāla nilaimaiyaic cuṭṭi viritturaittār. ivar kālam 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
ivarellārum ivvāṟu vevvēṟu vakaiyil orē karuttup paṭa vuraikka, irāmāṉucak kavirāyarō veṉiṉ.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytumē kāramō kāramey puḷḷi
eṉṟu nūṟpāvaiyē māṟṟiyamaittu.
ellā eḻuttukaḷum palavēṟu vakaippaṭa eḻuti vaḻaṅkum paḻaiya vaṭivaiyē yuṭaiyaṉavām. avvāṟu vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷum taṉi meykaḷum paḻaiya puḷḷiyaip peṟum. 
ivvāṟu kūṟutalāṟ piṟkālattār antap puḷḷiyai nīkkic cantēkappaṭa vaḻaṅkivantaṉa reṉpatāyiṟṟu.
varalāṟu : e, ē, o, ō, ke, kē, ko, kō, k, ka, ṅ, ṅa eṉa varum. maṟṟa vuyirmeykaḷun taṉimeykaḷum ivvāṟē puḷḷi peṟumeṉak kāṇka. tollāciriyar mutalāyiṉōr.
paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakaiyi ṉāṉē
eṉṟum, iṟantatu vilakkal etiratu pōṟṟal eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyāl, ēkāram ōkāra mey puḷḷi peṟum eṉat tiruppavēṇṭiṟṟu. eṉṉeṉiṉ, ikkālattār ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷukkē puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutuvatu peruvaḻakkā yiṉamaiyā leṉka.
eṉṟu urai varaintiruppatu, peritum pōṟṟattakkatām. ivar kālam 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti kālattiṟkuppiṉ, tamiḻk kāppum kalveṭṭut toṭarpum tamiḻat tamiḻp pulavar kaiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭantu viṭṭamaiyālum, pōṟṟuvāriṉṟip pulavar marapu varavara aruki vantamaiyālum, vīramāmuṉivar kālattiṟ (18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) pulamaiyillātavar paṭiyeṭukkavum ōlaiyeḻutavum nērntuviṭṭamaiyāl, kuṟil neṭilākavum mey uyir meyyākavum paṭikka muṭiyāvāṟum paṭippārkkup poruḷ viḷaṅkāvāṟum tāṟumāṟāka eḻutappaṭṭatu kaṇṭu, am


muṉivar ekara okarak kuṟilukku mēṟ kuṟuṅkīccum meykku mēṟcuḻiyum vaitteḻutumāṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytār.
nīṭṭal cuḻittal
kuṟiṉmeyk kirupuḷḷi  (12)
eṉpatu avaratu toṉṉūl viḷakka nūṟpā.
iṉi, ēkāra ōkāra uyirmeykaṭkuk kē, kō eṉṟu iraṭṭaic cuḻikkompu amaittatum tāmēyeṉṟu avartam koṭun tamiḻ nūliṟ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷum uyirmeykaḷumō, iṉṟu tolkāp piyariṉ puḷḷipeṟātum, vīramāmuṉivariṉ neṭumpuḷḷi (nīṇṭa puḷḷi) yeṉṉum kuṟuṅkīccup peṟātum, ēkāra vaṭivu kīḻccāyppuk kīccum ōkāra vaṭivu kīḻccuḻiyum peṟṟum, tol kāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē ēṉaikkuṟil neṭilkaḷpōla iyaṟkaiyāka vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkoṇṭatupōlat tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum eṉṟum, oru tamiḻeḻuttum pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟō vaṭa veḻuttiṉiṉṟō tōṉṟavillaiyeṉṟum, tolkāppiyam, icainuṇukkam, mativāṇar nāṭakat tamiḻnūl mutaliya kiṟittu viṟku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭai nūlkaḷellām tamiḻeḻuttilēyē yaṉṟip pirāmiyeḻuttil ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil eḻutappaṭa villai yeṉṟum, ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirāmik kal veṭṭup paṇṭait tamiḻeḻuttiṟkuc cāṉṟākāteṉṟum. pirāmiyeḻuttum vaṭaveḻuttum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintaṉaveṉṟum, tamiḻē ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻi yeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
nēriḻaiyār kūntaliṉōr puḷḷipeṟa nīḷmaramām
nīrnilaiyōr puḷḷi peṟaneruppām - cīraṇavu
kāṭṭoṉ ṟoḻippa icaiyām ataṉaḷavu
mīṭṭoṉ ṟoḻippa miṭaṟu.
nīṇmaratti loṉṟēṟa nēriḻaiyār kūntalām 
pūneruppi loṉṟēṟap pūṅkuḷamām - pēṇuṅ
kaḻuttiloṉ ṟēṟa icaiyām icaiyiṉ
eḻuttiloṉ ṟēṟavāṅ kāṭu
eṉpaṉa, tolkāppiyar kūṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka iṭaik kālatteḻunta māttiraic curukkam, māttiraip perukkam eṉṉum collaṇip pāṭṭukaḷ.  - centamiḻc celvi mārccu 1979
eccam
eccam eṉṉum col, makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai maṭṭumaṉṟip peṟṟōriṉ mukattōṟṟamum kuṇavamaitiyum eñci niṟpataik kuṟikkum. ataṉāl, makkaḷ eṉṉum colliṉum (eccam eṉṉum col) takutiyum poruṭ polivum uṭaiyatām.  (ti.ma. 94.)
eṇṭicait talaivar
kiḻakkil vēntaṉ (intiraṉ)
intiraṉ eṉṟu paḻaimainūl kūṟuvatu palaviṭattil kaṭārat taracaṉaiyē. intiraṉ yāṉaikku veḷḷai niṟamum airāvatam eṉṉum peyarum kūṟuppaṭuvataiyum, kaṭārattiluḷḷa airāvati eṉṉum āṟṟup pāṅkaril veṇpukar yāṉai vativatākak kūṟappaṭuvataiyum, ilaṅkaiyilirunta arakkarum acurarum aṭikkaṭi intiraṉai veṉṟatākak kūṟuvataiyum, kaṭāram kiḻakkiliruppataiyum, intiraṉ kaṭalaip pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ mēl varaviṭṭa kataiyaiyum, mēkam kīḻkkaṭalil tōṉṟik koṇṭal eṉṟu peyar peṟuvataiyum, kaṭāramum malēyā vum iṉṟum āṭalpāṭalkaḷiṟ ciṟan tiruppataiyum, tēvarula kiṟku nākalōkam eṉṟoru peyariruppataiyum nōkkuka.
eṇṭicait talaivaruḷ, aracarellām oru kālattil oru talai muṟaiyil tattamakkuriya ticaiyiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷai āṇṭukoṇṭiruntavarē. kaṭārattaracaṉukku intiraṉ eṉṟu paṭṭappeya riruntirukkalām.
teṉkiḻakkil tī (akkiṉi)
jāvāviliruntu pilippaiṉ tīvuvaraiyum, iṉṟum palaviṭattil erimalaikaḷ erintu koṇṭiruppatait tiṇai nūliṟ kāṇka.
teṟkil kūṟṟuvaṉ (yamaṉ)
maṟattiṟ ciṟanta erumaikkaṭā (All About Animals, pp. 54, 55) maṟalikku ūrtiyākak kūṟappaṭṭatu.
teṉmēṟkil arakkaṉ (niruti)
teṉṉāppirikka makkaḷaiyum paṇṭu tamiḻakamum āppirikkāvum iṇaintiruntataiyum nōkkuka.
mēṟkil vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ)
vaṅkāḷakkuṭāvilirunta nilaṅkaṭku munti, arapikkaṭalilirunta nilam amiḻntu pōṉatāl, mēṟkil vāraṇaṉ kuṟikkappaṭṭāṉ.

vaṭamēṟkil kāṟṟu (vāyu)
itu orukāl cakārāp pālainilak kāṟṟāyirukkalām.
vaṭakkil kupēraṉ
ivaṉ irāvaṇaṉ kālattavaṉ. iv viruvarum muṟaiyē ilaṅkaiyilirunta iyakkar (yakṣar), arakkar (rākṣasar) eṉṉum kulaṅkaṭkut talaivar. iv viruvarkkum pōr nikaḻntatu. irāvaṇaṉ kupēraṉai veṉṟu avaṉ ūrtiyaiyum (puṣpaka vimāṉam) kavarntukoṇṭāṉ. piṉpu kupēraṉ vaṭaticaikkup pōy oru nāṭṭaiyāṇṭāṉ. īḻattil poṉṉum muttum ērāḷamāyakappaṭṭamaiyiṉ, avaṉ oru peruñ celvaṉāyp pala karuvūlaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ.
vaṭakiḻakkil īcāṉaṉ
īcāṉaṉ oru civavaṭivākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. itu malaimakaḷiṉ kūṟākak karutappaṭum taṭātakaip pirāṭṭi, vaṭakiḻakkut ticaiyi lirunta cōmacuntaraṉaik kaliyāṇañ ceytatiṉālēyē. o.mo.nū.

eṇperum peṟṟi = aṭṭamācitti
nuṇmai - aṇimā
parumai - makimā
noymmai - lakimā
paḷumai - karimā
viruppup pēṟu - pirāpti
viruppiṭam cēṟal - pirākāmiyam
iṟaimai - īcurattuvam
vayappaṭuttam - vacittuvam  (ta.i.va. 95.)
eṇvakai ōka uṟuppukaḷ
kaṭivu (iyamam), nōṉpu (niyamam), irukkai (ācaṉam), vaḷinilai (pirāṇāyāmam), orukkam (pirattiyākāram), niṟai (tāraṇai), ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), oṉṟukai (camāti) eṉpaṉa eṇvakai ōka uṟuppukaḷ.  (ti.ma. 50)
tokainilaiyai orukkam eṉṟum, poṟainilaiyai niṟai eṉṟum, nocippai oṉṟukai eṉṟum collappaṭum (va. aṭṭāṅka yōkam)  (ta.i.va. 99).

eṇ vēṟupāṭu
tiṇai pāl vēṟupāṭu tamiḻilakkaṇattil ēṟpaṭṭa piṉpum, āṇ peṇ eṉṉum pāṟpeyarkaḷum, tantai tāy eṉṉum muṟaip peyarkaḷum, makavu, piḷḷai, pārppu, kuṭṭi mutaliya iḷamaip peyarkaḷum, cāttaṉ, cātti mutaliya viravup peyar kaḷum mākkaḷ eṉṉum paṉmaippeyarum, kaṭaikkuṭṭi, piḷḷai kuṭṭi, kaṉṟu kayantalai eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷum, uḷḷāṉ, kattarippāṉ mutaliya āṉ īṟṟup peyarkaḷum, maṇ veṭṭi, kāṭaikkaṇṇi mutaliya ikara īṟṟup peyarkaḷum irutiṇaip potuvāy iruvakai vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varutalāṉ, mutaṟ kālattil ellāc coṟkaḷum tiṇai vēṟupāṭiṉṟi eṇ vēṟupāṭṭōṭu maṭṭum vaḻaṅki vantaṉa eṉpatum, makkaṭkum vilaṅkukaṭkum peritum vēṟupāṭillai eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.  (col. 20).
etirmaṟaiyiṉ etirmaṟai
veḷipṭaiyum kuṟippumāṉa etirmaṟaiyiṉ etirmaṟai uṭaṉpāṭu ātal paṟṟi iṉmaiyiṉ iṉmai vēṇṭum allatu vaṟumaiyiṉ vaṟumai vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu pōl maṭiyai maṭiyāka oḻukal eṉṟār.  (kuṟaḷ 602).

etu tēvamoḻi ?
ulakiluḷḷa (ēṟattāḻa) mūvāyira moḻikaḷuḷ, orucila, maṟai nūluṭaimai paṟṟit tammait tūyamoḻi (Holy Language) eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum camaṟkirutam taṉṉait tēvamoḻi (Divine Language) eṉṟum, pōṟṟip pukaḻntu koḷkiṉṟaṉa. tūya moḻi eṉpatiṉum tēvamoḻi eṉpatu uyarvāṉatu. muṉṉatu maṇṇulakattil teyvat taṉmai yaṭainta makkaḷ moḻi yeṉṟum. piṉṉatu viṇṇulakattiṉiṉṟō vīṭṭulakattiṉiṉṟō maṇṇulakattiṟku vanta tēvarmoḻi yeṉṟum, karuttup piṟappippaṉa.
ulakil oru moḻiyum tēvamoḻi yaṉṟu. oṉṟait tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, atu puṉainturaivakaiyākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam puṉainturai vakaiyiṟ kuṟittaṟkum. kīḻk kuṟikkappeṟum kuṇaṅkaḷaṉaittum ataṟkiruttal vēṇṭum.
(1) ulakamoḻi mutaṉmai
(2) oliyeḷimai
(3) paṉmoḻit tāymai
(4) oppuyarvaṟṟa paṇpāṭu
(5) tūymai
(6) maṟainūlum palkalai ilakkiyamum uṇmai 
(7) makkaṭ potuvurimai
(8) naṭunilai aṟaṅkūṟal
iv veṇṇiyalpukaḷum oruṅkē tamiḻukkuḷa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭē camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷaṉa.
(1) vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa vēta āriya moḻiyum akkālattu vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷākiya (tamiḻ uṭpaṭṭa) pirākiruta moḻi kaḷum kalanta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻiyē vaṭamoḻiyeṉṟu ciṟappāyk kūṟappaṭum camaṟkirutamām.
(ki.mu. 2000).
tamiḻō, māntaṉ mutal mutal tōṉṟiya (Lemuria v‹D«) kumarināṭṭil, tāṉē tōṉṟiya iyaṟkai moḻiyām(ki.mu. 50,000).
ōṅka liṭaivantu ... taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ eṉṉum paḻaiya taṉippāvum itaṉaip pulappaṭuttum.
(2)		āṟṟal aḻiyumeṉ ṟantaṇarkaḷ nāṉmaṟaiyaip 
	pōṟṟiyurait tēṭṭiṉ puṟatteḻutār - ēṭṭeḻuti
	vallunarum vallārum vaḷḷuvaṉār muppālaic 
	colliṭiṉum āṟṟalcōr viṉṟu.
eṉṟu kōtamaṉārum.

vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻi māṇpu.
eṉṟu māṅkuṭi marutaṉārum, kūṟiyavaiyē. tamiḻoli meṉmaikkum camaṟkirutavoli vaṉmaikkum pōtiya cāṉṟām.
(3) tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāyāyiruppatu pōṉṟē. camaṟkirutam vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaṭkellām tāy eṉṟoru tavaṟāṉa karuttu neṭuṅkālamāka nilavi varukiṉṟatu. oru kālattilum ulaka vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkāta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiyak kalavaimoḻi, eṅṅaṉam tāymoḻiyā yiruttal kūṭum? oru moḻi mutaṟkaṇ taṉimoḻiyā yiruntālaṉṟō piṉṉart tāymoḻiyāyum amaital kūṭum! tamiḻ, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum. āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamumāyirukkum pōtu, av vāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oliyaḷavil mutu vaḷarcci yaṭainta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyākiya camaṟ kirutam eṅṅaṉam uṇmaiyil paṉmoḻit tāyāyiruttal ollum?

(4)		kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
	paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
	maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
	eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar tarukkumāṟu, ilakkaṇac cemmai yum vēṟem moḻiyiṉumillāta poruḷilakkaṇamumuṭaiya tamiḻē moḻip paṇpāṭṭil talaiciṟantatām.
(5) peruñ colvaḷa muṭaiyatum piṟamoḻit tuṇai vēṇṭātatum taṉittu vaḷarntōṅka vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē.
aṉṟiyum tamiḻnūṟ kaḷavilai avaṟṟuḷ
oṉṟē yāyiṉum taṉittami ḻuṇṭō?
eṉṉum cuvāmināta tēcikar kūṟṟu moḻiyārāycciyillāta iruṭkālat teḻuntatātaliṉ, atu iṟṟaikkup poruḷaṟṟa teṉa viṭukka.
camaṟkirutattiṟkuḷḷa colvaḷam paṉmoḻic cērkkaiyāl uṇṭāṉatātaliṉ, atuvum ciṟappilateṉṟu viṭukka.
(6)	pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē  (1336)
	maṟaimoḻi kiḷanta mantirat tāṉa  (1421)
	niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
	maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa  (1434)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyar kūṟṟum.
ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam
tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampanīr nilamu lōkam
māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa
eṉṉum paḻantaṉippāṭalum, paṇṭait tamiḻil maṟainūlum palkalai ilakkiyamum iruntu piṉṉar iṟantupaṭṭamaiyai uṇarttum. paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyam iṟantupaṭṭamai yiṉālēyē, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam iṉṟu mutal tōṉṟiya mūlamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
(7) āriya vētattaiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷaiyum tamiḻarāṉa cūttirar ōtiṉ, avar nāvai aṟuttuviṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟum, avaṟṟaik kēṭpiṉ avar kātil urukkiṉa īyattai vārttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āriya aṟanūlkaḷ aṟaikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻaiyum tamiḻ nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟaṟkum kēṭṭaṟkumō ettakaiya kulamata kaṭciyiṉa vilakkum vēṟupāṭumillai.
ceyyā moḻikkum tiruvaḷ ḷuvarmoḻinta
poyyā moḻikkum poruḷoṉṟē - ceyyā
ataṟkuriyar antaṇarē ārāyiṉ ēṉai

(8) āriya aṟanūlkaḷ yāvum kaṭukaḷavum naṭunilaiyaṟiyātavai yeṉpatu. avai kulattiṟkoru muṟaiyum taṇṭamum kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu teḷivāy aṟiyappaṭum.
vaḷḷuvarcey tirukkuṟaḷai maṟuvaṟanaṉ kuṇarntōrkaḷ
uḷḷuvarō maṉuvāti orukulattiṟ korunīti
eṉṉum cuntarampiḷḷai vāyurai īṇṭuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ pallāṟṟāṉum talaiciṟanta moḻiyāyum, civa neṟiyum tirumālneṟiyum tūyatamiḻ mataṅkaḷāyum iruppavum tamiḻ potuvaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takāta moḻiyeṉṟu taḷḷap paṭṭapiṉṉum tamiḻar āriyariṉum tāḻntavareṉṟu koḷḷap paṭṭapiṉṉum, tamiḻum tamiḻilakkiyamum iḻappiṟkum aḻip piṟkum iṭaṉāy iṟṟaic ciṟumaiyaṭaintuḷḷaṉa. aṟivum ārāycciyum mikka ikkālattilēṉum, tamiḻar viḻitteḻuntu tam muṉṉōr nilaimaiyaṭaiya muyalvārāka.  (teṉmoḻi  cepṭampar 1959)

taṉiyuyirāṉa ā, ī, ū, ē, ō eṉṉum iyaṟkai neṭilaintum viḷiyacaikaḷākap payaṉpaṭum. ātalāl viḷiyurupāka amaiyum. ivaṟṟuḷ, ī, ū eṉṉum iraṇṭum perumpālum, muṟaiyē ikara vukaraṅkaḷiṉ nīṭṭamākavē yirukkum. evvuyirāyiṉum, cēymai viḷiyellām neṭilākavē yiruttal kūṭum.

viḷiyacaikaḷ, muṉṉacai piṉṉacai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. ā muṉṉacaiyāka varuvatillai. ē ō iraṇṭum muṉṉacaiyākavum piṉṉacai yākavum varum.

muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṟ keḻutakaip potuccol
nilaikkuri marapiṉ iruvīṟṟu murittē.  (tol. po. poru. 26)
itu, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti pāṅkaṉ pāṅkiyeṉṉu muṟaip peyarākiya colpaṟṟip piṟantatōr vaḻu amaikkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- muṟaip peyariṭattu iru pāṟkum poruntiṉa takutiyaiyuṭaiya ellā veṉṉuñcol, pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟkuriya muṟaimaiyiṉāṉē vaḻuvākātu, āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum oppa uriyatāy vaḻaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
keḻutakai eṉṟataṉāṉē, talaiviyum tōḻiyum talaivaṉaik kūṟiyatē perumpāṉmai yeṉṟum, talaivaṉ talaiviyaiyum pāṅkaṉaiyuṅ kūṟutal ciṟupāṉmai vaḻuvamaitiyeṉṟum, koḷka.
