aṉṟām tāmtāṉ kiṉṟuniṉ ṟacaimoḻi  (naṉ. 441)
aṭē - va. arē. aṭē -ṭē - va. rē.
va. arē arē - arērē - ararē.
māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutali arē, ararē, arērē, rē eṉṉun tiricoṟkaḷ paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
arē - ind. interjection of calling (VS).
ararē - ind. a vocative particle (expressing haste) - L.
arērē - ind. (repetition of are) interjection of calling to inferiors or of calling angrily (L).
rē - ind. a vocative particle (generally used contemptuously or to express disrespect, often doubled) - Ka#v; Kathas etc.
ind = indeclinable.
VS = Va#jasneyi Samhita# vāja nēyi samhitā)
L = Lexicographers (akara mutaliyāḷar)
Kav = Ka#vya literature (kāviya ilakkiyam)
āṅkilar naṇparaik kāṇumpōtu makiḻcci viḷiyāka āḷum halō (hallō) eṉṉuñ col, ellā eṉṉum teṉcol loṭu toṭar puṭaiyatē.

hallo, halloa, int., n., and v.i. Int. calling attention or expr. surprise; informal greeting; (n. and v. i.) the cry ‘hallo’. var. of earlier hollo.
halloo, int. inciting dogs to the chase, calling attention or expressing surprise; (n.) the cry ‘halloo’; (perh.) var. of hollo; (v.i. and t.) Cry ‘halloi’, esp. to dogs; urge on (dogs etc.) with shouts; shout (t. and i.) to attract attention.
hallow, v. t. and i. Chase with shouts; incite with shouts; shout to incite dogs etc. (ME).
halowen prok. f. OF halloer.
hello, n., and v.i. hallo.
holla, int. calling attention f. F. hola.
hollo, int. calling attention; (n.) the cry ‘hollo’. (conn. w. holla.
hollo, hollow, holla, holloa, v. l, and t, shout (i and t); call to hounds (as prec).
hullo, hulloa, int. used to call attention, express surprise or answer call. esp. on telephone. of hallo.
ēl eṉpatu mutal rē eṉpatu varai kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa palvēṟu coṟkaḷellām, orē toṭarpuḷḷa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷ eṉpatu teḷḷat teḷivām.
avaṟṟuḷ, rē eṉṉum iṟutinilait tiricol maṭṭum vaṭamoḻi yilakkiyattil, atilum perumpālum piṟkāla ilakkiyattilum akaramutali kaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatum; muntu nilait tiricollāṉa ella (ellā) eṉpatai yotta hallō eṉṉum viḷiccol, āṟāyiram kal tolaivi luḷḷa āṅkila nāṭṭil toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatum kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
āṅkilac col vēṭṭai nāyai ēvum collākap payaṉ paṭuttappaṭiṉum, viḷiyaḷavil oṉṟē yeṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭai āṅkilac coṟkaḷ teṉ collum teṉcoṟ ṟiripumāyiruppatāl, ellā eṉṉum irupāṟ potu viḷiccol āṅkilattil vaḻaṅki varutal paṟṟi aiyuṟutaṟku eḷḷaḷavum iṭamillai.
kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu kaṅkaikkarai ceṉṟa paṇṭait tamiḻar tiraviṭarākat tirintapiṉ, airōppā aṭaintu āriyarāka māṟiyiruppatālum, avvāriyaruḷ oru kūṭṭattārē vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōrāka intiyāviṟku vantiruppatālum, teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivaṅkaḷ mēlaiyāriya moḻi 
kaḷilum pintuvaṭivaṅkaḷ kīḻaiyāriyamākiya vētamoḻiyilum camaṟkirutat tilum vaḻaṅkuvatu iyaṟkaiyē.
mēlaiyar ulaka muṉ moḻiyākiya tamiḻai aṭippaṭaiyākak koḷḷātu ataṉ tiripuk koṭumuṭiyākiya camaṟkirutattaiyē mūlamākak koṇṭu ārāyntu varuvatāl, moḻinūl paṟṟiya aṭippaṭai yuṇmaikaḷai aṟiyātu, vaḻitappiya cakārāp pālai nila vaḻippōkkar pōl aṅkumiṅkum cuṟṟic cuṟṟi iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyeṉṟum āriyam ataṉ tirimoḻi yeṉṟum aṟiyumvarai, avar tam kuruṭṭuk koḷkaiyil nilaittē niṟpar. ak kuruṭṭāṭṭam nīṅkuṅ kālamum aṭuttu varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ta.nā.co)
ellārāycciyum collārāycciyā?
makkaḷ aṟivaṭaiyum vaḻikaḷ, kalvi, kēḷvi, ārāycci, tuyppu eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. oruvar piṟar eḻuti vaittatait tāmāyk kaṟṟaṟivatu kalvi. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi aṟiñaraiyaṭuttut tamakku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kēṭṭaṟivatu kēḷvi; ivviraṇṭumaṉṟi, oṉṟaip putuvatākat turuviyāyntaṟivatu ārāycci; iṉi vāḻnāḷil paṟpala vakaiyil tāmē tuyttu aṟivatu tuyppu. (aṉupavam). ivaṟṟil kalvi eṉpatu ikkālattil kēḷviyaiyun taḻuvum.
ārāycci ārāyap peṟum poruḷ nōkkip palatiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ collārāycciyum oṉṟu. atu moḻiyārāycci yuṭpaṭṭatu. oru moḻikkuṭpaṭṭa colliṉ allatu coṟ kaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai āyvatu collārāycci; oru moḻikkup piṟa moḻiyōṭu allatu moḻikaḷōṭu uḷḷa toṭarpai āyvatu moḻiyārāycci. moḻikaḷellām perumpālum cilavum palavumāyt tammuṭ toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl, moḻiyā rāycciyillātār ceyyum collārāycci kaṭṭappaṭuvatē.
collārāycci ceyya virumpum oruvar mutaṟkaṇ, ētēṉu moru mēlai moḻiyil, ciṟappāka āṅkilattil, uḷḷa moḻinūlkaḷaiyum collārāycci nūlkaḷaiyum colliya lakarātikaḷaiyum kaṟṟal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam kaṟkumpōtē, moḻiyoliyiyal (Phonology), col vaṭiviyal (Morphology), poruṭpāṭṭiyal (Semasiology) mutaliya moḻi nūṟṟuṟai kaḷaic cevvaṉ uṇarntukoḷḷutal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ collārāycci ceyya virumpum moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇattaik kaṟpatuṭaṉ, atiluḷḷa coṟ kuṭumpaṅkaḷaiyellām taṉit taṉiyākavum tokuti tokutiyākavum nōkki, avaṟṟiṉ toṭarpukaḷai aṟintukoḷḷutal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar am moḻic coṟkaḷai am moḻik kuṭumpattaic cērnta piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷōṭum oppunōkkik kāṇṭal vēṇṭum. collārāyccikkuc col varicaikaḷai oppu nōkkuvatiṉum, ilakkaṇa neṟi muṟaikaḷai oppunōkkuvatē mikutiyum vēṇṭap peṟuvatākum.
moḻinūl allatu colliyalnūl, kaṇitampōlat taṟcārpuk kalaiyaṉṟu. ataṟkup piṟakalaiyaṟivum iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappeṟum.  uḷanūl (Psychology), varalāṟṟu nūl  (History), ñālanūl (Geography) māntaṉūl (Anthropology) eṉpaṉa moḻi nūṟkup peritun tuṇai ceyyum. vaṇikam paṟṟi oru moḻiyiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru moḻikkuc ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuñ coṟkaṭkellām varalāṟṟaṟivum ñāla nūlaṟivum iṉṟiya maiyātaṉa.
e-ṭu: tuki (tōkai), teak (tēkku).
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu karuvi nūlkaḷuṅ kalaikaḷum kaivarappeṟṟa piṉ, ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa colliyal neṟi muṟaikaḷaiyum, ārāyvāṉ eṭuttukkoṇṭa oru moḻik kēyuriya collākka neṟi muṟaikaḷaiyum aṟintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉa vāyiṉum pala coṟku moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā ātaliṉ avai iṭukuṟiyeṉak koḷḷutal kūṭātu. caṟṟu āḻntu nōkkiṉ avaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ mella mella miḷirntu tōṉṟum. cila coṟkaḷ kālakkaṭappil mikamikat tirintum iṉac coṟkaḷai iḻantum vērccol vaḻakkaṟṟum pōṉatiṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ ettuṇai āḻntu nōkkiṉum tōṉṟuvatillai. avaṟṟuḷ oru cilavaṟṟiṟku. ayaṉ moḻiyiluḷḷa iṉaccoṟkaḷ vēr kāṭṭiṉuṅ kāṭṭum.
kaṭalai, koṭukku mutaliya pala coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ nōkkiya maṭṭil tōṉṟum. maram poṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷ āḻntu nōkkiṉālaṉṟit tōṉṟā. tamiḻ, karuvi mutaliya cila coṟkaḷiṉ vērp poruḷ āḻntu nōkki ṉum tōṉṟuvatillai. poruntap pukalalāka oru coṟku poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu. oru coṟkuk kāṭṭum vēr ellāt taṭaikaṭkum viṭaiyamaiyumāṟu ulakat tōṭum, kalaikaḷōṭum, malaiyātiruttal vēṇṭum. ampu (vaḷaiyal) eṉṉum colliṉ vērp poruḷ, amphi (around) eṉṉum kirēkkac collāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
oru colliṉ poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kāṇutaṟku mutaṟkaṇ, ac colliṉ tiruntiya vaṭivaik kaṇṭukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. maṇattakkāḷi eṉpataṉ tiruntiya vaṭivu maṇittakkāḷi eṉpatu. takkāḷi iṉattiṟ ciṟiyatu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. maṇi eṉpatu oru kuṟumaip poruḷ muṉṉoṭṭu. maṇattakkāḷi eṉṉum tavaṟṟu vaṭiviṟku, maṇamuḷḷa takkāḷi eṉṟutāṉ poruḷ kūṟa muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉamē ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
oru colliṉ tiruntiya vaṭivaik kaṇṭapiṉ, ac collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpai nōkka vēṇṭum. oru poruḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpu, ap poruḷ muḻumaiyun taḻuviya tākavō ataṉ oru maruṅkupaṟṟiyatākavō irukkalām. pariti (vaṭṭamāṉatu) eṉpatu muḻutun taḻuviyatu. vāḻai (vaḻavaḻap pāṉatu) eṉpatu oru maruṅku (aṭimaram) paṟṟiyatu. vāḻaiyiṉuñ ciṟappākat toṭarntu vāḻvatu mūṅkilātalāl, vāḻvatu vāḻai eṉpatu poruntātu.
pala kuṇaṅkaḷ pala poruṭkum potuvāyiruttaliṉ, ciṟappiyal peṉṟatu perumpālum oru cār poruḷkaṭkuc ciṟappā yiruppatē.
e-ṭu: vaḷḷam, vaḷḷi, vaḷai, vaḷaiyam, vaḷaiyal, vaṇṭu, vaṇṭi, vaṇar, vaṇakkam, vaṭṭu, vaṭṭi, vaṭṭil, vaṭṭai ivaiyellām vaḷaintatu allatu vaḷaiyamāyiruppatu eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭavaiyē.
kuṇam eṉpatu toḻilaiyum taḻuvum.
e-ṭu: : kēḻal (nilattaik kiḷaippatu)
oppumaiyum oru kuṇamē.
e-ṭu: nuṇā (nuṇal pōluṅ kāyaiyuṭaiyatu).
oru collāṉ pala karuttukaḷ eḻuppap peṟalām.avaṟṟiṉ muṉmai piṉmait toṭarpu aṟiyappaṭṭa piṉṉarē. moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam tuṇiyappeṟal vēṇṭum. cilaviṭattup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ otta poruttamuṭaiyaṉavākat tōṉṟum. avaṟṟuḷ mikap poruttamāṉatai ēraṇa muṟaiyilum oppu nōkkiyumē tuṇital kūṭum.
e-ṭu:: viḻā eṉṉuñ coṟku viḻuttal, viḻaital eṉṉum iraṇṭum poruṭ kāraṇamākat tōṉṟalām. viḻuttal ciṟattal, viḻaital virumputal, virumpic ceyvatu eṉṉuṅ kāraṇattiṉum ciṟappuc ceyvatu eṉṉuṅ kāraṇamē porutta mām. ciṟappoṭu pūcaṉai eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruppatālum ciṟappuc ceytal eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmaiyālum piṉṉataṉ mikuporuttam aṟiyappaṭum. viḻu - viḻā, vēḷviyē virumpic ceyyappaṭuvatu. vēḷviyoṭu ceyyappaṭum viḻākkaḷ, ākupeyar muṟaiyil vēḷvi yeṉappeṟum.
iṉi coṟṟirivu muṟaikaḷaiyum muṟpaṭa aṟintukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. e-ṭu:: iru kuṟil oru neṭilākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟaiyāl, akal - āl (viḻutūṉṟip paṭarum ālamaram), vaṇaṅku - vāṅku mutaliyaṉavum; ṭakaram, rakaramākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟaiyāl, paṭavar - paravar, viṭicci - viricci (maṟaiporuḷai veḷippaṭuttalāṉa pākkattu viricci) mutaliyaṉavum, ḻakaram ṭakaramākat tiriyum eṉṉum tirivu muṟaiyāl, puḻal - puṭal (puḻalai - puṭalai), kuḻal - kuṭal mutaliyaṉavum il īṟu oru kuṟumaip poruṭpiṉṉoṭṭu eṉpatāl, toṭṭi - toṭṭil, kuṭi - kuṭil mutaliyaṉavum; am īṟu oru perumaip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu eṉpatāl, mati - matiyam (muḻumati), nilai - nilaiyam mutaliyaṉavum; ukaram akaramākat tiriyum tirivu muṟaiyāl, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu. nurai - narai (veṇmai) mutaliyaṉavum; ukara ūkāram muṟaiyē ikara īkāramākat tiriyum tirivu muṟaiyāl puraṇṭai - piraṇṭai, tūṇṭu - tīṇṭu mutaliyaṉavum; viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
centamiḻc colliyal neṟi muṟaikaḷum (Principles of Etymology) coṟṟirivu muṟaikaḷum (Modes of Derivation) ettuṇaiyō pala.
(1) olimuṟaic colliyal (Sound Etymology)
e-ṭu: pārāḷumaṉṟu - parliament pārāḷumaṉṟu eṉpatu pār āḷum maṉṟu eṉṉum muccoṟṟoṭar. Parliament eṉpatu par-ler (speak) eṉṉum pireñcuc collum ment eṉṉum āṅkila vikutiyum cērnta orē col.
(2) uṉṉippuc colliyal (Guessing Etymology)
e-ṭu: aṇil = aḻaku (aḻakiya varikaḷ) uṭaiyatu.
aṇi = aḻaku. kalam = kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. 
vēntaṉ = vemmaiyāy atikārañ celuttupavaṉ. vēm = vēntaṉ.
(3) aṟivākulac colliyal (Pedantic Etymology)
eṭu. kiḷi = kiḷappatu (pēcum paṟavai)
maṇ = maṇappatu (nāṟṟamuṭaiyatu)
(4) aṭippaṭṭa colliyal (Popular Etymology)
e-ṭu: nūl (puttakam) = iḻainūlum eṟṟu nūlum pōlvatu.
(5) kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) 
e-ṭu: : aiyaṉ < āriyaṉ (ārya)
accaṉ < ajja (pirākirutam) < ārya
veṟukkai (celvam) = veṟukkappaṭuvatu.
(6) vaḻūup pakuppuc colliyal (Malanalytic Etymology) 
e-ṭu:: cāppāṭu - cāvataṟku ētuvāṉatu (cā + pāṭu)
(7) nakaiyāṭṭuc colliyal (Playful Etymology)
e-ṭu: :  tōcai irumuṟai cai eṉṟu olippatu. 
tō (urutu) = iraṇṭu.
iṅku kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷaiyellām maṟukkavum viḷakkavum pukiṉ viriyumātaliṉ avaṟṟuḷ nāṉkai maṭṭum īṇṭārāyccik keṭuttukkoḷval.
(1) aṇil. aṇi = vari, varicai
aṇi - aṇil = mutukil mūvarikaḷai yuṭaiyatu.
(2) vēntaṉ : vēytal = mēlaṇital, muṭicūṭutal. vēy = vēyntōṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇiyum urimaiyuḷḷa cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyaruḷ oruvaṉ. vēḷ, maṉṉaṉ, kō, vēntaṉ eṉṉum nālvakaiyaracar peyaruḷ, vēntaṉ eṉpatu muṭiyaṇiyum urimaiyuḷḷa mūvēntarkkē potuvākavuriyatām. ivvurimai kiṟittuvukku muṟpaṭṭa kālamellām kaiyāḷap peṟṟu vantatu.
koṉṟai vēntaṉ (civaṉ) eṉpatu koṉṟai vēyntōṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
vēntu eṉṉum pālīṟillāp paḻavaṭivamum vēntaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatē; ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉai, muṟṟum ātal pōṉṟu.
(3) kiḷi, kiḷḷai : kiḷ - kiḷḷi - kiḷi, kiḷ - kiḷḷai, kiḷḷutal = kūriya mūkkāl kottutal, maraṅkaḷiluḷḷa kāykaṉikaḷaik kiḷḷi vaippatu kiḷiyiṉ iyalpu. paḻakāta kiḷiyai oruvaṉ piṭippāṉāyiṉ, uṭaṉē akappaṭṭa uṟuppaik kāyappaṭa valitāyk kottiviṭum kiḷi kottiṉa paḻam eṉpatu vaḻakku. pēcum iyalpu kiḷikkillai. collik koṭuttataic col lum iyalpē ataṟkuṇṭu. coṉṉataic collum kiḷip piḷḷai eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. coṉṉataic collum tiṟattil, kiḷiyiṉuñ ciṟantatu pūvaiyeṉa aṟika.


(4) accaṉ 
attaṉ - accaṉ (āṇpāl) tantai
atti - acci (peṇpāl) tāy, akkai.
ivai tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ. ta - ca : pōli.
o. nō. : pittaṉ - piccaṉ.
attaṉ, accaṉ eṉpaṉa muṟaippeyar.
āriyaṉ eṉpatu oru makkaḷiṉap peyar; attuṭaṉ ōr āriyac col.
attaṉ, accaṉ eṉpaṉavum atti acci eṉpaṉavum muṟaiyē āṇpālīṟākavum peṇpāl īṟākkavum eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻp peyarkaḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: taṭṭātti, vaṇṇātti, 
kiḷḷicci, vēṭṭuvacci - peṇpāl.
aracaṉ eṉṉum collē vaṭanāṭṭil ajja eṉṉum pirākirutac collāka varukiṉṟateṉka.
(1) palamoḻikaḷ tammuḷ toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāl collārāyccikku moḻiyārāycciyum vēṇṭum.
(2) coṟkaḷ kuṭumpam kuṭumpamāy iyalvatāl, avaṟṟait tokuttu nōkkiyē mūlaṅkāṇutal vēṇṭum; eṉṉum iru neṟimuṟaikaḷai yuṇarttutaṟku, oru coṟṟokutiyai īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām.
mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ oru cila :
kulavu - L. curvo, to bend, E. curve
kuraṅku (vaḷaivu, kokki), kuṟaṅku (kokki) - D. kring, krink, a curl bend, crook
E. crank to bend, wind and turn
E. cringe A.S. Cringan, Crincan to bend with servility. 
kaṟaṅku (vaḷaivu, vaṭṭam, kāṟṟāṭi, cuḻal) - A.S. hring O.H.G. hing Icel, hringr, G. ring, D. ring, Sw. ring, E ring.
Sw. kring, about, around; Icel, kringer, a circle,
Prov G. krink, kring, a ring, circle.
kuravai - Gk. choros, a dance in a ring. L. chorus E. chorus.
kuruḷ - D. krullen, Dan krolle, E. crull, curl,
kuruku - A.S. cran, D. kraan, G. krahu, kranich; Icel, trans, Dan 			     trane. Armor. karan, W. Garan, Gk. geranos, L. grus, E. crane.
kokki - A.S. hoc. E. hook. D. hock, Icel. haki, G. haken, O.H.G. hako, 	                   L.G. hake.
koṭukku - Icel. crokr, Sw. krok, Don crog. O.E. crok, E. croog, Dan crog, D. kruk, W. crwg, Gael, crocan.
O.F. croc. ṭa.ra. pōli o.nō. kuṭaku - coorg, aṭaikkāy - areca
kōṇam - Gk. Gonia, an angle.
kōṭṭai - L. castrum. E. caster.
mēṟkāṭṭiya teṉcoṟkaḷum, mēlaiyāriyac coṟkaḷum, collaḷavil maṭṭumaṉṟi aṭippakutiyilum oliyum poruḷum ottiruppataik kāṇalām. avaṟṟuḷ mūlam evai, tiripu evai eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittaṟku, ac coṟkaḷellām em moḻiyil oruṅkē vaḻimuṟait toṭarpuṟṟu oru kuṭumpamāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kāṇutal vēṇṭum. avai emmoḻiyil eṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉavō am moḻiyē avaṟṟuk kellām mūlam eṉpatu muṭinta muṭipākum. pala iṭaṅkaḷil oru marattiṉ ilaiyum pūvum kāyum kaṉiyumākiya ciṉaikaḷē citaṟikkiṭakkumāyiṉ, avai aṇmaiyilō, cēymaiyilō uḷḷa oru marattiṉiṉṟē paṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku māṟāka, ac ciṉaikaḷiliruntē am maram vantateṉṟu valippatu uttikkum uṇmaikkum poruttāta iṭumpuk kūṟṟākum. ōr ūriluḷḷa makkaḷ vēṟṟūr ceṉṟu vāḻiṉ, toṉṟutoṭṭu avvūrārē eṉat tammaic collik koḷḷa muṭiyātu.aṅṅaṉam collutaṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu varum uṟaiyuḷ, uṟavu, naṭpu, ayal mutaliyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum. mēṟkāṭṭiya āriyac coṟkaḷ mēlai moḻi kaḷil āṅkāṅkē oṉṟiraṇṭākac citaṟik kiṭappaṉavē yaṉṟi, tamiḻiṟpōl maraput toṭarpuṟṟa oru kuṭumpamāka allatu kulamāka vaḻaṅkivaruvaṉa valla. āriya moḻikaḷil nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ collālum, poruḷālum teṉcoṟkaḷai ottiruppatāl, avaṟṟait taṟceyalāka nērnta oppumaikaḷeṉat taḷḷiviṭavum muṭiyātu. avaṟṟiṉ uṇmait toṭarpaik kāṭṭutaṟku moḻi nūloṭu varalāṟu mutaliya piṟa cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟkaṭkellām mūlam teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu. piṉvarum coṟṟokutiyāl aṟiyalākum.
kul eṉpatu vaḷaivup poruḷ tarum oru vērccol. ataṉiṉṟu, vaḷaivu, kōṇal, vaṭṭam, vaḷaiyam, uruṭci, tiraṭci, kuḻaṟci, cuḻaṟci, cuṟṟal, cūḻal mutaliya pala uṟaviyaṟ karuttuḷḷa paṟpala coṟkaḷ toṭarntu kiḷaittuḷḷaṉa.

ik kuṭumpattaic cērnta piṟa coṟkaḷumuḷa.  (tamiḻppoḻil mē. 1956.)
eḻuttamaippu
īkārattiṉiṉṟu ēkāramum, ūkārattiṉiṉṟu ōkāramum mōṉait tirivākat tōṉṟiṉa.
mutaṟkaṇ neṭilākavē tōṉṟiya uyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilākak kuṟukiṉa.
akaramum ikaramum ai eṉṟum, akaramum ukaramum cērntu au eṉṟum iru puṇarolikaḷ (Diphthongs) eḻuntaṉa. avai oli yaḷavil muṟaiyē ay, av eṉa olittaṉa.  (ta.i.va. muṉṉurai.)
eḻuttu
mutaṉ mutalāka makkaḷ amaittatu paṭa eḻuttē. eḻuttu eṉṉum col mutaṉ mutalāka ōviyattaiyē kuṟittatu. iṉṟum paṭameḻututal eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka. iṉṉa palapala eḻuttu nilai maṇṭapam eṉṉum paripāṭal aṭiyil (19:53) eḻuttu eṉpatu ōviyattai uṇarttiṟṟu. 
ōviyaṉ kaiviṉai pōla eḻutappaṭuvatu uruveḻuttākum eṉpatu yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōḷ. in nūṟpāviṉiṉṟu muṟkālattirunta tamiḻeḻuttu paṭaveḻuttē (Hieroglyph) eṉṟu tuṇiyalām.
eḻututal
eḻuttu kōṭṭālamaivatālum, muṉṉiruntu piṉ allatu mēliruntu kīḻ iḻuttē iyalpāka naṭṭukkōṭu varaiyap paṭuvatālum, iḻuttaṟ karuttil eḻututaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. kōṭiḻuttal eṉṉum vaḻakku iyalpākak kōṭu kīṟum ticaiyai uṇarttum.
il - ilaku - ilakku = (eḻuttu, col, nūl)
ilakkutal = iḻuttu varaital, varaital, eḻututal.
irēkai yilakkuka (caivaca. potu. 274.)
ilakku - ilakkiyam = nūl, nūṟṟokuti.
ilakku - ilakkaṇam = nūṉmoḻi yoḻuṅku, ataik kūṟum nūl.
ilakku - ilakki. ilakkittal = varaital.
ivvuruvu neñceṉṉum kiḻiyiṉ mēlirun tilakkittu  (cīvaka. 180)
ilakkutal = eḻututal, kuṟittal.
ilakku = kuṟi, kuṟitta iṭam, iṭam, kuṟipporuḷ, nōkkam.
ilakku - ilakkam = eṇkuṟi, eṇ.
iḻu - iḻuku. iḻukutal = iḻuttut taṭavutal, taṭavutal.
iḻu - (iḻutu) - eḻutu - eḻuttu = varaivu, ōviyam, vari, ilakkiyam.
eḻututal = iḻuttu varaital, varaital.
eḻututal eṉṉum poruḷil, ilakku eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭamoḻiyil lik eṉṟu tiriyum.
ilakku eṉṉum col mikap paḻaimaiyāṉatātalāl, ataṉ pakuti iṉṟu iḻuttaṟ karuttai veḷippaṭaiyāy uṇartta villai. kumarināṭum toṉṉūlum pala paḻañ coṟkaḷum maṟaintu pōṉamaiyum itaṟkuk kāraṇamām.  (mu.tā.)
eḻutīvukaḷ
oru kālattil ñālanilappakuti ēḻu kaṇṭaṅkaḷākavum irunta tākat terikiṉṟatu. avai oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tīrntiruntamaiyāl tīvukaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. (tīrvu - tīvu). avai peru nilappakutikaḷāta lāl tīvam eṉṟum collappaṭum (tīvu - tīvam).
eḻupiṟappu
nilaittiṇai (tāvaram), nīrvāḻvaṉa, ūrvaṉa, paṟavai, vilaṅku, makkaḷ, tēvar eṉṉum piṟappukaḷ ēḻiṉum tīyavai tīṇṭā eṉpatu piṟappiṉ vakai paṟṟiyum tokai paṟṟiyum poruntā maiyiṉ eḻumakkaṭ piṟappām.  (ti.ma. 70.)
eḷḷutal
eḷḷutalāvatu pārttalum uraiyāṭutalum ceyyāmai.  (kuṟaḷ 752)
eḷḷutal eṉṉum uḷaviṉai, ikaḻtalākiya vāyviṉaiyai yum puṟakkaṇittalākiya ceyviṉaiyaiyum taḻuvum.
eṉ ulakkai kuttuk kuttu viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭu muṟai : irupatu ciṟumiyar pōl oru vīṭṭu muṟṟattil vaṭṭamāka neruṅkiyiruntu, naṭuvaṇ (maiyam) nōkkik kālnīṭṭi orutti pātattōṭu orutti pātattaic cērttu vaittu, ovvoruttiyum iru muḻaṅkaiyālum iru vilāvaiyum iṭaiviṭātu aṭittukkoṇṭum, eṉ ulakkai kuttuk kuttu, akkā ulakkai cantaikkup pō eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkip pāṭikkoṇṭum, mella mellap peyarntu cuṟṟic cuṟṟi iyaṅkikkoṇṭē yiruppar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : tamakkaik koṉṟum taṅkaik koṉṟu mākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa īrulakkaikaḷuḷ, tamakkaiyiṉatu cantaikku viṟpaṉaikkākap pōka, taṅkaiyiṉatu taṅkippōṉatiṉāl, avaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu ataṉāl kuttiṉa ceyalai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu niṉaivu kūrvikkiṉṟatu pōlum!  (ta.nā.vi.)

 eḻuvāy 	vēṟṟumaiyaṭi
yāṉ - nāṉ 	naṉ
yām - nām	nam
yāṅkaḷ 	- nāṅkaḷ	naṅkaḷ
ma. ñāṉ; f. nāṉu, nā; bj. nēṉu, nē.
k. nām, nōm, nammaḷ; bj. maṉamu; f. nāvu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, naṉ eṉṉum vēṟṟumaiyaṭi iṟṟait tamiḻil vaḻakkiṟantatu. atu teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻi kaḷil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu kuṭiyēṟṟap pōṟṟikkāppiṉ (Colonial Preservation) pāṟpaṭum.
iṟṟai vaḻakkil illāta paḻañcoṟkaḷum col vaṭivukaḷum, muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭa ulakavaḻakkilum iṟantupaṭṭa ilakkiya vaḻakkilum iruntaṉa eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum.
naṅkaḷ eṉpatu iṟṟai ilakkiya vaḻakkil uḷḷatu.
naṅkaḷ kōṉ vacutēvaṉ peṟṟilaṉē  (tiv. perumāḷ. 7,3)
naṅkaḷ varivaḷai yāyaṅkāḷō  (tiv. tiruvāy. 8, 2, 1)
kūrmati vāynta kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaḷ, yakara mutal nakara mutalākat tirinta nilaiyaip payaṉpaṭutti, paṉmai vaṭivil avviru mutalukkum piṉvarumāṟu vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭit tamiḻaip paṇpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.
yām, yāṅkaḷ = taṉit taṉmaip paṉmai allatu paṭarkkai yuḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmai.
nām, nāṅkaḷ = muṉṉilai yuḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmai.
ikkāla vaḻakkil, yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum coṟkup pakaramāka (patilāka), nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu tavaṟāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. itaṟku yāvaṭiccol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatē kāraṇam. taṉṉaiyum taṉṉōṭirukkum piṟaraiyum maṭṭum cērttuk kuṟippatu taṉit taṉmaip paṉmaiyām.
nām eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu ām īṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. atu am eṉṟu kuṟukavuñ ceyyum. ivviraṇṭum taṉmaiyoṭu muṉṉilaiyai uḷappaṭuttum.
e-ṭu: nām vantām, nām vantam, nām vantaṉam.
ām īṟu ōm eṉṟum tiriyum. itu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil paṭarkkaiyaiyum, ulaka vaḻakkil muṉṉilai paṭarkkai yiraṇṭaiyum, uḷappaṭuttum.
e-ṭu: yām (nāṉum avaṉum, nāṉum avarum, yāmum avaṉum, yāmum avarum) vantōm - ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum ulaka vaḻakkum.
nām (nāṉum nīyum, nāṉum nīrum) vantōm - ulaka vaḻakku.
amām eṉpaṉa muṉṉilai yāraiyum
emēm ōmivai paṭarkkai yāraiyum
umūr kaṭataṟa irupā lāraiyum 
taṉṉoṭu paṭukkum taṉmaip paṉmai  (naṉ. 332)
umūr kaṭataṟa (kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum). ēkāravaṭiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟāmaiyāl iṅkuk kuṟikkap paṭṭila.
ē 2. (viṉāccollaṭi)
ē = enta. e-ṭu: ēvūr = entavūr. itu teluṅka vaḻakku. ip peyarecca vaḻakku iṟṟait tamiḻil aṟṟatu.
ē īṟṟu viṉā veḻuttākavum varum.
e-ṭu:: vantāṉē = vantāṉā.
ētu = 1. etu. 2. eppaṭik kiṭaittatu.
ē - ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar, ētu, ēvai.
ēṉ = eṉṉa kāraṇam paṟṟi ka. ma. ēṉ.
ē - yā. yā = yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yāvatu, yāvai.
yā = yāvai. yāvar - yār - ār. yāvatu - yātu.

A.S. smoethe, E. smooth, LG. smoedig, G. schmeidig, metu - mel = metuvu, metuvāku, metuvākumpaṭi pallāl arai.
E. mellow, soft and ripe, A.S. mearu, Dut. mollig, L. mollis. Gr. malakos, soft. E. mollify, to make soft, to calm. L. mollusc, one of those animals which have a soft body. E melt, A.S. meltan, to soften, to become liquid.
E. mill, A.S. miln, G. muhle, L. mola, a mill-molo, to grind. Skt. mrid. to bruise.
mikal - mikkili(te.). Goth. mikilis, Ice. mjok, A.S. micel, O.E. michel, muchel, E. much. mī - miñcu - viñcu. miñcu + am = miccam; miñcu + il = miccal. micai = miñciyatai uṇ, uṇ. miccil micaivāṉ pulam (kuṟaḷ. 85). Cf. E. mess, to eat. O. F. mes, F. mets.
celavuk karuttu. ē = mēṟpaṭu, mēṟcel, acai, cel, poruntu, aṭai, aṟi. ē - iya - iyal. iya -iyaṅku - iyakkam. iyaṅku = acai, cel. iyakkam = acaivu, celavu, kiḷarcci. iya - iyavu = cellum vaḻi. iya = acai, cel. iyavu - iyavuḷ = vaḻic celuttum kaṭavuḷ. iyal = (vi.). acai, naṭa, nikaḻ, ceyyamuṭi, ceyyappaṭu, tōṉṟu; (pe.) naṭakkai, oḻukkam, taṉmai, taṉmaiyaik kūṟum nūṟpakuti, tōṟṟam, iyaṟkai. iyal - iyalvu - iyalpu = taṉmai, iyaṟkai. iyal + kai = iyaṟkai. iyal - ēl = nikaḻ, ceyyamuṭi. ēl + paṭu = ēṟpaṭu = nikaḻ, tōṉṟu, amai. ēṟpaṭu - ēṟpāṭu.
iya + am = iyam = tōṟṟam, tōṟṟam kuṟikkum īṟu. iyam - am - m. e-ṭu:: paṇ - paṇṇiyam, tāy - tāyam. āciriyaṉ - āciriyam (oru vakaippā). muttuvīriyam tolkāppiyam mutaliya nūṟ peyarkaḷum iyam īṟu peṟṟavaiyē.
ē = cel. ē - yā - jā - ga - go. yā + tirai = yāttirai - yātrā - jātrā. (i.) tirai oru toḻiṟpeyar vikuti. Dan, A.S. gaa, gan, E. go, G. gehen,Skt. gam. ē - ēku = cel. ē - ēvu = celuttu, tūṇṭu. ē = celuttum ampu. ē - ey = ampaic celuttu. eynar - eyiṉar = ampeyyum vēṭar. eyil = ampeyyum matil, matil.
ē - ēy = poruntu, oppāku, uṇmai pōlac colli ēmāṟṟu. ēya = porunta, pōla (uvamaiyurupu). ē - ey = poruntu. aṭai, kuṟi. eyyāmaiyē aṟiyāmaiyē (tol.) ey - eytu = aṭai. ē - iya - iyai - icai - iṇai - piṇai, icai = poruntu, oppāku, iṇaṅku, poruttu. ca - ṉa - ṇa pōli. e-ṭu::  pūcai - pūṉai. pōṭu - A.S. potian, L. positus - pose - pono, to place. iṇai - iṇar = kottu.
taṉmaip peyar. ē = uyarvu. nāṉ eṉṉum akaṅkāram māntaṉukku iyalpāka vuṇmaiyāl, muntiyal tamiḻaṉ taṟperumaiyum taṉṉalamum paṟṟi ēkārac cuṭṭāl taṉṉaik kuṟittāṉ.
ēṉ - yāṉ - nāṉ (orumai).
ēm - yām - nām (paṉmai).
viṉāppeyar. oru poruḷai etuveṉṟu viṉavum pōtu, oru kūṭṭamāṉa poruḷkaḷil oṉṟai mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭac colvatu pōlirukkiṟatu. itaṉāl, viṉāk karuttu eḻukaic cuṭṭil tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ē = enta (etu, evai). ē - e.
ētu - etu - etā - etō - etōḷi. etu - etaṉ. ēṉ > ēvaṉ > evaṉ. ēm > ēvam > evam. ēṉ - eṉ - eṉṉē - eṉṉai.
ē - ev. evaḷ - evaṇ.
evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evai.
eṉ - eṉṉa = ettakaiya. eṉ - eṉai = evvaḷavu. eṉ - entu - enta - entā. em - emmai. el + tu = eḵtu. el + tu = eṉṟu. el - ella = evai. ella + um = ellavum - ellām. o. nō: yā + um = yāvum = ellām.
ellām. A.S. eal, G. all, Gael, uile, W. oll, E. all.
iṉi, alla + um = allavum - allām - ellām eṉṟu mām, o.nō: aṉai - aṉaittu - aṉaittum, allām eṉṟu nāṭṭuppuṟa vaḻakkumuṇṭu.
ē - yā. yātu (o.) ah, ahit (g.), (ēṇṭu) - yāṇṭu. (ēṅku) - yāṅku. ēṅku - eṅku.
yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yāvatu, yāvai.
yār - ār. yā - ā - ō. o. nō: vantāṉ - vantōṉ.
ē - ā - ō īṟṟu viṉā veḻuttukaḷ.
e-ṭu: vantāṉē? vantāṉā? vantāṉō?
ōkāram uyarattaik kuṟittālum, ataṉaṭiyāy oru viṉāc collum piṟavāmaiyiṉ, atai yāviṉ kuṟaiyākiya ākārattiṉ tiripeṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum. āvōvākum peyaru māruḷavē  (tol. 680)  (cu.vi.)
ēkāraviṭaiccol.
ē = uyar, mēṟcel, toṭar.
eḻutikkoṇṭēpō = go on writing, oṉṟēkāl = one and a quarter. mēl eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum on eṉṉum col āṅkilattil toṭarcci kuṟittalaik kavaṉikka.
aracaṉē aṉmuṟaiyāy naṭantāl āriṭam colvatu eṉṉum vākkiyattil, ēkāram uyarvup poruḷ kuṟittatu.  (cu.vi.)
ētam
ētam : citaittal = kulaittal, veṭṭutal, aḻittal. citai - cit (va.). citai - cētam - ētam = kēṭu, kuṟṟam. cētam - cēta (va.).  (ti.ma. 738).
ētu
ētu - va. hētu
ē - ēvu - ēcu - ētu.
ēcutal = ēvutal, celuttutal.
kollam pēci  (tēvā. 380 : 6).
ētu = ēvukai, tūṇṭukai, kāraṇam.
ētu nikaḻcci = karumaṅkaḷākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷ tattam payaṉaik koṭuttataṟkut tōṉṟukai.
ētu nikaḻcci yetirntuḷa tātaliṉ  (maṇi. 3 : 4)
ētu - ētaṉ = mūlak kāraṇaṉāṉa kaṭavuḷ.
ētaṉai yētamilā vimaiyōr toḻum vētaṉai  (tēvā. 471:3)
vaṭavar vaṭacoṟkuk kūṟum vērpporuḷum tūṇṭutal eṉpatē. ataṟkuk kāṭṭum vēr ēvutaṟ poruḷuḷḷa hi eṉpatākum.
kūṟuta lucāta lētīṭu talaippāṭu  (tol. po. 207)
ētīṭu = kāraṇamiṭṭuraittal
ēmappuṇai
ēmappuṇaiyāvatu naṭukkaṭalil kappal mūḻkum pōtu kalavar ēṟit tappum ēmappaṭaku (Life-boat). vāḻkkaik kaṭalaik kaṭakkum illaṟattārkku iṭukkaṇ kālattil utavik kākkum iṉattār ēmappuṇaiyār. piṟavik kaṭalaik kaṭakkum tuṟavu payilvārkkut tava vōkaṅkaḷil tavaṟu nērntu avar keṭum nilaiyil avarait tirutti āṟṟuppaṭuttum muḻuttuṟavar ēmappuṇaiyār.  (ti.ma. 176).
